### [1] [Equivariant Latent Alignment via Flow Matching under Group Symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30705)
*Sunghyun Kim, Jaehoon Hahm, Jeongwoo Shin, Joonseok Lee*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 值得关注 (79.09) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Residual Latent Flow，一种基于流匹配的框架，用于修正等变表示学习中潜空间的错位问题，从而提升新视角合成在旋转群 SO(n) 下的几何一致性和视觉质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有等变表示学习方法在构建潜空间时，理论上要求群变换在潜空间中直接作用，但实际学习到的潜空间往往存在"潜错位"（latent misalignment），即预期的群作用与实际所需的变换之间存在不一致，导致新视角合成质量下降。作者旨在解决这一错位问题。

Method: 提出 Residual Latent Flow 框架，利用流匹配（flow matching）对错位的潜编码进行修正，使其更好地满足等变关系。具体地，学习一个残差流场来校正潜空间中的表示，使其与底层群对称性（如 SO(n) 旋转群）保持一致。

Result: 实验表明，该方法显著减少了潜空间错位，并在旋转群 SO(n) 下的新视角合成任务中提升了视觉质量和几何一致性。

Conclusion: Residual Latent Flow 是一种有效的潜空间对齐方法，能够增强等变表示学习在新视角合成中的表现，为几何感知生成模型提供了新的改进思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于流匹配的等变潜空间对齐方法，用于改进新视角合成（novel view synthesis）的质量和几何一致性，属于 3D 生成与重建方向下的新视角合成任务，因此映射到 canonical subtopic "3D 生成与重建"。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3D equivariant latent alignment for novel view synthesis, which is not a top priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to user's core interests in autoregressive video/image generation or continuous VLM. Signals: Equivariant representation learning, Flow matching for latent correction, Novel view synthesis, SO(n) rotation groups, Not aligned with user's priority subtopics or boost keywords

Abstract: Geometry-aware generative models and novel view synthesis approaches have shown strong potential in visual fidelity and consistency. In parallel, equivariant representation learning has emerged as a powerful framework for constructing latent spaces where analytically known group transformations could act directly, capturing geometric structure in data and enhancing both interpretability and generalization in novel view synthesis. However, we identify that existing approaches often suffer from latent misalignment, a discrepancy between the intended group action and the actually required transformations in the latent space. Consequently, the learned latents often fail to consistently preserve the equivariant relations imposed by the underlying group symmetry. To address this, we propose Residual Latent Flow, a flow-based framework that corrects the misaligned latents, thereby improving compliance with the underlying equivariance relation. Our comprehensive experiments show that our method significantly reduces latent misalignment and improves novel view synthesis quality, under rotation groups SO(n).

</details>


### [2] [Function2Scene: 3D Indoor Scene Layout from Functional Specifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30819)
*Ruiqi Wang, Qimin Chen, Daniel Ritchie, Angel X. Chang, Manolis Savva, Kai Wang, Hao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 普通相关 (59.93) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Function2Scene 提出从功能规格（自然语言设计简报）生成3D室内布局的框架，通过解析用户角色和活动，从17条功能设计约束（空间、人体工学、活动、环境）出发，结合几何测量、LLM推理和VLM视觉评估的迭代检查-修复循环，生成更符合人类使用需求的室内场景布局。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本驱动的3D室内场景合成方法大多基于物体中心提示（"放什么家具"），而非从空间如何使用（"谁使用、做什么活动"）出发。然而真实室内设计的关键在于布局能否支持 occupants 的活动和物理需求。作者希望将室内场景生成从"放置合理物体"转向"设计支持人类使用的空间"。

Method: Function2Scene 包含三个模块：(1) 功能解析器，从自然语言设计简报中解析 occupant 角色和活动，并从17条功能设计约束（空间、人体工学、活动、环境四类）中推导定制化约束集；(2) 布局生成器，基于约束引导生成初始布局；(3) 检查-修复循环，通过工具增强的几何测量、LLM上下文推理和VLM视觉评估迭代评估和优化布局，而非让LLM直接生成最终场景。

Result: 在30个专业室内设计案例上的实验表明，Function2Scene 生成的布局比最近的LLM-based场景合成基线更好地满足功能需求，在94.3%的成对比较中更受偏好。

Conclusion: Function2Scene 成功将文本驱动的室内场景合成从"放置合理物体"重新定义为"设计支持人类使用的空间"，通过功能规格驱动的迭代优化框架，显著提升了布局的功能合理性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究从功能规格（自然语言设计简报）生成3D室内场景布局，属于3D场景生成范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建"高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with multimodal generation and VLM-based iterative refinement, but lacks direct connection to priority subtopics like continuous VLM or autoregressive video generation. Signals: multimodal generation, VLM visual assessment, LLM+geometric reasoning, 3D layout from functional specs, iterative check-and-repair loop

Abstract: Most text-driven 3D indoor scene synthesis methods generate rooms from object-centric prompts, asking what furniture should be placed rather than how the space is used. Yet in real interior design, a layout is judged by how well it supports its occupants, e.g., their activities and physical needs. We introduce Function2Scene, a framework for generating 3D indoor layouts from functional specifications, i.e., natural-language design briefs describing who will use a room and what they need to do there. Given such a specification, our system parses occupant personas and activities, derives a customized set of functional design constraints from a taxonomy of 17 criteria spanning spatial, ergonomic, activity, and environmental considerations, and uses these constraints to guide layout generation. Rather than relying on an LLM to directly produce a final scene, Function2Scene performs iterative evaluation and refinement through a tool-augmented check-and-repair loop, combining geometric measurements, LLM-based contextual reasoning, and VLM-based visual assessment. Experiments on 30 professionally written interior-design cases show that Function2Scene produces layouts that better satisfy functional requirements than recent LLM-based scene synthesis baselines, with our results preferred in 94.3% of pairwise comparisons. Our work reframes text-driven indoor scene synthesis from placing plausible objects to designing spaces that support human use.

</details>


### [3] [Foundation VAEs for 3D CT Reconstruction, Augmentation, and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30893)
*Qi Chen, Shuhan Ding, Yu Gu, Nan Liu, Jiang Bian, Alan Yuille, Zongwei Zhou, Jingjing Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 普通相关 (41.84) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文发现，在自然图像和视频上大规模预训练的 Foundation VAE，其编码器和解码器冻结后，可作为 CT 重建、增强和生成的统一接口。该方法在保持解剖结构的同时抑制采集噪声，训练分割模型后表面精度提升 3.9% NSD；在潜空间中训练条件潜扩散模型，FVD 降低 3.9%，CT CLIP 分数提升 36.2%，18 种疾病的生成忠实度提升 2.76% AUC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练 CT 专用的 VAE 需要大量计算和工程成本，且在不同扫描仪、协议和疾病下性能容易退化。作者希望探索是否可以利用在自然图像和视频上预训练的 Foundation VAE（无需微调）作为 CT 重建、增强和生成的统一接口，降低计算开销并提升泛化能力。

Method: 作者利用在自然图像和视频上大规模预训练的 Foundation VAE（编码器和解码器均冻结），将 CT 体素数据压缩到紧凑的潜空间中。在潜空间中：(1) 直接通过 VAE 解码器重建 CT 体素，实现噪声抑制与结构保持；(2) 训练条件潜扩散模型进行 CT 生成。所有操作均在冻结的 Foundation VAE 潜空间中进行，无需对 VAE 进行任何微调。

Result: 在胰腺肿瘤和肺肿瘤数据集上，使用重建后的 CT 训练分割模型，表面 Dice（NSD）平均提升 3.9%。在潜空间中训练的条件潜扩散模型，FVD 降低 3.9%，CT CLIP 分数提升 36.2%，18 种疾病的生成忠实度提升 2.76% AUC。

Conclusion: Foundation VAE 可以作为 CT 重建、增强和生成的可扩展统一接口，无需针对 CT 数据进行微调即可实现良好的性能，为医学影像的表示复用和忠实生成提供了实用方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究基于 Foundation VAE 的 CT 体素重建、增强与生成，属于 3D 医学影像生成与重建范畴。3D 生成与重建（three_d_generation）是 multimodal_generation 下的 [CANONICAL] 子主题，涵盖 3D 物体/场景生成与重建，CT 体素重建是其中的重要应用方向。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical CT reconstruction, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and is flagged by negative keywords. Signals: Medical imaging, CT reconstruction, Foundation VAE, 3D generation, Negative keyword match

Abstract: Variational autoencoders (VAEs) compress high resolution CT volumes into compact latents while preserving clinically relevant structure. However, training CT-specific VAEs from scratch or heavily fine-tuning them incurs substantial computational and engineering cost, and often degrades under heterogeneous scanners, protocols, and diseases. This paper makes a progressive stride toward training-free medical VAEs by leveraging a critical observation: a single Foundation VAE, pretrained at scale on natural images and videos, can serve as a unified interface for CT Reconstruction, Augmentation, and Generation. With both encoder and decoder frozen, the Foundation VAE reconstructs CT volumes with preserved anatomy while suppressing acquisition noise; training segmentation models on these reconstructions improves surface accuracy by 3.9% NSD on average for pancreatic tumor and lung tumor. Within the same Foundation VAE latent space, a conditional latent diffusion model achieves 3.9% lower average FVD with 36.2% higher CT CLIP score, and improves multi-disease generation faithfulness across 18 types by 2.76% AUC. These results demonstrate Foundation VAEs as a practical interface for scalable CT representation reuse and faithful CT generation. Our code and demo are available at .

</details>


### [4] [Benchmarking Single-Step Inpainting Methods for Multi-Object 3D Gaussian Splatting Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30987)
*Finn Dröge, Cecilia Curreli, Abhishek Saroha, Daniel Cremers*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 普通相关 (50.26) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了单步修复方法在多物体3DGS场景中的物体移除与修复效果，发现基于重建的2D修复器在3D一致性上优于生成式扩散模型，从头初始化场景比微调现有场景效果更好，并提出了一个包含真实标注的多物体360°场景数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS场景中的物体移除与修复面临跨视角3D一致性的挑战，现有方法缺乏系统性的比较基准，且360°数据集缺少真实标注和遮挡场景。

Method: 比较多种2D修复器（重建型 vs 生成扩散型）在3DGS场景中的适用性，将2D修复器集成到不同的单步方法中（从头初始化场景 vs 微调现有场景），并引入一个包含真实标注的多物体场景数据集进行评测。

Result: 重建型修复器在3D一致性上优于生成扩散模型；从头初始化场景比微调现有场景产生更高质量的结果；在3D修复前先进行物体移除至关重要。

Conclusion: 为3DGS场景修复提供了系统的基准比较，揭示了不同2D修复器和初始化策略的优劣，并贡献了一个新的多物体场景数据集以推动该领域研究。

Classification reason: 论文聚焦于3D高斯泼溅(3DGS)场景中的物体移除与修复方法，属于3D场景生成与重建范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建"高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3D inpainting benchmarks, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous VLM, autoregressive video/image generation, or world models. Signals: 3D Gaussian Splatting, object removal, inpainting benchmark, reconstruction-based vs. generative, 360° dataset

Abstract: The tasks of object removal and inpainting 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) scenes face challenges such as 3D consistency across camera views. In comparing 2D inpainters and their suitability for the 3D domain, we find that reconstruction-based inpainters outperform generative diffusion models in 3D consistency. Integrating these 2D inpainters into different single-step methods for creating and finetuning 3DGS scenes, our results indicate that initializing the scene from scratch produces higher quality results than finetuning the existing scene. Using a state-of-the-art generative 2D inpainter, we create a straightforward baseline to underline the importance of object removal before inpainting in the 3D setting. Since 360° datasets rarely include real-world ground truths, and challenging occlusion scenarios are equally sparse, we introduce a novel multi-object scene with recorded ground truth data and many views with object occlusions.

</details>


### [5] [Topologically Consistent Multi-view 3D Head Reconstruction via Coarse-Guided Layered Surface Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31283)
*Timo Bolkart, Daoye Wang, Prashanth Chandran*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 普通相关 (52.09) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 SHELLS，一种高效的前馈式多视图3D头部重建框架。通过分层采样策略将特征提取与网格分辨率解耦，利用DINOv2+LoRA提取多视图特征，先预测稀疏粗网格，再构建分层表面感知采样壳进行精细重建。相比体积基线方法，GPU内存减少88%，推理速度提升3.5倍，注册误差降低21%-29%，且仅用合成数据训练即可泛化到真实场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D头部重建方法通常通过局部特征体素独立优化顶点，导致内存消耗与网格分辨率强耦合，难以扩展到高密度拓扑（>10k顶点），且易产生表面噪声。此外，这些方法依赖昂贵的预注册多视图数据集。本文旨在解决这些可扩展性和数据依赖问题。

Method: SHELLS 采用分层采样策略：1）使用 DINOv2 骨干网络结合 LoRA 适配提取多视图特征；2）投影采样稀疏全局特征云，预测中间粗网格作为先验；3）基于粗网格构建分层的表面感知采样壳（layered surface-aware sampling shells），作为精细重建的离散搜索空间；4）在采样壳上进行局部特征聚合，预测最终高密度网格。整个过程是前馈式的，无需测试时优化。

Result: 在18k顶点网格上，SHELLS 相比体积基线方法：GPU推理内存减少88%（2.4GB vs 20GB），中位注册误差降低21%-29%，推理速度提升3.5倍（0.08s vs 0.29s）。模型仅用合成数据训练，即可有效泛化到真实世界拍摄数据。

Conclusion: SHELLS 通过粗到细的分层采样策略，成功解耦了特征提取与网格分辨率，实现了高效、可扩展的多视图3D头部重建。该方法在保持表面一致性的同时大幅降低了计算开销，且摆脱了对昂贵真实多视图标注数据的依赖，为高密度拓扑3D重建提供了实用方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出从多视图图像进行3D头部重建的框架，属于3D网格生成与重建范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建"（three_d_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3D head reconstruction, which is not a priority subtopic, and lacks direct connection to the user's key interests in autoregressive generation, continuous VLMs, or world models. Signals: Efficient feed-forward reconstruction, 88% GPU memory reduction, synthetic-to-real generalization, not aligned with priority subtopics, no autoregressive or diffusion generation focus

Abstract: We present SHELLS (Semantic Head Estimation via Layered Local Sampling), an efficient feed-forward framework for 3D head reconstruction in dense semantic correspondence from multi-view images. Existing methods typically refine vertices independently via localized feature volumes. This approach couples memory-intensive feature sampling to mesh resolution, which limits scalability for dense topologies (> 10k vertices) and introduces surface noise. In contrast, SHELLS decouples feature extraction from mesh resolution via a hierarchical sampling strategy. We extract multi-view features using a DINOv2 backbone with LoRA adaptation, projectively sample a sparse global feature cloud, and predict an intermediate coarse mesh. This coarse prior guides the construction of layered, surface-aware sampling shells that serve as a discrete search space for the final reconstruction. SHELLS maintains surface consistency while using 88% less inference GPU memory (2.4GB vs. 20GB) than volumetric baselines. It reduces median registration error by 21% to 29% with a 3.5x inference speedup (0.08s vs. 0.29s) for 18k-vertex meshes. Notably, our model is trained exclusively on synthetic data yet generalizes effectively to real-world captures, eliminating the need for the costly, pre-registered multi-view datasets common in prior work.

</details>


### [6] [VolFill: Single-View Amodal 3D Scene Reconstruction with Volumetric Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31466)
*Tuan Duc Ngo, Chuang Gan, Evangelos Kalogerakis*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 高优先级 (82.59) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: VolFill 提出一种基于体积流匹配的生成式框架，从单张RGB图像重建完整3D场景。该方法使用混合3D VAE将稀疏截断有符号距离函数网格压缩到紧凑潜空间，再通过潜空间扩散Transformer去噪恢复完整场景，并利用几何基础模型提供空间先验。在SCRREAM和NRGB-D数据集上显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单张RGB图像重建场景的完整几何结构（包括被遮挡部分）极具挑战性，现有方法受限于逐射线约束或非结构化点云查询，难以推断隐藏结构。作者希望提出一种生成式方法，直接预测完整3D场景结构而非依赖像素对齐回归。

Method: VolFill 采用混合3D VAE将稀疏TUSDF（截断有符号距离函数）网格压缩为紧凑潜空间表示，然后使用潜空间扩散Transformer（DiT）对该表示进行去噪，恢复完整场景。模型以几何基础模型（如深度估计、法线估计等）提取的空间先验作为条件，实现鲁棒的推理。最终输出支持直接表面提取和大规模占据查询的结构化表示。

Result: 在SCRREAM和NRGB-D数据集上的大量实验表明，VolFill在完整场景重建质量上显著优于现有基线方法，能够有效恢复被遮挡区域的几何结构。

Conclusion: VolFill 提供了一种鲁棒的生成式框架用于单视图完整3D场景重建，为整体空间理解奠定了坚实基础。其结构化表示和生成式方法为后续3D场景理解任务提供了新的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究从单张RGB图像重建完整3D场景几何结构，属于3D场景生成与重建任务，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建"（three_d_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong 3D reconstruction method but only tangentially related to your priority subtopics on continuous VLMs and autoregressive video/image generation. Signals: 3D scene reconstruction, volumetric flow matching, single-image to 3D, diffusion transformer, geometry foundation model

Abstract: Reconstructing the complete geometry of a scene from a single RGB image remains challenging - especially when inferring hidden structures where visual evidence is incomplete. We introduce VolFill, a generative framework that predicts the 3D structure of the complete scene rather than relying on traditional pixel-aligned regression. Our method utilizes a hybrid 3D VAE to compress sparse truncated unsigned distance function grids into a compact latent space, paired with a latent Diffusion Transformer that denoises this representation to recover the complete scene. We condition the generation on geometry foundation models, leveraging rich spatial priors for robust reasoning. Unlike existing methods limited by per-ray constraints or unstructured point-cloud queries, VolFill provides a structured representation that supports direct surface extraction and occupancy queries at scale. Extensive experiments on the SCRREAM and NRGB-D datasets demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms current baselines, providing a robust foundation for holistic spatial understanding.

</details>

*Ulrich Prestel, Stefan Andreas Baumann, Nick Stracke, Björn Ommer*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.LG, cs.CV

TL;DR: RayDer 是一个统一的、前馈的 Transformer 架构，将相机估计、场景重建和渲染整合到单一骨干网络中，将自监督新视角合成转化为一个良态的单模型缩放问题。通过引入最小动态状态吸收时变内容，实现在无约束真实世界视频上的稳定训练，并展现出清晰的数据/计算幂律缩放行为，零样本性能可与有监督方法竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督新视角合成（NVS）虽然可以利用丰富的视频数据，但训练过程对真实视频的脆弱性以及多网络系统设计的不可预测缩放行为，使其难以规模化扩展。现有方法通常将相机估计、场景重建和渲染分离为多个网络，导致系统复杂且缩放行为难以预测。

Method: RayDer 提出一个统一的、前馈的 Transformer 架构，将相机估计、场景重建和渲染整合到单一骨干网络中。引入一个最小动态状态（minimal dynamic state）作为干扰因子，吸收视频中的时变内容（如运动、光照变化），使模型能够在无约束的真实世界视频上稳定训练。动态内容仅作为可扩展的监督信号，而目标任务仍然是静态场景 NVS，不进行动态场景（4D）重建。

Result: 在多个模型规模和数量级的数据上，RayDer 展现出清晰的数据和计算幂律缩放行为，优于静态场景数据混合。在大量基准测试上，零样本开放集性能与最先进的有监督方法相当。

Conclusion: RayDer 通过统一架构和最小动态状态设计，成功将自监督 NVS 转化为可预测缩放的单一模型问题，证明了利用真实世界视频进行自监督 NVS 的可行性和可扩展性，为大规模 3D 场景生成提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究从真实世界视频中进行自监督新视角合成（NVS），属于3D场景生成与重建范畴，与 canonical subtopic "3D 生成与重建" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong scaling laws and unified architecture for NVS, but tangential to user's core focus on autoregressive/diffusion video generation and continuous VLMs. Signals: Unified transformer for camera, scene, rendering, clean power-law scaling, self-supervised from real video, zero-shot competitive with supervised, dynamic state as nuisance factor

Abstract: Self-supervised novel view synthesis (NVS) remains challenging to scale, despite the abundance of video data, largely due to the brittleness of training on realistic videos and the hard-to-predict scaling behavior of multi-network system designs. We introduce RayDer, a unified, feed-forward transformer that consolidates camera estimation, scene reconstruction, and rendering into a single backbone, turning self-supervised NVS into a well-posed single-model scaling problem. A minimal dynamic state, treated as a nuisance factor, absorbs time-varying content and enables stable training on unconstrained real-world video. Importantly, RayDer keeps static-scene NVS as its target task: dynamic content is leveraged purely as scalable supervision, not reconstructed as in dynamic-scene (4D) NVS. Across multiple model sizes and orders of magnitude in data, RayDer exhibits clean power-law scaling with data and compute, and outperforms static-scene data mixtures. On a large number of benchmarks, RayDer achieves strong zero-shot open-set performance competitive with state-of-the-art supervised approaches. Project Page:

### [8] [Learning Global Motion with Compact Gaussians for Feed-Forward 4D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31595)
*Mungyeom Kim, Minkyeong Jeon, Honggyu An, Jaewoo Jung, Hyuna Ko, Jisang Han, Hyeonseo Yu, Donghwan Shin, Sunghwan Hong, Takuya Narihira, Kazumi Fukuda, Yuki Mitsufuji, Seungryong Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 值得关注 (77.44) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: C4G 提出一种前馈式 4D 重建框架，使用紧凑的、带时间戳条件的可学习高斯查询令牌，在全局时间上下文中聚合特征并解码 3D 高斯，实现无需逐场景优化的全局一致运动建模，并引入视频扩散模型增强渲染质量，支持新视角合成、点跟踪和动态场景理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈式动态场景重建方法逐像素预测每帧的 3D 高斯，导致高斯重复和视角依赖偏差，难以有效学习场景运动。本文旨在设计一种无需逐场景优化、能全局建模运动的前馈式 4D 重建方法。

Method: 提出 C4G 框架，核心是紧凑的、带时间戳条件的可学习高斯查询令牌。每个令牌在全时间上下文中聚合对应特征，解码出位置由目标时间戳调制的 3D 高斯，实现全局一致的运动建模。此外引入基于视频扩散模型的渲染增强模块，并将特征提升扩展到 4D 特征场以支持点跟踪和动态场景理解。

Result: C4G 在使用显著更少高斯且无需相机位姿的情况下，实现了强竞争力的新视角合成性能，展现出更强的运动建模能力和对大时间间隔的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: C4G 通过紧凑高斯查询令牌实现了高效的前馈式 4D 重建，在减少高斯数量的同时提升了运动建模质量，为动态场景重建提供了一种无需逐场景优化的新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出前馈式4D重建框架C4G，基于紧凑可学习高斯查询令牌实现动态场景重建和新视角合成，属于3D/4D场景生成与重建范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建"（three_d_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel feed-forward 4D reconstruction with compact Gaussians, but tangential to user's core focus on autoregressive generation and continuous VLMs. Signals: 4D reconstruction, feed-forward, compact Gaussians, video diffusion enhancement, novel-view synthesis

Abstract: Dynamic scene reconstruction from monocular video remains a fundamental challenge in computer vision. Existing feed-forward methods predict 3D Gaussians pixel-wise for each frame, suffering from duplicated Gaussians and view-dependent biases that hinder effective learning of scene motion. We present C4G, a feed-forward 4D reconstruction framework built upon a compact set of timestamp-conditioned learnable Gaussian query tokens. Each token aggregates corresponding features across the full temporal context and decodes a 3D Gaussian whose position is modulated by the target timestamp, enabling globally coherent motion modeling without per-scene optimization. To capture fine-grained details, we further introduce a video diffusion model-based rendering enhancement module. Since our framework effectively aggregates features into Gaussians, we extend this capability to feature lifting, producing a 4D feature field that supports point tracking and dynamic scene understanding. C4G achieves strong novel-view synthesis performance using significantly fewer Gaussians and without requiring camera poses, while exhibiting stronger motion modeling and robustness to large temporal gaps.

</details>


### [9] [Smaller and Faster 3DGS via Post-Training Dictionary Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30396)
*Jiarong Gong, Jonas Unger, Ehsan Miandji*

Main category: cs.GR, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 值得关注 (67.04) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出首个基于字典学习的 3DGS 后训练压缩框架，无需重新训练即可压缩任意 3DGS 模型，在 13 个基准场景上平均压缩比达 3.95x，同时提升渲染速度约 24%，并保持图像质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS 模型在训练后通常占用大量内存，限制了其在资源受限设备上的部署。现有压缩技术往往引入额外可训练参数，虽然压缩比高但会带来明显的图像质量下降。本文旨在设计一种无需重新训练、不修改模型结构、同时兼顾压缩率、渲染速度与图像质量的轻量化压缩方案。

Method: 提出基于字典学习的后训练压缩框架。将 3DGS 的高斯属性（如协方差、颜色、不透明度等）视为信号，通过字典学习（Dictionary Learning）进行稀疏编码，用少量字典原子线性组合近似原始高斯属性，从而实现压缩。该框架可直接应用于任意已训练的 3DGS 模型，无需重新训练或修改模型架构。

Result: 在 13 个基准场景上，对 3DGS、3DGS-MCMC 和 PixelGS 分别实现平均 3.95x、3.10x 和 4.55x 的压缩比，同时渲染速度分别提升 23.3%、24.3% 和 25.3%，且图像质量保持稳定。

Conclusion: 字典学习是一种有效的 3DGS 后训练压缩手段，能在显著降低存储开销和提升渲染速度的同时保持图像质量，且具有即插即用的通用性，适合部署到低算力设备上。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) 的压缩方法，属于 3D 场景表示与渲染的范畴，与 canonical 子主题 "3D 生成与重建"（three_d_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3DGS compression, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like continuous VLM or autoregressive video generation. Signals: 3DGS compression, dictionary learning, post-training, rendering speedup, no retraining needed

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) is a promising neural scene representation for real-time rendering, but trained models often suffer from large memory footprints, limiting deployment on less powerful devices. Existing compression techniques often lead to architectures with several additional trainable parameters. While achieving outstanding compression ratios, they introduce noticeable drops in image quality. In this work, we introduce the first dictionary-learning-based compression framework for 3DGS. The proposed post-training compression pipeline can be deployed in virtually any 3DGS model without the need for re-training or modifications to existing 3DGS models. Our compression framework is straightforward to implement, yet provides significant compression capabilities, preserves image quality, and improves real-time rendering performance. Across 13 benchmark scenes, our approach achieves an average compression ratio of 3.95x, 3.10x, and 4.55x when applied to 3DGS, 3DGS-MCMC, and PixelGS, respectively. This yields consistent rendering speedups of 23.3%, 24.3%, and 25.3%, while maintaining image quality.

</details>

### [10] [DSD-GS: Dynamic-Static Decomposition of Gaussian Splatting for Efficient and High-Fidelity Dynamic Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30863)
*Youngtae Han, Sung-hwan Han, Youngmin Yi*

Main category: cs.GR, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 4D 与动态场景生成

Importance: 普通相关 (55.34) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出DSD-GS框架，通过动静分解策略将3D高斯泼溅分为静态和动态组件，利用前馈编码器和光流模型减少静态区域冗余计算，实现高效高保真动态场景重建，在Neural 3D数据集上仅需10分钟训练，渲染速度超700 FPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态3DGS方法将所有高斯建模为动态组件，计算效率低下；而基于分解的方法虽有所改进，但仍存在重建质量下降和训练时间过长的问题。本文旨在通过高效的动静分解策略同时提升重建质量、训练/渲染速度和存储效率。

Method: 提出DSD-GS框架，核心是动静分解策略：使用前馈高斯泼溅编码器（Feed-Forward Gaussian Splatting encoder）和光流模型，将场景中的高斯分为静态和动态组件。静态区域仅需一次性重建，动态区域单独建模，从而消除静态区域的冗余计算。该方法无需COLMAP预处理，支持确定性初始化。

Result: 在Neural 3D数据集上，训练仅需10分钟，渲染速度超过700 FPS（1352×1014分辨率，单张RTX 5090 GPU）。在渲染质量、训练/渲染速度和存储效率方面均达到最先进水平，优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: DSD-GS通过高效的动静分解策略，在动态场景重建中实现了质量、速度和存储效率的全面提升，且消除了COLMAP预处理需求，增强了可复现性，为动态场景重建提供了实用且高效的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究动态场景重建与新视角合成，基于3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）的动静分解策略，属于多模态生成方向下的[4D 与动态场景生成]（dynamic_4d_generation）规范子主题，该子主题明确涵盖动态3D/4D场景生成与重建、4D高斯泼溅。

Importance reason: Strong engineering contribution to dynamic scene reconstruction but outside user's priority subtopics and lacks autoregressive/diffusion video generation focus. Signals: Dynamic scene reconstruction, 3D Gaussian Splatting, static-dynamic decomposition, high FPS rendering, no COLMAP needed

Abstract: Dynamic scene reconstruction and novel view synthesis are fundamental to next-generation visual intelligence applications such as virtual reality, robotics, and digital twins. However, high-fidelity reconstruction of complex, time-varying scenes from arbitrary viewpoints remains a significant challenge. Existing dynamic 3DGS methods suffer from computational inefficiency, since they model all Gaussians as dynamic components. While recent decomposition-based approaches address this issue, they still struggle with degraded reconstruction quality and prolonged training time. To mitigate these limitations, we propose a novel dynamic reconstruction framework built upon an efficient static-dynamic decomposition strategy using a Feed-Forward Gaussian Splatting encoder and an optical flow model. By eliminating redundant computations on static regions, our method achieves state-of-the-art performance, outperforming existing baselines across rendering quality, training and rendering speed, and storage efficiency. Notably, on the Neural 3D dataset, our framework requires only 10 minutes for training and achieves a rendering speed of over 700 FPS on a single NVIDIA RTX 5090 GPU at resolution of 1352x1014. Furthermore, our decomposition strategy eliminates the need for COLMAP preprocessing and enables deterministic initialization, thereby enhancing both efficiency and reproducibility.

</details>

### [11] [GGT-100K: Generative Ground Truth for Generalizable Real-World Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31039)
*Xiangtao Kong, Jixin Zhao, Lingchen Sun, Rongyuan Wu, Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 合成数据与数据集蒸馏

TL;DR: 本文提出 Generative Ground Truth (GGT) 方法，利用多模态基础模型（如 Nano-Banana-2）从真实低质量图像生成高质量目标，构建了包含 103,707 对训练样本的 GGT-100K 数据集，显著提升了多种图像复原模型在真实场景下的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界图像复原受限于高质量配对训练数据的稀缺性。合成数据集无法建模真实退化，而真实配对数据采集成本高昂，导致现有模型在真实场景中泛化能力有限。

Method: 系统评估了9种多模态基础模型（MFM）在不同场景和退化类型上的表现，发现 Nano-Banana-2 结合 VLM 自适应提示能生成感知真实且内容忠实的高质量目标。基于此构建了包含多阶段质量控制的 GGT 合成流水线，生成了 GGT-100K 数据集（103,707 训练对 + 500 测试对）。

Result: GGT-100K 在多种图像复原模型上一致提升了真实世界泛化能力，尤其对生成式 IR 模型的微调效果显著。实验表明 MFM 可作为面向复原的数据生成实用工具。

Conclusion: 多模态基础模型能够作为实用的复原导向数据生成工具，GGT-100K 数据集有助于扩展真实世界图像复原模型的泛化边界。

Classification reason: 该论文利用多模态基础模型（MFM）从真实低质量图像生成高质量目标，构建了GGT-100K合成配对数据集用于图像复原训练，属于合成训练数据生成范畴，与现有 canonical subtopic "合成数据与数据集蒸馏"（aigc_synthetic_data）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on synthetic data for image restoration, not directly on user's priority subtopics like continuous VLM or autoregressive generation. Signals: Image restoration, synthetic data, multimodal foundation models, Nano-Banana-2, GGT-100K dataset

Abstract: Real-world image restoration (IR) is bottlenecked by the scarcity of high-quality paired training data. Synthetic datasets are abundant but often fail to model real-world degradations, while real-world paired datasets are expensive and difficult to capture. As a result, IR models trained on these datasets show limited generalization in real-world scenarios. In this work, we propose Generative Ground Truth (GGT) by using generative multimodal foundation models (MFMs) to produce high-quality (HQ) targets from real-world low-quality (LQ) images. We first conduct a systematic evaluation of nine state-of-the-art MFMs, including Nano-Banana-2 and GPT-Image-2, on images of various scenes and degradation types. The results demonstrate that Nano-Banana-2 with VLM-based adaptive prompting shows the highest capability to synthesize perceptually realistic and content-faithful HQ targets, which can serve as the GGT for the LQ input. We then employ Nano-Banana-2 to build a GGT synthesis pipeline, which involves multi-stage quality control to ensure data reliability, and construct GGT-100K, an LQ-HQ paired dataset comprising 103,707 training pairs and covering diverse scenes and complex real-world degradations. A test set of 500 image pairs is also established. Extensive experiments show that GGT-100K consistently improves the real-world generalization of a wide range of IR models, with particularly strong benefits for finetuning generative models for IR tasks. Our results suggest that MFMs can serve as practical tools for restoration-oriented data generation, and GGT-100K is a useful resource to expand the generalization boundaries of real-world IR models.

</details>

### [12] [SANA-Streaming: Real-time Streaming Video Editing with Hybrid Diffusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30409)
*Yuyang Zhao, Yicheng Pan, Qiyuan He, Jincheng Yu, Junsong Chen, Tian Ye, Haozhe Liu, Enze Xie, Song Han*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

TL;DR: SANA-Streaming 提出了一种系统-算法协同设计的框架，通过混合扩散Transformer架构、循环反向正则化训练策略和高效系统协同设计（融合GDN内核+混合精度量化），在单张RTX 5090 GPU上实现了1280×704分辨率、24 FPS端到端的实时流式视频编辑，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时流式视频到视频编辑（V2V）在直播、游戏等交互式应用中至关重要，但现有方法受限于时序一致性和推理吞吐量，难以在消费级GPU上实现高分辨率实时编辑。

Method: (1) 混合扩散Transformer架构：在部分块中引入softmax注意力以增强局部建模能力，同时保留线性层的效率；(2) 循环反向正则化（Cycle-Reverse Regularization）：通过流匹配从生成内容预测源帧，强制语义一致性，无需成对长视频数据即可提升时序一致性；(3) 高效系统协同设计：融合GDN内核和针对NVIDIA Blackwell (RTX 5090)优化的混合精度量化（MPQ），最大化Tensor Core利用率。

Result: 在单张RTX 5090 GPU上实现1280×704分辨率、24 FPS端到端实时视频编辑，DiT核心达到58 FPS。在时序一致性和系统吞吐量上显著优于现有SOTA方法。

Conclusion: SANA-Streaming通过系统-算法协同设计，首次在消费级GPU上实现了高分辨率实时流式视频编辑，为交互式视频编辑应用提供了可行的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究实时流式视频到视频编辑（V2V），属于视频编辑范畴，与多模态生成方向下的[CANONICAL] subtopic "visual_editing（图像与视频编辑）"高度匹配。同时涉及AIGC方向下的视频音频生成应用。

Importance reason: High relevance due to hybrid diffusion transformer for real-time video editing, aligning with user's priority on diffusion video generation and efficient inference. Signals: real-time video editing, hybrid diffusion transformer, flow matching regularization, efficient system co-design, consumer GPU deployment

Abstract: Real-time streaming video-to-video editing (V2V) is critical for interactive applications such as live broadcasting and gaming, yet it remains a formidable challenge due to the stringent requirements for temporal consistency and inference throughput. In this paper, we present SANA-Streaming, a system-algorithm co-designed framework for high-resolution, real-time streaming video editing on consumer GPUs, with the following three core designs: (1) Hybrid Diffusion Transformer architecture introduces softmax attention in part of the blocks to improve local modeling capabilities while preserving the efficiency of linear layers. (2) Cycle-Reverse Regularization is a novel training strategy that enforces semantic consistency by predicting source frames from generated content via flow matching, improving temporal consistency without requiring paired long edited videos. (3) Efficient System Co-design combines fused GDN kernels and Mixed-Precision Quantization (MPQ) optimized for the NVIDIA Blackwell (RTX 5090) architecture. By profiling real-world throughput, our MPQ maximizes Tensor Core utilization while maintaining generation quality. The resulting system achieves real-time 1280 x 704 resolution editing at 24 end-to-end FPS on a single RTX 5090 GPU, with the DiT core running at 58 FPS. Experimental results demonstrate that our co-design approach significantly outperforms existing SOTA methods in both temporal coherence and system throughput.

</details>

*Hidir Yesiltepe, Koutilya PNVR, Gaurav Pathak, Navaneeth Bodla, Bharat Singh, Pinar Yanardag, Jinrong Xie*

TL;DR: 本文提出 DTG-Restore，一种无需训练的视频超分辨率框架，通过解耦分类器自由引导中的条件/无条件分支时序信号（Decoupled Time Guidance, DTG），利用更干净的扩散时间步提供前瞻先验，在保持几何结构的同时抑制扭曲内容复制，结合即插即用的现成恢复模块实现结构校正到细节精炼的平滑过渡。同时构建了 GenWarp480 基准（4400个480p扭曲视频）用于评估生成式退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频扩散模型在生成保真度上取得了显著进展，但利用这些先验进行视频恢复时，标准分类器自由引导（CFG）中条件分支与无条件分支的强耦合限制了恢复效果，导致扭曲内容被复制。作者希望在不重新训练模型的前提下，提升低分辨率、扭曲视频的结构保真度和时间稳定性。

Method: 提出 Decoupled Time Guidance (DTG)，在扩散采样过程中，无条件分支在更干净的扩散时间步进行评估，从而提供"前瞻先验"（lookahead prior），在保持几何结构的同时抑制扭曲内容的复制。该时序偏置在采样过程中逐步退火（annealed），使模型从结构校正平滑过渡到细节精炼。该方法可与任何现成的恢复模块即插即用结合。此外，构建了 GenWarp480 基准，包含 4400 个来自多种文生视频模型的 480p 扭曲视频，聚焦人脸扭曲、身体错位、空间伪影等生成式退化。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在结构保真度和时间稳定性上取得了显著提升，且无需任何模型训练。在 GenWarp480 基准上，相比基线方法在感知质量和结构一致性方面均有明显改善。

Conclusion: DTG-Restore 通过解耦 CFG 时序信号，以无需训练的方式有效提升了视频超分辨率的质量，为利用预训练扩散先验进行视频恢复提供了一种轻量级、即插即用的新范式。GenWarp480 基准也为生成式视频退化评估提供了标准化测试平台。

Classification reason: 该论文提出一种无需训练的视频超分辨率（VSR）框架，属于视频质量增强/修复任务，与 canonical subtopic "图像与视频编辑"（visual_editing）中的视频编辑、修复方向高度匹配。

Importance reason: Training-free video super-resolution with diffusion refinement is tangentially relevant but not a priority subtopic. Signals: video diffusion, training-free, classifier-free guidance, generative video super-resolution, GenWarp480 benchmark

Abstract: Recent progress in video diffusion models has enabled remarkable generative fidelity, yet leveraging these priors for restoration remains limited by the strong coupling between conditional and unconditional branches in standard classifier-free guidance. We introduce a training-free framework that enhances distorted and low-resolution videos by decoupling these signals in time. Our proposed Decoupled Time Guidance (DTG) evaluates the unconditional branch at a cleaner diffusion timestep, providing a lookahead prior that preserves geometry while suppressing replication of warped content. This temporal bias is annealed throughout sampling, allowing the model to transition from structure correction to detail refinement without retraining. Combined with any off-the-shelf restoration module in a plug-and-play manner, our approach improves perceptual coherence and restores plausible structure in AIgenerated and real-world videos alike. To facilitate evaluation, we curate GenWarp480, a benchmark of 4,400 distorted 480p videos synthesized from diverse text-to-video models. GenWarp480 focuses on characteristic generative degradations such as warped faces, body misalignments, and spatial artifacts, providing a purpose-built testbed for assessing robustness to generative errors. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves significant improvements in structural fidelity and temporal stability without any model training.

</details>

Method: 提出一个后处理融合框架，包含两个阶段：(1) 粗粒度空间与光度对应建立阶段，对齐原始输入图像与GenAI增强结果；(2) 融合阶段，将期望的增强效果从GenAI输出转移到原始图像，同时抑制幻觉内容。该方法以黑盒方式工作，不依赖GenAI模型的内部信息。

Result: 与真实感风格迁移和图像融合的代表性方法相比，该方法在保持美学质量的同时，更好地维持了像素级结构一致性和原始输入分辨率。

Conclusion: 所提出的后处理融合框架能有效缓解GenAI图像编辑中的内容偏移和幻觉问题，在保留感知增强效果的同时确保结构忠实性，为需要像素级保真度的下游应用提供了实用解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究GenAI图像编辑中的内容偏移与幻觉缓解问题，提出结构保持的后处理融合框架，属于图像编辑范畴。multimodal_generation方向下的[CANONICAL] subtopic "visual_editing"（图像与视频编辑）完美匹配。

Importance reason: Addresses a practical GenAI editing issue but lacks direct connection to the user's priority subtopics like continuous VLM or autoregressive generation. Signals: GenAI image editing, post-processing fusion, structural consistency, hallucination mitigation, black-box editor

Abstract: Generative AI (GenAI) image editors, such as Nano Banana, produce visually compelling results for retouching tasks, enabling non-experts to edit images through text prompts alone. However, the generative nature of these models often introduces spatial misalignment, texture distortion, and content hallucination, all of which are detrimental to downstream workflows that require pixel-level fidelity. We identify a problem setting we call "structure-preserving GenAI fusion" for black-box GenAI image retouching: retain the perceptual enhancements of a GenAI output while enforcing structural faithfulness to the original input image. To address this problem, we propose a post-processing framework that fuses an input image with its GenAI-enhanced counterpart by first establishing coarse spatial and photometric correspondences, then performing a fusion stage that transfers desired enhancements while suppressing hallucinated content. In the absence of direct prior work in this setting, we evaluate our framework against representative methods from photorealistic style transfer and image fusion. Our experiments demonstrate that our method better preserves aesthetic quality while maintaining pixel-level structural consistency and the input resolution.

</details>

Abstract: Unconditional diffusion models offer powerful generative priors, yet steering them toward aesthetically enhanced outputs remains largely unexplored. We show that h-space patching, the dominant paradigm for training-free diffusion editing, systematically fails for global, low-level transformations required for aesthetic and perceptual refinement. We introduce a novel, generalized framework for image-editing in unconditional diffusion models without explicit training. This inference-time mechanism operates on low-level features by extracting degradation concept vectors and combining bottleneck patching with classifier-free guidance to guide sampling away from the degraded manifold, producing consistently improved images without any model retraining.

</details>

Abstract: The layout-to-image (L2I) task enables fine-grained control over image generation via object categories and spatial layouts. However, existing L2I methods yield fragmented and distorted generations under few-shot atypical settings. We term this failure as representation fragmentation, arising from a granularity mismatch that entangles semantic identity with visual details. To address this issue, we propose a representation-driven framework that disentangles semantics from primitives for robust few-shot adaptation. Specifically, Semantic Anchoring aggregates categorical semantics into anchors for stable identity, while Primitive Imbuing models recomposable primitives for robust local detail modeling. Conceptual Steering further regulates optimization with a saliency-aware objective to preserve foreground semantic consistency. Extensive experiments demonstrate consistent improvements in the 5-shot regime over state-of-the-art L2I methods in both visual fidelity and alignment across diverse atypical domains. The source code is publicly available at .

*Gyu-Hwung Cho, Youngjune Lee, Kiyoon Jeong, Siyoung Lee, Sanggyu Han, Hervé Dejean, Stéphane Clinchant, Seung-won Hwang*

TL;DR: 本文提出 V-SPLADE，一种推理无关的多模态学习型稀疏检索器，用于大规模视觉文档检索。通过引入标题门控 token 监督（caption-gated token supervision），利用 VLM 生成的标题作为词汇线索激活检索相关维度，在不增加推理开销的情况下显著提升检索质量，在六个基准上平均 NDCG@5 提升 +13.8pp，在 1870 万文档语料上 R@5 翻倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉文档检索方法存在两难：基于 VLM 的稠密或多向量模型检索质量高但需要在服务时进行神经编码（推理开销大）；基于 OCR 或标题的 BM25 方法避免查询编码但需要耗时的文本提取或生成。缺乏一种可部署的、能对视觉文档进行词汇索引且无需推理时神经编码的检索系统。

Method: 提出 V-SPLADE，一种推理无关的多模态学习型稀疏检索器。核心创新是标题门控 token 监督（caption-gated token supervision）：在训练阶段，利用 VLM 生成的标题作为词汇线索，通过门控机制激活与检索相关的词汇维度，使视觉稀疏表示能够捕捉文档图像中的词汇内容。该方法仅在训练时使用 VLM 标题，推理时无需任何神经编码。

Result: 在六个视觉文档检索基准上，V-SPLADE 的平均 NDCG@5 比同规模稠密基线提升 +13.8pp，比基于 OCR 或标题的 BM25 基线提升最多 +6.3pp。在 1870 万文档语料上，R@5 比同规模稠密基线翻倍以上，并通过分数融合进一步将竞争检索器的 R@5 提升最多 +2.4pp。

Conclusion: V-SPLADE 填补了推理无关的多模态学习型稀疏检索的空白，证明了在无需推理时神经编码的条件下可以达到稠密级别的检索效果，为大规模视觉文档检索提供了可部署的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究面向视觉文档的推理无关（inference-free）学习型稀疏检索方法，属于跨模态/多模态文档检索范畴。multimodal_generation 方向和 multimodal_understanding_generation 方向下均有 id=multimodal_retrieval 的现有子主题，直接复用。

Importance reason: Focuses on retrieval efficiency for visual documents, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous VLM, autoregressive generation, or world models. Signals: Inference-free retrieval, Visual document search, Sparse retrieval, Not generative, No autoregressive or diffusion focus

Abstract: As large-scale visual-document corpora such as arXiv papers and enterprise PDFs continue to grow, visual-document retrieval has gained increasing attention; yet it still lacks a deployable system that lexically indexes visual documents to serve queries without neural encoding at scale. Existing methods either achieve strong retrieval quality with VLM-based dense or multi-vector models but require neural query encoding at serving time, or avoid query encoding with OCR- or caption-based BM25 at the cost of time-consuming text extraction or generation. To fill this missing serving regime, we present V-SPLADE, an inference-free sparse retriever for visual-document retrieval. However, such inference-free multimodal learned sparse retrieval systems remain underexplored and have not yet shown dense-level effectiveness under high sparsity. We attribute this limitation to a lexical grounding problem: visual sparse representations often fail to capture the lexical content embedded in document images. To address this problem, we introduce caption-gated token supervision, a training-only signal that uses VLM-generated captions as lexical cues to activate retrieval-relevant vocabulary dimensions. With this supervision, V-SPLADE improves average NDCG@5 across six visual-document retrieval benchmarks by +13.8pp over the same-scale dense baseline and by up to +6.3pp over OCR- or caption-based BM25 baselines. On an 18.7M-document corpus, it more than doubles R@5 over the same-scale dense baseline and further improves competing retrievers through score fusion by up to +2.4pp R@5. Code will be released soon at .

</details>

Motivation: 现有4D人-物交互生成研究主要关注单物体操作，忽略了人类行为中双手协调和同时操作多个物体的自然特性。作者旨在填补这一空白，实现更真实、更具表现力的多物体双手交互动作生成。

Method: 提出Dex2HOI统一扩散模型，采用双流扩散架构（Dual-Stream Diffusion），每个物体有独立交互流，通过双向交叉注意力协调；设计运动融合网络（Motion Fusion Network）结合手-物体相对表示和全序列接触感知条件；通过自回归前缀条件窗口采样实现任意长度序列生成，无需测试时优化。

Result: 在单物体和双物体基准上均达到SOTA定量结果，推理速度相比先前方法提升高达540倍，支持实时生成任意长度序列。

Conclusion: Dex2HOI首次实现了从文本到双手双物体交互动作的统一生成，超越了传统单物体HOI生成范式，向更具表现力的多物体操作生成迈出了重要一步。

Classification reason: 该论文研究双手双物体的人-物交互（HOI）动作生成，属于人体动作生成范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "数字人与动作生成"（avatar_motion_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on dexterous bimanual interaction generation, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and lacks alignment with key boosted keywords. Signals: diffusion model, human-object interaction, motion generation, dual-stream architecture, real-time inference

Abstract: Recent advances in 4D Human-Object Interaction (HOI) generation have enabled increasingly realistic motion synthesis, particularly for single-object manipulation. Yet current research overlooks an inherent property of human behavior: people naturally coordinate both hands and manipulate multiple objects simultaneously. To address this gap, we present Dex2HOI, a unified diffusion model for single- and two-object HOI synthesis from text. At its core, Dex2HOI employs a Dual-Stream Diffusion approach, where each object is processed in a dedicated interaction stream and coordinated through bidirectional cross-attention. To synthesize the final motion, we introduce a Motion Fusion Network integrated with novel hand-relative object representations and contact-aware conditioning applied across the whole sequence. By sampling the diffusion process autoregressively over prefix-conditioned windows, Dex2HOI generates arbitrarily long sequences at real-time speed omitting redundant test-time optimization, achieving up to x540 inference speed-up over prior state-of-the-art methods. Extensive evaluation on both single- and two-object benchmarks demonstrates state-of-the-art quantitative results, marking a step beyond conventional single-object HOI generation and toward expressive multi-object manipulation. Code and models will be released upon acceptance.

Conclusion: MultiAct 有效解决了复合文本到动作生成中的语义坍缩问题，是一种即插即用的推理时方法，无需重新训练即可提升预训练模型在复合提示下的生成质量。

Abstract: Text-to-motion generation has progressed rapidly in recent years, offering an expressive interface for animation and human-computer interaction. However, current models remain brittle when handling prompts that describe multiple actions occurring at the same time. Rather than realizing all components of a composite description, models frequently prioritize a single dominant action and neglect the rest, leading to incomplete or ambiguous motion. We present MultiAct, an unpaired, inference-time framework for compositional text-to-motion synthesis that operates directly on pretrained motion generators without retraining or architectural modification. Our method counteracts semantic collapse by adaptively amplifying cross-attention scores associated with underrepresented prompt components. We note that effective modulation depends on prompt-specific choices, such as which tokens and layers to target, and introduce a lightweight auxiliary decision scheme that determines the most effective attention-strengthening parametrization. Extensive quantitative and qualitative evaluations demonstrate that MultiAct consistently outperforms existing baselines on composite prompts, achieving improved semantic coverage while preserving motion realism. Method: 提出 Omni-Supervised Positive-Negative Learning 框架 (OmniME)，包含三个组件：(1) 回顾性特征监督——在 transformer 层间施加由粗到细的一致性约束；(2) 动作保持机制——根据源-目标相似性关注细微变化；(3) 三重语义对齐——增强文本-动作对应关系。三者形成统一的监督范式来平衡变化与不变性。

Result: 在 MotionFix 和 STANCE Adjustment 数据集上的大量实验表明，OmniME 在编辑对齐方面达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 提出的统一正负学习框架有效平衡了动作编辑中的变化与不变性，验证了回顾性特征监督、动作保持机制和三重语义对齐三个组件的互补有效性。

Classification reason: 论文研究基于文本的人体动作编辑，属于人体动作生成与编辑范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "数字人与动作生成" (avatar_motion_generation) 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on human motion editing, not aligned with user's priority subtopics like VLM, video generation, or world models. Signals: motion editing, positive-negative learning, text-based editing, diffusion-based, SOTA on MotionFix

Abstract: Text-based human motion editing aims to modify existing motion sequences according to natural language instructions while maintaining the consistency of the original motion. Existing diffusion-based approaches often rely on heuristic similarity cues or coarse global conditioning, leading to motion distortion and suboptimal semantic alignment. The key challenge lies in balancing change (i.e. precisely editing target regions) and invariance (i.e. preserving unedited parts). To handle such challenge, we propose an Omni-Supervised Positive-Negative Learning framework, named OmniME. Our method integrates three complementary components: (1) retrospective feature supervision that enforces coarse-to-fine consistency across transformer layers,(2) motion preservation mechanism that focuses on subtle variations according to the source-target similarity, and (3) triplet-based semantic alignment that strengthens text-motion correspondence. Together, these components form a unified supervision paradigm that balances change and invariance. Extensive experiments on the MotionFix and STANCE Adjustment datasets demonstrate that OmniME achieves state-of-the-art performance in editing alignment, validating the effectiveness of our unified learning framework. Our source codes and models have been released at:

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: Polyphony 包含三个阶段：(1) 交替双手视觉 Transformer（Alternating Dual-Hand Vision Transformer），通过交替训练左右手小批量确保梯度平衡；(2) 语义特征条件化（Semantic Feature Conditioning），将视觉特征与结构化组合动作描述对齐；(3) 基于扩散的分割（Diffusion-Based Segmentation），结合跨手特征融合与自适应损失加权。

Result: 在 HA-ViD 和 ATTACH 两个双手数据集上达到最优，提升高达 16.8 个点；在单流 Breakfast 数据集上达到 82.5%，超越使用 12 倍大骨干网络的先前最佳方法。

Conclusion: Polyphony 通过交替训练、语义条件化和扩散分割有效解决了双手动作分割中的关键挑战，且统一单骨干模型超越了需要独立手模型的基线方法。

Classification reason: 该论文研究双手动作分割（dual-hand action segmentation），属于人体动作理解与生成范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "数字人与动作生成"（avatar_motion_generation）中的人体动作分析高度相关，因此复用该 subtopic。

Importance reason: Focuses on dual-hand action segmentation, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: action segmentation, dual-hand, diffusion-based, vision transformer, semantic conditioning

Abstract: Dual-hand action segmentation, densely predicting actions for both hands from untrimmed videos, is essential for understanding complex bimanual activities. However, it poses several unique challenges: complex inter-hand dependencies, visual asymmetry between hands, representation conflicts where the dominant hand monopolizes gradients, and semantic ambiguity in fine-grained actions. We propose Polyphony, a three-stage method to address these challenges through: (1) an Alternating Dual-Hand Vision Transformer that alternates training between left- and right-hand mini-batches to ensure balanced gradient contributions from both hands while sharing a spatio-temporal encoder; (2) Semantic Feature Conditioning that aligns visual features with structured, compositional action descriptions to enhance discrimination of semantically similar actions; and (3) Diffusion-Based Segmentation with cross-hand feature fusion for inter-hand coordination and adaptive loss weighting for balancing performance. Polyphony achieves state-of-the-art on both dual-hand datasets (HA-ViD, ATTACH) with improvements up to 16.8 points, and on the single-stream Breakfast dataset (82.5%), outperforming the prior best method that uses a 12x larger backbone. Notably, our unified model with a single shared backbone surpasses baselines requiring separate per-hand models. Code is at .

### [22] [DriveMA: Driving Vision-Language-Action Models with verifiable Meta-Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31271)
*Weicheng Zheng, Yixin Huang, Qiao Sun, Derun Li, Hang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 数字人与动作生成

TL;DR: DriveMA 提出了一种基于可验证元动作（meta-actions）的驾驶视觉-语言-动作模型框架，通过轨迹标注管道构建语言域意图，利用规则投影进行验证，并结合动作中心监督训练和回合级信用分配强化学习，在 Waymo Open Dataset 上取得了端到端驾驶的最新最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驾驶视觉-语言-动作模型（Driving VLA）旨在利用语言改进端到端规划，但语言与动作之间存在语义鸿沟（language-action gap），导致语言指令难以有效指导底层轨迹规划。现有方法缺乏对高层语言决策与低层轨迹规划之间对齐的有效机制。

Method: DriveMA 提出可验证元动作（verifiable meta-actions）作为语言域与动作域之间的桥梁。元动作通过轨迹标注管道从专家轨迹中自动构建，并通过规则投影对生成轨迹进行验证。框架包含两个训练阶段：(1) 动作中心监督训练（action-centric supervised training），对齐元动作与轨迹；(2) 回合级信用分配强化学习（turn-level credit assignment RL），通过密集奖励和精确信用分配显式对齐高层决策与低层规划。

Result: 在 Waymo Open Dataset 视觉端到端驾驶任务上，DriveMA 2B 模型达到 8.060 Rater Feedback Score，4B 模型进一步提升至 8.079，均达到新最优水平；同时在 NAVSIM 上获得有竞争力的闭环规划性能。

Conclusion: 即使使用简单的元动作接口，只要使其可验证并针对语言-动作对齐进行优化，就能在端到端驾驶规划中取得最优性能。这表明可验证元动作是弥合语言-动作鸿沟的有效范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究自动驾驶中的端到端规划，涉及未来自车运动（ego motion）的元动作（meta-actions）生成与轨迹规划，属于数字人与动作生成（avatar_motion_generation）中"人体动作"的广义延伸（车辆运动规划可类比为动作生成）。同时，论文使用视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA），也涉及多模态理解与生成方向。

Importance reason: Focuses on driving VLAs, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous VLM or autoregressive video generation. Signals: Driving VLA, meta-actions, language-action alignment, Waymo SOTA, reinforcement learning for planning

Abstract: Driving Vision-Language-Action Models (Driving VLAs) aim to use language to improve end-to-end planning, but the language-action gap limits this promise. We propose DriveMA, a Driving VLA framework built on verifiable meta-actions, which summarize future ego motion into compact language-domain intentions and can be constructed from expert trajectories with a trajectory-grounded annotation pipeline and can be verified against generated trajectories through rule-based projection. DriveMA exploits this verifiability with action-centric supervised training and a data-efficient turn-level credit assignment reinforcement learning framework, explicitly aligning high-level decisions with low-level trajectory planning through dense rewards and precise credit assignment. DriveMA sets a new state of the art on the Waymo Open Dataset Vision-based E2E Driving, achieving a Rater Feedback Score of 8.060 with a 2B model and further improving it to 8.079 with a 4B model; it also obtains competitive closed-loop planning performance on NAVSIM. These results show that even a simple meta-action interface can achieve state-of-the-art planning when made verifiable and optimized for language-action alignment. Code, data, and models will be released to facilitate future research.

</details>

TL;DR: TokTalk 提出一种直接从 Audio-LLM 音频 token 实时生成 3D 面部表情动画的系统，使用基于分块的连续条件流匹配模型，无需经过传统的语音识别-文本生成-语音合成-面部动画多阶段流水线，在低延迟下实现了富有表现力的面部动画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话式虚拟人面部表情和对话流程仍显机械，主要因为需要经过语音识别、文本生成、语音合成、音频驱动面部动画等多个串行阶段，导致延迟高且表现力不足。作者观察到 Audio-LLM 的音频 token 已包含足够信息来重建合理的面部表现，因此希望直接利用这些 token 生成面部动画。

Method: 构建了一个音频 token 到 3D 面部运动的配对数据集；提出基于分块的条件流匹配（Chunk-based Conditional Flow Matching）模型，从 Audio-LLM 的流式音频 token 中直接生成面部动画；设计轻量级适配策略，使训练好的模型能以最小计算开销连接到任意基于 token 的 Audio-LLM；分块处理支持在延迟和面部质量之间进行参数化权衡。

Result: 消融实验验证了分块处理在延迟与质量之间的权衡效果；与现有方案相比，TokTalk 在延迟上相当，在面部动画质量、表现力和可控性方面通过感知研究显著优于先前方案；展示了聊天机器人 Avatar、语音驱动用户 Avatar 和动画导演界面三种应用场景。

Conclusion: TokTalk 证明了 Audio-LLM 的音频 token 足以直接驱动富有表现力的 3D 面部动画，无需传统多阶段流水线，实现了实时、高质量且可控的面部动画生成，为对话式虚拟人提供了更自然流畅的交互体验。

Classification reason: 该论文研究从Audio-LLM音频token实时生成3D面部动画，属于虚拟人/说话头/面部动画生成，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "数字人与动作生成" (avatar_motion_generation) 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel use of Audio-LLM tokens for real-time facial animation, but tangential to user's core focus on continuous VLM, autoregressive generation, and world models. Signals: Real-time facial animation, Audio-LLM tokens, Chunk-based Conditional Flow Matching, Low latency, Expressive 3D facial performance

Abstract: Recent advances in Audio-LLMs like GPT-4o have ushered in an era of conversational interaction with language models. Conversational avatars however, still seem robotic in facial expression and conversational flow, in part due to sequential stages of speech recognition, text generation, turn-based text response, speech synthesis, and audio driven facial animation. Based on our insight that audio-tokens produced by current Audio-LLMs carry sufficient information to reconstruct a plausible facial performance, we present TokTalk, a system that directly outputs expressive facial animation in real-time from streaming audio-tokens. We construct a novel audio-token to 3D facial motion dataset, on which TokTalk is trained using a Chunk-based Conditional Flow Matching model. A lightweight adaptation strategy allows our trained model to seamlessly connect to any token-based Audio-LLM at minimal computational overhead. Our chunk-based processing further enables parametric trade-off between latency and facial quality, shown through ablation studies. We further show that the real-time performance of TokTalk is comparable in latency to prior art solutions, and significantly favorable (via a perceptual study) in terms of quality, expressivity and control of the 3D facial performance. We showcase TokTalk's flexibility using a chatbot Avatar, a voice-driven user Avatar, and an animation Director's interface, as diverse audio-visual face applications.

</details>


### [24] [Dreaming Of Others: Latent Teammate Modeling In World Models For Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31361)
*Tomas Leroy-Stone*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.LG, cs.MA

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 数字人与动作生成

Importance: 值得关注 (72.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出将多智能体强化学习中的队友视为世界模型内可学习的结构化组件，通过将Dreamer风格RSSM的潜在状态分解为环境和队友两部分，并引入心智理论（ToM）头来推断队友的潜在表征（如角色、意图、预测动作），从而支持零样本和少样本协作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在协作式多智能体强化学习中，智能体需要与内部策略和意图不可直接观测的伙伴进行协调。现有的世界模型（如Dreamer）在单智能体场景中表现出色，但无法处理队友带来的不确定性，限制了其在MARL中的应用。

Method: 提出一种架构，将Dreamer风格的循环状态空间模型（RSSM）的潜在状态分解为环境组件和队友组件，并学习一个辅助的心智理论（ToM）头，从部分轨迹中推断队友行为的潜在嵌入（如角色、意图和预测动作）。这些队友潜在表征用于条件化actor和critic网络，使智能体能够想象并适应不同的协作者。

Result: 论文提出了方法框架和评估协议，但尚未给出具体的实验数值结果，属于方法提案和理论框架阶段。

Conclusion: 这项工作将世界模型定位为不仅是环境动态的预测器，更是社会行为的模拟器，为可泛化、与人类兼容的AI系统开辟了新方向。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多智能体强化学习中基于世界模型的队友建模，涉及智能体对合作伙伴行为（角色、意图、动作）的推理与模拟，与"数字人与动作生成"（avatar_motion_generation）中的人体动作、智能体行为建模有合理关联。虽然该论文更偏向MARL，但其核心是使用Dreamer-style世界模型对队友行为进行潜在建模和模拟，属于生成式世界模型在智能体行为模拟方面的应用。

Importance reason: Focuses on multi-agent RL world models, not aligned with user's priority on multimodal generation or autoregressive video/image generation. Signals: Multi-agent RL, World model, Theory of Mind, Zero-shot coordination, Not multimodal generation

Abstract: In cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL), agents must coordinate with partners whose internal policies and intentions are not directly observable. While world models such as Dreamer have demonstrated strong generalization and sample efficiency in single-agent settings, their application to MARL remains limited by an inability to handle teammate-induced uncertainty. We propose a new perspective: treat teammates as structured, learnable components within the agent's world model. We introduce an architecture that factorizes the latent state of a Dreamer-style recurrent state-space model (RSSM) into environment and teammate components, and learns an auxiliary Theory-of-Mind (ToM) head to infer latent embeddings of partner behavior such as character, intent, and predicted actions from partial trajectories. These teammate latents condition the actor and critic, enabling the agent to imagine and adapt to diverse collaborators. We outline how this approach can support zero-shot and few-shot coordination in partially observable settings and propose a set of benchmarks and evaluation protocols to assess its impact. This work positions world models as not only predictors of environmental dynamics, but as simulators of social behavior, opening new directions for generalizable, human-compatible AI.

</details>

### [25] [OmniMem: Scalable and Adaptive Memory Retrieval for Long Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30519)
*Lin Zhao, Yushu Wu, Yifan Gong, Yanzhi Wang, Pu Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 文生视频与视频扩散

Importance: 高优先级 (89.42) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: OmniMem 提出了一种显式全范围记忆检索框架，通过自适应窗口排除、查询共享 KV 选择和逐头分散 KV 访问等机制，在自回归长视频生成中实现高效的稀疏 KV 检索，显著提升长视频的动态性（Dynamic Degree 提升 52.3%）同时保持一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频生成在生成长视频时需要反复访问不断增长的历史 KV 缓存，现有方法通过截断 KV 缓存或压缩为隐式记忆来降低开销，但两者都丢失了对查询相关历史细节的显式访问能力，导致长视频生成质量受限。

Method: OmniMem 提出显式全范围记忆检索框架，核心包含三个设计：(1) 自适应窗口排除（Adaptive Window Exclusion）——当有足够长程历史时，从候选集中排除局部窗口块，将稀疏预算留给信息量更大的长程检索；(2) 查询共享 KV 选择（Query-Shared KV Selection）——减少跨查询的多样性，降低检索复杂度；(3) 逐头分散 KV 访问（Per-Head Scattered KV Access）——避免将逐头选择扩展为大的 KV 缓冲区，允许每个注意力头根据自身选择模式检索非连续的 KV 块。

Result: 在长视频生成任务上，OmniMem 将 Dynamic Degree 提升了 52.3%，同时保持了强一致性，且内存使用与强基线方法相当。

Conclusion: OmniMem 通过显式稀疏 KV 检索有效解决了自回归长视频生成中历史信息访问的难题，在提升视频动态性的同时保持了生成一致性和内存效率，为长视频生成提供了一种可扩展且自适应的记忆检索方案。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于长视频生成中的记忆检索机制，属于视频生成范畴。虽然论文使用自回归（AR）范式生成视频，但核心任务是视频生成（text-to-video/long video generation），与"文生视频与视频扩散"（text_to_video_diffusion）这一[CANONICAL]子主题最为匹配。该子主题涵盖视频生成方法，包括但不限于扩散模型，且论文的AR生成方式仍属于视频生成大类。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient autoregressive long video generation with a novel sparse KV retrieval method, highly relevant to your priority on autoregressive video generation and model efficiency. Signals: autoregressive video generation, efficient inference, sparse KV retrieval, long video generation, dynamic degree improvement

Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) video generation extends videos by producing latent chunks sequentially, but scaling to long videos requires repeated access to a growing historical KV cache. Existing methods reduce this cost by truncating the KV cache or compressing it into implicit memory, but both lose explicit access to query-relevant historical details. We propose OmniMem, an explicit full-range memory retrieval framework that performs sparse KV retrieval over the historical cache. To make this practical for chunk-based AR video generation, OmniMem addresses two issues: (i) local bias in sparse KV selection and (ii) Union Explosion in memory access. Adaptive Window Exclusion removes local-window blocks from the selection candidates when sufficient long-range history is available, preserving the sparse budget for informative long-range retrieval. Query-Shared KV Selection reduces cross-query diversity, while Per-Head Scattered KV Access avoids expanding head-specific selections into a large selected KV buffer. This allows each attention head to retrieve non-contiguous KV blocks according to its own selection pattern. Experiments on long-video generation show that OmniMem improves Dynamic Degree by 52.3% and preserves strong consistency over strong baselines, while maintaining comparable memory usage.

</details>


### [26] [CameraNoise: Enabling Faithful Camera Control in Video Diffusion through Geometry-Flow-Guided Noise Warping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30774)
*Haoyu Zhao, Jiaxi Gu, Haoran Chen, Qingping Zheng, Yeying Jin, Hongyi Yang, Junqi Cheng, Yuang Zhang, Zenghui Lu, Huan Yu, Jie Jiang, Peng Shu, Zuxuan Wu, Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 文生视频与视频扩散

Importance: 高优先级 (90.07) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: CameraNoise 提出一种流到噪声的扭曲方法（flow-to-noise warping），通过几何引导的重投影流和噪声扭曲算法将相机运动编码到噪声空间中，在保持扩散高斯先验的同时实现精确的相机控制，显著提升了视频生成的结构一致性和轨迹忠实度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频扩散模型中的相机控制方法通常直接将数值相机参数注入扩散骨干网络，但抽象坐标与视觉内容之间存在语义鸿沟，导致生成视频出现结构畸变。需要一种能忠实编码相机运动同时解耦运动与场景外观的方法。

Method: 提出 CameraNoise 框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 几何引导的重投影流（Geometry-guided Reprojection Flow），根据相机参数计算帧间像素对应关系；(2) 噪声扭曲算法（Noise Warping），将相机运动编码到噪声空间中，通过扭曲噪声保持扩散过程的高斯先验，实现一致的噪声传播。该方法将相机姿态直接嵌入噪声空间，而非作为条件注入网络。

Result: 大量实验表明，CameraNoise 在视觉质量和轨迹忠实度上显著优于现有方法，能够生成结构一致、相机运动精确的高保真视频。

Conclusion: CameraNoise 通过将相机控制从条件注入范式转变为噪声空间编码范式，有效解决了视频扩散中相机控制的几何一致性问题，为可控视频生成提供了新的思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究视频扩散模型中的相机姿态控制，属于视频扩散生成范畴，与 canonical 子主题"文生视频与视频扩散"（text_to_video_diffusion）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances controllable video generation with a novel geometry-guided noise warping method, highly relevant to your priority on diffusion video generation and world models. Signals: video diffusion, camera control, noise warping, geometry-guided flow, trajectory faithfulness

Abstract: Precise camera pose control is critical for video diffusion, yet maintaining geometric consistency remains a challenge. Existing methods that directly inject numerical camera parameters into the diffusion backbone often fail to bridge the gap between abstract coordinates and visual content, leading to structural distortions. To address this issue, we propose CameraNoise, a flow-to-noise warping method that encodes camera motion into a temporally coherent stochastic representation. Unlike conventional conditioning, CameraNoise embeds camera poses directly into the noise space. This decouples motion from scene appearance while faithfully preserving trajectory dynamics. Specifically, we introduce a novel Geometry-guided Reprojection Flow and a noise warping algorithm, which jointly preserve the Gaussian prior of diffusion and ensure consistent noise propagation under camera transformations. By integrating CameraNoise into the diffusion process, our framework delivers stable, high-fidelity videos. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms prior methods in both visual quality and trajectory faithfulness. The project page and code are available at: .

</details>


### [27] [SlotMemory: Object-Centric KV Memory for Streaming Long-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31033)
*Weijia Dou, Hui Li, Jiahao Cui, Lei Zhou, Jingdong Wang, Siyu Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 文生视频与视频扩散

Importance: 高优先级 (95.24) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出SlotMemory，一种面向流式长视频生成的物体级键值记忆机制。通过将Transformer的KV流形分解为离散、可复用的语义槽，实现实体级持久化和提示感知检索，在60秒交互叙事生成中取得SOTA质量分数81.61，动态一致性相对提升22.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式视频生成模型依赖时间中心记忆（原始帧、片段或未聚类token），当实体离开画面或提示词切换时，常出现身份漂移和语义不一致问题。作者希望从"何时发生"的记忆抽象转向"什么被表示"，通过物体级语义记忆解决长视频生成中的实体一致性问题。

Method: 提出SlotMemory，将Transformer的KV流形分解为离散、可复用的语义槽作为路由地址，用于索引和存储高保真KV token。该机制支持实体级持久化和提示感知检索，在长时程交互叙事中保持物体身份一致性。基于Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B骨干进行实验。

Result: 在60秒交互叙事生成任务上，SlotMemory取得SOTA质量分数81.61，动态一致性相对最强流式基线提升22.8%。

Conclusion: 结构化语义表示（而非原始时间容量）是实现持久长视频合成的关键原语。SlotMemory通过物体级KV记忆有效缓解了身份漂移和语义不一致问题。

Classification reason: 该论文提出面向流式长视频生成的物体级KV记忆机制SlotMemory，基于视频扩散模型（Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B骨干），属于文生视频与视频扩散方向，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "文生视频与视频扩散"。

Importance reason: Directly advances continuous VLM and visual latent flow with object-centric KV memory for streaming video generation, matching top priority subtopics. Signals: object-centric KV memory, streaming long-video generation, entity-level persistence, prompt-aware retrieval, dynamic consistency improvement

Abstract: Streaming video generation models typically rely on temporal-centric memory, which organizes historical context as raw frames, chunk segments, or unclustered tokens. This organization frequently leads to identity drift and semantic inconsistency when entities exit the frame or during interactive prompt transitions. To address these limitations, we propose SlotMemory, an object-centric Key-Value memory mechanism for streaming video diffusion. Our approach shifts the memory abstraction from "when" an event occurred to "what" is being represented by decomposing the transformer's key-value manifold into discrete, reusable semantic slots. By utilizing these slots as routing addresses to index and store high-fidelity key-value tokens, we enable entity-level persistence and prompt-aware retrieval across long horizons. Evaluated on 60-second interactive narratives using the Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B backbone, SlotMemory achieves a state-of-the-art quality score of 81.61 and a 22.8 percent relative improvement in dynamic consistency over the strongest existing streaming baseline. Our results demonstrate that structured semantic representation, rather than raw temporal capacity, is the essential primitive for persistent long-form video synthesis. Our codes and checkpoints are available at .

</details>

*Ruotong Liao, Guowen Huang, Qing Cheng, Guangyao Zhai, Lei Zhang, Xun Xiao, Thomas Seidl, Daniel Cremers, Volker Tresp*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 文生视频与视频扩散

Importance: 高优先级 (93.65) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: TunerDiT 是一种无需额外训练的视频扩散 Transformer 渐进式引导方法，通过事件分区掩码和跨事件提示融合，实现多事件文生视频生成，并在自建基准 Meve 上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生视频模型在生成包含多个事件的长时间视频时表现不佳，难以保持事件间的语义一致性和边界清晰性。作者希望在不引入额外训练的前提下，利用扩散过程的内在特性来引导多事件视频生成。

Method: 首先通过探测视频 DiT 去噪轨迹，发现条件文本对生成的影响存在从全局布局到细节的转折点。基于此提出 TunerDiT，包含两个引导手柄：(1) 事件分区掩码（Event-Partitioned Masking），强制事件边界同时允许跨事件过渡带；(2) 跨事件提示融合（Cross-Event Prompt Fusion），在后期细化阶段注入相邻事件语义。此外，自建了多事件生成评测基准 Meve。

Result: 在 8 个指标上达到 SOTA，相比其他无需训练的方法，在视频一致性和事件分离之间实现了可调节的权衡。文本对齐的提升随事件数量增加而增长，表明该方法具有随事件数扩展的潜力。

Conclusion: TunerDiT 通过分析扩散 Transformer 的内在去噪特性，提出了一种简单有效的无需训练的多事件视频生成引导方法，在多个指标上取得最优性能，并展示了随事件数量增加而扩展的能力。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 TunerDiT，一种无需训练的视频扩散 Transformer 渐进式引导方法，用于多事件文生视频生成。核心方法基于视频 DiT 去噪轨迹分析，属于文生视频与视频扩散方向，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "文生视频与视频扩散" (text_to_video_diffusion)。同时论文涉及多事件生成评测基准 Meve，也可归入 AIGC 方向。

Importance reason: Directly advances controllable multi-event video generation with a training-free DiT steering method, highly relevant to your priority on diffusion video generation and efficient inference. Signals: Training-free progressive steering, Multi-event video generation, Diffusion Transformer (DiT) probing, Event-Partitioned Masking, Cross-Event Prompt Fusion

Abstract: Text-to-video (T2V) generation faces challenging questions when generating videos with long horizons containing multiple events. Inspired by the intrinsics of the diffusion process, we probe video diffusion transformers (DiTs) and uncover intrinsic turning points in the DiT denoising trajectory where conditioning text affects generation from global layout to fine-grained details. Building on this finding, we present TunerDiT, a simple yet effective progressive steering method that requires no additional training for multi-event generation. TunerDiT comprises two steering handles: (1) Event-Partitioned Masking that enforces event boundaries while allowing cross-event transition bands; (2) Cross-Event Prompt Fusion that injects neighboring event semantics for late-stage refinement. We contribute a self-curated prompt suite for benchmarking multi-event generation, i.e., Meve. TunerDiT achieves state-of-the-art performance across 8 metrics and offers a tunable trade-off between video consistency and event separation, compared with other training-free methods. The improvement in text alignment increases with the event count, indicating a scaling possibility with increasing event count.

</details>

### [29] [Unlearning in Diffusion Models: A Unified Framework with KL Divergence and Likelihood Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30825)
*Shervin Khalafi, Alejandro Ribeiro, Dongsheng Ding*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.LG, stat.ML, math.OC

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 概念擦除与遗忘学习

Importance: 普通相关 (53.41) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于约束优化的扩散模型遗忘学习统一框架，通过KL散度和似然约束将遗忘学习形式化为最小化与预训练模型偏差的同时满足与遗忘分布的分离约束，建立了三个优化问题并证明了强对偶性，实验表明该方法在保留-遗忘权衡上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型中的遗忘学习需要在移除不需要的数据/概念与保持预训练模型效用之间取得平衡，这两个目标本质上是冲突的。现有方法缺乏统一的理论框架，难以系统性地处理这种权衡。

Method: 提出一个约束优化框架，将遗忘学习形式化为在显式分离约束下最小化与预训练模型的偏差。具体构建了三个基于逆向KL散度、前向KL散度和似然约束的约束优化问题，证明了非凸KL约束下的强对偶性，从而显式刻画最优解作为遗忘目标，并开发了相应的原始-对偶算法。

Result: 在概念遗忘和数据遗忘任务上，基于KL约束的方法在保留-遗忘权衡上优于基于权重的基线方法；基于似然的方法在匹配遗忘效果的同时更好地保留了保留概念。

Conclusion: 本文提出的统一约束优化框架为扩散模型遗忘学习提供了理论基础和实用算法，在概念和数据遗忘场景下均取得了更优的权衡表现。

Classification reason: 该论文研究扩散模型中的遗忘学习（unlearning），属于生成模型概念擦除与遗忘学习的核心主题。该主题在 multimodal_generation 和 aigc 两个方向下均有对应的 [CANONICAL] 子主题 aigc_unlearning_erasure（概念擦除与遗忘学习），因此直接复用该子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on diffusion model unlearning, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to user's key interests in autoregressive generation, continuous VLMs, or world models. Signals: Diffusion model unlearning, KL divergence constraints, Strong duality proof, Retention-unlearning tradeoff, Not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Unlearning in diffusion models aims to remove undesirable data or concepts while preserving the utility of pretrained models -- two fundamentally conflicting objectives. We propose a principled constrained optimization framework that formulates unlearning as minimizing the deviation from a pretrained model, subject to explicit separation constraints from the unlearning distributions. Specifically, we formulate three constrained optimization problems based on reverse and forward KL divergences, and likelihood constraints. The first two generalize existing approaches for concept and data unlearning, while the third offers a novel and natural formulation for unlearning. Despite the nonconvexity of the KL constraints, we establish strong duality for all three problems, enabling us to explicitly characterize their optimal solutions as unlearning targets and develop primal-dual algorithms for each formulation. Experimental results demonstrate that our KL-constrained approach achieves superior retention-unlearning tradeoffs compared to weight-based baselines for concept and data unlearning, and that our likelihood-based approach matches unlearning effectiveness while better preserving retained concepts compared to baselines.

</details>

### [30] [MergeTok: Unified Continuous and Discrete Visual Tokenization via Token Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30904)
*Luyuan Zhang, Siyuan Li, Zedong Wang, Qingsong Xie, Cheng Tan, Anna Wang, Yanhao Zhang, Chen Chen, Haonan Lu, Haoqian Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

Importance: 高优先级 (92.31) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MergeTok 提出了一种统一连续（VAE）和离散（VQ）视觉分词器的架构，通过 token 合并技术作为语义桥接，在编码阶段聚类相似 token，为 VAE 分支提供语义对齐监督，为 VQ 分支提供组内多样性和组间排他性约束，从而在单一架构下同时实现高保真重建和语义组织的离散 token 表示，兼容自回归和扩散生成器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉分词器分为连续 VAE 和离散 VQ 两大流派，各有互补性局限：VAE 重建质量高但潜在表示稠密且纠缠，不利于语义控制；VQ 模型支持自回归生成但存在梯度稀疏、训练不稳定和码本坍塌问题。作者希望设计一个统一架构，同时兼顾两者的优势。

Method: MergeTok 采用编码器-解码器架构，在编码阶段引入 token 合并（token merging）技术，将相似视觉 token 聚类合并。合并后的 token 提供双重监督信号：(1) 在 VAE 分支施加合并 token 的语义对齐约束，正则化潜在空间使其解耦且语义感知；(2) 导出分组约束，促进组内多样性和组间排他性，稳定 VQ 训练。最终输出同时支持连续和离散两种 token 表示。

Result: 在 ImageNet-256 上，MergeTok 在相同 token 预算下取得了比强 VAE 和 VQ 模型更低的 rFID，重建和生成性能均具有竞争力，且产生的 token 表示具有语义组织性，兼容自回归和扩散生成器。

Conclusion: MergeTok 证明了单一架构可以赋予视觉分词器鲁棒的语义组织和生成器友好的离散性，为统一连续与离散视觉分词提供了有效方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出MergeTok，一种统一连续(VAE)和离散(VQ)视觉分词器的架构，通过token合并技术实现语义桥接，提升分词器的重建质量和生成友好性。这属于视觉分词器（tokenizer）的效率与质量优化，与"生成模型加速与高效化"（efficient_generation）主题下的tokenizer/分词器优化方向高度吻合，且该子主题已有相关论文积累。

Importance reason: Directly addresses continuous VLM and visual latent flow via token merging, a priority subtopic with weight 2.0, and unifies continuous/discrete tokenization for autoregressive and diffusion generation. Signals: continuous visual token, token merging, unified continuous and discrete tokenizer, autoregressive and diffusion compatible, semantic alignment

Abstract: Most visual tokenizers for image generation are bifurcated into two families with complementary limitations: continuous VAEs offer high-fidelity reconstruction but suffer from dense, entangled latents that are poorly suited for semantic control, whereas discrete VQ-based models enable autoregressive generation yet struggle with gradient sparsity, unstable training, and codebook collapse. In this work, we introduce MergeTok, a unified tokenizer that jointly optimizes continuous (VAE) and discrete (VQ) tokenizers within a encoder-decoder architecture, leveraging token merging techniques as a semantic bridge. By clustering similar tokens during encoding, MergeTok establishes a structural prior that provides dual supervision signals: (i) it imposes merged-token semantic alignment in the VAE branch, regularizing its latent space toward disentangled, semantic-aware representations; (ii) it derives group-wise constraints, promoting intra-group diversity and inter-group exclusivity that stabilize VQ training. MergeTok shows competitive reconstruction and generation performance on ImageNet-256, with substantially lower rFID than strong VAE and VQ models under matched token budgets, while producing semantically-organized token representations compatible with both autoregressive and diffusion generators. This shows that a single architecture can endow visual tokenizers with robust semantic organization and generator-friendly discreteness.

Abstract: Sampling from high-dimensional, non-log-concave distributions with unnormalized densities remains a fundamental challenge in machine learning, particularly in black-box settings where gradient information is inaccessible or computationally prohibitive. While Langevin dynamics provides a principled framework for sampling when gradients are accessible, its extension to the black-box settings suffers from high variance and lacks non-asymptotic convergence guarantees for non-log-concave sampling. To address these limitations, we propose a variance-reduced zeroth-order Langevin sampling method. Our method employs a gradient estimator that substantially reduces the variance of the classical batched zeroth-order estimator and eliminates the unfavorable dimensional dependence of the batch size required for accurate estimation, enabling practical and stable sampling. We establish the first non-asymptotic convergence guarantees for zeroth-order non-log-concave sampling in terms of $\varepsilon$-relative Fisher information, and, under a Poincaré inequality assumption, squared total variation distance. We further propose ZO-APMC, a posterior sampling algorithm for black-box inverse problems with pre-trained score-based generative priors, establishing the first non-asymptotic convergence guarantees for such methods. We validate our theory through synthetic experiments and demonstrate strong empirical performance on practical linear and nonlinear inverse problems.

Abstract: Dense self-attention is the compute and quality bottleneck of long-video diffusion inference: cost grows quadratically with the sequence length, and beyond the training horizon the model converges to near-static output, that is, "frozen" repetitive video. State of the art approaches are either too costly, e.g., they require retraining, or fail to satisfy both performance and quality objectives in a scalable manner. To this end, we introduce Long Video Sparse Attention (LVSA), a training-free model-agnostic block-sparse attention for video diffusion transformers that combines a structured window pattern with rotating global anchors, thus removing the fixed-grid bias which causes long-range temporal artifacts. LVSA, combined with a FlashInfer kernel, reduces compute up to 3.17x on Wan 2.1 1.3B at a 6x horizon, 2.98x on Wan 2.1 14B at a 6x horizon, and 3.33x on HunyuanVideo 1.5 at a 1.5x horizon, compared to dense attention. Beyond reducing compute, LVSA enables HunyuanVideo 1.5 generation at a 2x horizon, which is otherwise out-of-memory on a single GPU. Moreover, LVSA provides speedups up to 2.41x compared to RIFLEx and 3.27x compared to UltraViCo on Wan 2.1 1.3B. To demonstrate applicability across diverse platforms, we apply LVSA on NPUs and achieve speedups up to 2.71x on Wan 2.2 A14B and 3.24x on Wan 2.1 1.3B compared to dense attention. To evaluate quality in a fair way, we introduce VQeval, a tool properly scoring loopy video failures, which instead are rewarded in state of the art evaluators like VBench-Long. LVSA is quality-neutral for generation at training horizon length and quality-positive at extended lengths.
