TL;DR: QVGGT提出了一种针对VGGT（1.2B参数视觉几何Transformer）的后训练量化框架，通过选择性混合精度策略、token过滤与相机信息补偿、以及任务感知尺度搜索，实现了W4A16近无损量化，在3D几何感知任务上保持精度同时获得3~4.9倍内存压缩和最高2.8倍实际加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VGGT模型虽然能从单张图像高效预测相机参数、深度图和点云，但其1.2B参数量严重限制了在无人机、移动AR等资源受限平台上的部署。现有量化方法未考虑VGGT中不同Transformer块对量化的异质敏感性，以及高方差token（相机token、register token）带来的量化误差放大问题。

Method: 1) 逐块量化敏感性分析 + 选择性混合精度策略：为最敏感的Transformer块分配更高精度；2) Token过滤与相机信息补偿：从激活校准中移除高方差异常token，并用PCA导出的全局补偿token恢复几何信息；3) 任务感知尺度搜索：通过层重建损失、多头监督和跨头几何一致性（相机位姿、深度图、点图）联合评估候选量化尺度。

Result: 在多个几何感知基准上，QVGGT实现近无损W4A16量化，所有3D预测头精度保持，内存减少3~4.9倍，实际硬件加速最高2.8倍（相比FP32）。

Conclusion: QVGGT使得高保真3D感知在边缘设备上变得可行，推动了前馈式3D重建模型在真实受限环境中的实际部署。

Classification reason: 该论文针对VGGT（视觉几何基础Transformer）模型进行后训练量化压缩，属于生成模型/3D感知模型的效率优化，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型加速与高效化"（efficient_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong model efficiency work with practical quantization for 3D vision, but not directly aligned with top-priority continuous VLM or autoregressive generation subtopics. Signals: Post-training quantization, 3D geometry transformer, Mixed-precision strategy, Token filtering, Task-aware scale search

Abstract: Estimating 3D attributes directly from images has advanced rapidly with the Visual Geometry Grounded Transformer (VGGT), which predicts camera parameters, depth maps, and point clouds in a single forward pass. However, its 1.2B-parameter scale severely limits deployment on resource-constrained platforms such as UAVs and mobile AR devices. To address this limitation, we introduce QVGGT, a tailored quantization framework designed to compress VGGT. Our approach starts from the observation that transformer blocks within VGGT exhibit heterogeneous sensitivity to quantization. We thus analyze per-block quantization sensitivity and propose a selective mixed-precision strategy that allocates higher precision to the most fragile transformer blocks. To address the amplification of quantization error caused by high-variance camera and register tokens, we further introduce token filtering with camera information compensation, which removes these outliers from activation calibration and restores their geometric cues using a PCA-derived global compensation token. Finally, we develop a task-aware scale search mechanism that evaluates candidate quantization scales not only through layer reconstruction but also through multi-head supervision and cross-head geometric consistency among camera poses, depth maps, and point maps. Extensive experiments on multiple geometry perception benchmarks demonstrate that QVGGT achieves near-lossless W4A16 quantization, preserving the accuracy of all 3D prediction heads while delivering 3$\sim$4.9$\times$ memory reduction and up to 2.8$\times$ real hardware speedup over FP32. Our approach makes high-fidelity 3D perception feasible on edge devices, enabling practical deployment of feed-forward 3D reconstruction models in real-world constrained environments.

</details>

*Yaocheng Zhang, Jiajun Chai, Songjun Tu, Yuqian Fu, Xiaohan Wang, Wei Lin, Guojun Yin, Qichao Zhang, Yuanheng Zhu, Dongbin Zhao*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 on-policy distillation (OPD) 在训练时需要生成完整的 rollout 轨迹，计算开销大，且早期训练阶段长 rollout 末尾的教师反馈不可靠。作者希望探索是否必须使用完整 rollout 才能实现有效的 OPD，从而提升训练效率。

Method: 提出两种 horizon 控制策略：(1) Progressive OPD (POPD) — 训练过程中逐步扩展 rollout 长度；(2) Truncated OPD (TOPD) — 永久使用截断的可靠 rollout 进行蒸馏。两种方法均基于 OPD 不需要完整轨迹或最终答案奖励即可提供学习信号的观察。

Result: 在数学推理任务上，POPD 将 OPD 训练效率提升最高3倍；TOPD 仅用10%的 rollout 长度即可匹配完整 OPD 的性能，显著减少 wall-clock 时间和内存消耗。

Conclusion: 控制 rollout 长度是提升 on-policy distillation 效率的简单有效途径，全 rollout 并非 OPD 的必要条件。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 on-policy distillation 中 rollout horizon 的控制策略以提升训练效率，属于生成模型训练加速与高效化范畴，与 canonical subtopic "生成模型加速与高效化"（efficient_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on training efficiency for on-policy distillation in math reasoning, not directly on user's priority subtopics like continuous VLM or video generation. Signals: on-policy distillation, rollout horizon control, training acceleration, math reasoning, cs.CL

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) provides dense teacher feedback along rollouts generated by the student and has emerged as a promising post-training paradigm for long-horizon reasoning. However, standard OPD typically generates full rollouts during training, which is computationally expensive and may expose the student to unreliable teacher feedback at late rollout positions, especially during early training. We identify the rollout horizon as a key bottleneck in OPD that substantially impacts training efficiency. Unlike Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR), OPD does not require a complete trajectory or a final answer reward to provide learning signals. This observation suggests that full rollouts may not always be necessary for effective OPD. Motivated by this insight, we propose two simple horizon-control strategies: Progressive OPD (POPD), which gradually expands the rollout horizon during training, and Truncated OPD (TOPD), which permanently performs distillation on reliable truncated rollouts. Experiments on mathematical reasoning show that POPD improves the training efficiency of OPD by up to 3$\times$, while TOPD matches OPD performance using only 10\% of the rollout horizon, leading to substantial wall-clock and memory reductions. These results demonstrate that controlling the rollout horizon offers a simple and practical path to more efficient OPD.

</details>


### [35] [Fixed-Point Masked Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31215)
*Andrea Miele, Yiming Qin, Alba Carballo-Castro, Justin Deschenaux, Pascal Frossard*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

Importance: 值得关注 (67.26) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Fixed-Point Masked Generative Models (FP-MGMs)，通过将部分去噪器替换为共享注意力层上的固定点求解器，实现自适应深度、更少参数和更低训练成本。配合跨步一致性损失和三状态重用（3SR）策略，形成完整训练-推理框架 CoFRe，在文本和图像生成任务上显著提升质量-成本权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码生成模型（MGM）虽支持并行解码且跨模态表现优异，但每一步都需要全序列双向 Transformer，导致训练成本高、低采样预算下质量差。现有改进方法（如更好的采样器或固定深度去噪器）仍为每个精炼步骤分配固定计算量，缺乏自适应深度。因此，本文希望用固定点求解器替代部分去噪器，实现自适应深度、减少参数并提升低预算生成质量。

Method: 提出 CoFRe 框架：(1) 将部分去噪器层替换为共享注意力层上的固定点求解器，实现自适应深度；(2) 引入跨步一致性损失（cross-step consistency loss），对齐相邻去噪步的隐藏表示；(3) 提出三状态重用（3SR），对未改变、仍掩码和新揭示的 token 分别处理，用前一步解热启动求解器。此外，预训练 MGM 可通过短时微调转换为 FP-MGM，避免完整重训练。

Result: 在 OpenWebText 上，CoFRe 减少参数 38.8%、训练时间 11.5%、VRAM 16.9%，在 96 次 Transformer 块前向传播预算下将生成困惑度从 830.8 降至 101.8。在 ImageNette 上，减少训练时间 48.6%、VRAM 50.7%，在所有采样预算下均改善 FID。

Conclusion: CoFRe 提供了一种实用的框架，能够以更低的训练成本和更强的低预算生成质量实现掩码生成，且兼容预训练模型的快速转换，具有良好的实用性和扩展性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出固定点掩码生成模型（FP-MGMs），通过共享注意力层上的固定点求解器实现自适应深度，减少参数和训练成本，同时提升低预算采样质量。这属于生成模型效率优化（加速、少步采样、蒸馏等），与 canonical 子主题"生成模型加速与高效化"高度匹配。

Importance reason: Proposes a novel fixed-point solver framework for masked generative models that significantly improves efficiency and quality trade-offs, directly aligning with your priority on model efficiency and generation acceleration. Signals: Fixed-point solver for adaptive depth, Cross-step consistency loss, Three-state reuse (3SR) for warm-starting, 38.8% parameter reduction, 11.5% training time reduction

Abstract: Masked Generative Models (MGMs) enable parallel decoding and achieve strong performance across modalities, but require full-sequence bidirectional transformers at every step, making training costly and degrading quality under low sampling budgets. Existing work improves efficiency via better samplers or cheaper fixed-depth denoisers, but they still allocate a fixed amount of denoiser computation to each refinement step. We introduce Fixed-Point Masked Generative Models (FP-MGMs), which replace part of the denoiser with a fixed-point solver over shared attention layers to enable adaptive depth with fewer parameters. To make it more effective for masked generation, we first introduce a cross-step consistency loss, which aligns hidden representations at neighboring denoising steps and, second, three-state reuse (3SR) which warm-starts the solver using the previous solution by treating differently unchanged, still-masked, and newly revealed tokens respectively. Together, these components define our complete training-to-inference framework for fixed-point masked generation, \emph{CoFRe}. We also show that pre-trained MGMs can be converted into FP-MGMs with short fine-tuning, avoiding full retraining. Across modalities, CoFRe improves the quality and cost trade-off. On OpenWebText, CoFRe reduces parameters by 38.8\%, training time by 11.5\%, and VRAM by 16.9\%, while improving generative perplexity from 830.8 to 101.8 at a budget of $96$ transformer-block forward passes, compared to MDLM. In ImageNette, CoFRe reduces training time by 48.6\% and VRAM by 50.7\%, while improving FID in all sample budgets tested. Overall, CoFRe offers a practical framework for cheaper training and stronger low-budget masked generation.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文形式化定义了标签排序（label ranking）中的校准概念，建立了包含完整排序、子排序和top-k排序的校准层次体系，并证明各层次之间的关系。实验发现流行的标签排序模型校准性较差，且将框架应用于RLHF奖励模型后发现校准与基准准确率强相关但不完全一致，表明校准捕捉了超越top-1准确率的质量维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 校准（预测概率与真实结果频率的对齐）在分类和回归中已被广泛研究，但在概率标签排序（预测标签集排序上的分布）中尚未被正式定义。标签排序在RLHF奖励建模等应用中至关重要，但现有模型往往缺乏校准性分析，因此需要建立标签排序的校准理论框架。

Method: 形式化定义了标签排序的校准概念，建立包含完整排序校准、子排序校准和top-k排序校准的层次体系，并证明其蕴含关系。通过实验评估流行标签排序模型的校准性，并将框架应用于RLHF奖励模型，分析校准与基准准确率之间的关系。

Result: 证明了完整排序校准蕴含子排序和top-k校准，但反之不成立；子排序校准与top-k校准不可比较。实验发现流行标签排序模型校准性较差，子排序与top-k指标存在显著差异。在RLHF奖励模型上，校准与基准准确率强相关但不完全一致。

Abstract: Calibration, the alignment of predicted probabilities with true outcome frequencies, is essential for reliable decision-making. While extensively studied for classification and regression, calibration has not been formally addressed for probabilistic label ranking, where the goal is to predict a distribution over orderings of a label set. Naively treating rankings as classes ignores their structure and fails to capture important modalities such as pairwise and top-k predictions. We formalize calibration for label ranking and develop a hierarchy of notions covering full rankings, sub-rankings, and top-k rankings. We prove that full-rank calibration implies the others but not conversely, and sub-ranking and top-k calibration are incomparable. Empirically, we find popular label ranking models are often poorly calibrated, with substantial differences between sub-ranking and top-k metrics. Applying our framework to RLHF reward models, we find that calibration correlates strongly but not perfectly with benchmark accuracy, suggesting it captures a meaningful quality dimension beyond top-1 accuracy. These findings motivate future work on understanding the downstream effects of miscalibration and developing methods to correct it.

</details>

Abstract: Pairwise preference data is widely used in language-model evaluation and alignment, often for model ranking, reward modeling, or preference optimization. This note formulates a more basic measurement question: given a reference distribution of pairwise preferences, what model-level quantity is estimated when we test whether a model ranks preferred responses above rejected responses? We define pairwise reference alignment as an ordinal observable induced by a model scoring function. Given a reference pair distribution $P_{\mathrm{pair}}$ over triples $(x,y^+,y^-)$, and a scalar model score $S_M(x,y)$, we define the alignment observable as the probability that the model-induced ordering agrees with the reference preference ordering. We further define a centered order-parameter-like statistic and discuss a margin-based extension. The resulting quantities admit simple finite-sample estimators and concentration bounds under independent sampling assumptions. This note does not introduce a new benchmark. It provides a conceptual and statistical formulation for pairwise reference alignment, clarifies the role of the reference pair distribution, and distinguishes the general ordinal observable from scoring choices such as normalized log-probability or energy-based scores. We also provide an initial empirical study on Qwen2.5 models and RewardBench, where the proposed statistics increase with model size and instruction tuning and vary across reference-pair subsets as predicted by the formulation.

</details>

*Yanjiang Liu, Jie Lou, Xinyan Guan, Yuqiu Ji, Hongyu Lin, Ben He, Xianpei Han, Le Sun, Xing Yu, Yaojie Lu*

TL;DR: 本文发现 on-policy 蒸馏中教师监督信号随学生生成序列增长而衰减（SFD），并提出 Lookahead Group Reward（LGR）机制，通过评估学生候选 token 在后续步骤中诱导的教师置信度来分配组归一化奖励，配合熵触发的树注意力机制，在数学和代码推理任务上显著提升学生模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 on-policy 蒸馏中，学生模型使用自身生成的轨迹进行训练，教师提供 token 级反馈。然而随着学生生成前缀变长，教师对下一个 token 的预测分布变得不自信且区分度下降，导致反向 KL 散度蒸馏中的纠正信号减弱，学生模型在长推理链上出现漂移。现有方法未能有效解决这一监督保真度衰减问题。

Method: 提出 Lookahead Group Reward（LGR）：基于"当前步教师置信度反映未来反向 KL 监督的区分强度"这一洞察，LGR 通过评估学生 top-K 候选 token 在下一步诱导的教师置信度来分配组归一化奖励。同时设计熵触发的树注意力机制以保持计算效率，仅在教师分布熵较高时展开树搜索。

Result: 在六个数学和代码基准上，LGR 在 7B 学生模型上相比 on-policy 蒸馏平均提升 mean@8 达 2.57 点，在长生成任务上增益更大，AIME-26 上在 39k token 长度下提升达 4.92 点。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了 on-policy 蒸馏中的监督保真度衰减问题，并提出 LGR 有效缓解该问题，为长链推理场景下的知识蒸馏提供了新的奖励机制设计思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 on-policy 蒸馏中教师模型的监督信号衰减问题，并提出基于未来 token 置信度的组奖励机制（Lookahead Group Reward），属于生成模型的对齐与奖励优化范畴。该论文同时涉及 AIGC 方向（生成工作流中的蒸馏与对齐），但核心贡献在奖励建模与偏好优化，因此主方向为多模态生成下的 generation_alignment 子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on on-policy distillation for reasoning, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous VLM or video generation. Signals: on-policy distillation, supervision fidelity decay, lookahead group reward, math/code reasoning, not multimodal generation or video/image generation

Abstract: On-policy distillation transfers reasoning capabilities by training a student model on its own generated trajectories using token-level feedback from a teacher. However, we identify a critical bottleneck, \textbf{Supervision Fidelity Decay (SFD)}: as student-generated prefixes lengthen, the teacher's next-token distribution becomes less confident and less discriminative. Consequently, the teacher-dependent corrective signal in reverse-KL distillation weakens, causing student drift to compound across long reasoning chains. To mitigate SFD, we introduce \textbf{Lookahead Group Reward (\ours{})}. Building on the insight that next-step teacher confidence reflects the discriminative strength of future reverse-KL supervision, \ours{} evaluates the student's top-K candidate tokens by the teacher confidence they induce at the subsequent step and assigns a group-normalized reward. To maintain computational efficiency, we further design an entropy-triggered tree-attention mechanism. Across six math and code benchmarks, \ours{} improves mean@8 by \textbf{2.57} points over OPD for a 7B student, with gains increasing in longer-generation and reaching +\textbf{4.92} points on AIME-26 at 39k tokens.

</details>

Abstract: Building strong reward models (RMs) for language model alignment is bottlenecked by the cost and difficulty of acquiring diverse and reliable preference data from human annotation or judge models. It is dramatically worse as the policy evolves beyond the static RM training. Therefore, we propose SAVE (Self-supervised reward model improvement via Value-Anchored On-policy feedback), a framework that grades on-policy responses as feedback by using the value function for on-policy RM training. SAVE naturally converts the reward-graded on-policy responses into supervision with a prompt-specific value head as an adaptive anchor. It computes RM advantages and filters ambiguous samples to update the RM via a contrastive objective. The effectiveness of SAVE for enhancing RM training is strongly validated through rigorous empirical evaluation across six diverse benchmarks. It achieves outperforming results across all datasets while maintaining consistent improvements across three RL algorithms (GRPO, RLOO, GSPO) and different policy backbones.

</details>

Conclusion: FedVPA-GP 通过联邦混合先验和正交损失有效解决了联邦场景下变分偏好学习中的后验坍塌问题，实现了隐私保护下的个性化偏好对齐，为联邦 LLM 对齐提供了新范式。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) offers a privacy-preserving pathway for aligning Large Language Models (LLMs); however, existing frameworks typically enforce a monolithic reward model, inevitably averaging out inherently conflicting user preferences (e.g., helpfulness vs. harmlessness). While Variational Preference Learning (VPL) offers a pathway to personalization, adapting it to decentralized settings presents a fundamental challenge: posterior collapse driven by severe local data scarcity and heterogeneity. In this paper, we propose Federated Variational Preference Alignment with Gumbel-Softmax Prior (FedVPA-GP), a framework designed to disentangle diverse preferences without compromising privacy. To stabilize variational inference, we introduce a Federated Mixture Prior that enables clients to leverage the aggregate population distribution as a dynamic prior. Furthermore, we incorporate an Orthogonal Loss that explicitly enforces the separation of preference prototypes in the latent space. Experiments on the HH-RLHF dataset demonstrate that FedVPA-GP significantly outperforms monolithic baselines, successfully disentangling conflicting user intents and enabling dynamic preference switching.

</details>

Conclusion: 并行回火作为一种有效的初始采样策略，能显著提升推理时奖励对齐在复杂奖励景观下的表现，为扩散/流模型的推理时可控生成提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究推理时奖励对齐（inference-time reward alignment），通过并行回火（parallel tempering）改进扩散/流模型的初始采样，以更好地满足用户指定的奖励函数。这属于生成模型对齐与奖励优化的范畴，与 canonical subtopic "generation_alignment（生成模型对齐与奖励）" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly improves reward alignment for diffusion/flow generative models, a key technique for controllable generation in your priority subtopics. Signals: inference-time reward alignment, parallel tempering for sampling, diffusion/flow generative models, layout-to-image generation, quantity-aware generation

Abstract: Inference-time reward alignment steers pretrained diffusion and flow-based generative models to satisfy user-specified rewards without retraining. Recently, Sequential Monte Carlo (SMC) has emerged as a powerful framework for this task by iteratively filtering and propagating multiple particles. However, we show that standard SMC-based methods often suffer from poor performance because they initialize particles from a standard prior, whereas high-reward regions in complex reward landscapes are extremely rare. Further, we show that even recent reward-aware initial sampling approaches remain vulnerable to getting trapped in local modes, as complex reward landscapes are often multi-modal. To overcome these limitations, we propose PATHS (PArallel Tempering for High-complexity reward Sampling), a novel initialization method that couples multiple sampling chains through parallel tempering. PATHS maintains a ladder of reward-tempered chains and periodically performs Metropolis swaps, enabling efficient exploration across flattened reward landscapes, thereby mitigating the mode-trapping issues. Our analysis reveals that this mechanism substantially enhances the finite-budget exploration of rare, high-reward regions that are typically challenging to sample. Experiments on layout-to-image and quantity-aware generation show that PATHS achieves consistent gains in alignment quality, particularly on complex prompts.

</details>

Conclusion: TRB 作为一种简单有效的预热方法，能够显著提升 on-policy 蒸馏的效果，尤其适用于学生策略早期 rollout 质量较差的场景。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student on prefixes sampled from its own policy while matching a stronger teacher. This addresses the prefix mismatch of offline distillation, but early student rollouts can still be poor, placing teacher supervision on weak or low-quality prefixes. We propose Trust-Region behavior Blending (TRB), a warmup method that replaces the early rollout policy with the closest-to-teacher behavior policy inside a student-centered KL trust region, while keeping the per-prefix reverse-KL OPD loss unchanged. The KL budget is annealed to zero, so training returns to pure student rollouts after warmup. Across two math-reasoning distillation settings, TRB attains the strongest average among the compared methods.

</details>

Importance reason: Provides a unifying theoretical framework for generative models via Wasserstein flows, but lacks direct application to the user's priority subtopics like continuous VLM or video generation. Signals: Unifying theory for generative models, JKO scheme connections, extends to IPM and MMD, clarifies GANs links, no direct video/image generation focus

Abstract: Many modern generative models can be viewed as minimizing divergences between probability distributions, yet they rely on different algorithmic and geometric principles. Wasserstein gradient flows provide a continuous-time formulation for optimizing over distributions, and can be approximated through their implicit discretization via the Jordan-Kinderlehrer-Otto (JKO) scheme. In this work, we present a unified theoretical framework for generative modeling based on Wasserstein gradient flows, which we refer to as Generative Wasserstein Flows (GWF). We show that a broad class of existing methods can be derived as instances of parametric JKO schemes for $f$-divergence objectives, and we establish equivalences between several recently proposed algorithms. We extend this framework beyond f-divergence to Integral Probability Metrics and squared Maximum Mean Discrepancy, deriving new JKO-based generative algorithms, and clarifying their connections with GANs. We study empirically the impact of the JKO regularization for a wide set of objectives. Finally, we analyze parametric Wasserstein flows, where the dynamics are restricted to distributions induced by parametrized maps.

</details>

Result: 在跨域计数任务上，Count Anything在多个领域（通用场景、遥感、病理、细胞、农业、微生物）均取得领先性能，显著优于现有开放世界计数方法，展示了强大的多域泛化能力。

Classification reason: 该论文构建了跨域大规模目标计数数据集CLOC（涵盖6个视觉领域、约220K图像、619个类别），并提出通用文本引导计数模型Count Anything。核心贡献在于统一评测基准和跨域泛化能力，与[multimodal_generation]方向下的[generation_benchmark]（生成模型评测与数据集）高度匹配。

Abstract: Object counting remains fragmented across domain-specific datasets and task formulations, despite rapid progress in generalist vision models. Existing counting models are often tailored to scenarios such as crowds, vehicles, cells, crops, or remote-sensing objects, and thus struggle to generalize across categories, visual domains, object scales, and density distributions. In this paper, we study text-guided object counting across domains, where a model takes an image and a natural-language query as input and returns an instance-grounded set of target points whose cardinality gives the count. This formulation unifies category-conditioned counting with interpretable spatial localization. To support this setting, we construct CLOC, a Cross-domain Large-scale Object Counting dataset that reorganizes diverse public data sources into a unified benchmark. CLOC covers six visual domains: General Scene, Remote Sensing, Histopathology, Cellular Microscopy, Agriculture, and Microbiology, with about 220K images, 619 categories, and 15M object instances. Based on CLOC, we propose Count Anything, a generalist model for text-guided object counting. Unlike density-map-based methods, which dominate counting models, Count Anything adopts discrete instance points and performs dual-granularity instance enumeration. A Region-level Sparse Counter provides object-level anchors for large and sparse targets, while a Pixel-level Dense Counter handles small, crowded, and weakly bounded targets via dense point prediction. A point-centric supervision strategy enables learning from heterogeneous annotations, and Complementary Count Fusion combines both counters in a parameter-free manner. Extensive experiments show that Count Anything achieves strong accuracy and multi-domain generalization, outperforming existing open-world counting methods. Code is available at: .

</details>

Result: 在多个SOTA语言模型上评估发现：模型能回答图属性问题，但在完整图变换任务上表现不佳，存在"理解-执行鸿沟"；在更大图实例上性能显著下降，暴露出扩展性障碍。

Conclusion: GraphARC将节点分类、链接预测和图生成整合在一个框架中，为未来图基础模型提供了有前景的测试平台，揭示了当前模型在图结构抽象推理方面的局限性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了GraphARC，一个基于图结构的抽象推理基准，属于生成模型评测与数据集（generation_benchmark）范畴。它扩展了ARC的少样本变换学习范式，涵盖图变换生成任务，可系统评估模型的泛化能力，与[多模态生成]方向下的[生成模型评测与数据集]高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on graph-based abstract reasoning benchmark, not aligned with user's priority on multimodal generation, video/image generation, or efficient inference. Signals: Graph ARC benchmark, abstract reasoning on graphs, comprehension-execution gap, not multimodal generation, not video/image generation

Abstract: Relational reasoning lies at the heart of intelligence, but existing benchmarks are typically confined to formats such as grids or text. We introduce GraphARC, a benchmark for abstract reasoning on graph-structured data. GraphARC generalizes the few-shot transformation learning paradigm of the Abstraction and Reasoning Corpus (ARC). Each task requires inferring a transformation rule from a few input-output pairs and applying it to a new test graph, covering local, global, and hierarchical graph transformations. Unlike grid-based ARC, GraphARC instances can be generated at scale across diverse graph families and sizes, enabling systematic evaluation of generalization abilities. We evaluate state-of-the-art language models on GraphARC and observe clear limitations. Models can answer questions about graph properties but often fail to solve the full graph transformation task, revealing a comprehension-execution gap. Performance further degrades on larger instances, exposing scaling barriers. More broadly, by combining aspects of node classification, link prediction, and graph generation within a single framework, GraphARC provides a promising testbed for future graph foundation models.

</details>

Abstract: Conditional generative models have recently achieved remarkable success in various applications. However, a suitable metric for evaluating the reliability of these models, which takes into account their inherent uncertainty, is still lacking. Existing metrics, which typically assess a single output, may fail to capture the variability or potential risks in generation. In this paper, we propose a novel evaluation metric called reliability score based on conformal prediction, which measures the worst-case performance within the prediction set at a pre-specified confidence level. However, computing this score is challenging due to the high-dimensional nature of the output space and the nonconvexity of both the metric function and the prediction set. To efficiently compute this score, we introduce Conformal ReLiability (CReL), a framework that can (i) construct the prediction set with desired coverage; and (ii) accurately optimize the reliability score within the constructed prediction set. We provide theoretical results on coverage and demonstrate empirically that our method produces more informative prediction sets than existing approaches. Experiments on synthetic data and the image-to-text and text-to-image tasks further demonstrate the interpretability of our new metric, and the validity and effectiveness of our computational framework. Source code can be found at .

</details>

Abstract: AI systems are increasingly used to support educational content creation, yet it remains unclear whether they can generate outputs that faithfully represent the pedagogical concepts they are intended to teach. Thus, we introduce equation-to-visual generation, a task that, in contrast to conventional image generation, requires producing pedagogically meaningful visuals from arithmetic equations while precisely preserving their numerical and relational structure. Informed by interviews with teachers and an analysis of educational materials, we construct E2V-Bench, a benchmark spanning four pedagogically grounded visual types, along with automatic metrics for evaluating visual correctness. Our evaluation reveals that recent text-to-image (T2I) models frequently fail on this task, with errors dominated by incorrect object counts and broken relational structure. Building on this, we explore benchmark-guided enhancement strategies. These strategies improve representative models, while the remaining gap calls for stronger numerical and relational grounding in future T2I models.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于连接器的视频统一模型在指令引导的视频合成方面表现出色，但将大型高保真生成器集成到统一训练循环中计算成本过高，限制了可实现的视觉质量。因此，作者希望在不增加训练负担的前提下，提升统一模型的视频生成保真度。

Conclusion: Lumos-Nexus 通过训练高效的连接器设计和推理阶段的渐进式频率桥接，成功实现了推理驱动的视频生成，在保持推理质量的同时大幅提升了视觉保真度，为视频统一模型的发展提供了有效路径。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Lumos-Nexus，一个连接器式的视频统一模型，将理解模块与轻量生成器对齐，并通过渐进式频率桥接（UPFB）在共享潜空间中利用高保真预训练生成器，实现推理驱动的视频生成。这属于理解与生成一体化的统一多模态模型方向，与 multimodal_generation 和 multimodal_understanding_generation 下的现有 subtopic "统一多模态模型"（unified_multimodal_model）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Proposes a training-efficient video unified model with frequency bridging in shared latent space, directly aligning with unified multimodal and efficient inference priorities. Signals: unified multimodal model, efficient training, frequency bridging, shared latent space, reasoning-driven video generation

Abstract: Connector-based video unified models have demonstrated strong capability in instruction-grounded video synthesis, but integrating a large high-fidelity generator into the unified training loop is computationally prohibitive, limiting achievable visual quality. We therefore propose Lumos-Nexus, a training-efficient unified video generation framework that facilitates the development of strong reasoning-driven generation capabilities while significantly enhancing visual fidelity. Lumos-Nexus adopts a two-stage design: 1) During training, only a lightweight generator is aligned with the understanding block to learn to take in reasoning-driven semantic control. 2) During inference, we introduce Unified Progressive Frequency Bridging (UPFB) to progressively hand off generation to a high-capacity pretrained generator in the shared latent space, enabling coarse-to-fine refinement and producing high-fidelity videos without compromising reasoning quality. To fill the gap in reasoning-driven video generation benchmarks, we introduce VR-Bench, which assesses a model's capability to translate inferred intent into coherent and semantically aligned video content. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Lumos-Nexus achieves substantial gains in visual realism and temporal coherence on VBench, while exhibiting strong reasoning-based generative performance on VR-Bench. Code and models are available at .

</details>

TL;DR: 本文指出现有观测预测型世界模型在具身AI中存在结构性缺陷：不同物理系统可能视觉上相同但在干预下表现不同。作者提出物理可行的世界模型应识别出足以回答干预查询的最简物理抽象，由模块化组件（环境表征、状态估计、动作规范、干预动力学、查询响应）和自主编排器组成，并展示了该方法在现有系统失败案例上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型仅预测未来观测，能生成视觉合理但物理错误的推演结果。不同物理系统可能外观相同但在干预下行为迥异，导致模型推荐不可行动作、错误预测交互结果或认证不安全行为。需要一种物理可行的世界模型设计原则。

Method: 提出物理可行世界模型框架：模型应识别出足以回答干预查询的最简物理抽象，包含模块化组件（环境表征、潜状态与参数估计、动作规范、干预动力学、查询级响应），并由自主编排器动态组合兼容的学习组件和结构化组件。当封闭形式物理不可用时，转移模型可以是解析、模拟、学习或混合的，但必须保留决定干预结果的结构。

Result: 在现有系统无法正确回答的查询上展示了该方法的有效性，证明了正确的抽象不是最详细的世界模型，而是保留与查询相关区分的最简模型。

Conclusion: 物理可行的世界模型应基于查询条件构建最简物理抽象，使模型可解释、组件可验证、输出可审计。该框架为设计新世界模型和测试现有模型提供了设计原则和可行性检验标准。

Classification reason: 该论文讨论具身AI的世界模型，强调物理可行的世界模型应能回答干预查询而非仅预测观测，属于视频世界模型（video_world_model）这一[CANONICAL]子主题的核心范畴。

Importance reason: Directly addresses a fundamental flaw in world models for embodied AI, aligning with the user's high-priority subtopic on video generation world models and boosting autoregressive/video generation keywords. Signals: World model structural critique, Query-conditioned physical abstraction, Embodied AI intervention queries, Modular world model design, High priority subtopic match

Abstract: World models for embodied AI must be physically viable: constructed to answer intervention queries by representing the physical structure governing action outcomes, rather than merely predicting future observations. Existing observation-predictive world models can produce visually plausible but physically wrong rollouts. This failure is structural; distinct physical systems can look identical yet diverge under intervention. We expose this problem with controlled benchmarks that fix the visible scene while varying latent physics. We show that such models may recommend infeasible actions, mispredict interaction outcomes, or certify unsafe behavior. We argue that embodied AI requires world models that identify the simplest physical abstraction sufficient to answer an intervention query. Such a model comprises modular components, including environment representation, latent state and parameter estimation, action specification, interventional dynamics, and query-level response. An autonomous orchestrator should identify the relevant abstraction and compose compatible learned and structured components per query. When closed-form physics is unavailable, uncertain, or costly, the transition model may be analytic, simulated, learned, or hybrid, but it must preserve the structure that determines interventional outcomes. This decomposition makes the model interpretable, its components verifiable, and its outputs auditable against the query. It also provides a design principle for new world models and a feasibility test for existing ones: the right abstraction is not the most detailed model of the world, but the simplest model that preserves the distinctions relevant to the query. We demonstrate this approach on queries that existing systems fail to answer correctly, and outline how an orchestrator can dynamically assemble and adapt physically viable models for planning, control, and verification.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出SLAP（语义最小作用量原理），将视频语言模型中的时序插值问题从概率生成范式转向变分力学范式。通过建立经典力学与语义动力学之间的严格同构，将潜在视频轨迹建模为黎曼流形上的路径，由语义拉格朗日量控制，并通过离散欧拉-拉格朗日方程求解边界值问题，实现无需像素级渲染的物体持久性保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视频语言模型（LVLMs）因稀疏帧采样导致"时序鸿沟"，无法捕捉关键的因果转换。现有基于生成幻觉（如潜在扩散）或自回归外推的方法在长时序上难以保持语义一致性，存在物体消失和能量不稳定性问题。

Method: 提出语义最小作用量原理（SLAP），建立经典力学与语义动力学的同构关系。将视频潜在轨迹建模为黎曼流形上的路径，由语义拉格朗日量（动能-势能）控制。将帧插值任务形式化为边界值问题（BVP），通过离散欧拉-拉格朗日方程求解，自然实现物体持久性约束而无需像素级渲染。

Result: 大量实验表明SLAP在语义一致性和物体持久性方面优于现有方法，有效缓解了长时序下的物体消失和能量不稳定问题。

Conclusion: SLAP为视频语言模型中的时序建模提供了一种全新的变分力学范式，从概率生成转向确定性物理约束，为长视频理解与生成开辟了新方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出SLAP（语义最小作用量原理），将视频帧插值建模为黎曼流形上的变分力学路径，通过离散欧拉-拉格朗日方程求解边界值问题，实现语义一致的视频轨迹建模。这与[视频世界模型]子主题高度相关，因为其核心是建模视频中的因果时序动态和物理一致性，属于视频世界模型/神经模拟器范畴。

Importance reason: Directly advances continuous VLM and visual latent flow via a novel variational mechanics framework, aligning with top-priority subtopics. Signals: continuous vision-language model, visual latent flow, variational mechanics, object persistence, temporal gap

Abstract: In the era of Large Video-Language Models (LVLMs), the computational necessity of sparse frame sampling creates a fundamental ``temporal gap'', rendering models blind to critical causal transitions. Existing solutions relying on generative hallucination (e.g., latent diffusion) or autoregressive extrapolation often fail to maintain semantic consistency over long horizons, suffering from object vanishing and energetic instability. We propose a paradigm shift from probabilistic generation to variational mechanics with the \textbf{Semantic Least Action Principle (SLAP)}. Drawing a rigorous isomorphism between classical mechanics and semantic dynamics, we model the latent video trajectory as a path on a Riemannian manifold governed by a Semantic Lagrangian. By formulating the interpolation task as a Boundary Value Problem (BVP) solved via the discrete Euler-Lagrange equations, SLAP naturally enforces object persistence without pixel-level rendering. Extensive experiments show the effectiveness of our proposed SLAP.

</details>

TL;DR: Robust Dreamer 提出了一种记忆增强的动作控制视频生成框架，通过潜高斯记忆（Latent Gaussian Memory）和偏差学习（Deviation Learning）解决长程自回归视频生成中的累积漂移问题，在ScanNet、DL3DV和OmniWorldGame上取得了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作控制图像到视频生成方法在长程自回归生成中面临严重的累积漂移问题，主要源于两个障碍：(1) Latent-RGB循环导致的信息损失——生成的潜变量反复解码为RGB再重新编码用于后续条件化；(2) 训练-推理差距——干净训练记忆与预测损坏的推理记忆不匹配。

Method: 提出 Robust Dreamer 框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) Latent Gaussian Memory——将生成过程中继承的扩散潜变量锚定到高斯原语上，通过潜空间高斯泼溅（Gaussian Splatting）召回，提供密集的几何感知条件化，避免VAE反复转换的累积退化；(2) Deviation Learning with Dynamic Deviation Archive——通过一步近似合成自回归 rollout 导致的潜偏差，按自回归阶段和去噪时间戳存储，在训练时注入历史记忆，使生成器暴露于真实损坏的记忆状态并学习内部修正。

Result: 在ScanNet、DL3DV和OmniWorldGame三个数据集上的实验表明，Robust Dreamer在长程视频生成任务中取得了最先进的性能，有效缓解了累积漂移问题。

Conclusion: Robust Dreamer通过记忆增强和偏差学习有效解决了动作控制视频生成中的长程漂移问题，为交互式世界模拟提供了一种鲁棒的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出动作控制的图像到视频生成框架用于交互式世界模拟，属于视频世界模型（video_world_model）这一[CANONICAL]子主题。同时涉及AIGC方向下的视频生成应用。

Importance reason: Directly addresses long-horizon autoregressive video generation with world model potential, matching top priority subtopics. Signals: autoregressive video generation, world model, video generation, latent Gaussian memory, deviation learning

Abstract: Frame-wise action-controlled image-to-video generation is a promising paradigm for interactive world simulation, where each control signal should elicit an immediate visual response. However, maintaining visual fidelity and 3D consistency over long autoregressive rollouts remains challenging. Existing 3D-aware methods often suffer from catastrophic drift due to two impediments: information loss from \textit{Latent--RGB Cycling}, where generated latents are repeatedly decoded to RGB and re-encoded for future conditioning, and the training--inference gap induced by the \textit{error-free hypothesis}, where clean training memory fails to match prediction-corrupted inference memory. To address these challenges, we present \textbf{Robust Dreamer}, a memory-augmented framework built around how to design 3D memory and how to use it robustly. First, we introduce \textbf{Latent Gaussian Memory}, which anchors diffusion latents inherited from the generation process to Gaussian primitives and recalls them via latent-space Gaussian splatting. This provides dense, geometry-aware, view-aligned conditioning while avoiding accumulated degradation from repeated VAE conversion. Second, we propose \textbf{Deviation Learning with Dynamic Deviation Archive}, which synthesizes rollout-induced latent deviations through a one-step approximation, stores them by autoregressive stage and denoising timestamp, and injects them into historical memory during training. This exposes the generator to realistic corrupted memory states and teaches internal correction before inference. Experiments on ScanNet, DL3DV, and OmniWorldGame demonstrate state-of-the-art long-horizon performance.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: Light Interaction 包含三个核心组件：(1) 自适应上下文管理——探索新区域时丢弃检索到的空间记忆，根据局部潜动态调整时间上下文；(2) 去噪缓存加速——当相机回到熟悉区域时复用早期步骤的模型输出；(3) 硬件-软件协同设计的3D块稀疏注意力，使用融合Triton内核实现高效计算。

Result: 在 HY-WorldPlay 和 Matrix-Game-3.0 基准上，Light Interaction 实现了最高2.59倍加速，无需模型重新训练，同时保持了有竞争力的视觉质量。

Conclusion: 本文证明了交互式视频世界模型可以通过利用交互轨迹的自适应计算特性实现显著推理加速，为实时交互式视频生成应用提供了实用的高效推理方案。

Importance reason: Directly addresses inference acceleration for interactive video world models, a high-priority subtopic, with a training-free method. Signals: video world model, inference acceleration, training-free, interactive, 3D block sparse attention

Abstract: Interactive video world models generate video chunk by chunk in response to user-controlled camera movements, enabling applications such as real-time game simulation, virtual scene navigation, and embodied AI training. However, scaling to long interactive trajectories is prohibitively expensive due to growing context memory, quadratic attention complexity, and repeated denoising steps. We present Light Interaction, a training-free inference acceleration framework for interactive video world models. Our key insight is that interaction naturally enables trajectory-dependent adaptive computation: retrieved spatial memory can be discarded during novel exploration, temporal context can be adjusted according to local latent dynamics, and early-step model outputs can be reused when the camera revisits familiar regions. Based on this insight, Light Interaction combines adaptive context management, denoising cache acceleration, and hardware-software co-designed 3D block sparse attention with fused Triton kernels. Evaluated on HY-WorldPlay and Matrix-Game-3.0, Light Interaction achieves up to 2.59x speedup without model retraining while maintaining competitive visual quality.

</details>

Abstract: Accurate weather forecasts are essential across various domains and are safety-critical in extreme weather conditions. Compared to simulation-based forecasting, data-driven approaches show greater efficiency, enabling short-term, high-resolution nowcasting. In particular, diffusion models proved effective in weather nowcasting due to their strong probabilistic foundation. However, existing methods rely on deterministic compression to reduce the complexity of high-dimensional weather data, limiting their ability to capture uncertainty in the decoding process. In this work, we introduce $\textbf{FREUD}$, a $\textbf{Fr}$ame-wise $\textbf{E}$ncoder and $\textbf{U}$nited $\textbf{D}$ecoder model based on rectified flow transformers for efficient compression of spatio-temporal weather data. Frame-wise encoding enables continuous forecast updates, while the unified video decoder ensures temporal consistency. Our uncertainty-preserving first stage allows us to capture aleatoric uncertainty via ensembling, which is particularly beneficial for extreme weather events with high decoding variability. We achieve state-of-the-art performance in precipitation nowcasting with a compact latent-space rectified flow transformer on the SEVIR benchmark and show further performance gains by model and test-time scaling. </details>

Signals: video world model, long video generation, decoupled memory, attention dispersion, minute-level consistency

Abstract: Recent advances in video generative models have promoted rapid progress in controllable world models. However, maintaining fine-grained spatio-temporal consistency under long-horizon reasoning remains a key challenge. In this work, we move beyond explicit 3D memory and coarse frame-level implicit modeling, and propose a fine-grained, learnable, and scalable memory for consistent world generation. We first identify two fundamental limitations of naïve learnable memory architectures in long-horizon extrapolation, namely computational inefficiency and attention dispersion. Through a systematic analysis of attention dispersion, we propose DecMem, a decoupled memory architecture that employs Sparse Global Memory for efficient fine-grained access to global history and Anchored Local Memory for stable and high-quality extrapolation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DecMem significantly outperforms current state-of-the-art methods. By ensuring precise and efficient long-term memory and achieving superior extrapolation capabilities, DecMem enables minute-level controllable long video generation with high fidelity and consistency.

</details>

*Lucas Thil, Jesse Read, Rim Kaddah, Guillaume Doquet*

Main category: cs.LG

Direction: 多模态生成

TL;DR: 本文提出SD-JEPA，将JEPA潜空间分解为正交的进度子空间（低维，通过余弦间隔三元组损失塑造）和内容子空间（高维，通过SIGReg正则化），实现任务进度与内容表征的解耦。该方法在控制基准上优于LeWM基线，且进度坐标可作为场景感知的"罗盘"，在回溯、扰动等情况下表现出语义合理的重定位能力，在定位语义事件上比传统预测误差方法AUROC提升最多0.18。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有JEPA（联合嵌入预测架构）学习紧凑的潜世界模型，但潜空间中没有任何单一坐标被指定编码任务进度，导致进度信息与内容信息纠缠在一起，不利于规划、异常检测和场景理解。

Method: 将JEPA潜空间分解为两个正交子空间：低维进度子空间（通过余弦间隔三元组损失塑造，使同一任务阶段的嵌入靠近、不同阶段的远离）和高维内容子空间（通过SIGReg目标正则化）。证明两个抗坍缩力作用于不相交的坐标上，因此它们加性组合而非竞争。在LeWM基线基础上引入子空间分解。

Result: 在控制基准上以相同计算量优于LeWM基线，在Push-T上优于最强非LeWM JEPA基线。进度坐标（1维角度）随任务进度前进、回溯时后退、扰动时尖峰并重定位到语义合适的任务阶段。|Δθ_t|在定位40个保留立方体情节的语义事件上，AUROC提升最多+0.18（±1步容忍度下每情节胜率97.5%）。8维进度子空间（占潜空间4.2%）解释了72-95%的任务进度方差。

Conclusion: SD-JEPA通过子空间分解有效解耦了世界模型中的进度与内容信息，进度坐标可作为场景感知的罗盘，在规划、异常检测和场景理解中具有潜力。子空间消融实验证实分解是性能提升的关键因素。

Classification reason: 该论文提出SD-JEPA，在潜空间中学习世界模型，通过子空间分解解耦任务进度与内容表征，属于视频世界模型/生成式世界模型方向，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "视频世界模型"（video_world_model）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances world model and continuous latent representation for video generation, aligning with top priority subtopics. Signals: World model with disentangled progression/content, Cosine-margin triplet loss for latent subspace, Outperforms LeWM on control benchmarks, Scene-aware compass for semantic relocalization, Strong quantitative surprise detection results

Abstract: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) learn compact latent world models by predicting future embeddings, but no single coordinate of the latent is designated to encode task progression. We carve the JEPA latent into two orthogonal subspaces with disjoint roles: a low-dimensional progression subspace shaped by a cosine-margin triplet loss, and a high-dimensional content subspace regularised by the existing SIGReg objective of LeWM. We prove that the two anti-collapse forces act on disjoint coordinates, so they compose additively rather than competing on the same dimensions. Our method, SD-JEPA improves over the LeWM baseline on the majority of its control benchmarks at matched compute, and outperforms the strongest non-LeWM JEPA baseline on Push-T; a subspace-ablation falsifier confirms the split is the load-bearing ingredient. Beyond planning, the resulting 1-D angular progression coordinate functions as a scene-aware compass on the latent. It advances with task progress, regresses when the agent backtracks, and under controlled perturbations both spikes and relocalises to a semantically appropriate new task-phase sector, separating the moment of surprise from its meaning in a way that prediction-error scalars cannot. Three quantitative tests back this up: $|\Delta\theta_t|$ outperforms the standard latent-prediction-error surprise at localising semantic events on 40 held-out cube episodes by up to +0.18 pooled AUROC (97.5% per-episode win rate at $\pm 1$-step tolerance); a within-episode linear probe across all four environments (40 episodes per env) shows the 8-dimensional progression subspace (4.2% of the latent) explains 72-95% of task-progress variance..

</details>

<details>

Result: 在视频到空间音频和文本到空间音频两个任务上均取得了优于现有方法的性能，展示了高质量的空间音频生成效果。

Conclusion: SwanSphere 通过因果自回归扩散Transformer和对比学习策略，实现了流式、高质量的空间音频生成，为沉浸式体验提供了有效方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出SwanSphere，一个从全景视频和文本提示生成高质量空间音频的流式框架，属于音视频联合生成与跨模态条件音频生成，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "audio_av_generation（音频与多模态联合生成）"高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel streaming autoregressive diffusion transformer for spatial audio, but only tangentially touches priority subtopics like autoregressive generation and multimodal understanding. Signals: autoregressive diffusion transformer, streaming spatial audio generation, multimodal contrastive learning, direct preference optimization, automated annotation pipeline

Abstract: Real-time and accurate spatial audio generation is pivotal for delivering an immersive experience. However, existing spatial audio synthesis technologies are often encumbered by a tradeoff between generation quality and high inference latency, as well as difficulty in capturing precise spatial information from multimodal inputs. To address these challenges, we propose SwanSphere, a unified streaming framework for high-fidelity spatial audio generation from panoramic videos and text prompts. SwanSphere mainly makes the following contributions: 1) We introduce a causal autoregressive diffusion transformer architecture that enables streaming high-quality spatial audio generation. 2) We design a Spatial Video-Audio Contrastive (SVAC) learning strategy to align the video encoder with the acoustic domain, and further employ a multi-objective online direct preference optimization (ODPO) scheme, resulting in strong spatial perception and robust multimodal spatial audio synthesis. 3) To alleviate the current scarcity of spatial audio datasets, we also develop an automated annotation pipeline for generating detailed spatial captions. Experimental results demonstrate that SwanSphere achieves superior performance in both video-to-spatial and text-to-spatial audio generation tasks. Demos can be found at: .

### [57] [Chatterbox-Flash: Prior-Calibrated Block Diffusion for Streaming Zero-Shot TTS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30748)
*Deokjin Seo, Gangin Park, Kihyun Nam*

Main category: eess.AS, cs.AI, cs.SD

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 音频与多模态联合生成

Importance: 普通相关 (57.94) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Chatterbox-Flash 是一种零样本文本到语音（TTS）模型，通过对预训练自回归 TTS 解码器进行微调，将其转换为块扩散解码器，实现块内并行 token 生成同时保持逐块流式推理。针对离散语音 token 的长尾分布导致并行位置选择偏向高频 token 的问题，提出了先验校准评分和早期解码调度两种推理时技术，在不修改架构的情况下提升生成质量。在标准零样本 TTS 基准上达到与强自回归和非自回归基线相当的高保真合成质量，同时支持流式推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归 TTS 模型生成质量高但推理速度慢，非自回归模型速度快但流式推理能力有限。块扩散解码在图像生成中已展现出兼顾并行生成与流式推理的潜力，但直接迁移到离散语音 token 上会导致质量下降，原因是语音 token 的长尾分布使得并行位置选择偏向高频 token。本文旨在解决这一迁移问题，实现高质量、低延迟的流式零样本 TTS。

Method: 1) 将预训练的自回归 TTS 解码器微调为块扩散解码器，每个块内并行生成多个 token，块间保持自回归流式生成。2) 提出先验校准评分（prior-calibrated scoring），在扩散迭代的去噪评分中减去块级边际 token 分布，消除高频 token 的偏差。3) 提出早期解码调度（early-decoding schedule），基于校准置信度自适应终止迭代，进一步加速推理。

Result: 在标准零样本 TTS 基准上，Chatterbox-Flash 达到了与强自回归和非自回归基线相当的高保真合成质量。流式推理的首包时间与流式自回归系统相当，实时因子显著更低。

Conclusion: 通过先验校准评分和早期解码调度两种轻量级推理时技术，成功将块扩散解码从图像领域迁移到离散语音 token 的 TTS 任务中，在不修改架构的前提下解决了长尾分布导致的生成退化问题，实现了高质量、低延迟的流式零样本 TTS 生成。

Classification reason: 该论文提出零样本文本到语音（TTS）生成模型，属于音频生成方向，与多模态生成方向下的 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "audio_av_generation（音频与多模态联合生成）" 高度匹配。同时该工作也属于 AIGC 范畴下的内容生成应用。

Importance reason: Novel block-diffusion for streaming TTS with prior calibration, but outside user's priority subtopics and negative keywords apply. Signals: Block-diffusion decoding, zero-shot TTS, streaming inference, prior-calibrated scoring, early-decoding schedule

Abstract: We present Chatterbox-Flash, a zero-shot text-to-speech model obtained by fine-tuning a pretrained autoregressive TTS decoder into a block-diffusion decoder, enabling parallel token generation within each block while retaining block-by-block streaming. We find that naively transferring mainstream block-diffusion decoding to discrete speech tokens degrades quality, as a long-tail token distribution biases parallel position selection toward a few high-frequency tokens. To mitigate this without architectural modification, we introduce two inference-time techniques: prior-calibrated scoring, which subtracts the block-level marginal token distribution, and an early-decoding schedule, which adaptively terminates iteration based on calibrated confidence. On standard zero-shot TTS benchmarks, Chatterbox-Flash attains high-fidelity synthesis comparable to strong autoregressive and non-autoregressive baselines, while supporting streaming inference with time-to-first-packet on par with streaming AR systems and substantially lower real-time factor. Code and audio samples are available at .

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出3DAE，一个面向音频新视角合成（Audio Novel View Synthesis）的双耳质量评估框架，通过时频音频误差图（幅度、ILD、IPD、时间对齐、响度、高频失败）构建可解释的诊断工具，并形成模型无关的基准3DAE Bench。实验揭示了不同模型（ViGAS在Replay-NVAS vs SoundSpaces）的失败模式差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D音频和新视角声学合成模型通常使用全局指标评估，但这些全局指标往往掩盖了双耳预测在何处以及为何失败，缺乏可解释的诊断工具来指导模型优化。

Method: 提出3DAE Map，一种全参考诊断框架，利用时频音频误差图对幅度、ILD（耳间电平差）、IPD（耳间相位差）、时间对齐、响度及高频失败进行可视化分析。在此基础上构建3DAE Bench，一个模型无关的基准，接受任意真实-预测双耳音频对并报告预测质量。

Result: 在ViGAS模型的两个数据集（Replay-NVAS和SoundSpaces）上进行实验，发现不同数据集上主导失败模式不同：Replay-NVAS上主要是时间错位，SoundSpaces上主要是ILD不匹配。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了可解释的失败模式总结和直观的可视化误差图，有助于音频新视角合成模型的开发和优化。

Classification reason: 论文关注双耳音频质量评估与空间音频误差分析，属于音频生成模型的评测方法，与 [CANONICAL] 音频与多模态联合生成 (audio_av_generation) 中的音频生成评测方向高度相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on audio evaluation, not on user's priority visual/multimodal generation or autoregressive video/image topics. Signals: Audio novel view synthesis, binaural quality assessment, spatial audio error maps, model-agnostic benchmark, not in user's priority subtopics

Abstract: 3D audio and novel-view acoustic synthesis models are usually evaluated with global , global metrics often hide where and why binaural prediction fails. We propose a full-reference diagnostic framework that uses time-frequency audio error maps for magnitude, ILD, IPD, temporal alignment, loudness, and high-frequency failures, forming a 3D Audio Error Map (3DAE Map) for visual inspection. We frame these diagnostics into a model-agnostic benchmark, Spatial Audio Error Bench (3DAE Bench), which takes arbitrary ground-truth and predicted binaural pairs and reports the prediction quality of audio novel-view synthesis models. Experiments on ViGAS outputs over Replay-NVAS and SoundSpaces show different dominant failure modes: temporal misalignment on Replay-NVAS and ILD mismatch on SoundSpaces. Overall, the framework provides interpretable failure-mode summaries and intuitive visual maps for audio Novel-view-synthesis model development optimization.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本引导音频生成在音效、语音、音乐等领域取得了进展，但联合生成与环境上下文融合的语音仍然困难，因为语音与环境音频在声学模式和时序动态上存在固有差异。现有方法缺乏对跨模态交互的显式建模。

Result: 在客观指标和人类听力测试中，ImmersiveTTS 在自然度、可懂度和音频保真度上均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: ImmersiveTTS 通过显式建模跨模态交互和领域特定表征对齐，有效实现了环境感知的语音生成，为将语音自然融入环境上下文的TTS任务提供了有效方案。

Importance reason: Novel multimodal diffusion TTS with environment-aware alignment, but outside user's core visual/video generation focus. Signals: Multimodal diffusion transformer, Domain-specific representation alignment, Environment-aware TTS, Audio-visual joint generation, Not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Recent advancements in text-guided audio generation have yielded promising results in diverse domains, including sound effects, speech, and music. However, jointly generating speech with environmental audio remains challenging due to the inherent disparities in their acoustic patterns and temporal dynamics. We propose ImmersiveTTS, an environment-aware text-to-speech (TTS) model that generates natural speech seamlessly integrated within environmental contexts by explicitly modeling cross-modal interactions. Our model builds on a multimodal diffusion transformer and fuses transcript-aligned speech latent with text-conditioned environmental context via joint attention. To enhance semantic consistency, we introduce a domain-specific representation alignment objective tailored to environment-aware TTS, leveraging complementary self-supervised representations from speech and audio encoders. Experimental results show that ImmersiveTTS achieves higher naturalness, intelligibility, and audio fidelity than existing approaches across objective metrics and human listening tests.

</details>

TL;DR: PhyDrawGen 是一个神经符号流水线，用于从自然语言问题生成物理上准确的示意图。它通过 LLM 提取场景图，经确定性求解器转换为精确几何图元，再通过 Qwen-VL 的提议-验证循环迭代修正约束违反，在力学、光学和电磁学基准上显著优于 GPT-5 和 Gemini 等模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型在生成物理示意图时虽然视觉上合理，但系统性地出现幻觉（如错误力向量）、忽略守恒定律、违反几何约束等问题，无法满足物理学科对严格物理定律遵循的要求。

Method: 提出 PhyDrawGen 神经符号流水线：(1) LLM 从问题文本提取类型化场景图；(2) 确定性求解器将场景图转换为平面直线图（PSLG），编码力平衡、光路和场拓扑为精确几何图元；(3) 微调后的 Qwen-VL 模型实现视觉上基于提议-验证的循环，迭代修正约束违反。

Result: 在包含 1,449 个问题的力学、光学和电磁学基准上，PhyDrawGen 显著优于 GPT-5-image、Gemini 2.5 Flash 和 Gemini 3 Pro，在非常规物体问题上也展现出鲁棒的物理准确性。

Conclusion: 将神经语义理解与确定性物理约束求解相结合的神经符号方法，能够有效解决生成模型在物理示意图生成中的幻觉和违反物理定律问题，为科学领域的内容生成提供了可行范式。

Classification reason: 该论文专注于从自然语言生成物理示意图，属于科学/物理领域的垂直内容生成应用，与现有 subtopic "领域内容生成应用"（aigc_domain_application）高度匹配。该 subtopic 在 multimodal_generation 和 aigc 方向下均有定义，可直接复用。

Importance reason: Niche application in physics diagram generation with limited methodological novelty for core multimodal generation priorities. Signals: neuro-symbolic pipeline, propose-verify loop, domain-specific benchmark, no autoregressive or diffusion video/image generation, no continuous VLM or latent flow

Abstract: Generating physics diagrams from text requires strict adherence to physical laws. While current generative models produce visually plausible outputs, they systematically hallucinate force vectors, ignore conservation laws, and violate geometric constraints. We present PhyDrawGen, a neuro-symbolic pipeline that decouples semantic scene understanding from physical constraint satisfaction. First, a large language model extracts a typed scene graph from the problem text. A deterministic solver then converts this graph into a Planar Straight-Line Graph (PSLG), encoding force balance, optical paths, and field topologies as exact geometric primitives. Finally, a fine-tuned Qwen-VL model implements a visually grounded propose-verify loop to iteratively correct any constraint violations. Evaluated on a benchmark of 1,449 problems spanning mechanics, optics, and electromagnetism, PhyDrawGen significantly outperforms GPT-5-image, Gemini 2.5 Flash, and Gemini 3 Pro, demonstrating robust physical accuracy even on unusual-object problems.

</details>


### [61] [Controllable Lung Nodule Synthesis via Histogram-Regularized Latent Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30631)
*Arunkumar Kannan, Yanbo Zhang, Han Liu, Michael Baumgartner, Jianing Wang, Alexander Hertel, Bogdan Georgescu, Sasa Grbic*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.LG, cs.CV

Importance: 普通相关 (37.62) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可控潜扩散模型，通过直方图正则化项约束体素强度分布，在3D CT体积中合成肺结节，提升不同亚型（实性、部分实性、磨玻璃）结节的视觉真实感与亚型一致性，并验证了其在数据增强和下游分类任务中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的肺结节合成方法主要优化空间重建损失，关注体素级相似性，但未能充分约束病灶级别的强度分布，导致生成结节纹理过于平滑、不同亚型（实性/部分实性/磨玻璃）的衰减特征区分不足。为解决这一问题，需要一种能精确建模结节特异性强度分布的生成方法。

Method: 提出一种可控潜扩散模型，在潜空间中进行生成。模型以结节亚型、空间掩码和HU直方图作为条件输入，并引入可微的特征空间直方图正则化项，在生成过程中约束体素强度分布，使其与目标亚型的真实强度分布对齐，而非仅依赖空间重建损失。

Result: 在肺部CT数据上的实验表明，该方法在定量指标和视觉图灵测试中均达到强视觉真实感。用于数据增强时，生成的结节能提升下游临床任务性能，尤其对欠表示的结节亚型效果显著，并显示出对亚型感知的恶性分类的潜在益处。

Conclusion: 本文证明了直方图正则化的潜扩散模型能有效合成具有亚型特异性强度分布的肺结节，为医学影像数据增强和临床诊断提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于医学影像（肺部CT）中的肺结节可控生成，属于医疗垂直领域的生成应用，因此映射到现有 subtopic "领域内容生成应用"（aigc_domain_application），该 subtopic 在 multimodal_generation 和 aigc 方向下均有收录。

Abstract: While automated diagnosis systems have achieved remarkable success in computed tomography (CT)-based lung cancer screening, their development remains limited by the scarcity of diverse, annotated pulmonary nodule datasets. Diffusion-based generative models offer a promising strategy for data synthesis; however, many existing conditional approaches primarily optimize spatial reconstruction losses, which encourage voxel-wise similarity but may inadequately constrain lesion-level intensity distributions. As a result, these methods may produce over-smoothed texture profiles and underrepresent the distinct attenuation characteristics of different nodule subtypes, including solid, part-solid, and ground-glass nodules. To address this challenge, we propose a controllable latent diffusion model that synthesizes pulmonary nodules within full 3D CT volumes while accurately modeling nodule-specific intensity distributions. Specifically, rather than relying solely on spatial losses, we introduce a histogram-based regularization term that constrains voxel intensity distributions during the generative process. The model combines subtype, spatial mask, and Hounsfield unit (HU) histogram conditioning with the differentiable feature-space histogram regularization term to better align lesion-level intensity distributions, improving the visual plausibility and subtype consistency of synthesized nodules. Extensive experiments on lung CT data demonstrate that our framework achieves strong visual realism, validated through both quantitative metrics and a visual Turing test. Furthermore, when used for data augmentation, the generated nodules improve performance in downstream clinical tasks, particularly for underrepresented nodule subtypes, and show a potential benefit for subtype-informed malignancy classification.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 生成的 fMRI 数据在保留生理脑动力学方面优于现有方法，并在下游基于脑网络的分类任务中取得了改进的性能。

Conclusion: DSFM 通过双变换结构施加结构化频率先验，能够生成生理上可信的 fMRI 时间序列，为脑疾病识别等下游任务提供了有效的数据增强手段。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 fMRI 时间序列生成方法（DSFM），属于医疗/科学领域的生成应用，与 multimodal_generation 和 aigc 方向下的现有 subtopic "领域内容生成应用"（aigc_domain_application）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical fMRI generation, far outside user's priority subtopics and negative-keyword-filtered domains. Signals: fMRI generation, brain disorder, wavelet transform, spectral flow matching, medical application

Abstract: Functional Magnetic Resonance Imaging (fMRI) provides non-invasive access to dynamic brain activity by measuring blood oxygen level-dependent (BOLD) signals over time. However, the resource-intensive nature of fMRI acquisition limits the availability of high-fidelity samples required for data-driven brain analysis models. While modern generative models can synthesize fMRI data, they often remain challenging in replicating their inherent non-stationarity, intricate spatiotemporal dynamics, and physiological variations of raw BOLD signals. To address these challenges, we propose Dual-Spectral Flow Matching (DSFM), a novel fMRI generative framework that cascades dual frequency representation of BOLD signals with spectral flow matching. Specifically, our framework first converts BOLD signals into a wavelet decomposition map via a discrete wavelet transform (DWT) to capture globalized transient and multi-scale variations, and projects into the discrete cosine transform (DCT) space across brain regions and time to exploit localized energy compaction of low-frequency dominant BOLD coefficients. Subsequently, a spectral flow matching model is trained to generate class-conditioned cosine-frequency representation. The generated samples are reconstructed through inverse DCT and inverse DWT operations to recover physiologically plausible time-domain BOLD signals. This dual-transform approach imposes structured frequency priors and preserves key physiological brain dynamics. Ultimately, we demonstrate the efficacy of our approach through improved downstream fMRI-based brain network classification. The code is available at .

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出TCFDNet，利用文本引导的特征解耦网络解决LiDAR-相机跨模态步态识别（LCCGR）问题。通过大语言模型构建步态模态文本字典（GMTD），使用CLIP多粒度特征编码器对齐视觉-文本特征，并通过文本引导特征解耦（TFD）模块和特征稳定性增强（FSE）模块提取模态共享特征，在SUSTech1K和FreeGait数据集上取得SOTA结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实场景中步态识别常涉及异构传感模态（如LiDAR和RGB相机），2D视频与3D点云序列之间存在巨大的模态鸿沟，使得跨模态步态识别极具挑战性。现有方法缺乏有效的跨模态对齐机制，难以提取鲁棒的模态共享特征。

Method: 1) 构建Gait Modality Text Dictionary (GMTD)：利用大语言模型生成跨模态、跨视角的步态语义描述。2) CLIP-based Multi-grained Feature Encoder：在统一视觉-语言空间中对齐视觉与文本特征。3) Text-guided Feature Disentanglement (TFD)模块：选择topk匹配的文本描述重构模态特定特征，通过残差分解和正交约束提取模态共享特征。4) Feature Stability Enhancement (FSE)模块：建模空间和通道相关性增强特征鲁棒性。5) 跨模态patch交换策略进一步提升泛化能力。

Result: 在SUSTech1K和FreeGait数据集上，TCFDNet在LiDAR-相机跨模态步态识别任务上取得了新的SOTA结果，各项消融实验验证了各模块的有效性。

Importance reason: Focuses on gait recognition, a niche biometric application, not aligned with user's priority subtopics like video/image generation or multimodal reasoning. Signals: Cross-modal gait recognition, LiDAR-camera, text-guided feature disentanglement, CLIP-based alignment, not autoregressive/diffusion generation

Abstract: Gait recognition is a biometric technique that identifies individuals based on their walking patterns, offering advantages in long-range, non-intrusive scenarios. However, real-world scenarios often involve heterogeneous sensing modalities such as LiDAR and RGB cameras, making LiDAR-Camera Cross-modal Gait recognition (LCCGR) a critical yet challenging task due to the substantial modality gap between 2D videos and 3D point cloud sequences. To address this challenge, we propose TCFDNet, a Text-guided Cross-modal Feature Disentanglement Network, which leverages modality-aware textual priors as semantic anchors to guide the learning of disentangled modality-shared representations. Specifically, we construct a Gait Modality Text Dictionary (GMTD) using large language models to generate rich semantic descriptions of gait across modalities and viewpoints. A CLIP-based Multi-grained Feature Encoder then aligns visual and textual features within a unified vision-language space. Furthermore, the Text-guided Feature Disentanglement (TFD) module selects the topk matched textual descriptions to reconstruct modality-specific representations and derive modality-shared features via residual decomposition and orthogonality constraints. To mitigate the fragility of the disentangled shared features, we propose a Feature Stability Enhancement (FSE) module, which models spatial and channel-wise correlations to improve feature robustness. In addition, a cross-modal patch exchange strategy is introduced to further improve generalization. Extensive experiments on SUSTech1K and FreeGait datasets demonstrate that TCFDNet achieves new state-of-the-art results and validate the effectiveness of the proposed modules.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了掩码扩散语言模型（MDLM）在图到文本生成任务中的解码轨迹，发现MDLM自然优先生成实体词，再生成关系词和功能词，最后生成结构词。SFT会破坏这一策略，导致结构词过早锚定从而引发信息遗漏或幻觉。作者提出λ缩放结构解码（训练无关的推理时修改）恢复性能，并引入Graph-LLaDA将图Transformer编码器集成到LLaDA解码过程中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图到文本生成任务中，现有方法主要依赖自回归LLM，但掩码扩散语言模型（MDLM）在该任务上的行为尚未被研究。作者希望理解MDLM的解码轨迹特性，并解决SFT带来的结构词过早锚定问题。

Method: 1) 系统分析MDLM在迭代解码过程中token被掩码恢复的顺序（解码轨迹）；2) 发现SFT导致结构句尾token过早被锚定，提出λ缩放结构解码（lambda-scaled structural decoding），在推理时降低结构token的置信度权重；3) 提出Graph-LLaDA，将图Transformer编码器集成到LLaDA的掩码解码过程中，显式编码关系图结构。

Result: λ缩放结构解码在BLEU-4上提升+9.4；跨数据集评估（LAGRANGE）显示，先前基线过拟合数据集特定模式，而LLM和MDLM方法泛化能力显著更强。

Conclusion: MDLM在图到文本生成中展现出与自回归模型不同的解码策略（实体优先），SFT会破坏这一自然策略，而提出的训练无关推理时修改可以有效恢复性能。将图结构显式编码到MDLM中进一步提升了生成质量。

Classification reason: 该论文研究图到文本生成（graph-to-text generation），属于结构化数据到文本的生成任务，可归入领域内容生成应用（aigc_domain_application）这一现有子主题。该子主题在 multimodal_generation 和 aigc 方向下均有收录，涵盖垂直领域生成应用。

Importance reason: Focuses on graph-to-text generation, not aligned with user's priority subtopics like continuous VLM, video generation, or unified multimodal models. Signals: graph-to-text generation, masked diffusion language model, trajectory analysis, SFT failure mode, lambda-scaled structural decoding

Abstract: We present the first systematic study of masked diffusion language models (MDLMs) for graph-to-text generation. We analyze MDLM generation trajectories -- the order in which tokens are unmasked during iterative decoding -- and find that, unlike autoregressive LLMs which generate text linearly, MDLMs naturally prioritize entities first, followed by relational and function words, with structural tokens resolved last. We further identify a previously undocumented failure mode of supervised fine-tuning: SFT disrupts this strategy by prematurely anchoring structural sentence-ending tokens early in the decoding trajectory, effectively fixing the output length which can lead to omitted or hallucinated information. To address this, we propose lambda-scaled structural decoding, a training-free inference-time modification that downweights structural token confidence and recovers +9.4 BLEU-4. Finally, we introduce Graph-LLaDA, which integrates a Graph Transformer encoder into LLaDA's decoding process to explicitly incorporate relational graph structure. Cross-dataset evaluation on LAGRANGE reveals that previous baselines overfit to dataset-specific patterns, while LLM- and MDLM-based approaches generalize significantly better.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出 Rationalize，一个基于角色对（role-pair）的共享语义推理框架，用于人类与 AI 模型在数据驱动意义构建中的对齐。该框架将人机交互建模为互补角色对（探索者-引导者、调查者-信息提供者、教师-学生、法官-倡导者），在共享推理空间中使目的、问题、假设、证据、推断和含义显式化，实现意图和行动的理性对齐，而非仅输出层面的对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人机对齐方法主要关注输出层面的对齐（如 RLHF），缺乏对意图和推理过程的对齐。人类与 AI 在数据驱动的意义构建中需要更深层次的语义对齐，使双方能够理解彼此的推理过程和意图。

Method: 提出 Rationalize 角色对框架，定义四种互补角色对（Explorer-Guide, Investigator-Informant, Teacher-Student, Judge-Advocate），在共享推理空间中显式化目的、问题、假设、证据、推断和含义六个推理要素，实现双向人机对齐（AI 对齐人类 vs 人类对齐 AI）。

Result: 论文是概念性框架设计，提出了角色对框架与双向对齐的关系映射，并勾勒了使用要素级和角色特定方法进行对齐设计与评估的合作研究议程。

Conclusion: Rationalize 框架为人类与 AI 之间的共享语义推理提供了结构化方法，通过角色对和显式推理要素促进更深层次的人机对齐，为未来人机协作对齐研究提供了新的方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Rationalize 框架，将人机交互建模为互补角色对（Explorer-Guide, Investigator-Informant, Teacher-Student, Judge-Advocate），在共享推理空间中实现人类与 AI（如 LLM）的对齐。这属于 AI 智能体与生成工作流（aigc_agent_workflow）范畴，涉及 LLM 智能体、人机协作工作流与对齐设计。同时与多模态理解与生成方向下的对齐主题相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on human-AI alignment in sensemaking, not on the user's priority subtopics like video generation or continuous VLM. Signals: Human-AI alignment, shared semantic reasoning, role-pair framework, not generative model, no video/image generation

Abstract: We introduce Rationalize, a role-pair framework for shared semantic reasoning between humans and AI models in data-driven sensemaking. Building on ideas in human-machine teaming and critical thinking, we conceptualize human-AI interaction as a series of complementary role pairs (Explorer-Guide, Investigator-Informant, Teacher-Student, Judge-Advocate) operating in a shared reasoning space. In this space, human analysts and AI models (such as LLMs) make purposes, questions, assumptions, evidence, inferences, and implications explicit, facilitating alignment not only at the output level but at the level of rationalization of intent and action by each side. We relate these role pairs to the bidirectional human-AI alignment framework, illustrating how "aligning AI to humans" and "aligning humans to AI" differ by role, and sketch a collaborative research agenda for alignment design and assessment using element-level and role-specific approaches.

</details>

Conclusion: 本文通过将生成式AI整合到UXR工作流中，扩展了传统的UXR PoV框架，为以人为中心的AI金融系统提供了方法论支持。GenAI作为认知支持工具而非替代人类判断，在保持可追溯性和伦理监督的前提下，提升了UXR在复杂社会技术环境中的有效性。

Importance reason: Focuses on UX research methodology for financial AI, not on core generative model architectures or multimodal generation. Signals: Human-centred AI, UX methodology, financial services, prompt architecture, not core generation model

Abstract: Rising household debt and cost-of-living pressures in the United Kingdom have intensified the role of AI-driven financial technologies in mediating credit assessment, repayment structuring, and debt support services. These systems increasingly shape consequential financial decisions, yet they operate within complex socio-technical environments characterised by regulatory constraint, algorithmic opacity, and heightened vulnerability risk. User Experience Research (UXR) Points of View (PoVs) are critical in translating heterogeneous research evidence into strategic direction for product and governance decisions. However, the existing UXR PoV framework was not designed for AI-mediated financial systems where interpretability, fairness, and accountability are central. This paper extends the UXR PoV pyramid into an AI-augmented methodological framework for Human-Centred AI debt management technologies in the UK financial services context. We formalise (1) an AI-Augmented PoV Pyramid, (2) a structured prompt architecture for synthesis and hypothesis generation, and (3) an AI-enabled Playbook Card system that embeds Generative AI into UXR workflows while preserving traceability and ethical oversight. Generative AI is positioned not as an analytic authority, but as an epistemic support mechanism subject to human validation and regulatory awareness. By grounding the framework in debt management technologies, including affordability assessment, repayment planning, and financial stress prediction systems, this work advances UXR methodology for high-stakes financial AI environments and contributes to the evolution of responsible, AI-powered UXR practice within the CHI community.

</details>

<details>

Motivation: 科学图表是论文准备中最耗时的环节之一，现有自动化系统仅支持单一图表类型和纯文本输入，且输出为不可局部修改的光栅格式，无法满足研究人员的实际多样化需求。

Method: 提出 Crafter 多智能体框架，通过多个 LLM 智能体协作完成科学图表生成，无需修改架构即可泛化到多种图表类型和输入条件；CraftEditor 将光栅输出转换为可编辑 SVG 格式；CraftBench 覆盖三种图表类型和四种输入条件，并有人工质量标注。

Result: Crafter 在 PaperBanana-Bench 和 CraftBench 上显著优于独立生成模型和智能体基线；CraftEditor 能忠实转换输出为可编辑 SVG，超越所有基线方法。

Conclusion: 多智能体框架能有效解决科学图表生成中多样化的类型和输入条件问题，且可编辑 SVG 输出为局部修正提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出多智能体框架 Crafter 用于科学图表生成，属于 AI 智能体驱动的自动化创作流水线，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "AI 智能体与生成工作流" 高度匹配。

Abstract: Scientific figures are among the most effective means of communicating complex research ideas, yet producing publication-quality illustrations remains one of the most labor-intensive parts of paper preparation. Existing automated systems each target a single figure type under text-only input, leaving the diversity of types and conditions researchers actually use unaddressed; their raster outputs further cannot be locally revised. Because scientific figures are structured compositions of discrete semantic components, the localized errors generators produce on such layouts demand not a stronger backbone but a harness. We instantiate this harness in two complementary systems: Crafter, a multi-agent harness for figure generation that generalizes across figure types and input conditions without architectural changes, and CraftEditor, which applies the same pattern to convert raster outputs into editable SVGs. Moreover, we introduce CraftBench, a benchmark spanning three figure types and four input conditions with human quality annotation. Experiments show that Crafter substantially outperforms both standalone generators and the agentic baseline on PaperBanana-Bench and CraftBench, with ablations confirming each component's independent contribution; CraftEditor faithfully converts outputs into editable SVGs that surpass all baselines. Our code and benchmark are available at .

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 在通用任务上表现优异，但难以适应专业领域，主要瓶颈在于缺乏高质量领域特定数据。当前基于 LLM 的数据策展方法依赖人工设计的工作流，尚未探索 LLM 能否自主执行端到端数据工程流水线来实现模型专业化。

Result: GPT-5.2 作为自主数据工程师，通过迭代的智能体驱动数据适配，构建的训练课程使学生模型性能提升 57.29%，验证了自主数据工程的有效性。

Conclusion: 自主数据工程是一种可衡量的能力，LLM 能够作为自主数据工程师驱动模型专业化，同时揭示了当前方法的潜力与瓶颈，为智能体驱动的模型专业化指明了方向。

Importance reason: Focuses on agent-driven data engineering for model specialization, which is tangential to the user's core interests in multimodal generation and continuous VLM. Signals: agentic data engineering, model specialization, LLM as data engineer, iterative data optimization, GPT-5.2

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong performance on general tasks, while often struggling to adapt to specialized domains without high-quality domain-specific data. Existing LLM-based data curation methods primarily rely on human-designed workflows, leaving it unexamined whether LLMs can autonomously execute an end-to-end data engineering pipeline for model specialization. We formalize \textbf{Autonomous Agentic Data Engineering}, a novel task designed to evaluate LLMs as autonomous data engineers that drive model specialization through end-to-end data curation. We frame data as an optimizable component and study agents that plan, generate, and iteratively optimize training data across multiple domains, guided by post-training performance improvement. Experiments show that autonomous LLM data engineers yield substantial gains, as GPT-5.2 constructs a training curriculum that improves a student model by \textbf{57.29\%}, entirely through iterative, agent-driven data adaptation. By illuminating both potential and bottlenecks, our study establishes autonomous data engineering as a measurable capability and charts a path toward agent-driven model specialization\footnote{Code will be released at .}.

</details>


### [69] [Harness Updating Is Not Harness Benefit: Disentangling Evolution Capabilities in Self-Evolving LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30621)
*Minhua Lin, Juncheng Wu, Zijun Wang, Zhan Shi, Yisi Sang, Bing He, Zewen Liu, Tianxin Wei, Zongyu Wu, Zhiwei Zhang, Dakuo Wang, Xiang Zhang, Benoit Dumoulin, Cihang Xie, Yuyin Zhou, Suhang Wang, Hanqing Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AI 智能体与生成工作流

Importance: 值得关注 (73.89) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了 LLM 智能体在自演化过程中两种关键能力——harness-updating（产生有用 harness 更新的能力）和 harness-benefit（从更新中获益的能力）——与基础能力之间的关系。发现 harness-updating 能力在不同能力层级的模型间差异不大，而 harness-benefit 能力呈非单调关系：弱模型获益少，中等模型获益最大，强模型获益反而下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体越来越多地使用可编辑的外部 harness（prompt、技能、记忆、工具）来执行任务，且可通过自演化机制从执行经验中更新这些 harness。然而，模型的基础任务解决能力与其自演化能力之间的关系尚不明确：哪些模型能产生有用的 harness 更新？哪些模型能真正从更新中获益？现有研究缺乏对此的系统分析。

Method: 作者将智能体自演化能力分解为两种独立能力：harness-updating（从执行证据中产生有用持久 harness 更新的能力）和 harness-benefit（在任务求解中从更新后的 harness 中获益的能力）。通过设计对照实验，在不同能力层级的模型（从弱到强）上分别评估这两种能力，并分析其与基础能力的关系。

Result: （1）harness-updating 能力在基础能力上呈现"平坦"特征：不同能力层级的模型产生的 harness 更新带来的增益惊人地相似，甚至 Qwen3.5-9B 的更新效果与 Claude Opus 4.6 相当。（2）harness-benefit 能力在基础能力上呈非单调关系：弱模型获益很少，中等模型获益最大，强模型获益反而低于中等模型。（3）弱模型获益低的原因可归结为两种失败模式：无法激活相关的 harness 工件，或激活后无法忠实地遵循 harness 指令。

Conclusion: 研究表明，在构建自演化智能体系统时，应将能力预算投入到任务求解智能体本身而非演化器（evolver）中；同时应针对 harness 调用和长程指令遵循进行智能体训练，以提升自演化效果。

Classification reason: 论文研究 LLM 智能体的自演化能力（harness self-evolution），涉及智能体通过更新 prompt、skills、memories、tools 等外部 harness 来提升任务执行能力，属于 AI 智能体与生成工作流范畴，因此映射到 canonical 子主题 aigc_agent_workflow。

Importance reason: This paper focuses on LLM agent self-evolution, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks the boost keywords you specified. Signals: LLM agent self-evolution, harness-updating vs harness-benefit, non-monotonic capability relationship, no direct connection to multimodal generation or video/image generation

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly deployed as systems built around editable external harnesses, including prompts, skills, memories and tools, that shape task execution without changing model parameters. Harness self-evolution adapts such agents by updating these harnesses from execution evidence. Yet it remains unclear whether a model's base capability in task-solving predicts its capabilities in harness self-evolution: which models produce useful harness updates, and which actually benefit from them? We analyze two harness self-evolution capabilities: (i) harness-updating, the capability to produce useful persistent harness updates from execution evidence; (ii) harness-benefit, the capability to benefit from updated harnesses during task solving. Our analysis reveals two findings. First, harness-updating is flat in base capability: models from different capability tiers produce harness updates that lead to surprisingly similar gains; even Qwen3.5-9B's updates yield gains comparable to those of Claude Opus~4.6. Second, harness-benefit is non-monotonic in base capability: weak-tier models benefit little from updated harnesses, mid-tier models benefit most, and strong-tier models benefit less than mid-tier. We trace low gains at the weak tier to two failure modes: weak-tier models may fail to activate relevant harness artifacts, or activate them but fail to follow them faithfully. These findings suggest investing capability budget in the task-solving agent rather than the evolver, and targeting harness invocation and long-horizon instruction following in agent training. Our source code is publicly available at .

</details>
