<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度研究训练范式要么依赖短格式可验证问答作为代理，要么优化整体长轨迹，导致规划与执行难以解耦，且对规划过程的信用分配薄弱。需要一种显式结构化规划的方法来提升深度研究能力。

Method: 将研究计划表示为类型化有向无环图（DAG），使规划显式化、结构化、可奖励。两阶段训练：规划器 RL 先学习图结构和查询分解以改进研究规划，回答器 RL 再学习分支级执行和最终综合，将奖励分配给显式的规划 token 和结构化组件而非扁平轨迹。

Result: DecomposeR-8B 在多个长文本基准上比强开源基线提升 5.1-8.0 分，验证了结构化规划奖励对深度研究任务的有效性。

Conclusion: 将规划显式结构化为 DAG 并分配细粒度奖励，能够有效提升 LLM 在深度研究任务中的规划与回答能力，为复杂多步推理任务提供了一种新的训练范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于规划器的深度研究框架 DecomposeR，将研究计划表示为有向无环图（DAG），并通过强化学习优化规划与执行。这属于 LLM 智能体与自动化工作流范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "AI 智能体与生成工作流" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on RL for research planning, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous VLM or video generation. Signals: RL for planning, DAG-based research, long-form QA, not multimodal generation, not autoregressive video/image

Abstract: Deep research tasks require LLMs to plan what to investigate, retrieve evidence, and synthesize long-form answers across multiple branches of inquiry. Existing training paradigms either rely on short-form verifiable QA as a proxy or optimize monolithic long trajectories, which makes planning and execution difficult to disentangle and yields weak credit assignment for the planning process. We propose DecomposeR, a planner-centric deep research framework that represents research plans as typed directed acyclic graphs (DAGs), allowing planning to be made explicit, structured, and rewardable. We train a Qwen3-8B model in two stages: planner reinforcement learning (RL) first learns graph structure and query decomposition to improve research planning, and answerer reinforcement learning (RL) then learns branch-level execution and final synthesis conditioned on the learned plan. By assigning rewards to explicit planner tokens and structured components rather than to a flat trajectory, DecomposeR enables finer-grained optimization of planning while reducing the ambiguity of end-to-end training. Experiments show that DecomposeR-8B improves over strong comparable open baselines by 5.1-8.0 points on popular long-form benchmarks due to improved planning and answering capabilities.

</details>


### [71] [UniScale: Adaptive Unified Inference Scaling via Online Joint Optimization of Model Routing and Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30898)
*Kaiyu Huang, Xingyu Wang, Mingze Kong, Zhubo Shi, Yuqian Hou, Hong Xu, Zhongxiang Dai, Minchen Yu, Qingjiang Shi*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CL

Direction: AIGC

TL;DR: UniScale 提出统一推理扩展（UIS）框架，将模型路由与测试时扩展（TTS）联合建模为上下文多臂老虎机问题，通过 LinUCB 在线学习最优推理策略，在动态推理场景中实现更细粒度的质量-成本权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将模型路由（在不同规模模型间切换）和测试时扩展（TTS，在固定模型内调整推理计算量）作为两个独立维度处理，导致模型路由只能提供粗粒度的离散性能变化，而单模型TTS存在容量上限和收益递减问题，两者分离限制了动态推理环境中的适应性。

Method: 提出统一推理扩展（UIS）框架，将模型路由和TTS统一到单一优化空间中。在此基础上设计UniScale在线框架，将自适应UIS建模为上下文多臂老虎机问题，使用LinUCB算法学习推理策略，并引入效率感知学习和成本建模以在高维动作空间上实现稳定可扩展的优化。

Result: 实验表明，UniScale能够有效利用UIS空间中的协同效应，在多样化的动态推理场景中实现更细粒度且持续更优的质量-成本权衡。

Conclusion: 将模型路由与测试时扩展统一优化是提升LLM推理效率的有效范式，UniScale通过在线学习框架实现了两者协同，为动态推理环境中的质量-成本平衡提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出UniScale框架，将模型路由（在不同规模模型间切换）与测试时扩展（TTS）统一为在线联合优化问题，属于LLM推理工作流的智能调度与自动化优化，与AIGC方向下的"AI智能体与生成工作流"（aigc_agent_workflow）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on inference efficiency and model routing, which aligns with model efficiency priority but lacks direct connection to top-priority continuous VLM or video generation topics. Signals: Unified inference scaling, model routing + test-time scaling, online learning via LinUCB, quality-cost trade-off, dynamic inference optimization

Abstract: In real-world deployments of large language models (LLMs), balancing inference quality and computational cost has become a central challenge. Existing approaches tackle this trade-off along two largely independent dimensions: model routing, which switches among models of different scales to match request complexity, and test-time scaling (TTS), which adjusts inference-time compute within a fixed model for fine-grained control. However, this decoupled design introduces inherent limitations. Model routing yields coarse-grained, discrete performance changes due to the sparse set of model scales, while single-model TTS often encounters capacity ceilings and exhibits diminishing returns as compute increases. Moreover, treating the two mechanisms separately restricts adaptability in dynamic inference environments. To overcome these limitations, we introduce Unified Inference Scaling (UIS), which unifies model routing and TTS in a single optimization space. Building on this formulation, we propose UniScale, an online framework that models adaptive UIS as a contextual multi-armed bandit problem and learns inference policies via LinUCB. The framework incorporates efficiency-aware learning and cost modeling to ensure stable and scalable optimization over high-dimensional action spaces. Evaluation shows that UniScale effectively exploits the synergy in the UIS space to deliver a fine-grained and consistently better quality-cost trade-off across diverse, dynamic inference scenarios.

</details>


### [72] [Counterfactual Graph for Multi-Agent LLM Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30653)
*Jiatan Huang, Mingchen Li, Ziming Li, Sunjae Kwon, Hong Yu, Chuxu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AI 智能体与生成工作流

TL;DR: 本文提出CAGE-CAL框架，通过反事实图对比来校准多智能体LLM系统的置信度，解决通信导致的虚假共识问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统常将智能体间的一致同意视为可靠信号，但通信后智能体之间会产生相关性故障和虚假共识，导致相同投票比例在不同拓扑结构下可能反映不同的真实可靠性。现有方法无法区分这种差异。

Method: 提出CAGE-CAL框架，对每个查询构建一个观测到的通信后智能体图，并与一个匹配的反事实无通信图进行对比，捕获成对故障相关性和组级依赖关系。通过估计观测图与无通信图之间的反事实偏移来校准置信度。

Result: 在五个基准测试上，CAGE-CAL提升了可靠性判别能力，取得了有竞争力的ECE（期望校准误差），其校准后的置信度进一步改进了拓扑选择，优于最佳固定拓扑策略。

Conclusion: 多智能体LLM系统中的通信会导致相关性故障和虚假共识，CAGE-CAL通过反事实图对比有效校准了置信度，提升了系统可靠性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多智能体LLM系统的校准问题，属于LLM智能体工作流中的可靠性提升方向，与[AIGC]方向下的[AI智能体与生成工作流]（aigc_agent_workflow）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on multi-agent LLM calibration, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to user's core interests in multimodal generation and autoregressive models. Signals: Multi-agent calibration, Counterfactual graph, LLM confidence, False consensus, Topology selection

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems often treat agreement as evidence: when many agents in a panel give the same answer, that answer is assumed to be more reliable. We show that this assumption can fail after agents communicate. Communication can induce correlated failures and false consensus, so the same vote share may reflect reliable agreement in one topology but over-confidence in another. We propose CAGE-CAL, a counterfactual agent-graph calibration framework for multi-agent LLMs. For each query, CAGE-CAL compares an observed post-communication agent graph with a matched counterfactual no-communication graph, capturing both pairwise failure correlations and group-level dependencies. Rather than simply counting how many agents agree, CAGE-CAL estimates the counterfactual shift between observed and no-communication dependence, and calibrates confidence accordingly. Across five benchmarks, CAGE-CAL improves reliability discrimination with competitive ECE, and its calibrated confidence further improves topology selection over the best fixed-topology strategy.

</details>


### [73] [ElasticMem: Latent Memory as a Learnable Resource for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30690)
*Tao Feng, Chongrui Ye, Tianyang Luo, Jingjun Xu, Xueqiang Xu, Haozhen Zhang, Ge Liu, Jiaxuan You*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AI 智能体与生成工作流

Importance: 普通相关 (43.34) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ElasticMem 提出了一种可学习的弹性潜在记忆框架，通过离线记忆库、自适应检索、可变潜在预算分配和策略优化，使 LLM 智能体能够更高效地利用长期记忆，在记忆密集型 QA 和具身智能体控制任务上显著提升性能并降低 token 开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆增强方法将记忆视为固定资源：文本空间方法将检索到的记忆拼接到上下文中，导致大量 token 开销且对噪声敏感；潜在空间方法虽减少文本成本，但仍依赖刚性检索或固定容量记忆接口。这造成了查询相关的记忆效用与固定记忆分配之间的不匹配。

Method: ElasticMem 构建离线潜在记忆库（含检索键和内容缓存），从推理器的隐藏状态自适应检索记忆，通过学习到的策略为每条检索到的记忆分配可变的潜在预算，并将选中的潜在状态作为软记忆 token 注入生成过程。整个记忆使用过程通过 GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimization）以下游任务奖励进行优化。

Result: 在 MemorySuite 上，基于 Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct 和 Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct 骨干，ElasticMem 在加权平均 QA 准确率上分别提升 26.2% 和 24.6%，在 ALFWorld 成功率上分别提升 66.3% 和 27.2%，同时实现了最低的 ALFWorld token 开销。

Conclusion: 自适应检索和弹性预算分配帮助 ElasticMem 优先利用有用证据和可迁移计划，超越了刚性余弦相似度方法。该工作展示了将记忆作为可学习弹性资源的有效性，为 LLM 智能体的长期记忆管理提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 ElasticMem，一种为 LLM 智能体设计的可学习弹性记忆框架，核心是增强智能体的长期记忆与推理能力，属于 LLM 智能体与生成工作流范畴，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic aigc_agent_workflow。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM agent memory, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous VLM, video generation, or unified multimodal models. Signals: LLM agent memory, latent memory bank, adaptive retrieval, token efficiency, not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: Long-term memory is essential for LLM agents to reason coherently across extended interactions, personalize responses, and reuse past experience. However, existing memory-augmented methods typically treat memory as a fixed resource: text-space approaches concatenate retrieved memories into the context window, causing substantial token overhead and sensitivity to noisy evidence, while latent-space approaches reduce textual cost but still rely on rigid retrieval or fixed-capacity memory interfaces. This creates a mismatch between query-dependent memory utility and fixed memory allocation. We propose ElasticMem, a memory-augmented LLM framework that learns to use memory as an elastic latent resource. ElasticMem builds an offline latent memory bank with retrieval keys and content caches, retrieves memories adaptively from the reasoner's hidden state, assigns each retrieved memory a variable latent budget through a learned policy, and injects selected latent states as soft memory tokens for generation. The full memory-use process is optimized with downstream task rewards through group-relative policy optimization. We evaluate ElasticMem on MemorySuite, covering memory-intensive QA and embodied agent control. Across Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct and Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct backbones, ElasticMem improves weighted average QA accuracy by 26.2% and 24.6%, and improves ALFWorld success rate by 66.3% and 27.2%, respectively, over the strongest baselines, while achieving the lowest ALFWorld token cost. Ablations and qualitative analyses further show that adaptive retrieval and elastic budget allocation help ElasticMem prioritize useful evidence and transferable plans beyond rigid cosine similarity. Our code for ElasticMem will be released at .

</details>

*Tianyi Zhou, Dongrui Liu, Leitao Yuan, Jing Shao, Xia Hu*

TL;DR: 本文提出了 COLLEAGUE.SKILL，一个自动化 trace-to-skill 蒸馏系统，能够将目标人物或角色的异构痕迹（如实践、思维模型、沟通风格等）蒸馏为可检查、可修正、可版本化、可跨平台部署的 AI 技能包，包含能力轨道和边界行为轨道，支持自然语言反馈更新和回滚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 智能体的记忆和角色系统只能捕获碎片化的证据，而技能框架虽然提供了可移植的封装格式，但缺乏端到端的工作流来将异构痕迹蒸馏为可检查、可修正、可被智能体使用的技能包。

Method: 设计了一个自动化 trace-to-skill 蒸馏系统，输入目标人物或角色的材料，输出带版本号的技能包，包含两个协调轨道：能力轨道（实践、思维模型、决策启发式）和边界行为轨道（沟通风格、交互规则、修正历史）。技能包支持检查、调用、自然语言反馈更新、回滚、跨平台安装和受控分发。

Result: 开源仓库获得约 18.5k GitHub stars；技能库列出 215 个技能（来自 165 位贡献者），累计超过 100k stars。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了如何将人物驱动的技能表示为可移植、可修正的包，而非不透明的提示词或隐藏的记忆，为构建人格化 AI 智能体提供了系统化的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一种将专家知识蒸馏为可检查、可修正、可部署的 AI 技能包的自动化工作流，属于 LLM 智能体与生成工作流范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "AI 智能体与生成工作流" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on AI agent skill packaging, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous VLM, video generation, or multimodal models. Signals: AI agent skill generation, expert knowledge distillation, trace-to-skill workflow, person-grounded agents, no direct link to user's core interests

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly expected not only to complete isolated tasks, but also to carry bounded representations of human expertise, judgment, and interaction style. Building such person-grounded agents remains difficult because actionable knowledge associated with a person or role is usually embedded in heterogeneous traces rather than written as clean instructions. Existing memory and persona systems capture fragments of this evidence, while skill frameworks provide portable packaging formats; however, there is no end-to-end workflow for distilling these traces into inspectable, correctable, and agent-usable skills. We present an automated trace-to-skill distillation system for generating person-grounded AI skills via expert knowledge distillation. Given materials from a target person or role, produces a versioned skill package with two coordinated tracks: a capability track for practices, mental models, and decision heuristics, and a bounded behavior track for communication style, interaction rules, and correction history. The package can be inspected, invoked, updated through natural-language feedback, rolled back, installed across agent hosts, and optionally prepared for controlled distribution. We describe the artifact contract, generation workflow, correction lifecycle, deployment surface, and domain presets implemented in the open-source system. At the time of writing, the public repository has approximately 18.5k GitHub stars; the gallery lists 215 skills from 165 contributors and more than 100k cumulative stars across listed skill cards. The system illustrates how person-grounded skills can be represented as portable, correctable packages rather than opaque prompts or hidden memories.

</details>


### [75] [Learning to Adapt: Self-Improving Web Agent via Cognitive-Aware Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31365)
*Weile Chen, Bingchen Miao, Qifan Yu, Wendong Bu, Guoming Wang, Wenqiao Zhang, Shengyu Zhang, Juncheng Li, Siliang Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AI 智能体与生成工作流

TL;DR: 提出SCALE框架，通过Selector、Predictor、Judger三个对抗角色实现Web智能体的自认知探索与学习，结合SCALE-Hop图探索策略避免局部陷阱，并构建SCALE-20k数据集，显著提升MLLM在Web环境中的表现和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Web智能体依赖手工设计的执行流程或昂贵的专家轨迹，难以适应复杂动态环境，缺乏自主发现自身局限并扩展认知边界的能力。

Method: 提出SCALE框架，包含三个对抗角色：Selector选择探索方向、Predictor预测结果、Judger评估差距，通过三者协作自主发现智能体局限并扩展认知边界；提出SCALE-Hop图探索策略实现全局规划避免局部陷阱；构建SCALE-20k数据集（来自19个真实网站的多样化任务及结构化演示）。

Result: 在多个Web环境中显著提升了多种MLLM的性能和泛化能力，验证了框架的可扩展性和通用性。

Conclusion: SCALE提供了一种可扩展、可泛化的解决方案，用于构建真正自主和自适应的Web智能体，无需人工标注或专家轨迹。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于MLLM的Web智能体SCALE，通过自认知探索与环境交互实现自主学习和适应，属于LLM智能体与自动化工作流范畴，与[AIGC]方向下的[AI智能体与生成工作流]规范主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on web agent self-improvement, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous VLM or video generation. Signals: Web agent, MLLM adaptation, cognitive-aware exploration, graph exploration, dataset construction

Abstract: Recent advances in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have led to promising progress in web agents. However, existing web agents often rely on handcrafted execution pipelines or expensive expert trajectories, limiting their adaptability to complex, dynamic environments. To address these challenges, we propose SCALE (Self-Cognitive-Aware Learning and Exploration), which leverages three adversarial roles, Selector, Predictor, and Judger to autonomously discover the agent's limitations and expand its cognitive boundaries through environmental exploration. Moreover, we propose SCALE-Hop, a graph exploration strategy that facilitates global planning and helps agents avoid local exploration traps. To further support learning, we construct SCALE-20k, a large-scale dataset collected from 19 real-world websites, containing diverse task types and structured demonstrations generated from SCALE's exploration traces. Experimental results show that our approach significantly improves the performance and generalization of multiple MLLMs in various web environments. Our framework offers a scalable and generalizable solution for building truly autonomous and adaptive web agents.

</details>

*Seungho Choi, Jihyong Oh*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AI 智能体与生成工作流

TL;DR: DiTTo 提出了一种可扩展的、顺序感知的全能图像恢复智能体框架，通过模拟器高效构建最优恢复动作轨迹数据集，并采用顺序感知恢复对齐（ORA）实现即插即用的专家扩展，在多重退化图像恢复任务上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于智能体的图像恢复方法需要 O((N^D)²) 次专家调用构建最优轨迹数据集，且智能体训练与固定专家池耦合，无法灵活扩展新的恢复专家，效率和可扩展性严重受限。

Method: 提出 DiTTo 框架，包含两个组件：1) DiTTo 模拟器，结合 ∪S-IR（单步恢复动作模拟）和 AiO-IQA（每动作质量预测），将 ORTD 构建复杂度降至 O(N^D)；2) DiTTo 智能体，在模拟器生成的 ORTD 上通过 SFT 训练，再经顺序感知恢复对齐（ORA）沿退化识别、恢复动作排序和输出格式三个独立轴进行对齐，支持即插即用的专家扩展。

Result: 在 MiO-100 评测集（最多五种并发退化）上，DiTTo Agent 在多重退化图像恢复质量上超越了所有先前基于智能体的图像恢复方法。

Conclusion: DiTTo 通过模拟器驱动的训练数据构建和顺序感知对齐，有效解决了基于智能体的图像恢复在效率和可扩展性上的瓶颈，为实际场景中灵活扩展恢复专家提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于视觉语言模型的图像恢复智能体（DiTTo Agent），通过调度多个预构建的恢复专家模块实现多退化图像恢复，属于LLM智能体与自动化生成工作流的范畴，与[AIGC]方向下的[AI 智能体与生成工作流]规范主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on agent-based image restoration, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like continuous VLM or video generation. Signals: Agent-based image restoration, Order-aware restoration alignment, Scalable expert extension, Multi-degradation image restoration, SFT and ORA training

Abstract: Real-world images rarely suffer from a single degradation, and the order in which degradations are removed substantially affects the final restoration quality, motivating agent-based image restoration (IR), where a vision-language model schedules a pool of pre-built restoration-experts. However, existing training-based agents require $\mathcal{O}((N^{\mathbf{D}})^{2})$ restoration-expert calls per image to construct the Optimal Restoration-action Trajectory Dataset (ORTD), where $N^{\mathbf{D}}$ denotes the number of degradation types in the universe $\mathbf{D}$, and couple agent training to a fixed restoration-expert pool, preventing extension to newly introduced restoration-experts without full retraining. To overcome these efficiency and extensibility bottlenecks, we propose \textbf{DiTTo}, a novel order-aware image restoration agent framework consisting of the DiTTo Simulator and the DiTTo Agent. The DiTTo Simulator combines $\cup$S-IR for single-step restoration-action simulation and AiO-IQA for per-action quality prediction, reducing ORTD construction to $\mathcal{O}(N^{\mathbf{D}})$ simulator calls per image; the DiTTo Agent is trained by SFT on the simulator-generated ORTD, followed by \textbf{Order-aware Restoration Alignment (ORA)} that aligns degradation identification, restoration-action-ordering, and output format along independent axes. This enables \textbf{plug-and-play scalable extensibility}: adding a new restoration-expert requires updating only the lightweight ORA stage. On the MiO-100 evaluation set with up to five concurrent degradations, our DiTTo Agent achieves state-of-the-art multi-degradation restoration quality among previous agent-based IR methods.

</details>


### [77] [ExpGraph: Model-Agnostic Experience Learning with Graph-Structured Memory for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30712)
*Tao Feng, Chongrui Ye, Tianyang Luo, Jingjun Xu, Xueqiang Xu, Haozhen Zhang, Zhigang Hua, Yan Xie, Shuang Yang, Ge Liu, Jiaxuan You*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AI 智能体与生成工作流

Importance: 值得关注 (65.56) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ExpGraph 是一个模型无关的经验学习框架，通过自演化的经验图结构记忆，使冻结的 LLM 执行器能够复用历史成功策略和失败教训，无需参数更新即可提升性能。在问答、数学推理、代码生成和智能体环境（ALFWorld、AppWorld）上，ExpGraph 相比最强基线提升 4.7%-21.4%，同时减少交互步骤 12.7%-21.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 智能体通常从零开始解决任务，无法有效复用历史经验。微调方法虽然可以复用经验，但缺乏灵活性——当出现更强或更合适的执行器时，需要重新微调。因此，需要一个模型无关的经验学习框架，使冻结的 LLM 执行器能够通过外部经验复用来持续改进。

Method: ExpGraph 将历史轨迹总结为可复用的技能和失败教训，作为节点组织在自演化的经验图中。通过图扩散和效用感知排序检索相关经验。训练一个轻量级检索副驾驶（retrieval copilot），使用强化学习（基于有无检索经验时执行器性能对比的反馈）进行训练。图结构根据下游任务结果在线更新。

Result: 在 ExpSuite 上评估，覆盖问答、数学推理、代码生成和多步智能体环境（ALFWorld、AppWorld）。ExpGraph 在静态任务上超过最强基线 12.2%（小执行器）和 4.7%（大执行器），在智能体环境中提升 21.4% 和 12.7%，同时平均减少交互步骤 12.7% 和 21.6%。消融实验验证了图结构经验、效用感知排序和自适应检索的联合有效性。

Conclusion: ExpGraph 通过模型无关的经验图学习框架，有效解决了 LLM 智能体经验复用问题，在多种任务和不同执行器上均取得显著提升，且无需更新模型参数。图结构记忆、效用感知排序和自适应检索三者协同是实现有效经验复用的关键。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 ExpGraph，一种模型无关的经验学习框架，通过图结构记忆帮助 LLM 智能体复用历史经验（成功策略与失败教训），属于 LLM 智能体与生成工作流方向。该论文与 AIGC 方向下的 [CANONICAL] subtopic "AI 智能体与生成工作流" 高度匹配，同时与多模态理解与生成方向下的智能体工作流也有交叉。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM agent experience learning, not aligned with user's priority on multimodal generation and video/image autoregressive models. Signals: LLM agent, experience graph, model-agnostic, no multimodal generation, no video/image generation

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents have shown strong capabilities in reasoning, tool use, and multi-step interaction, but they often solve tasks from scratch and fail to reuse successful strategies or failure lessons from prior experience. Fine-tuning on collected experience can improve reuse, but it is inflexible when stronger or more suitable executors emerge. We propose ExpGraph, a model-agnostic experience learning framework that enables frozen and replaceable LLM executors to improve through external experience reuse without parameter updates. ExpGraph summarizes historical trajectories into reusable skills and failure lessons, organizes them as nodes in a self-evolving experience graph, and retrieves useful experiences through graph diffusion and utility-aware ranking. A lightweight retrieval copilot is trained with reinforcement learning using feedback that compares executor performance with and without retrieved experiences, while the graph is updated online from downstream task outcomes. We evaluate ExpGraph on ExpSuite, covering question answering, mathematical reasoning, code generation, and multi-step agentic environments including ALFWorld and AppWorld. ExpGraph improves over the strongest baseline by 12.2% and 4.7% on static tasks with smaller and larger executors, and by 21.4% and 12.7% in agentic environments, while reducing average interaction steps by 12.7% and 21.6%. Ablations show that graph-structured experience, utility-aware ranking, and adaptive retrieval jointly enable effective experience reuse across diverse tasks and executor models.

</details>


### [78] [Skill is Not One-Size-Fits-All: Model-Aware Skill Alignment for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30723)
*Jianxiang Yu, Jiapeng Zhu, Bochen Lin, Qier Cui, Zichen Ding, Xiang Li*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AI 智能体与生成工作流

Importance: 普通相关 (40.19) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出MASA（Model-Aware Skill Alignment）框架，通过层次化技能进化流水线和轻量级模型条件技能重写器，为不同能力的LLM智能体自适应地定制技能库，实验表明该方法在多个环境和骨干模型上显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能库通常被视为与模型无关的，不同能力的LLM骨干使用相同的技能描述。然而实验发现，同一技能对不同骨干模型的效果差异巨大——对某个模型有益的技能可能损害另一个模型的表现。因此需要一种模型感知的技能对齐方法。

Method: MASA包含两个阶段：(1) 层次化技能进化流水线，利用爬山算法和UCB驱动的树搜索，结合环境反馈和模型能力画像，迭代重写通用技能和任务特定技能；(2) 轻量级模型条件技能重写器，在进化轨迹上训练，能够单次前向传播复现技能适配过程。

Result: 在三个交互环境和四个骨干模型上，MASA一致取得最佳整体性能，相比最强基线提升高达25.8个点。训练好的重写器能泛化到未见任务和环境，无需额外搜索，以极低的推理成本持续超越更大的教师LLM。

Conclusion: 技能有效性具有强烈的模型依赖性，MASA通过模型感知的技能对齐框架有效解决了这一问题，为构建更高效、更自适应的LLM智能体系统提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究LLM智能体的技能对齐与自适应工作流，属于AIGC方向下的"AI智能体与生成工作流"（aigc_agent_workflow）规范子主题，该子主题涵盖LLM智能体、工作流、工具使用与自动化等核心内容。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM agent skill alignment, not on the user's priority subtopics like multimodal generation or autoregressive video/image generation. Signals: LLM agents, skill alignment, model-aware, hierarchical evolution, interactive tasks

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly retrieve externally curated skills-procedural instructions retrieved at decision time-to improve performance on long-horizon interactive tasks. Existing skill libraries are typically treated as model-agnostic, reusing the same skill formulations across backbones with substantially different capacities and behaviors. However, our controlled experiments across multiple model scales show that skill effectiveness is strongly model-dependent: a skill that benefits one backbone can harm another. Motivated by this observation, we propose MASA Model-Aware Skill Alignment, a framework that adapts skills to each target backbone without modifying agent weights. MASA operates in two stages: (1) a hierarchical skill evolution pipeline that iteratively rewrites general and task-specific skills using hill climbing and UCB-driven tree search, guided by environment feedback and model capability profiles; and (2) a lightweight model-conditioned skill rewriter trained on evolution trajectories to reproduce the adaptation in a single forward pass. Experiments across three interactive environments and four backbones show that MASA consistently achieves the best overall performance, with gains of up to 25.8 points over the strongest baseline. The learned rewriter further generalizes to unseen tasks and environments without additional search, consistently outperforming a much larger teacher LLM at a fraction of the inference cost.

</details>


### [79] [Eywa: Provenance-Grounded Long-Term Memory for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30771)
*Resham Joshi*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AI 智能体与生成工作流

Importance: 普通相关 (43.44) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Eywa 是一种基于溯源（provenance-grounded）的 AI 智能体长时记忆架构，通过先存储不可变源证据再推导规范事实、基于类型信号和源支持验证提取的记忆、以及确定性多路径读取（零 LLM 调用）来提升记忆系统的可审计性和可诊断性。在 LoCoMo、LongMemEval-S 和 BEAM 基准上取得了领先的检索准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AI 智能体记忆系统将源证据、提取事实、检索上下文和回答策略混入一条不透明的提示路径中，导致故障难以诊断——错误答案可能来自缺失证据、无依据的提取、过时状态、检索损失或回答模型行为。需要一种可审计、可诊断的记忆架构。

Method: Eywa 提出"证据先于信念"（evidence before belief）的记忆架构：1) 先存储不可变的源证据，再从中推导规范事实；2) 基于类型信号和源支持验证提取的记忆；3) 通过确定性多路径读取路径检索有界记忆上下文，检索过程中零 LLM 调用；4) 检索到的上下文与回答指令分离，允许同一记忆基座在不同模型上评估。

Result: 在冻结的、工件记录的检索配置下，Eywa 在 LoCoMo C1-C4 分割上使用 Claude Sonnet 4.6 达到 90.19% 的评判准确率；在 LongMemEval-S 上达到 88.2% 的检索充分性准确率；在包含 700 个问题的技术记忆压力基准 BEAM 上达到 81.45% 的平均 nugget 分数和 85.29% 的 pass@score >= 0.5。

Conclusion: Eywa 通过溯源驱动的记忆架构有效提升了 AI 智能体长时记忆的可审计性、可诊断性和检索准确性，为构建可靠的持久化智能体系统提供了新的设计范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Eywa，一种面向 AI 智能体的、基于溯源的长时记忆架构，核心关注智能体的记忆检索、审计、更新与擦除能力，属于 LLM 智能体与生成工作流范畴，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "AI 智能体与生成工作流" (aigc_agent_workflow)。

Importance reason: Focuses on AI agent memory provenance, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous VLM, video generation, or multimodal models. Signals: AI agent memory, provenance-grounded, retrieval accuracy, no LLM in retrieval, not multimodal generation

Abstract: AI agents that persist across sessions need memory they can retrieve, audit, update, and erase. Existing memory systems often collapse source evidence, extracted facts, retrieved context, and answer policy into one opaque prompt path, making failures difficult to diagnose: a wrong answer may come from missing evidence, unsupported extraction, stale state, retrieval loss, or answer-model behavior. We present Eywa, a provenance-grounded memory architecture built around evidence before belief. Eywa stores immutable source evidence before deriving canonical facts, validates extracted memories against typed signals and source support, and retrieves bounded memory context through a deterministic multi-route read path with zero LLM calls inside retrieval. Retrieved context is returned separately from answer instructions, allowing the same memory substrate to be evaluated across frontier, budget, and local answer models. Under a frozen, artifact-recorded retrieval configuration, Eywa reaches 90.19% judge accuracy on the LoCoMo C1-C4 split with Claude Sonnet 4.6 write and QA roles. On LongMemEval-S, it reaches 88.2% retrieval-sufficiency accuracy. On BEAM, a 700-question technical-memory stress benchmark, it reaches 81.45% mean nugget score and 85.29% pass@score >= 0.5. Full per-question artifacts, including questions, gold answers, model answers, retrieved context, and labels, are published at .

</details>


### [80] [AutoSci: A Memory-Centric Agentic System for the Full Scientific Research Lifecycle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31468)
*Weitong Qian, Beicheng Xu, Zhongao Xie, Bowen Fan, Guozheng Tang, Jiale Chen, Xinzhe Wu, Mingtian Yang, Chenyang Di, Jiajun Li, Lingching Tung, Peichao Lai, Yifei Xia, Ziyi Guo, Yanwei Xu, Yanzhao Qin, Shaoduo Gan, Xupeng Miao, Bin Cui*

TL;DR: AutoSci 是一个以记忆为中心的智能体系统，通过 SciMem（结构化科研记忆）、SciFlow（五阶段科研工作流）、SciDAG（DAG 多智能体算子）和 SciEvolve（反馈驱动的自我进化）四个模块，实现从文献调研到审稿回复的全科研生命周期自动化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统科学研究依赖研究人员手动协调文献、想法、实验、论文和审稿回复，过程耗时且难以系统化管理。现有 LLM 科研智能体系统要么只覆盖部分环节，要么缺乏持久化记忆和自我进化能力，无法支持完整的科研生命周期。

Method: AutoSci 包含四个核心模块：(1) SciMem——基于 schema 的科研记忆系统，分为长期知识记忆和活跃研究记忆；(2) SciFlow——通过 harness 控制状态、上下文、验证、反馈和编排，执行五阶段科研生命周期（文献理解→想法生成→实验→论文写作→审稿回复）；(3) SciDAG——用有向无环图（DAG）形式组织多智能体算子，提供可复用的阶段特定模板；(4) SciEvolve——将来自用户、实验、审稿和外部环境的反馈信号转化为对 SciMem、SciFlow 和 SciDAG 的版本化更新。

Result: 论文展示了 AutoSci 在多个科研项目中的执行能力，验证了其持久化记忆、工作流执行和自我进化机制的有效性。代码已开源。

Conclusion: AutoSci 通过记忆中心化的架构设计，首次实现了覆盖完整科研生命周期的自动化智能体系统，具备持久化记忆和自我进化能力，为未来全自动科研助手的发展奠定了基础。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 AutoSci，一个基于 LLM 的科研智能体系统，覆盖文献理解、实验、写作、审稿回复等完整科研工作流，属于 AI 智能体与自动化工作流范畴，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic aigc_agent_workflow。

Importance reason: Focuses on AI agent workflow for scientific research, not on core multimodal generation or understanding. Signals: AI agent system, full research lifecycle, memory-centric, not multimodal generation, not video/image generation

Abstract: Scientific research has traditionally been human-intensive, requiring researchers to coordinate literature, ideas, experiments, manuscripts, and review responses across long project cycles. The rise of LLM-based scientific agents creates an opportunity to automate this process. Such a system must support the full research lifecycle, maintain structured persistent memory across projects, and improve its own research procedures over time. However, existing systems either partially satisfy or fail to satisfy these requirements, leaving a gap for a unified automated scientific research system. As a result, we present AutoSci, a memory-centric agentic system for the full scientific research lifecycle. AutoSci is organized around four modules. SciMem provides schema-governed research memory, separating Long-Term Knowledge Memory for reusable scientific knowledge from Active Research Memory for project-level artifacts such as ideas, experiments, manuscripts, and reviews. SciFlow executes a five-stage lifecycle from literature understanding to rebuttal through a harness that controls state, context, verification, feedback, and orchestration. SciDAG augments difficult skills with DAG-shaped multi-agent operators and reusable stage-specific templates. SciEvolve converts feedback signals from users, experiments, reviews, and external environments into versioned updates to SciMem organization, SciFlow skills, and SciDAG templates. Together, these modules make AutoSci a persistent research environment that can execute, remember, and evolve across research projects. The code repository is available at .

</details>


### [81] [PatchWorld: Gradient-Free Optimization of Executable World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30880)
*Jiaxin Bai, Yue Guo, Yifei Dong, Jiaxuan Xiong, Tianshi Zheng, Yixia Li, Tianqing Fang, Yufei Li, Yisen Gao, Haoyu Huang, Zhongwei Xie, Hong Ting Tsang, Zihao Wang, Lihui Liu, Jeff Pan, Yangqiu Song*

TL;DR: PatchWorld 是一个无梯度的框架，通过反例引导的代码修复将离线轨迹转化为可执行的 Python 世界模型，在 AgentGym 环境中实现无需 LLM 调用的代码规划，达到 76.4% 的宏观成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本智能体环境通常被建模为 POMDP，但很少有工作研究能否通过可执行代码来构建世界模型，用于部分可观测条件下的预测与规划。作者希望用符号化的信念状态程序替代黑盒预测模型，使动作更新可检查、可重放、可局部修补。

Method: PatchWorld 采用无梯度优化框架，通过反例引导的代码修复（counterexample-guided code repair）将离线轨迹转化为可执行的 Python 世界模型。该方法诱导出符号化的信念状态程序，其动作更新可被检查、重放和局部修补，并在世界模型预测模块内部不调用任何 LLM。

Result: 在七个 AgentGym 环境中，PatchWorld-Simple 在基于代码的规划评分中达到最高，宏观成功率为 76.4%（单步前瞻），且世界模型预测模块内部无需 LLM 调用。实验还发现人类指定的残差记忆偏差能提高表面观测保真度，但会削弱决策效用，揭示了可执行世界模型中观测保真度与动作判别动力学之间的权衡。

Conclusion: PatchWorld 证明了通过反例引导的代码修复可以从离线轨迹中构建可执行的符号化世界模型，在无需 LLM 调用的情况下实现有效的规划。同时揭示了可执行世界模型中观测保真度与决策效用之间的内在权衡，为未来智能体世界模型研究提供了新方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 PatchWorld 框架，通过反例引导的代码修复将离线轨迹转化为可执行的 Python 世界模型，用于文本智能体环境中的预测与规划。这属于 LLM 智能体与生成工作流（aigc_agent_workflow）范畴，同时涉及世界模型生成（video_world_model 方向），但核心贡献是智能体世界模型的代码化构建，因此主方向为 AIGC。

Importance reason: Focuses on agent world models via code repair, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like continuous VLM or video generation. Signals: gradient-free world model, executable code planning, AgentGym environments, no LLM calls in prediction, observation fidelity vs. decision utility tradeoff

Abstract: Text-agent environments are typically modeled as partially observable Markov decision processes (POMDPs), assuming that the simulator's latent state and transition dynamics are hidden from the agent. Yet little work has examined whether executable code can be induced to serve as a world model for prediction and planning under partial observability. We introduce PatchWorld, a gradient-free framework that turns offline trajectories into executable Python world models through counterexample-guided code repair. Instead of predicting the next observation with a black-box model, PatchWorld induces symbolic belief-state programs whose action updates can be inspected, replayed, and locally patched. Across seven AgentGym environments, PatchWorld-Simple achieves the highest code-based planning score among evaluated methods, reaching 76.4\% macro success in live one-step lookahead while invoking no LLM calls inside the world-model prediction module itself. We further find that a human-specified residual-memory bias improves surface observation fidelity but weakens decision utility. This exposes a tradeoff in executable world models, since improving observation fidelity can come at the expense of action-discriminative dynamics, and vice versa. Code is available at .

</details>


### [82] [Extending AI for Research to the Humanities: A Multi-Agent Framework for Evidence-Grounded Scholarship](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30947)
*Yating Pan, Jiajun Zhang, Jun Wang, Qi Su*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AI 智能体与生成工作流

TL;DR: 本文提出 SPIRE，一个基于学术原始操作理论的多智能体框架，专为人文学科中基于证据的论证推理设计。通过将人文学科研究操作建模为协作智能体角色（来源发现、证据注释、比较、溯源检查、采样、引用绑定、论证综合），并在多尺度精读基底（段落、上下文图社区、跨上下文语义聚类）上运行，SPIRE 在古典中文和希腊-罗马拉丁文学基准上比 Naive LLM、Text RAG 和 GraphRAG 更可靠地恢复引用的原始来源证据，并在答案准确性、深度、覆盖面和证据质量方面获得更高的盲审评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 LLM 的研究智能体主要针对科学和工程领域优化，擅长执行、检索和定量信号处理，但人文学科研究需要不同的推理模式——基于原始来源的解释性、证据支撑的论证，要求忠实引用、可验证溯源和细读。现有研究智能体缺乏这种基于证据的解释性推理能力。

Method: SPIRE 框架基于 Scholarly Primitives 理论，将人文学科中的重复性操作建模为七个协作智能体角色：来源发现、证据注释、比较、溯源检查、采样、引用绑定和论证综合。系统在多尺度精读基底上运行，包括段落级、上下文图社区级和跨上下文语义聚类级。通过多智能体协作和分层检索机制实现基于证据的论证生成。

Result: 在古典中文和希腊-罗马拉丁学术文献的同行评审论文基准上，SPIRE 在恢复引用的原始来源证据方面优于 Naive LLM、Text RAG 和 GraphRAG。盲审评分显示 SPIRE 在答案准确性、深度、覆盖面和证据质量方面得分更高。消融实验表明，学术操作智能体和细读检索都对生成基于证据的论文有贡献。

Conclusion: SPIRE 成功将多智能体框架扩展到人文学科领域，证明了基于学术原始操作理论的智能体设计可以有效支持人文学科中基于证据的论证推理。该工作填补了现有研究智能体在人文学科应用中的空白，为 AI 辅助人文学术研究提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 论文提出 SPIRE 多智能体框架，将人文学科研究操作（来源发现、证据注释、比较、溯源检查等）建模为协作智能体角色，属于 LLM 智能体与生成工作流方向，与 canonical 子主题 aigc_agent_workflow 高度匹配。

Importance reason: The paper focuses on humanities scholarship, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: humanities, multi-agent, evidence-grounded, not in priority subtopics, negative keywords not triggered but irrelevant

Abstract: LLM-based research agents have advanced rapidly in science and engineering, where research is organized around executable experiments, code, and quantitative signals. Humanities scholarship, however, requires a different mode of reasoning: interpretive, evidence-grounded argument over primary sources, where scholarly value depends on faithful quotation, verifiable provenance, and close reading. Existing research agents remain largely optimized for execution and retrieval, not evidence-grounded interpretive reasoning. To address this gap, we introduce SPIRE (Scholarly-Primitives-Inspired Research Engine), a multi-agent framework for evidence-grounded humanities scholarship. Drawing on Scholarly Primitives theory, SPIRE casts recurring humanities operations as cooperating agent roles (source discovery, evidence annotation, comparison, provenance checking, sampling, citation binding, and argumentative synthesis) over a multi-scale close-reading substrate of passages, intra-context graph communities, and cross-context semantic clusters. On a peer-reviewed-paper benchmark over classical Chinese and Greco-Roman Latin scholarship, SPIRE recovers cited primary-source evidence more reliably than Naive LLM, Text RAG, and GraphRAG, and receives higher blind-judge scores on answer accuracy, depth, coverage, and evidence quality. Ablations show that both the scholarly-operation agents and close-reading retrieval contribute to evidence-grounded essays. Code, data catalogues, and reproduction scripts are released at .

</details>


### [83] [An Organization-Scoped LLM Agent Runtime Architecture for Regulated Cybersecurity Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30604)
*George Fatouros, Georgios Makridis, George Kousiouris, John Soldatos, Dimosthenis Kyriazis*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI, cs.IR, cs.CR

Subtopic: AI 智能体与生成工作流

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向受监管网络安全运营的组织级LLM智能体运行时架构，通过类型化的安全上下文、共享运行时核心、逻辑子智能体、受控工具适配层、结构化发现报告、分层人机交互门控和仅追加审计日志等组件，实现组织范围的安全策略执行与可审计性，并设计了可验证的评估方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体在孤立网络安全任务上表现良好，但缺乏一个可审计的平台架构来支持受监管的安全运营中心（SOC）和合规工作流，无法在组织范围内统一执行检索、工具调用、内存、发现、报告和审计，且需要与现有SIEM/XDR系统深度集成。

Method: 提出组织级LLM智能体运行时架构，核心组件包括：类型化安全上下文（Security Context）在每个入口点创建并强制执行；共享运行时核心（Runtime Core）与逻辑子智能体；受控工具适配层（Tool Adapter Layer）统一暴露SIEM/XDR查询、增强和响应原语；结构化发现与证据引用；分层人机交互门控（HITL gates）；仅追加审计日志。MCP、扩展遥测、数字孪生、图检索和联邦知识共享作为可选扩展路径。

Result: 论文提出了一个可实现的架构切片作为可测试性表面，并设计了包含架构就绪度、安全策略执行、证据可追溯性、输出质量和运营可观测性等指标的可证伪评估计划，但尚未给出实验性结果。

Conclusion: 该架构为受监管的网络安全运营提供了一个模型无关、可本地部署的组织级LLM智能体运行时方案，通过统一的安全上下文和审计机制弥补了现有LLM智能体在合规性和可审计性方面的不足，为金融等受监管行业的网络安全自动化提供了可行的架构参考。

Classification reason: 该论文提出面向网络安全运营的LLM智能体运行时架构，涉及多智能体协作、工具调用、人机交互工作流等核心内容，与AIGC方向下的[AI智能体与生成工作流]规范子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on regulated cybersecurity operations, not on the user's priority subtopics like multimodal generation or autoregressive video. Signals: Cybersecurity agent architecture, regulated workflows, SIEM/XDR integration, not multimodal, not generative

Abstract: Regulated cybersecurity workflows lack a runtime substrate that enforces organization-level scope across retrieval, tool calls, memory, findings, reports, and audit while remaining model-agnostic and locally deployable. Recent large language model (LLM) agent systems report strong results on isolated cybersecurity tasks, yet they do not by themselves define an auditable platform architecture for regulated security operations centre (SOC) and compliance workflows, where a single analyst may trigger actions that bind the organization, and where the runtime must integrate with existing SIEM/XDR stacks as a primary source of context and alert-driven triggers rather than operate as a standalone analytical layer. This paper proposes an organization-scoped LLM agent runtime architecture for financial cybersecurity. The contribution is a typed Security Context that is created at every entry point, including SIEM/XDR notifications ingested as first-class triggers, and enforced at every component boundary, combined with a shared Runtime Core, logical specialist subagents, a governed Tool Adapter Layer exposing SIEM/XDR query, enrichment, and response primitives under uniform policy and audit, structured findings with evidence references, tiered human-in-the-loop (HITL) gates, and append-only audit. Model Context Protocol (MCP), extended telemetry, digital twins for pentesting, graph retrieval, and federated knowledge sharing are treated as optional extension paths rather than mandatory runtime assumptions. We describe an implementable slice as the architecture's testability surface, and we propose a falsifiable evaluation plan with metric-level pass criteria for architecture readiness, security-policy enforcement, evidence traceability, output quality, and operational observability.

</details>


### [84] [SCOPE: Self-Play via Co-Evolving Policies for Open-Ended Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31433)
*Wai-Chung Kwan, Aryo Pradipta Gema, Joshua Ong Jun Leang, Pasquale Minervini*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AI 智能体与生成工作流

Importance: 值得关注 (70.64) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SCOPE是一个无需外部数据的自我对弈框架，通过协同进化Challenger（生成文档基础任务）和Solver（多轮检索回答）两个策略，并利用冻结的初始模型作为自评判器，在开放任务上提升语言模型性能。在8个基准上最高提升+10.4分，在7个保留的短问答基准上最高提升+13.8分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自我对弈方法需要规则可验证的答案（如数学、代码），无法处理开放任务（如问答、推理）。开放任务依赖人工策划的提示词或前沿模型作为评判器，成本高且不可扩展。因此需要一种无需外部监督、无需人工数据的自我对弈框架来训练开放任务能力。

Method: SCOPE框架包含三个组件：(1) Challenger策略——从文档中生成开放任务；(2) Solver策略——通过多轮检索回答任务；(3) Self-Judge——冻结的初始模型副本，根据源文档编写任务特定评分标准并评分。Challenger和Solver通过自我对弈协同进化：Challenger生成当前Solver能力边界附近的任务（保持难度适中），Solver尝试回答，Self-Judge提供反馈信号，两者交替更新。

Result: 在Qwen2.5、Qwen3、OLMo-3三个7-8B指令微调模型上，SCOPE在8个开放任务基准上最高提升+10.4分，匹配或超过使用约9K策划提示词的GRPO_data。在7个保留的短问答基准上最高提升+13.8分，全面超越GRPO_data。消融实验表明：协同进化Challenger对保持任务难度在Solver能力边界附近至关重要；性能提升来自检索和合成两方面改进，贡献因任务而异；评分标准生成质量是自评判的瓶颈。

Conclusion: SCOPE证明了无需外部数据的自我对弈可以显著提升语言模型在开放任务上的表现，且能泛化到短问答任务。协同进化Challenger是保持训练效率的关键，而评分标准生成质量是当前自评判的主要瓶颈，为未来改进指明了方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出SCOPE框架，通过自我对弈（self-play）协同进化两个策略（Challenger生成任务、Solver多轮检索回答），实现开放任务的语言模型训练。这属于LLM智能体与自动化工作流范畴，与aigc_agent_workflow（AI智能体与生成工作流）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on self-play for open-ended tasks, not directly on user's priority subtopics like video generation or continuous VLM. Signals: Self-play framework, Co-evolving policies, Open-ended tasks, No external data, Self-judge rubric generation

Abstract: Self-play can train language models without external supervision. However, existing methods require rule-checkable answers, leaving open-ended tasks dependent on curated prompts or frontier-model judges. We introduce SCOPE, a data-free self-play framework for open-ended tasks that co-evolves two policies: a Challenger that generates document-grounded tasks, and a Solver that answers them through multi-turn retrieval. A frozen copy of the initial model serves as the self-judge, which writes task-specific rubrics from the source document and grades Solver responses against them. Across three 7-8B instruction-tuned models (Qwen2.5, Qwen3, OLMo-3), SCOPE improves open-ended performance by up to +10.4 points on eight benchmarks and matches or exceeds GRPO_data trained on ~9K curated prompts. Although trained only on open-ended tasks, SCOPE also improves held-out short-form QA by up to +13.8 points on seven held-out benchmarks, surpassing GRPO_data on all three models. Ablations show that co-evolving the Challenger is necessary to keep tasks near the Solver's frontier, that gains arise from improvements in both retrieval and synthesis with the relative contribution varying by task, and that rubric generation quality is the bottleneck for self-judging.

</details>


### [85] [Learning Multi-Agent Coordination via Sheaf-ADMM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31005)
*Jeffrey Seely, Bartłomiej Cupiał, Llion Jones*

Main category: cs.LG

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AI 智能体与生成工作流

Importance: 普通相关 (40.19) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Sheaf-ADMM，一种可微优化框架，用于多智能体协调。输入被分解为重叠局部视图，每个智能体通过神经编码器参数化的凸子问题处理，智能体间通过 ADMM 和细胞层（cellular sheaf）指定的约束进行协调。通过展开优化反向传播联合训练整个系统。在迷宫寻路、图像分类和数独任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有消息传递架构（如 GNN）在多智能体协调中缺乏可解释性和可控性，且难以直接分析协调动态。作者希望提出一种结构化的可微优化框架，使多智能体系统既能端到端训练，又能提供对协调过程的直接分析和干预能力。

Method: 将输入分解为重叠局部视图，每个智能体求解一个由神经编码器参数化的凸子问题。智能体间通过 ADMM 协调，约束由细胞层（cellular sheaf）指定，允许异构的全局一致性定义。通过展开 ADMM 迭代并反向传播，联合训练所有组件。

Result: 在迷宫寻路、MNIST 图像分类和数独任务上，局部视图不足的智能体通过协调产生正确的全局输出。MNIST 上对分布偏移的鲁棒性优于标准 CNN。数独上求解率显著高于参数匹配的 MPNN 基线。ADMM 结构暴露了 primal、consensus 和 dual 状态变量，可直接分析和干预协调动态。

Conclusion: Sheaf-ADMM 提供了一种可微、可解释的多智能体协调框架，兼具端到端训练能力和结构化协调分析能力，在多个任务上优于传统消息传递方法，为多智能体系统的设计提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一种基于可微优化的多智能体协调框架（Sheaf-ADMM），属于多智能体系统与自动化协作工作流范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "AI 智能体与生成工作流" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: The paper focuses on multi-agent coordination via optimization, which is not aligned with the user's priority subtopics in multimodal generation, video/image generation, or efficient inference. Signals: Multi-agent coordination, ADMM optimization, cellular sheaf, not multimodal generation, not autoregressive video/image

Abstract: We present a differentiable optimization framework for multi-agent coordination. An input is decomposed into overlapping local views, each processed by an agent that solves a convex subproblem parameterized by a neural encoder. Agents coordinate through the Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (ADMM) with inter-agent constraints specified by a cellular sheaf. The sheaf specifies which aspects of neighboring solutions must agree, allowing for heterogeneous notions of global consensus. Backpropagating through the unrolled optimization jointly trains all components of the multi-agent system. We evaluate on maze pathfinding, image classification, and Sudoku, where agents with individually insufficient local views learn to coordinate to produce correct global outputs. On MNIST, the local-view decomposition yields improved robustness to distribution shifts relative to a standard CNN. On Sudoku, the optimization-derived structure yields markedly higher solve rates than parameter-matched MPNN baselines. Finally, the ADMM structure exposes distinct primal, consensus, and dual state variables, opening the coordination dynamics to direct analysis and intervention -- a property unavailable in standard message-passing architectures.

</details>


### [86] [Design and Evaluation of Multi-Agent AI Oracle Systems for Prediction Market Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30802)
*Tarun Kota*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.MA

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AI 智能体与生成工作流

Importance: 值得关注 (70.74) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文设计并评估了多智能体 AI 预言机系统用于预测市场结果裁决。在 KalshiBench 的 1,189 个问题上，独立聚合+置信度加权投票达到 83.43% 准确率，优于最佳单模型 1.01 个百分点；而协商共识因错误传播导致准确率降至约 76%。提出混合 AI-人类预言机路由策略：仅自动裁决一致高置信度问题，可在 47% 数据上达到 97.87% 准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测市场依赖可靠的结果裁决，现有预言机系统在快速但脆弱的自动化与准确但昂贵的人工仲裁之间存在权衡。单 LLM 预言机虽有一定准确率，但继承了底层模型的所有失败模式且缺乏自我纠正机制。因此需要探索多智能体架构能否提升裁决准确率。

Method: 在 KalshiBench 的 1,189 个已解决预测市场问题上，比较独立聚合（置信度加权投票）和协商共识两种多智能体架构与单 LLM 基线（GPT-5 Nano、DeepSeek V3、Llama-3.3-70B）。所有智能体共享 Exa 证据层，检索按发表日期过滤以隔离推理与检索质量。分析模型间错误相关性（0.529-0.689）并设计混合路由策略。

Result: 独立聚合+置信度加权投票达到最高准确率 83.43%，超过最佳单模型 1.01 个百分点。协商共识准确率降至约 76%，低于所有单模型基线。模型间错误相关性解释了聚合增益低于理论 Condorcet 上限的原因。混合路由策略在 47% 数据上达到 97.87% 准确率。

Conclusion: 多智能体独立聚合可适度提升预测市场裁决准确率，但协商共识因错误传播而失效。模型间错误相关性对集成方法构成根本限制。建议采用混合 AI-人类预言机系统，仅自动裁决一致高置信度问题，其余交由人工仲裁。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多智能体 LLM 架构用于预测市场结果裁决，属于 LLM 智能体与自动化工作流范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "AI 智能体与生成工作流" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on multi-agent LLM oracles for prediction markets, which is outside the user's core interests in multimodal generation and autoregressive video/image generation. Signals: Multi-agent LLM, prediction market oracle, accuracy 83.43%, error propagation, hybrid AI-human routing

Abstract: Prediction markets aggregate collective intelligence to forecast uncertain events, but their utility depends on reliable outcome resolution. Existing oracle systems tradeoff fast but brittle automation against accurate but costly human arbitration. Single-LLM oracles achieve meaningful accuracy but inherit all failure modes of their underlying model with no self-correction mechanism. We evaluate whether multi-agent LLM architectures can improve oracle resolution accuracy over single-model baselines. We compare independent aggregation and deliberative consensus against single-LLM baselines (GPT-5 Nano, DeepSeek V3, and Llama-3.3-70B) on 1,189 resolved prediction market questions from KalshiBench. All agents share a common evidence layer through Exa, with retrieval filtered by publication date to isolate reasoning from retrieval quality. Independent aggregation with confidence-weighted voting achieves the highest accuracy at 83.43 percent, outperforming the best individual model by 1.01 percentage points. Deliberative consensus degrades accuracy to approximately 76 percent, below every single-model baseline, attributed to error propagation during debate where confidently wrong models flip correct ones. Error correlations across models (0.529-0.689) explain why aggregation gains fall short of the theoretical Condorcet ceiling, placing a fundamental limit on ensemble approaches. Many questions resist correction by any multi-agent architecture, motivating escalation to human arbitration. We propose routing criteria for hybrid AI-human oracle systems: auto-resolving only unanimous, high-confidence questions yields 97.87 percent accuracy on 47 percent of the dataset, with inter-agent disagreement flagging the remainder for human review.

</details>

### [87] [idSCD: Identifying Training Datasets through Semantic Correlation Descriptors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30462)
*Andrada Gobeaja, Ionut Hodoroaga, Elena Burceanu, Marius Leordeanu*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.LG

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AIGC 检测与溯源

Importance: 普通相关 (41.51) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 idSCD 方法，利用模型在训练过程中学到的语义相关性结构（SCD）来识别训练数据集的归属。通过白盒方式提取模型的语义相关性描述符，并与目标数据集的独立 SCD 进行比对，实现数据集级别的成员推断。实验表明该方法在多个任务上优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集成员推断方法依赖行为或分布证据（如置信度、损失、生成样本等），但忽略了数据集在模型中学到的特定语义相关性结构。作者希望利用数据集在训练中留下的"语义指纹"来实现更精确的数据集归属识别。

Method: 提出语义相关性描述符（SCD），通过白盒方式提取模型内部语义相关性结构。设计基于 SCD 的成员推断分数，通过比较目标数据集的独立 SCD 与训练后模型的 SCD 来判断数据集是否属于训练混合。采用留一数据集诊断方法验证 SCD 的区分能力。

Result: 在自然语言推理、情感分类和医学文本分类三个实验设置中，基于 SCD 的分类器在平均性能和标准差上均优于黑盒基线（RMIA、Attack-P、LiRA）和白盒基线（SIF），最大相对增益在 ROC-AUC 上超过 60%。

Conclusion: 数据集可以通过模型内部语义相关性结构进行溯源识别，SCD 方法在数据集成员推断任务上具有显著优势，尤其当数据集组具有明显语义特殊性时效果更佳。

Classification reason: 该论文研究通过语义相关性描述符（SCD）识别训练数据集归属，属于AIGC检测与溯源范畴（模型训练数据溯源/归因），与 canonical subtopic "AIGC 检测与溯源" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on dataset membership inference via semantic correlations, which is outside the user's core interests in generation, multimodal models, and efficiency. Signals: Dataset membership inference, Semantic correlation descriptors, White-box fingerprinting, Not generation or multimodal, No overlap with priority subtopics

Abstract: Can a dataset be recognized from the spurious correlations it induces during training? We argue that datasets leave dataset-specific traces in a model's learned semantic correlation structure: incidental regularities that are predictive within a dataset, but not causal for the underlying task, can be internalized during training. We use this insight to study dataset-level membership inference, moving beyond existing methods that rely on behavioral or distributional evidence such as confidence scores, losses, margins, generated samples, or query responses. We introduce a white-box semantic fingerprinting approach based on semantic correlation descriptors (SCDs), which capture the semantic correlation structure learned by a model and make it comparable across dataset mixtures. In a controlled leave-one-dataset-out diagnostic, SCDs recover dataset-specific changes and perfectly separate matching from non-matching dataset pairs. We then propose a practical SCD-based membership score that tests whether a target dataset is part of a model's training mixture using only the model's SCD and the target dataset's standalone SCD, without requiring leave-one-dataset-out models. Across three diverse experimental settings, with dataset groups for natural language inference, emotion classification, and medical text classification, we test both the advantages and limitations of SCD-based membership inference with different degrees of semantic separation and keyword support between dataset splits. On average, the classifier based on this score achieves the highest performance and the lowest std, outperforming black-box baselines RMIA, Attack-P, and LiRA, as well as the white-box SIF baseline. These results show that dataset membership can be traced through internal semantic correlations, with the largest relative gain exceeding 60% in ROC-AUC when dataset groups expose distinct semantic particularities.

</details>


### [88] [BIAS-ID: A Framework for Analyzing Transformation Biases in AI-Generated Image Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31153)
*Jonas Ricker, Asja Fischer, Erwin Quiring*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AIGC 检测与溯源

Importance: 普通相关 (41.51) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 BIAS-ID 框架，用于系统分析和量化 AI 生成图像检测器中的变换偏差（transformation biases），揭示现有检测器在真实场景下因训练数据偏差而依赖虚假相关性，而非真正的取证特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AI 生成图像检测器在受控设置下表现良好，但在真实数据上性能急剧下降。潜在原因是训练数据中存在细微偏差，导致检测器依赖虚假相关性而非真正的取证特征。目前缺乏统一的协议来评估检测器的偏差程度。

Method: 首先从理论上讨论检测器偏差的含义及其与鲁棒性不足的区别；然后提出 BIAS-ID 框架，通过系统性地分析检测器在不同图像变换下的行为来量化和揭示变换偏差的存在。

Result: 在两个数据集上对六种检测器进行评估，发现多个最先进的检测方法受到严重偏差影响，验证了 BIAS-ID 框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 偏差感知的评估对于开发可靠的 AI 生成图像检测器至关重要，BIAS-ID 为系统分析检测器偏差提供了透明且可复现的框架。

Classification reason: 论文研究 AI 生成图像检测器的变换偏差分析框架，属于 AIGC 检测方向，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "AIGC 检测与溯源" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on AI-generated image detector bias, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative keywords apply. Signals: AIGC detection, transformation bias, forensic artifacts, detector evaluation, spurious correlations

Abstract: Given the surge of harmful AI-generated imagery online, reliably distinguishing authentic images from generated ones has become an urgent research topic. While many proposed detection methods perform well under controlled settings, they often collapse when tested on real-world data. A potential root cause are subtle biases in the detectors' training data. As a result, detectors may rely on spurious correlations instead of learning true forensic artifacts. While a recent line of work has identified the problem, there is not yet an established protocol to evaluate how biased a detector actually is. In this work, we therefore take a step back: First, we discuss what it means for a detector to be biased, and how this differs from a lack of robustness. Second, we propose BIAS-ID, a transparent framework for analyzing and quantifying the presence of transformation biases in AI-generated image detectors. We validate our framework by performing an evaluation of six detectors across two datasets, revealing that several state-of-the-art detection methods are strongly affected by biases. Our results highlight the importance of bias-aware evaluation for developing reliable AI-generated image detectors.

</details>


### [89] [The Regularizing Power of Language-Training Deepfake Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31192)
*Benedikt Hopf, Zongwei Wu, Radu Timofte*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AIGC 检测与溯源

Importance: 普通相关 (64.79) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出利用语言训练的正则化能力来提升深度伪造检测器的泛化性和可解释性。采用双编码器架构（冻结的专家检测器 + LoRA微调的MLLM编码器），通过两阶段训练：先进行二值对齐缓解过拟合，再通过强化学习阶段鼓励模型先描述再分类，从而优先利用高层级可描述特征。在多个基准数据集上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度伪造检测面临两大挑战：泛化性（容易过拟合到低层领域特定伪影）和可解释性。现有方法通常分别处理这两个问题，但本文认为可描述的伪影通常泛化性更好，因此可以利用语言作为正则化机制来联合解决这两个挑战。

Method: 提出双编码器架构：一个冻结的专家检测器（如LSDA）与一个LoRA微调的MLLM编码器（如Phi-3.5-V）配对。两阶段训练：第一阶段进行二值对齐训练，让MLLM学习结合特征以缓解过拟合；第二阶段采用强化学习（REINFORCE算法），鼓励模型在分类前先生成描述性推理（"explain-then-classify"），通过奖励机制优先使用高层级鲁棒特征。

Result: 在多个基准数据集（如FaceForensics++、Celeb-DF、DFDC等）上的跨数据集评估中，该方法显著优于现有最先进方法，同时生成可解释的描述性推理。即使推理时省略推理链，性能仍然保持领先。

Conclusion: 语言训练的正则化能力可以有效提升深度伪造检测器的泛化性和可解释性。通过强化学习鼓励"先解释后分类"的行为，模型能够优先利用高层级可描述特征，在跨数据集泛化上取得显著提升，为可解释深度伪造检测提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究深度伪造检测（deepfake detection），属于 AIGC 检测与溯源这一 [CANONICAL] 子主题的核心范畴。同时涉及 MLLM 用于检测，也与多模态理解与生成方向相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on deepfake detection, which is not a priority subtopic, and lacks direct connection to user's key interests in generation and multimodal reasoning. Signals: Deepfake detection, Language regularization, MLLM encoder, Generalization, Interpretability

Abstract: Recently, thanks to the advent of Multimodal-LLMs, deepfake detectors are striving not only to be generalizable but also interpretable. We propose that these two challenges can effectively be tackled jointly, since describable artifacts typically generalize better, opening the possibility to use language as a regularization mechanism. Since deepfake detection generally suffers from overfitting to low-level domain-specific artifacts, our intuition is that an LLM that has been pretrained on language would prefer high-level artifacts that can be described better. This way, we can use high-level features where possible, while training the model to use low-level features where necessary. We utilize a dual-encoder architecture, pairing a frozen specialist detector with a LoRA-tuned MLLM encoder, and a two-stage training curriculum: first, a binary alignment phase demonstrates that the intrinsic capability of MLLMs can effectively combine features to mitigate overfitting to dataset-specific artifacts. To further bolster generalization and achieve interpretability, we employ a reinforcement learning stage that encourages the model to generate descriptive reasoning before classifying, using only binary labels. By rewarding this "explain-then-classify" behavior, we explicitly incentivize the model to prioritize high-level, robust features. Crucially, this process yields both interpretable descriptions and a further boost in cross-dataset performance, even when reasoning chains are omitted at inference. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets validate our approach, outperforming state-of-the-art methods by a large margin.

</details>


### [90] [Authentication of Copy Detection Patterns via Cross-Camera Dual-Synthetic Referencing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31292)
*Ivan Oleksiyuk, Roman Chaban, Slava Voloshynovskiy*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AIGC 检测与溯源

Importance: 普通相关 (38.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于跨相机双合成参考的 CDP 认证框架，通过注册相机捕获与数字模板联合生成高质量参考图像，提升印刷防伪码在不同移动相机下的认证鲁棒性，并提供了信息论解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 CDP 认证方法在验证时仅依赖数字模板与验证图像对比，但打印随机性和相机畸变导致匹配困难，限制了防伪鲁棒性。先前工作虽通过合成参考图像缓解了相机效应，却忽略了打印变异性，因此需要一种同时建模打印与相机双重失真的认证方法。

Method: 提出 enrolment-based 跨相机双合成参考框架：首先用受控注册相机捕获每个印刷 CDP，然后训练一个深度学习翻译器，联合利用数字模板和注册捕获图像，为验证相机生成高质量参考图像。该方法从信息论角度证明双参考比仅基于模板的参考包含更多互信息。实验使用异构移动相机进行验证。

Result: 在多种移动相机上验证了改进的认证性能，对基于机器学习的复制攻击具有鲁棒性，并且能够从小尺寸 CDP 区域和低端设备上实现可靠验证。

Conclusion: 跨相机双合成参考框架有效提升了 CDP 认证的鲁棒性和实用性，兼顾了打印变异性和相机畸变，为低成本防伪认证提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 Copy Detection Patterns (CDP) 的认证方法，涉及印刷品防伪检测与对抗伪造攻击，属于 AIGC 检测与溯源方向下的深伪检测/生成内容认证范畴，因此映射到 canonical 子主题 aigc_detection_attribution。

Importance reason: Focuses on AIGC detection/traceability for physical copy patterns, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative-keyword-filtered. Signals: AIGC detection, cross-camera authentication, copy detection patterns, no autoregressive/video/diffusion/world model, no continuous VLM

Abstract: Copy Detection Patterns (CDPs) are structures printed on physical objects to enable cost-effective authentication. Verification is achieved by comparing a captured image with the digital template from which the CDP was printed. In practice, printer stochasticity and camera distortions hinder this comparison, limiting robustness against counterfeiting. Prior work addressed camera effects by synthesising reference images in the verification camera domain, but it ignored printing variability. We introduce an enrolment-based cross-camera dual-synthetic referencing framework. Each printed CDP is first captured by a controlled enrolment camera, and a deep-learning-based translator jointly exploits the digital template and the enrolled capture to generate a high-quality reference for the verification image. We provide an information-theoretic justification showing that the dual reference is more informative than template-based references. Experiments on heterogeneous mobile cameras demonstrate improved authentication performance, robustness to machine-learning-based copy attacks, and reliable verification from small CDP regions and on low-end devices.

</details>

### [91] [EUDAIMONIA: Evaluating Undesirable Dynamics in AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30654)
*Jun Rui Huang, Wang Bill Zhu, Ziyi Liu, Nathanael Fast, Ravi Iyer, Robin Jia*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CL, cs.HC

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AIGC 评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (68.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Social AI Design Code 框架和 EUDAIMONIA 基准，用于评估 LLM 在社交互动中是否对用户福祉产生负面影响（如有害亲密、依赖、过度参与）。对 22 个最新 LLM 的评测发现，即使最强模型（Claude-Opus-4.7 和 GPT-5.5）也分别违反 30.7% 和 27.2% 的设计要求，且扩展推理无法降低违规率，表明这是持久的社会对齐问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 安全评测主要关注能力导向或传统安全指标（如有害内容生成），但忽略了 LLM 作为社交伴侣时可能产生的社会动态风险，如鼓励有害亲密关系、情感依赖或过度参与。需要新的评测框架来评估这些与用户福祉相关的风险。

Method: 提出 Social AI Design Code 框架，定义 LLM 在社交互动中应遵循的设计原则。基于该框架构建 EUDAIMONIA 基准，包含 969 个用户输入和 3,147 个设计要求违规检查项，通过弱到强过滤、多模型重标注和受控改写从 WildChat 数据集中构建。对 22 个最新 LLM 进行系统评估。

Result: 最强模型 Claude-Opus-4.7 和 GPT-5.5 分别违反 30.7% 和 27.2% 的设计要求检查项。扩展推理（extended thinking）不能降低违规率，表明这些失败是持久的社会对齐问题，而非可通过测试时推理解决的缺陷。

Conclusion: LLM 在社交互动中存在系统性的社会对齐问题，即使最强模型也有较高违规率，且扩展推理无法缓解。需要新的对齐方法专门针对社交互动中的用户福祉进行优化。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 Social AI Design Code 框架和 EUDAIMONIA 基准，用于评估 LLM 在社交互动中对用户福祉的负面影响（如有害亲密、依赖、过度参与），属于 AIGC 生成内容的质量与安全评测范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 的 aigc_evaluation_benchmark（AIGC 评测与基准）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on social alignment and user welfare in LLM interactions, which is outside the user's core technical priorities in multimodal generation and efficient models. Signals: Social AI alignment, LLM welfare evaluation, not multimodal generation, not autoregressive video/image, not efficient inference

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as conversational partners for companionship, emotional disclosure, and interpersonal advice, but the social dynamics of these interactions can create harms that are not captured by capability-oriented or traditional safety evaluations. We introduce the Social AI Design Code, a framework for evaluating whether LLMs align with user welfare in social interactions, including whether they encourage harmful intimacy, dependence, or prolonged engagement. To evaluate these risks in natural and diverse user-LLM interactions, we operationalize the code with EUDAIMONIA, a benchmark of 969 user inputs and 3,147 design-requirement violation checks built from WildChat through weak-to-strong filtration, multi-model relabeling, and controlled rewriting. Evaluating 22 recent LLMs, we find that even the strongest models, Claude-Opus-4.7 and GPT-5.5, violate 30.7% and 27.2% of checks, respectively. Extended thinking does not reduce violation rates, suggesting that these failures are persistent social-alignment problems rather than deficits solvable through test-time reasoning alone.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管生成式AI在全球范围内被广泛使用，但不同国家、不同收入水平和语言背景的用户如何使用AI的差异尚缺乏大规模实证研究。理解这些差异对于评估AI是否扩大或缩小数字鸿沟至关重要。

Method: 使用一个广泛可用的免费AI聊天机器人的大规模匿名化交互数据集，按国家收入水平和语言对用户使用模式进行统计分析，考察教育、工作、休闲等不同使用领域的分布差异及其与GDP、语言的关系。

Result: 教育是大多数国家最常见的AI使用领域，尤其在低收入国家中占比更高，与GDP呈显著负相关；休闲用途则与GDP正相关。英语交互在非英语主导语言国家中被过度使用，表明现有模型的语言覆盖存在不足。

Conclusion: 生成式AI的使用模式因国家收入水平和语言而异，改善多语言性能可能是决定该技术能否缩小数字鸿沟的关键因素。

Classification reason: 该论文研究全球不同国家用户对生成式AI聊天机器人的使用模式差异，属于AIGC应用层面的实证分析与评测，与[AIGC 评测与基准]（aigc_evaluation_benchmark）中关于AIGC使用行为、人类偏好评估的主题高度相关。

Importance reason: This paper focuses on AIGC usage patterns and digital divides, which is outside the user's core technical priorities in multimodal generation and model efficiency. Signals: AIGC 评测与基准, 用户行为分析, 语言差异, 数字鸿沟, 非技术方向

Abstract: AI is being used by people globally, but not everyone is using it in the same ways. Using a large-scale dataset of anonymized, de-identified, and privacy-scrubbed interactions with a widely available and free AI chatbot, we empirically characterize differences in early adopters' usage across countries. Schooling is the most common domain of use in most countries, particularly low-income countries, with a strong inverse association evident between schooling and country-level GDP. Leisure-related use, by contrast, is positively associated with country-level income. Language, we find, also shapes use: English-language interactions are overrepresented in places where the predominant languages were not well-served by existing models during the period of the study. Improving performance across languages may be a key factor, our work suggests, in whether this technology expands digital divides or enables leapfrogging.

</details>

Conclusion: 提出的协议为评估 LLM 在生物医学领域生成关联的可靠性提供了系统框架，RAG 增强的语义验证方法能有效补充本体精确匹配的不足，有助于提升生成内容的可信度。

Classification reason: 该论文提出评估 ChatGPT 生成生物医学关联能力的协议，属于 AIGC 生成内容的质量评测范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "AIGC 评测与基准" 高度匹配。

Abstract: We present a protocol to evaluate ChatGPT's ability to generate disease-centric biomedical associations. It outlines how we generate the associations, validate the biological entities using biomedical ontologies, and verify associations using literature. The protocol includes a self-consistency strategy to assess generative reliability across ChatGPT models. To address ontology exact-match limitations, we provide a use case performing semantic verification through a workflow enabled by Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) powered by open-source large language models (LLMs). This enables LLMs to establish truth over content generated by other LLMs and expose hallucination.

</details>

TL;DR: EHRBench 是一个基于电子健康记录（EHR）的自动化、可靠的临床决策基准，通过 EHR-LLM-知识库交互流水线构建了近 100 万条 QA 数据，覆盖诊断、治疗和预后三类任务，并对 30 多个 LLM 进行了系统评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有临床决策基准缺乏对真实 EHR 数据的依托，且构建过程难以兼顾规模与质量。LLM 在临床决策中的可靠性尚未被充分理解，需要一个自动化且可靠的 EHR 基准来系统评估 LLM 的临床决策能力。

Method: 提出 EHR-LLM-KB 交互流水线：先用专用 LLM 自动将 EHR 轨迹转换为结构化模板，再确定性实例化为 QA 条目；同时利用知识库进行系统验证和丰富，过滤幻觉或模糊关系，提升可靠性。最终构建了约 96 万条 QA 数据，涵盖诊断、治疗、预后三类推理型临床决策任务。

Result: 对 30 多个代表性 LLM 进行了基准测试，展示了不同设置下一致的能力趋势，验证了 EHRBench 的可靠性，并揭示了 LLM 在临床可靠性方面的可改进差距。

Conclusion: EHRBench 提供了一个大规模、自动化、可靠的临床决策评测基准，能够有效评估 LLM 在真实临床场景中的决策能力，为构建更可靠的临床 LLM 系统指明了方向。

Classification reason: 该论文构建了 EHRBench，一个基于电子健康记录的临床决策 LLM 评测基准，属于 AIGC 评测与基准（aigc_evaluation_benchmark）范畴，该 [CANONICAL] 子主题涵盖生成式 AI 的评测基准构建。

Importance reason: Focuses on clinical LLM benchmarks, which is outside your configured priority subtopics and includes negative keywords. Signals: Clinical decision-making, EHR benchmark, LLM evaluation, medical domain, low personal relevance

Abstract: Clinical decision-making (CDM) is central to real-world clinical workflows, where clinicians infer diagnoses, select treatments, or anticipate future health outcomes under incomplete evidence. LLMs are increasingly used to support these decisions due to strong language capabilities, broad biomedical knowledge, and efficiency, yet the reliability of LLMs on real-world clinical decision tasks remains insufficiently understood. To evaluate CDM models, especially LLM-based models, an ideal and practical medical decision benchmark should be constructed via an automated yet reliable pipeline to ensure both scale and quality. Moreover, the grounding of a CDM benchmark in real patient EHRs can better support evaluation on practical CDM tasks that require substantive biomedical knowledge and clinical inference. To fill the gaps, we introduce EHRBench, an automated and reliable EHR-grounded benchmark for evaluating LLM-based clinical decision-making at scale. To ensure scalability and reliability, EHRBench is constructed through an EHR-LLM-KB(knowledge-base) interaction pipeline. For efficiency, we use a specialized LLM to automatically convert encounter-level EHR trajectories into structured templates and deterministically instantiate the templates into QA items. In parallel, we apply systematic KB-based verification and enrichment to filter hallucinated or ambiguous relations and to improve reliability. Using this pipeline, we construct nearly 1M (960,067) QA items spanning three core inference-required clinical decision tasks: diagnosis, treatment, and prognosis. We benchmark more than 30 representative LLMs on EHRBench and provide detailed analyses of performance and robustness. The results show consistent capability trends across settings, further validating the reliability of EHRBench and highlighting actionable gaps toward clinically reliable LLM systems.

</details>


### [95] [NumLeak: Public Numeric Benchmarks as Latent Labels in Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30393)
*Anany Kotawala*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.LG, cs.CR

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AIGC 评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (43.34) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: NumLeak 是一个测量框架，用于检测基础模型在公开数值基准上的记忆性召回（memorized recall）而非真正的泛化能力。实验表明，顶级LLM对Fama-French市场超额收益、美国失业率、CPI通胀等公开数值基准表现出极高的记忆精度（Pearson r=0.97-0.99），而新发布的holdout数据上解析率骤降至21-57%。该框架还发现闭源API的黑盒探测低估了记忆程度，并提出一行系统提示防御可阻断99.8%的非自适应单轮后缀攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公开数值基准（如经济指标、金融因子）常出现在预训练数据中，导致模型在评测时可能只是记忆了训练数据中的数值，而非真正具备推理或泛化能力。现有评测方法未充分区分记忆与泛化，因此需要系统性的测量框架来量化这种记忆污染。

Method: 提出NumLeak框架，结合两个层面：(1) API边界探测——对生产级闭源模型进行数值召回测试，使用Fama-French因子、美国失业率、CPI通胀、NOAA温度等公开数值基准，通过Pearson相关系数、解析率等指标量化记忆程度；(2) 白盒可控验证——在开源因果LM上复现剂量-反应关系，使用logprob排名检测记忆。此外，还设计了"日期到市场情绪"回归实验来残差化模型记忆，以及一行系统提示防御策略。

Result: 顶级LLM对Fama-French市场超额收益的3-seed pooled Pearson r=0.97-0.99，五个兄弟因子在25bps内；失业率、CPI通胀、NOAA温度同样高精度。新发布holdout上解析率降至21-57%，但已回答月份r≈0.99。白盒实验复现剂量-反应关系，logprob排名能检测到开放生成遗漏的记忆。Sonnet的"日期到市场情绪"回归与真实Mkt-RF的r=0.74在残差化后降至r=0.02。一行系统提示防御阻断99.8%的非自适应单轮后缀攻击，且对概念和历史叙事查询的效用损失接近零。

Conclusion: 公开数值基准在预训练数据中造成了严重的记忆污染，导致评测结果高估了模型的真实泛化能力。闭源API的黑盒探测会低估记忆程度，而logprob排名是更敏感的检测手段。简单的系统提示防御可以有效缓解数值记忆泄露，且不影响模型在其他任务上的表现。研究呼吁社区重新审视基于公开数值基准的评测方法。

Classification reason: 该论文研究公开数值基准在预训练数据中导致模型记忆而非真正泛化的评测污染问题（NumLeak框架），属于AIGC评测与基准方向下的评测可靠性问题，与aigc_evaluation_benchmark（AIGC评测与基准）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on benchmark memorization detection, not on generation or multimodal models. Signals: benchmark memorization, foundation model evaluation, numeric recall, defense prompt, not generative

Abstract: Public numeric benchmarks appear in pretraining, so an evaluation that conditions on a date may be measuring memorized recall rather than out-of-sample skill. We introduce NumLeak, a measurement framework that combines API-boundary probes on production models with a white-box controlled validation on an open causal LM. Top-tier frontier LLMs recall the Fama-French market excess return at 3-seed pooled Pearson r=0.97-0.99 while staying within 0.15 within-25bps on the five sibling factors; comparable fidelity appears on U.S. unemployment, CPI inflation, and NOAA temperature. On a recent-release holdout, parse rate collapses to 21-57% but r stays at approximately 0.99 on months answered, the refuse-or-recall asymmetry a memorized channel predicts. The white-box experiment reproduces the dose-response, and logprob ranking detects memorization that open-ended generation misses, implying closed-API black-box probes understate the channel. A Sonnet "date to market-sentiment" regression that correlates with true Mkt-RF at r=0.74 collapses to r=0.02 once the model's own recall is residualized out. A one-line system-prompt defense blocks 99.8% of a non-adaptive single-turn suffix attack set at near-zero utility cost on conceptual and historical-narrative queries

</details>


### [96] [Bounded Behavioral Indistinguishability for Black-Box LLM Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30448)
*Munawar Hasan*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CL

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AIGC 评测与基准

TL;DR: 本文提出有界行为不可区分性（bounded behavioral indistinguishability）框架，用于评估黑盒LLM蒸馏的效果。通过对抗性评估发现，即使语义相似度提升，蒸馏模型在行为层面仍与教师模型存在可区分的差异，表明语义保真度不足以衡量蒸馏质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑盒LLM蒸馏评估仅关注输出语义相似性或任务一致性，但输出相似并不等同于行为不可区分。需要更严格的评估框架来衡量蒸馏模型是否真正模仿了教师模型的行为。

Method: 形式化定义了(ε,q,t,𝔸)-行为不可区分性，包含区分优势界、查询预算、计算预算和对抗器类别四个维度。在Qwen和Llama教师-学生对上构建5000提示的行为探测套件，使用对抗性评估（学习判别器、成对教师识别、A/B交换一致性过滤）和查询预算实验来评估蒸馏效果。

Result: LoRA蒸馏将Qwen的语义相似度从0.788提升至0.862，Llama从0.814提升至0.874。但对抗评估显示行为差异仍然存在：判别器保持非零优势，风格/格式、鲁棒性和领域技术提示中存在伪影。跨族Llama评判器下，Qwen区分优势从0.158降至0.081。查询预算实验表明，不一致性引导采样并不总是优于分层随机采样。

Conclusion: 语义保真度有用但不充分，黑盒LLM蒸馏需要结合有界、对抗性和类别感知的评估方法。行为不可区分性应作为蒸馏质量的核心评估维度。

Classification reason: 该论文研究黑盒LLM蒸馏的行为不可区分性评估，提出有界行为不可区分性形式化框架，属于AIGC评测范畴（评估蒸馏模型是否真正模仿了教师模型的行为），因此映射到[AIGC评测与基准]子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM distillation evaluation, not aligned with user's priority on multimodal generation and autoregressive video/image generation. Signals: LLM distillation evaluation, behavioral indistinguishability, adversarial evaluation, not multimodal, not generation-focused

Abstract: Black-box LLM distillation is usually evaluated as an output-matching problem: a student is considered successful when its responses are semantically similar to, or task-consistent with, those of a teacher. However, output similarity does not imply that the student is behaviorally indistinguishable from the model it imitates. We introduce bounded behavioral indistinguishability, formalized as $(\epsilon,q,t,\mathbb{A})$-behavioral indistinguishability over an explicit prompt distribution, where $\epsilon$ bounds distinguishing advantage, $q$ bounds oracle queries, $t$ bounds computation, and $\mathbb{A}$ denotes the adversary class. We instantiate this notion on Qwen and Llama teacher-student pairs using a controlled $5,000$-prompt behavioral probe suite. For each family, we compare the teacher with both the base student and the LoRA-distilled student, measuring whether distillation reduces distinguishability rather than merely improving similarity. LoRA raises semantic similarity from $0.788$ to $0.862$ for Qwen and from $0.814$ to $0.874$ for Llama. Yet adversarial evaluation reveals remaining behavioral differences: learned discriminators retain nonzero advantage, and pairwise category analysis shows artifacts concentrated in style/format, robustness, and domain-technical prompts. A pairwise teacher-identification adversary confirms this trend. With a different-family Llama judge and A/B-swap consistency filtering, Qwen distinguishing advantage drops from $0.158$ for the base student to $0.081$ after LoRA distillation. Query-budget experiments show that disagreement-guided acquisition does not consistently outperform stratified random sampling, indicating that coverage and diversity remain strong baselines. Our results show that semantic fidelity is useful but insufficient: black-box LLM distillation requires bounded, adversarial, and category-aware evaluation.

</details>


### [97] [Auditing LLM Benchmarks with Item Response Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30504)
*Sander Land, Daniel M. Bikel*

Importance: 普通相关 (46.59) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出基于项目反应理论（IRT）的指标，用于检测LLM基准测试中的错误标签。在7个偏好和多选题基准测试中，使用114个模型的响应，该方法在前200个样本中达到95%的精确率，优于有监督分类器。研究发现错误标签源于机械标注启发式、上游数据集继承的标注错误以及本质模糊的题目。模型拟合还揭示了奖励模型在风格偏好而非事实知识上的专业化，并发现一个前沿奖励模型与检测到的错误标签有78%的一致性（其他模型仅38%），暗示可能存在基准污染或过度优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM基准测试的标签在发布后即被冻结，错误标签会无声地传播到下游基准测试中，影响评估的可靠性。现有方法缺乏系统性地检测和追溯这些错误标签的手段。

Method: 提出基于项目反应理论（IRT）的统计指标，利用114个模型在7个偏好和多选题基准测试上的响应，通过模型拟合识别与模型能力曲线不一致的异常标签，从而检测可能的错误标签。

Result: 在前200个样本中达到95%的精确率，优于有监督分类器。发现错误标签的三种来源：机械标注启发式、上游数据集继承的错误、本质模糊的题目。发现一个前沿奖励模型与检测到的错误标签有78%的一致性（其他模型仅38%），暗示可能存在基准污染。

Conclusion: IRT方法能有效检测LLM基准测试中的标签错误，揭示基准测试的质量问题，并可用于检测奖励模型的过度优化或基准污染。该方法为提升LLM评估的可靠性提供了新的工具。

Classification reason: 该论文使用项目反应理论（IRT）审计LLM基准测试中的标签错误，属于对AIGC/LLM评测基准的质量审计与评估，与[AIGC 评测与基准]（aigc_evaluation_benchmark）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM benchmark auditing, not on the user's priority subtopics of multimodal generation or autoregressive video/image generation. Signals: Benchmark auditing, Item Response Theory, Error detection, Reward model specialization, Not aligned with user's priority subtopics

Abstract: LLM benchmark labels are frozen at release and silently propagated into downstream benchmarks, errors and all. We introduce an Item Response Theory-based indicator that surfaces likely mislabels at 95% precision in the top 200 examples across seven preference and multiple-choice benchmarks using responses from 114 models, outperforming a supervised classifier. We trace these errors to mechanical labeling heuristics, upstream annotation mistakes inherited unchanged from source datasets, and fundamentally ambiguous items without a defensible single label. The same model fit reveals that reward models specialize in stylistic preference rather than factual knowledge, and identifies one frontier reward model that agrees with detected mislabels at 78% accuracy versus 38% for its peers, consistent with benchmark contamination or benchmark-specific over-optimization.

</details>


### [98] [A Persona-Based Evaluation Framework for Pluralistic Alignment in Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31021)
