TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于合成认知画像（persona）的多元对齐评估框架，用状态空间约束的仿真方法替代传统的单一聚合基准，使生成式AI评估能够反映文化、人口统计和语境多样性。同时分析了这些仿真评估者在连续推理下的稳定性退化问题，论证了嵌入动态调控机制的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生成式AI的对齐评估范式依赖单一聚合基准，将人类判断的多元性简化为统计平均值，掩盖了文化、人口统计和语境差异。需要一种能够反映多元人类视角的评估方法。

Method: 提出状态空间约束的仿真框架，利用生成式架构实例化并维持多种合成认知画像（evaluative personas），构建结构化流形来替代单一评估函数。通过顺序推理和随机提示扰动实验分析画像一致性退化（状态空间漂移和语义不一致）。

Result: 现代生成架构能够以较高一致性实例化和维持评估性人格，实现多元视角依赖的基准测试。但顺序推理和随机提示扰动会导致画像一致性系统性退化，表现为状态空间漂移和语义不一致。

Conclusion: 静态对齐约束不足以维持评估行为的长期鲁棒性，需要嵌入动态的、可行性驱动的调控机制来保持连贯的认知仿真。将基于人格的评估建模为潜表征流形上的结构化动力系统，为更自适应、更对齐人类、更上下文敏感的AI评估奠定基础。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于人格（persona）的多元对齐评估框架，核心关注生成式AI的评测方法，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "AIGC 评测与基准"（aigc_evaluation_benchmark）高度匹配。同时涉及对齐（alignment）和评估多样性，但评测是主线。

Importance reason: Focuses on AI alignment evaluation, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous VLM, video generation, or model efficiency. Signals: AIGC evaluation, persona-based benchmarking, pluralistic alignment, state-space drift, not matching priority subtopics

Abstract: Current alignment paradigms for generative artificial intelligence rely predominantly on monolithic benchmarking frameworks that reduce the plurality of human judgment to aggregated statistical baselines, thereby obscuring cultural, demographic, and contextual variability in evaluation. We introduce a state-space constrained emulation framework for AI evaluation that replaces singular assessment functions with a structured manifold of synthetic cognitive profiles representing diverse human perspectives. We show that modern generative architectures can instantiate and maintain these evaluative personas with high consistency, enabling a form of pluralistic, perspective-dependent benchmarking that more closely reflects real-world consensus variability. However, we further analyze the stability of these simulated evaluators under sequential inference and stochastic prompt perturbations, revealing systematic degradation in persona coherence that manifests as state-space drift and semantic inconsistency. These findings suggest that static alignment constraints are insufficient for sustaining robust evaluative behavior over time. Instead, we argue for the necessity of embedding dynamic, viability-driven regulatory mechanisms within generative systems to preserve coherent cognitive emulation. By framing persona-based evaluation as a structured dynamical system over latent representation manifolds, this study provides a foundation for more adaptive, human-aligned, and context-sensitive approaches to AI evaluation.

</details>


### [99] [The Architecture of Errors: From Universal Impossibility to Patch-Local LLM Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30628)
*Mikhail L. Arbuzov, Lee Mosbacker, Sisong Bei, Ziwei Dong, Dmitri Kalaev, Alexey Shvets*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.LG, cs.CL

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AIGC 评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (68.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文从理论上证明通用LLM可靠性无法通过有限干预字典保证（无限失败模式），但在操作有界补丁（如法律审查、医疗RAG等）中，失败模式稀疏且重复，可靠性转化为局部目录发现与干预覆盖问题，所需干预预算随序列长度呈多对数增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM可靠性方法依赖有限干预字典，但理论上无法覆盖无限域中所有可区分的失败模式。作者希望从理论上阐明这一根本限制，并论证在操作有界场景下可靠性是可实现的。

Method: 提出两个命题和一个推论的形式化框架：命题1证明无限域中有限干预字典无法覆盖所有可区分失败模式；推论1给出模式发现的对数上界；命题2证明在操作有界补丁中，在log活跃模式暴露和头部重覆盖条件下，每次硬决策的干预预算随序列长度呈多对数增长，并在目录饱和后趋于常数。

Result: 理论结果表明：通用LLM可靠性在无限域中不可实现，但在操作有界补丁中可靠性是可实现的局部目录发现与覆盖问题；长上下文困难被重新定位为硬决策数量随任务长度增长的问题，而非指数级token长度问题。

Conclusion: 该框架将LLM可靠性问题从"通用不可行"重新定位为"局部可实现"，为构建可靠LLM系统提供了理论基础，并指出长上下文场景中硬决策数量增长时可靠性仍然困难，需要进一步研究。

Classification reason: 论文从理论上论证了通用LLM可靠性无法通过有限干预字典保证，但提出了在操作有界补丁（如法律审查、医疗RAG、代码修复等）中可靠性是可实现的局部问题。这属于AIGC生成内容可靠性评测与基准的理论框架，与AIGC评测与基准（aigc_evaluation_benchmark）方向高度相关。

Importance reason: Theoretical LLM reliability analysis with no empirical results, far from user's priority on generative models and multimodal systems. Signals: Theoretical impossibility proof, patch-local reliability, no empirical validation, no generative model focus, no multimodal or video/image generation

Abstract: Universal LLM reliability is not a finite-library problem: across all possible tasks, tools, schemas, knowledge sources, and evaluator expectations, new intervention-distinguishable failure modes can appear without bound, so no finite intervention dictionary can guarantee bounded residual error for every such mode. But deployed systems do not operate over the whole universe. They operate inside operationally bounded patches (legal review, medical RAG, code repair, customer-support agents, contract extraction) with recurring tasks, schemas, tools, and evaluator expectations. Within such patches, empirical evidence suggests failures are sparse, repetitive, and concentrated in a small recurring catalogue, so reliability becomes a local catalogue-discovery and intervention-coverage problem rather than an exponential token-length problem. We formalize this transition with two propositions and one corollary. Proposition 1 is the worst-case-mode-wise negative result: no finite intervention dictionary covers every distinguishable failure mode of an unbounded domain. Corollary 1 is the inverse-discovery implication: the logarithmic upper bound on mode discovery cannot accommodate linearly more distinct tail modes without exponentially more observed hard-failure events. Proposition 2 is the positive patch-local result: under log active-mode exposure and head-heavy coverage, a sufficient per-hard-decision intervention budget grows polylogarithmically in sequence length and becomes domain-constant once the patch catalogue saturates. The framework relocates rather than dissolves long-context difficulty: where the number of hard decisions itself grows with task length, reliability remains hard; the contribution is to identify the on-axis intervention rather than to make those regimes easy.

</details>


### [100] [LLM-FACETS: A Privacy-Preserving Framework for Evaluating LLM Transparency and Accountability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31167)
*Tom Lucas, Alessio Buscemi, Alfredo Capozucca, German Castignani, Barbara Delacroix*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AIGC 评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (68.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: LLM-FACETS 是一个面向非技术从业者的开源 LLM 审计框架，提供浏览器界面和插件架构，支持确定性指标本地运行和 LLM-judge 指标可控调用，通过 token 级 log-probability 可视化、多裁判共识和 RAG Triad 指标实现透明度与可复现性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 审计工具需要编程技能和复杂环境配置，且云平台会将评估数据发送到外部服务，导致领域专家和合规官员难以进行 AI 监督。需要一种无需编程、保护隐私、可复现的 LLM 评估框架。

Method: 提出 LLM-FACETS 框架，包含浏览器可访问界面和插件架构。架构围绕三类从业者（技术专家、领域专家、合规官员）设计，对应 EU AI Act 和 NIST AI Risk Management Framework 的利益相关者分类。数据流明确：确定性指标（BLEU、ROUGE、BERTScore）在自托管服务器本地运行；LLM-judge 指标显式调用外部 API 且用户保留凭据控制权。通过 token 级 log-probability 可视化、多裁判共识和 RAG Triad 指标（Faithfulness、Answer Relevance、Context Relevance）实现透明度。

Result: 通过 18 个指标实现与规范参考库的交叉验证，验证了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: LLM-FACETS 为 LLM 审计提供了可访问、隐私保护、可复现的解决方案，将 AI 问责制与系统构建团队解耦，支持跨检查多种指标以确保评估可靠性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 LLM-FACETS 框架，用于评估 LLM 输出的事实性、校准性和可复现性，属于 AIGC 生成内容质量评测范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "AIGC 评测与基准" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM auditing and transparency, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks alignment with user's core interests in generation and multimodal models. Signals: LLM auditing, privacy-preserving, non-technical practitioners, EU AI Act, RAG Triad metrics

Abstract: Assessing whether Large Language Models outputs are factually grounded, epistemically calibrated, and methodologically reproducible is a prerequisite for responsible AI deployment. Yet auditing LLMs remains inaccessible to non-technical practitioners: existing tools require programming expertise and non-trivial environment setup, and cloud-hosted platforms transmit evaluation data to external services, creating barriers for domain experts and compliance officers legally responsible for AI oversight. We introduce LLM-FACETS (LLM FActuality Cross-EvaluaTion System): an open-source framework with a browser-accessible interface and a plugin architecture, structured around three practitioner profiles (technical experts, domain experts, compliance officers) that mirror the stakeholder categories identified in the EU AI Act and the NIST AI Risk Management Framework. The architecture makes data flows explicit: deterministic metrics (BLEU, ROUGE, BERTScore) run entirely within the self-hosted server with no outbound transmission; LLM-judge metrics contact external APIs explicitly, with users retaining full credential control. The framework operationalizes transparency through three mechanisms: token-level log-probability visualization for epistemic uncertainty, multi-judge consensus to mitigate judge bias, and RAG Triad metrics (Faithfulness, Answer Relevance, Context Relevance) to detect and localize hallucinations. A plugin architecture allows any new metric or dataset to be integrated without modifying the evaluation pipeline. The open-source implementation enables cross-checking across multiple metrics targeting the same property, ensuring reproducibility and decoupling AI accountability from the teams building the systems assessed. We verify the framework through cross-validation of 18 metric implementations against canonical reference libraries.

</details>


### [101] [Industrializing Prediction-Powered Inference: The GLIDE Library for Reliable GenAI and Agentic Systems Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31278)
*Grégoire Martinon, Ibrahim Merad, Mohammed Raki*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.LG, stat.ME

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AIGC 评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (41.51) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 GLIDE，一个开源的 Python 库，统一了多种预测驱动推断（PPI）方法，用于对生成式 AI 和智能体系统进行无偏估计和有效置信区间评估，显著减少人工标注成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前智能体系统的可靠评估面临两难：人工标注成本高昂，而 LLM-as-judge 代理存在偏差。预测驱动推断（PPI）虽能结合两者优势，但其多种方法分散在不同论文中且实现不完整，缺乏统一易用的工具库。

Method: GLIDE 库统一了多种 PPI 估计器（PPI++、分层 PPI、Predict-Then-Debias 及其分层变体、主动统计推断）和采样器（均匀、分层、主动、成本最优），采用 scipy 风格 API 专门针对均值估计。库包含可复现的蒙特卡洛验证套件、基于经验的方法选择决策树，以及智能体评估案例研究。

Result: 在智能体评估案例研究中，GLIDE 在同等精度下实现了显著的人工标注节省。库提供了可复现的验证套件和决策树，帮助用户选择最优 PPI 方法。

Conclusion: GLIDE 为生成式 AI 和智能体系统的可靠评估提供了统一、易用的工具，降低了 PPI 方法的使用门槛，有望推动更严谨的评估实践。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 GLIDE 库，统一了预测驱动推断（PPI）方法用于可靠评估生成式 AI 与智能体系统，核心贡献在于评估方法论与工具，属于 AIGC 评测与基准方向，因此复用 canonical 子主题 aigc_evaluation_benchmark。

Importance reason: Focuses on evaluation methodology for GenAI/agents, not on core generation or multimodal modeling priorities. Signals: PPI library, evaluation cost reduction, not aligned with priority subtopics, no autoregressive/diffusion/video/image generation, no continuous VLM or world model

Abstract: Reliable evaluation of agentic systems requires unbiased estimates with valid uncertainty, but standard practice navigates between costly human annotation and biased LLM-as-judge proxies. Prediction-powered inference (PPI) combines both into debiased estimates with valid confidence intervals, yet its various methods remain scattered across papers under partial implementations. We introduce GLIDE, an open-source Python library that unifies state-of-the-art PPI estimators (PPI++, Stratified PPI, Predict-Then-Debias and its stratified variants, Active Statistical Inference) and samplers (uniform, stratified, active, cost-optimal) under a scipy-style API specialized to mean estimation. GLIDE ships with a reproducible Monte Carlo validation suite, an empirically grounded decision tree for method selection, and an agentic evaluation case study showing substantial annotation savings at equivalent precision. The GLIDE package is available at this URL:

</details>


### [102] [Anchoring LLM Gender Bias to Human Baselines: A Cross-Lingual Audit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30804)
*Jiwoo Choi, Seonwoo Ahn, Tongxin Zhang, Seohyon Jung*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AIGC 评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (41.51) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文对六种LLM（Claude、GPT、Gemini、DeepSeek、Syn-Pro、HyperCLOVA X）在英语、韩语、中文和日语四种语言中进行性别刻板印象审计。使用HEXACO-100人格量表，将模型输出锚定到覆盖48个国家的跨文化人类基线数据上，发现模型性别刻板印象的波动范围约为人类跨国差异的2.5倍，且跨语言效应会叠加。作者提出了四模式框架（一致、抑制、重组、放大）来刻画模型行为，并发现翻译不仅改变刻板印象强度，还会改变与之关联的属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM偏见研究通常只检测模型是否存在偏见，但缺乏与真实人类基线的对比，也忽略了跨语言部署场景下偏见如何漂移的问题。作者希望回答：模型性别归因相对于其部署人群的真实基线偏离了多远？

Method: 采用HEXACO-100人格量表作为评估工具，将六种LLM在四种语言下的输出锚定到覆盖48个国家的跨文化人类数据集上。引入四模式框架（concordance一致、suppression抑制、reorganization重组、amplification放大）对24个（模型×语言）单元格进行分类。进行项目级分析以揭示翻译对刻板印象属性的影响。

Result: 模型性别刻板印象的波动范围约为人类跨国差异的2.5倍；跨语言效应会叠加，一个英语中心模型在韩语提示下达到当地基线的5倍；翻译不仅改变刻板印象强度，还会改变与之关联的属性，导致表面校准良好但实质内容发生显著重组。

Conclusion: 没有任何单一的偏见消除管线能够均匀地解决跨语言边界的偏见问题。模型在跨语言部署中的性别刻板印象问题比表面观察到的更为复杂，需要针对不同语言和文化背景的差异化处理策略。

Classification reason: 该论文对多个LLM进行跨语言的性别刻板印象审计，属于AIGC内容质量与公平性评测范畴，与[AIGC 评测与基准]（aigc_evaluation_benchmark）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM gender bias auditing, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: LLM gender bias, cross-lingual audit, HEXACO-100, four-pattern framework, not aligned with user's direction weights

Abstract: We audit six large language models (LLMs) for gender stereotyping across English, Korean, Chinese, and Japanese. Three were developed primarily for English-language use (Claude, GPT, Gemini) and three for East Asian use (DeepSeek, Syn-Pro, HyperCLOVA X). We adopt the HEXACO-100 personality inventory and anchor each model against a cross-cultural human dataset spanning 48 countries to ask not whether LLMs are biased, but how far their gender attributions drift from the populations they are deployed among. Our findings show that their stereotyping spans a range roughly 2.5 times wider than the entire cross-country range found in humans, and the effect can compound across languages. One English-centric model, prompted in Korean, reached 5 times the local baseline, even when the prompt stated the candidate had already been hired, which often dampens human stereotyping. To characterize such behaviors without ranking them, we introduce a four-pattern framework -- concordance, suppression, reorganization, and amplification -- across 24 (model x language) cells. Item-level analysis reveals that translation does not just rescale stereotypes, but changes the attributes tied to it, hiding significant rearrangement under the surface while appearing well-calibrated. Our results ultimately suggest that no single debiasing pipeline is likely to address bias evenly across linguistic boundaries.

</details>


### [103] [Counterfactual Evaluation Reveals Hidden Capability Profiles in Clinical LLMs and Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30590)
*Matt Turk*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.LG, cs.CL

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AIGC 评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (38.16) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出因果敏感性评分（CSS），一种基于反事实干预的临床LLM评测指标，通过对肿瘤病例沿五个临床维度进行因果扰动，评估模型是否根据输入变化正确更新推荐。在六个前沿模型上的实验表明，CSS与传统的覆盖度指标（CMS）排名几乎相反，并揭示了所有模型在外科手术状态干预上的安全盲点。该指标还可迁移至工具使用智能体场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有临床AI系统评测主要依赖覆盖度指标（如CMS），但两个得分相近的系统可能在输入变化时表现出截然不同的行为——一个能根据新临床信号更新推荐，另一个则输出不变。现有指标无法捕捉这种响应能力差异，因此需要一种能评估模型因果响应能力的评测方法。

Method: 提出因果敏感性评分（CSS），一种预注册的干预式评测指标。对肿瘤多学科团队（tumor-board）病例沿五个临床维度（生物标志物翻转、既往治疗失败、生物标志物移除、手术状态变化、分期扰动）进行因果突变，然后评估模型是否在预注册的正确方向上更新其推荐，评分采用{0, 0.5, 1.0}三级制。在六个前沿模型上进行单次推理测试（224个病例），并与覆盖度指标CMS进行对比。此外，在ReAct风格的工具使用智能体实验中验证了CSS的迁移性。

Result: 六个模型在CSS和CMS上的排名几乎完全相反：所有模型排名均发生变化，CMS最差的模型成为CSS最佳，而一个CMS中上排名的模型在CSS上排名垫底。所有前沿模型在外科手术状态干预上均存在安全盲点（CSS最高仅17.2%）。工具使用提升了五个模型的CSS（+2.5到+20.3个百分点），但CSS最低的模型即使检索了相同图表仍无法更新推荐，暴露出结构性响应缺陷。

Conclusion: 干预式预注册指标（如CSS）能够补充覆盖度指标在临床AI智能体评测中的不足，捕捉覆盖度指标无法发现的响应能力差异，并为未来的智能体强化学习系统提供候选的密集奖励信号。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一种新的因果敏感性评分（CSS）来评估临床LLM和智能体的响应能力，属于AIGC评测与基准（aigc_evaluation_benchmark）这一规范主题。同时，论文涉及临床AI智能体的评估，也与AI智能体与生成工作流（aigc_agent_workflow）相关，但核心贡献是评测方法，因此主方向为AIGC评测。

Importance reason: Focuses on clinical LLM evaluation, not aligned with user's priority subtopics in multimodal generation or video/image generation. Signals: Clinical LLM evaluation, counterfactual metric, safety blind spot, not multimodal generation, not autoregressive video/image

Abstract: Two clinical AI systems can score nearly identically on coverage-based rubrics yet behave radically differently when their patient inputs change: one updates its recommendations to match the new clinical signal, while the other produces the same output regardless. We introduce the Causal Sensitivity Score (CSS), a pre-registered interventional metric that mutates oncology tumor-board cases along five clinically meaningful dimensions - biomarker flips, prior-treatment failures, biomarker removals, surgery-status changes, and stage perturbations - and scores whether each model updates its recommendations in the pre-registered correct direction using a {0, 0.5, 1.0} scale. Benchmarked against the Consensus Match Score (CMS), a coverage-based weighted recall metric, six frontier models from three labs evaluated in single-shot inference across 224 cases rank in nearly opposite orders: all six models change rank, the CMS-worst model becomes CSS-best, and one upper-mid CMS model ranks last on CSS. We further surface a universal safety blind spot: every frontier model fails on surgery-status interventions (at most 17.2% CSS on Family D), a finding CMS does not expose. The metric also transfers to tool-using agents: in a ReAct-style experiment, tool use improves CSS for five of six models (+2.5 to +20.3 percentage points), yet the lowest-CSS model retrieves the same chart sections and still fails to update its recommendations - revealing a structural responsiveness deficit visible only under counterfactual evaluation. Cross-judge replication and three-rater medical-professional validation confirm the aggregate findings. Interventional pre-registered metrics like CSS complement coverage-based evaluation for clinical AI agents: they capture responsiveness that coverage metrics miss and offer a candidate dense reward signal for future agentic RL systems.

</details>


### [104] [CodeGolf Bench: A Multi-Language Benchmark for Evaluating Concise Code Generation Capabilities of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30394)
*Vedant Padwal*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.SE

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AIGC 评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (37.4) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 CodeGolf Bench，一个基于代码高尔夫（code golf）的多语言基准，用于评估 LLM 在 60 种编程语言中生成简洁高效代码的能力。实验表明推理模型显著优于非推理模型，尤其在 C++ 中差距更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码生成基准受限于固定问题集和有限的语言覆盖，且缺乏对代码简洁性（而非仅功能性）的评估。代码高尔夫作为一种追求最短字符/字节解法的编程竞赛，为衡量 LLM 生成高效简洁代码的能力提供了独特视角。

Method: 基于 Code Golf Stack Exchange 平台构建动态基准，支持 60 种编程语言，提供实时人类表现基线。在 Python 和 C++ 任务上评估了 9 个 LLM（包括推理模型如 o1、o3-mini 和非推理模型如 GPT-4o、Claude 3.5 Sonnet 等），使用平均百分位排名作为评估指标。

Result: 推理模型在 Python 和 C++ 任务上均显著优于非推理模型，最佳平均百分位达 70.97%。非推理模型在效率优化方面表现较弱，最佳百分位远低于推理模型。C++ 中推理模型优势更明显，表明推理能力对语法严格的语言尤为重要。

Conclusion: CodeGolf Bench 提供了一个动态框架，用于评估 LLM 代码生成能力并跟踪其与人类表现的差距。推理模型在生成简洁代码方面具有明显优势，该基准可作为衡量 LLM 代码生成能力的新维度。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 CodeGolf Bench，一个评估 LLM 在 60 种编程语言中生成简洁代码能力的基准，属于 AIGC 领域中的代码生成评测，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "AIGC 评测与基准" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on code golf and LLM code generation, which is outside the user's core interests in multimodal generation, video generation, and world models. Signals: code golf benchmark, multi-language LLM evaluation, not multimodal, no video/image generation, no world model

Abstract: This paper introduces Code Bench, a benchmark capable of evaluating Large Language Models (LLMs) concise code generation abilities in 60 programming languages. Based on code golf, a recreational programming competition focused on minimal character or byte solutions, the benchmark provides a distinctive measure of LLMs ability to produce efficient, concise code. Unlike existing benchmarks limited by fixed problem sets and language coverage, CodeGolf Bench leverages the platform to provide new problems and live human performance baselines. Evaluation of nine LLMs on Python and C++ tasks demonstrates that reasoning models significantly outperform non-reasoning models, achieving best average percentile of 70.97%. This performance gap is particularly pronounced in C++, highlighting reasoning's importance for languages with strict syntax requirements. Non-reasoning models struggle more with efficiency optimization across both languages, with best percentiles significantly lower than reasoning counterparts. CodeGolf Bench offers a dynamic framework for evaluating LLM code generation capabilities against evolving human performance on code golf.

</details>


### [105] [Beyond Static Dialogues: Benchmarking Realistic, Heterogeneous, and Evolving Long-Term Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31086)
*Han Zhang, Zihao Tang, Xin Yu, Xiao Liu, Yeyun Gong, Haizhen Huang, Yan Lu, Weiwei Deng, Feng Sun, Qi Zhang, Hanfang Yang*

Main category: cs.IR, cs.CL

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AIGC 评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (43.44) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 RHELM，一个面向 LLM 长期记忆的现实、异构、动态演进的评测基准。通过精心设计的用户画像和 LOOP 模块，构建具有长期语义一致性和时间演进的多样化对话，并整合异构外部数据源。基准涵盖 7 类查询和 27 种关键记忆特征，实验表明现有全上下文模型、RAG 方法和记忆框架在复杂真实场景中仍存在显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 记忆评测基准存在对话缺乏长期语义一致性、用户画像扁平静态、无法模拟真实世界中文档/邮件等异构数据流等问题，严重限制了评测的现实性和有效性。

Method: 提出 RHELM 基准，包含：(1) 精心设计的用户画像；(2) LOOP（pLan-rOllout-evOlve-Prune）模块用于构建具有时间演化和长期一致性的真实对话；(3) 将对话与用户时间事件轨迹同步的异构外部数据源深度融合；(4) 涵盖 7 类查询类型和 27 种关键记忆特征的问答对。

Result: 在多种方法（全上下文模型、RAG 方法、代表性记忆框架）上的实验表明，现有方法在复杂真实场景中仍存在关键弱点，特别是在多源聚合和真实世界上下文推理方面表现不佳。

Conclusion: RHELM 揭示了当前 LLM 长期记忆方法在现实复杂场景中的不足，为未来研究提供了更具挑战性和现实意义的评测基准。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个面向 LLM 长期记忆的评测基准 RHELM，属于 AIGC 方向下的评测与基准范畴，与 canonical 子主题 aigc_evaluation_benchmark（AIGC 评测与基准）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM long-term memory benchmarks, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous VLM, video generation, or model efficiency. Signals: LLM memory benchmark, realistic heterogeneous evolving, not generative, not multimodal, no autoregressive or diffusion focus

Abstract: In existing memory benchmarks for Large Language Models (LLMs), the evaluated dialogue sessions often lack long-term semantic consistency, and the underlying personas tend to be flat and static. Furthermore, in real-world scenarios, interactions between users and assistants involve more diverse, heterogeneous data streams, such as documents and emails. These shortcomings significantly limit the realism and effectiveness of current evaluations. To address these limitations, we introduce RHELM (Realistic, Heterogeneous, and Evolving Long-term Memory). Driven by meticulously crafted user profiles and a novel LOOP (pLan-rOllout-evOlve-Prune) module, we construct realistic dialogues across diverse interaction scenarios that exhibit dynamic temporal evolution and long-term coherence. Crucially, these dialogues are deeply integrated with heterogeneous external sources synchronized with the user's temporal event trajectory. The resulting benchmark encompasses challenging question-answer pairs spanning seven inquiry types, with each question mapping to at least one of 27 critical memory characteristics that we identify as essential yet underexplored in current research. Comprehensive experiments across full-context models, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) methods, and representative memory frameworks reveal that contemporary approaches still expose critical weaknesses in complex, real-world settings, particularly in resolving multi-source aggregation and real-world contextual reasoning.

</details>


### [106] [KnowledgeGain: Evaluating and Optimizing Science News Generation for Reader Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31099)
*Dominik Soós, Meng Jiang, Jian Wu*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CL

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AIGC 评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (41.51) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 KnowledgeGain，一个衡量科学新闻生成文本对读者知识获取效果的评估指标，并通过 LLM 模拟读者进行文章筛选，实验表明该方法能显著提升读者阅读后的知识获取水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成文本评估指标（如语义相似度、事实一致性）无法衡量读者从科学新闻中实际学到的知识量，缺乏面向知识获取的生成质量评估方法。

Method: 提出 KnowledgeGain 指标，通过对比读者阅读前后的知识水平差异来评估科学新闻质量。首先进行受控人类实验验证指标有效性，然后基于实验数据校准一个仅用提示的 LLM 读者模拟器，用于对候选文章进行排序和筛选，最后通过第二次人类实验验证模拟器筛选的文章能提升读者知识获取。

Result: 人类实验表明 KnowledgeGain 指标能有效捕捉读者阅读不同类型科学媒体后的知识差异；LLM 读者模拟器筛选的文章相比强生成基线，显著提升了读者阅读后的准确率和归一化 KnowledgeGain。

Conclusion: KnowledgeGain 为科学新闻生成提供了一种面向读者知识获取的评估新范式，结合 LLM 模拟器可优化生成内容，使其更好地满足 Bloom 认知分类学中的知识与理解目标。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 KnowledgeGain 指标，用于评估科学新闻生成内容对读者知识获取的效果，属于生成内容质量评测范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "AIGC 评测与基准" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on science news evaluation for reader learning, not on the user's priority subtopics like multimodal generation or autoregressive video. Signals: AIGC evaluation, science news generation, reader knowledge gain, LLM reader simulator, not multimodal generation

Abstract: Science news is an important medium to communicate discoveries between the research communities and the public. Yet, most metrics for generated or summarized text evaluate semantic similarity and factual consistency, but do not measure how much knowledge readers learn from the news. We introduce KnowledgeGain, a metric that evaluates the quality of science news by measuring how much knowledge readers gained after reading it. To evaluate the metric, we first performed a controlled human study and showed that the metric successfully captures the differential knowledge gained by human readers reading different types of science media. The data allowed us to calibrate a prompt-only LLM reader simulator. We use it to rank and filter candidate articles before human evaluation. A second human study shows that articles selected with this simulator improve post-reading accuracy and normalized KnowledgeGain over a strong generation baseline. Our work is a step toward generating science news that better meets the knowledge and comprehension goals of Bloom's Taxonomy.

</details>


### [107] [TSM-Bench: Detecting LLM-Generated Text in Real-World Wikipedia Editing Practices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31113)
*Gerrit Quaremba, Elizabeth Black, Denny Vrandečić, Elena Simperl*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AIGC 评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (46.59) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 TSM-Bench，一个多语言、多生成器、多任务的基准，用于评估 AI 生成文本检测器在真实维基百科编辑场景中的表现。实验表明，现有 SOTA 检测器在任务特定（如摘要、改写）的 MGT 上准确率下降 10-40%，且存在泛化不对称性：在任务特定数据上微调可泛化到通用数据，反之则不行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AI 生成文本检测基准主要关注通用文本生成任务（如"写一篇关于机器学习的文章"），但实际用户（如维基百科编辑）通常使用 LLM 执行特定写作任务（如摘要、改写），这些任务特定 MGT 更接近人类写作风格，现有检测器难以识别。作者希望构建一个更贴近真实场景的基准来评估检测器的实际能力。

Method: 构建 TSM-Bench 基准，包含多语言（英语、德语、法语、西班牙语、中文）、多生成器（GPT-4o、Claude 3.5 Sonnet、Gemini 1.5 Pro、Llama 3.1 70B）、多任务（摘要、改写、事实核查、风格转换、通用写作）的维基百科编辑场景数据集。使用多种 SOTA 检测器（包括统计方法、微调模型、零样本分类器）进行系统评估，分析任务特定 vs 通用 MGT 的检测差异及泛化特性。

Result: 1）任务特定 MGT 检测准确率相比通用 MGT 下降 10-40%；2）存在泛化不对称性：在任务特定数据上微调可泛化到通用数据（甚至跨领域），但反之不成立；3）仅在通用 MGT 上微调的模型会过拟合到机器生成的表面伪影；4）大多数检测器在真实维基百科场景中仍不可靠。

Conclusion: 现有 AI 生成文本检测器在真实世界的维基百科编辑场景中表现不佳，任务特定 MGT 的检测是一个被忽视但至关重要的问题。TSM-Bench 为开发更鲁棒的检测器提供了关键基础，未来检测器需要针对任务特定场景进行专门训练和评估。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 TSM-Bench，一个用于评估 AI 生成文本检测器在真实维基百科编辑场景中表现的多语言、多生成器、多任务基准。这属于 AIGC 检测与评测交叉领域，但核心贡献是评测基准，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 的 aigc_evaluation_benchmark（AIGC 评测与基准）。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM-generated text detection in Wikipedia, which is outside the user's core interests in multimodal generation and autoregressive models. Signals: MGT detection benchmark, real-world editing tasks, generalization asymmetry, not multimodal, no video/image generation

Abstract: Automatically detecting machine-generated text (MGT) is critical to maintaining the knowledge integrity of user-generated content (UGC) platforms such as Wikipedia. Existing detection benchmarks primarily focus on \textit{generic} text generation tasks (e.g., ``Write an article about machine learning.''). However, editors frequently employ LLMs for specific writing tasks (e.g., summarisation). These \textit{task-specific} MGT instances tend to resemble human-written text more closely due to their constrained task formulation and contextual conditioning. In this work, we show that a range of SOTA MGT detectors struggle to identify task-specific MGT reflecting real-world editing on Wikipedia. We introduce \textsc{TSM-Bench}, a multilingual, multi-generator, and \textit{multi-task} benchmark for evaluating MGT detectors on common, real-world Wikipedia editing tasks. Our findings demonstrate that (\textit{i}) average detection accuracy drops by 10--40\% compared to prior benchmarks, and (\textit{ii}) a generalisation asymmetry exists: fine-tuning on task-specific data enables generalisation to generic data -- even across domains -- but not vice versa. We demonstrate that models fine-tuned exclusively on generic MGT overfit to superficial artefacts of machine generation. Our results suggest that, in contrast to prior benchmarks, most detectors remain unreliable for automated detection in real-world contexts such as UGC platforms. \textsc{TSM-Bench} therefore provides a critical foundation for developing and evaluating future models.

</details>


### [108] [Welfare, Improvability, and Variance: A Principal-Agent Approach to Optimal Benchmark Item Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30916)
*Andreas Haupt, Justin Hartenstein, Anka Reuel, Mykel Kochenderfer, Sanmi Koyejo*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.GT, econ.TH

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AIGC 评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (68.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文将基准测试建模为多任务主-从博弈，证明基准的社会福利损失由三个项目级原语决定：与规范福利优先级的一致性、边际可改进性和性能方差。基于此理论提出审计框架，在 OLMES 基准上识别出帕累托劣势的测试项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AI 基准测试存在污染、饱和和构造不明确等问题，但聚合方法（通常简单平均所有测试项分数）受到的关注很少。本文旨在从理论上分析基准测试聚合的福利损失，并提供识别低质量测试项的方法。

Method: 将基准测试建模为多任务主-从博弈（principal-agent game），推导出福利损失由三个项目级原语决定：与规范福利优先级的对齐度、边际可改进性和性能方差。将此理论转化为审计框架，对每个测试项沿这三个维度进行排序，并在 OLMES 基准上使用 WORKBank（福利）、EvoLM 4B（可改进性）和 PolyPythias 410M（方差）进行实证分析。

Result: 在 OLMES 基准中识别出在亲工作者福利操作化下帕累托劣势的测试项，展示了该框架能够有效筛选出对整体基准质量贡献低甚至有害的测试项。

Conclusion: 基准测试的简单平均聚合方式存在根本性问题，本文提出的主-从博弈框架为理解和改进基准测试聚合提供了理论基础和实用工具，有助于构建更高质量的 AI 评测基准。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 AI 基准测试的聚合方法，提出基于主-从博弈的框架来评估 benchmark 中每个测试项的社会福利、可改进性和方差，属于 AIGC 评测与基准方向，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic 'AIGC 评测与基准' 高度匹配。

Importance reason: This paper focuses on benchmark aggregation theory for AI evaluation, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to the user's core interests in generative models and multimodal systems. Signals: Principal-agent benchmark model, Welfare loss decomposition, OLMES audit framework, No generative model focus, No multimodal or video/image generation content

Abstract: AI benchmarks have well-documented limitations, with prior work examining contamination, saturation, and construct underspecification. Aggregation has received far less attention: benchmarks are typically summarized by uniformly averaging item-level scores, implicitly treating every test item as equally valuable. We model benchmarking as a multitask principal-agent game and show that the welfare loss from a benchmark is determined jointly by three item-level primitives: alignment with normative welfare priorities, marginal improvability, and performance variance. We translate the theory into an audit framework that ranks items along each of these three axes, and apply it to OLMES items using WORKBank for welfare, the EvoLM 4B suite for improvability, and the PolyPythias 410M panel for variance. The framework surfaces items that are Pareto-inferior within OLMES subject to a pro-worker welfare operationalization. All code is available at .

</details>


### [109] [Preference-Aware Rubric Learning for Personalized Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31545)
*Yilun Qiu, Xiaoyan Zhao, Yang Zhang, Yuxin Chen, Cilin Yan, Jiayin Cai, Xiaolong Jiang, Yao Hu, Yoko Yamakata, Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AIGC 评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (70.64) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出PARL框架，将个性化评估建模为学习问题，通过从用户历史交互中学习偏好感知的评估准则（rubric），并引入自验证机制和对比强化学习目标，实现对个性化文本生成的高保真评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方法（自动指标、LLM-as-a-judge）无法捕捉用户长期交互历史中蕴含的主观、个性化的偏好，缺乏对个性化对齐效果的有效评估手段。

Method: 提出"个性化评估即学习"范式，将评估建模为学习问题。PARL框架包含两个核心模块：(1) 准则归纳（Rubric Induction），从原始用户历史中学习偏好感知的评估准则；(2) 自验证机制（Self-Validation），确保准则与用户偏好一致。同时引入判别式强化学习目标，对比用户撰写的回复与个性化模型输出，学习精确的用户特定决策边界。

Result: 在真实个性化文本生成任务上，PARL能够持续归纳出高保真度的评估准则，可靠地识别与用户对齐的回复，并在不同用户和任务间具有良好的泛化能力，同时捕捉稳定的风格偏好和细粒度评估模式。

Conclusion: 将个性化评估作为学习问题而非静态判断，能够更有效地捕捉用户特定偏好，为个性化对齐评估提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究个性化评估方法，属于AIGC评测范畴，与[AIGC 评测与基准]（aigc_evaluation_benchmark）高度匹配。同时涉及个性化对齐，也与多模态理解与生成方向下的对齐相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on personalized evaluation for text generation, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to the user's core interests in multimodal generation and autoregressive video/image generation. Signals: AIGC evaluation, personalized alignment, rubric learning, text generation, no multimodal or generation focus

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) evolve from general-purpose assistants to user-centric agents, personalization has become central to aligning model behavior with individual preferences, making the evaluation of personalized alignment a critical bottleneck. Existing evaluation methods-ranging from automatic metrics to LLM-as-a-judge approaches-fail to capture subjective, user-specific preferences embedded in long-term interaction histories. We identify three essential principles for reliable and effective personalized evaluation: Representativeness, User-Consistency, and Discriminativeness. To address these principles, we introduce Personalized Evaluation as Learning, a paradigm that formulates personalized evaluation as a learning problem rather than a static judgment. Under this paradigm, we propose PARL (Preference-Aware Rubric Learning for Personalized Evaluation), a framework that learns to induce preference-aware evaluation rubrics directly from raw user histories and performs a self-validation mechanism to ensure consistency with the user's preferences. PARL integrates rubric induction with a discriminative reinforcement learning objective that contrasts user-authored responses against competitive personalized model outputs, enabling the learned rubrics to capture precise, user-specific decision boundaries. Experiments on real-world personalized text generation tasks show that PARL consistently induces high-fidelity rubrics that reliably identify user-aligned responses and generalize across users and tasks, while capturing stable stylistic preferences and fine-grained evaluative patterns. To ensure reproducibility, our code is available at .

</details>


### [110] [BlueFin: Benchmarking LLM Agents on Financial Spreadsheets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30907)
*Srivatsa Kundurthy, Clara Na, Colton Moraine, Anoushka Mohta, Case Winter, George Fang, John Ling, Emma Strubell, Zach Kirshner*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI, cs.LG, cs.SE

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AIGC 评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (35.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: BlueFin 是一个面向金融电子表格场景的 LLM 智能体评测基准，包含 131 个复杂任务和 3,225 条细粒度评分标准，由专家人工标注验证 LM 评判器，前沿模型平均得分不足 50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子表格软件拥有数亿用户，远超专业开发者数量，但 LLM 在电子表格领域的能力探索不足，尤其是面向金融专业场景的复杂任务评测资源匮乏。

Method: 构建包含 131 个金融电子表格任务的基准数据集，涵盖数据合成、操作和理解三类任务；设计 3,225 条细粒度评分标准，由专家人工标注验证；使用 LM 评判器进行评估，并与专家共识进行一致性验证（α=0.826）。

Result: LM 评判器与专家共识达到高度一致（α=0.826，macro-F1=0.839）；前沿 LLM 平均得分低于 50%，在动态正确性方面表现尤为薄弱。

Conclusion: BlueFin 填补了金融电子表格领域 LLM 智能体评测的空白，揭示了当前模型在该专业场景下的显著不足，为后续研究提供了高质量基准和开源评估框架。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个针对金融电子表格场景的 LLM 智能体评测基准 BlueFin，属于 AIGC 评测与基准（aigc_evaluation_benchmark）这一规范主题。同时，该基准涉及 LLM 智能体在专业领域的应用，也与多模态理解与生成方向下的 AI 智能体与生成工作流（aigc_agent_workflow）相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on financial spreadsheets, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and has negative keyword overlap. Signals: Financial domain, spreadsheet benchmark, LLM agents, low model performance, expert-validated rubric

Abstract: We present BlueFin, a benchmark that tasks large language model (LLM) agents with synthesis, manipulation, and comprehension tasks over spreadsheet workbooks in the professional finance domain. Though estimates of the global population of paying users of spreadsheet software range in the hundreds of millions -- an order of magnitude more than the estimated global population of professional developers -- comparatively fewer resources have been devoted to exploring and expanding LLM capabilities in the spreadsheet domain, with fewer still dedicated to mirroring real occupational tasks encountered by those in professional finance roles. In response, we curate a set of 131 challenging, complex tasks with real-world relevance in the domain, containing 3,225 granular rubric criteria; notably, our rubric criteria and LM judge evaluations are validated by a team of expert human annotators, resulting in high-quality, granular evaluations of complex tasks that are difficult to verify programmatically but can be reliably evaluated by an LM judge agent. Our judge achieves parity with expert consensus ($\alpha=0.826$) with a macro-F1 score of 0.839. Frontier LLMs demonstrate poor performance on the challenging benchmark, with the strongest LLMs achieving less than 50\% average scores across tasks -- models exhibit particular weaknesses in dynamic correctness. Our contributions include a dataset of examples across three categories of spreadsheet tasks, an open source harness and agentic evaluation framework, and a characterization of existing frontier models' performance on our benchmark.

</details>

### [111] [Personalized to Persuade: The Effects of Contextualization and Warmth on Trust and Reliance in Conversational AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31275)
*Mert Yazan, Suzan Verberne, Frederik Bungaran Ishak Situmeang*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.HC

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 个性化与对话生成

Importance: 普通相关 (38.26) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文通过2×2被试间实验（N=380）研究AI助手的个性化（contextualization）与对话温暖感（warmth）如何影响用户对AI的信任和依赖。结果表明：个性化降低了AI的说服力，但与温暖感结合后通过交叉交互恢复说服力；用户对AI的依赖普遍存在且不受对话设计影响；AI素养高的用户虽信任度更低，却更易被说服和依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化（contextualization）已被证明在政治和营销中具有说服效果，但在日常任务中用户缺乏先验知识时，个性化的说服效应尚不明确。本文旨在探究个性化与对话温暖感如何共同影响用户对AI助手的信任和依赖。

Method: 采用2×2被试间实验设计（N=380），操纵两个因素：contextualization（有/无）和warmth（温暖/中性），让AI助手针对专家建议提出相反论点，测量用户的说服程度、依赖程度和信任水平。

Result: 个性化降低了AI的说服力，但与温暖感结合后通过交叉交互恢复说服力；用户对AI的依赖在所有条件下普遍存在且不受对话设计影响；信任是说服和依赖的强预测因子，但对话设计不通过信任起作用；AI素养高的用户报告更低信任，却更易被说服和依赖。

Conclusion: 用户倾向于依赖AI代理而非人类专家判断，但界面级别的对话设计选择对用户行为的影响有限。个性化与温暖感的组合可以恢复AI的说服力，但信任机制独立于对话设计。

Classification reason: 该论文研究对话式AI中个性化（contextualization）与温暖感（warmth）对用户信任和依赖的影响，属于AIGC方向下的个性化与对话生成（aigc_personalization_persona）这一规范子主题，该子主题涵盖个性化助手与对话生成。

Importance reason: Focuses on human-AI trust and persuasion, not on core generative model architectures or efficiency. Signals: HCI study, no generative model contribution, no technical novelty for AIGC, user trust experiment, low alignment with priority subtopics

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) agents personalize their responses by tailoring explanations to users' backgrounds, interests, and prior interactions, referred to as contextualization. Personalization has been identified as a persuasive strategy in politics or in marketing. However, the persuasive effect of contextualization in everyday tasks, where users often lack prior knowledge, remains unclear. We conducted a $2\times2$ between-subjects experiment ($N = 380$) examining how contextualization, combined with conversational warmth, shapes reliance and persuasiveness of an AI assistant arguing against expert recommendations. Our findings reveal that contextualization reduces the persuasive power of AI, but its combination with warmth restores persuasiveness through a crossover interaction. Reliance on AI is present across conditions and is invariant to the conversational design. Trust strongly predicts both persuasion and reliance, yet neither contextualization nor warmth operates through trust. AI literacy decouples trust from behavior: more literate users report lower trust in the assistant, yet are more persuaded and more reliant on its advice. These results suggest that users are prone to deferring to AI agents over human expert judgment; however, interface-level conversational design choices have a limited role in shaping the behavior.

</details>

### [112] [Latent Space Disentanglement via Activation Steering for Interpretable Attribute Control in Symbolic Music Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31295)
*Ioannis Prokopiou, Pantelis Vikatos, Maximos Kaliakatsos-Papakostas, Theodoros Giannakopoulos, Themos Stafylakis*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.SD, cs.IR, cs.LG

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 创意与艺术生成

Importance: 普通相关 (40.86) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于激活引导（activation steering）的框架，利用 Difference-in-Means 方法在 Multitrack Music Transformer 的残差流中解耦音高（Pitch）和时长（Duration）的潜在方向，并通过 Gram-Schmidt 正交化实现多属性独立控制，无需重新训练即可实现可解释的属性调制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer 架构在符号音乐生成中取得了显著进展，但缺乏细粒度、可解释的属性控制能力。现有方法通常需要重新训练或引入额外模块，难以在推理时实现独立、确定性的属性调制。

Method: 采用 Difference-in-Means (DiffMean) 方法从残差流中提取音高和时长的潜在方向，验证线性表示假设；针对多属性引导中的特征纠缠问题，提出 Dual Steering 框架，利用 Gram-Schmidt 正交化解耦不同属性的方向向量，实现独立控制。

Result: 实验表明，引导强度与属性偏移之间具有高相关性；与朴素向量加法相比，Gram-Schmidt 正交化解耦方法显著减少了概念干扰和信号退化，即使在强自回归条件下也能实现独立的确定性控制。

Conclusion: 本文证明了在符号音乐生成中可以通过推理时激活引导实现可解释的属性控制，Gram-Schmidt 正交化有效解决了多属性引导中的特征纠缠问题，为音乐生成模型的可控性提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究符号音乐生成中的可解释属性控制，属于创意与艺术生成范畴，与 [CANONICAL] aigc_creative_art（创意与艺术生成）高度匹配。同时，符号音乐生成也属于多模态生成中的音频生成方向。

Importance reason: Focuses on symbolic music generation, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and boosted keywords. Signals: activation steering, interpretable control, symbolic music, difference-in-means, gram-schmidt orthogonalization

Abstract: Transformer-based architectures have significantly advanced the generation of complex symbolic sequences, yet a significant gap remains in achieving fine-grained, interpretable control over discrete signal attributes. This paper investigates the mechanistic interpretability of the Multitrack Music Transformer (MMT) and proposes a framework for deterministic attribute modulation without retraining to bridge this gap via inference-time activation steering. Utilizing the Difference-in-Means (DiffMean) methodology, we isolate latent directions for signal attributes, specifically Pitch and Duration, within the residual stream. We validate the Linear Representation Hypothesis in this domain, achieving high correlation between steering magnitude and attribute shift. To address the inherent feature entanglement in multi-attribute steering, we introduce a Dual Steering framework utilizing Gram-Schmidt Orthogonalization. Experimental results demonstrate that this geometric decoupling reduces conceptual interference and signal degradation compared to naive vector addition, enabling independent deterministic control even against strong autoregressive conditioning.

</details>

### [113] [SteerFace: Debiasing Synthetic Face Generation via Adaptive Residue Perturbation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30894)
*Yuxi Mi, Qiuyang Yuan, Jianqing Xu, Yichun Zhou, Xuan Zhao, Jun Wang, Rizen Guo, Shuigeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 合成数据与数据集蒸馏

Importance: 普通相关 (38.26) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SteerFace提出了一种自适应残差扰动方法，通过在身份嵌入上施加朝向随机正交方向的扰动，作为身份保持的正则化项，减少合成人脸数据中不自然的视觉倾向性，从而缩小合成数据与真实数据在下游人脸识别任务上的性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成人脸数据用于训练人脸识别模型时存在显著的合成-真实性能差距。现有工作关注身份保持和数据多样性，但忽略了合成数据中视觉属性分布不自然（视觉倾向性）的问题，这种倾向性源于生成器在条件化身份嵌入时无意中吸收了共现的残差视觉线索。

Method: 提出SteerFace训练框架，在训练时将身份嵌入向嵌入超球面上的随机正交方向扰动，作为身份保持的正则化项，惩罚生成器对非身份成分的依赖。同时引入自适应策略，学习样本级别的扰动强度，兼顾样本偏好和整体统计特性。

Result: 实验表明SteerFace有效缓解了视觉倾向性，在下游人脸识别任务上优于先前方法，并在不同训练数据集和生成流水线上具有良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: SteerFace通过简单的身份嵌入扰动正则化，有效改善了合成人脸数据的分布质量，缩小了合成-真实差距，为合成数据在生物识别等安全敏感场景中的应用提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究利用合成人脸数据训练人脸识别模型，核心是改进合成数据的质量以缩小与真实数据的差距，属于合成训练数据生成范畴。aigc_synthetic_data（合成数据与数据集蒸馏）是[CANONICAL]子主题，完美匹配。同时该论文也涉及多模态生成（扩散模型生成人脸），因此也匹配multimodal_generation方向。

Importance reason: Focuses on face recognition synthetic data debiasing, not aligned with user's priority subtopics in multimodal generation, video/image generation, or efficient models. Signals: Face recognition, synthetic data debiasing, identity embedding perturbation, not in priority subtopics, no boost keywords matched

Abstract: The shortage of legally compliant data for face recognition training has sparked growing interest in using synthetic data as an alternative. While recent diffusion-based methods enable the generation of photorealistic face images with strong identity adherence and data diversity, their downstream recognition performance still exhibits a significant synthetic-real gap. This paper identifies visual tendency as a previously underexplored limitation, whereby synthetic data exhibit an unrealistic prevalence of visual attributes and thus deviate from the real-data distribution. Visual tendency can be attributed to the generator's conditioning on identity embeddings, through which co-occurring residual visual cues are unintentionally absorbed into learned identity semantics. To discourage the generator from exploiting such visual cues, this paper proposes SteerFace, a simple and efficient training framework that perturbs identity embeddings by steering them toward random orthogonal directions on the embedding hypersphere. The perturbation serves as an identity-preserving regularizer that penalizes the generator's reliance on non-identity components, as supported by theoretical analysis. This paper further introduces an adaptive strategy that learns perturbation strengths with both sample-wise preference and favorable overall statistics. Extensive experiments show that SteerFace effectively mitigates visual tendency, outperforms prior methods in downstream face recognition, and generalizes well across different training datasets and generation pipelines.

</details>


### [114] [Not All Synthetic Data Is Yours to Learn From](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31126)
*Sina Alemohammad, Li Chen, Richard G. Baraniuk, Zhangyang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.LG, cs.CL

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 合成数据与数据集蒸馏

Importance: 值得关注 (75.46) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出"潜在能力浮现假说"：无提示无监督的自训练（语言模型用BOS token生成的文本微调自身）只有在合成数据与学生模型兼容时才能提升能力。研究发现：合成数据的效用是关系性的（自生成数据最优，同源迁移优于异源，跨族迁移最弱）；常见的固有代理指标（语义相似度、token似然）无法预测数据效用；该机制能解耦能力提升与逐字记忆（提取攻击成功率下降95%以上），无需显式遗忘目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索语言模型能否仅通过自身生成的纯文本（无提示、无教师、无验证器、无奖励模型）进行自我改进，以及这种自训练的有效条件是什么。

Method: 在无提示无条件的自训练设定下，对预训练语言模型仅用BOS token生成的文本进行微调。通过控制合成数据来源（自生成、同族模型、异族模型），分析数据兼容性对能力提升的影响，并评估能力与逐字记忆的分离程度。

Result: 1) 自生成数据最有效，同源迁移优于异源，跨族迁移最弱；2) 语义相似度和token似然等固有指标无法预测数据效用；3) 在Pythia实验中，能力保持或提升的同时，逐字记忆提取成功率下降超95%。

Conclusion: 无提示自训练通过放大模型已有能力而非从数据中导入结构来工作；该机制可以在无需显式遗忘目标的情况下实现能力与逐字记忆的分离，为隐私保护提供新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究语言模型使用自身生成的合成文本进行自训练的效果与条件，核心关注合成数据的效用（compatibility）及其与模型能力的关系，属于合成数据领域。现有 [CANONICAL] subtopic "aigc_synthetic_data"（合成数据与数据集蒸馏）完全匹配。

Importance reason: Explores synthetic data utility in self-training, but focuses on language models and data distillation, not the user's priority multimodal generation or video/image generation. Signals: synthetic data utility, latent capability resurfacing, self-training, language model, relational data compatibility

Abstract: Can a language model improve from plain text sampled from itself, with no prompts, no teacher, no verifier, and no reward model? Yes, but only when the synthetic corpus is compatible with the student, a relational property of the source-student pair rather than an intrinsic property of the data. We call this the latent capability resurfacing hypothesis: weak self-training can amplify capabilities already present in the pretrained model, but only under this compatibility condition. We study this in the minimal setting of prompt-free unconditional self-training, where base language models are fine-tuned on text generated from the BOS token alone, with no task specification or external supervision. We report three findings. First, synthetic utility is relational rather than intrinsic: self-generated data is the most effective source, same-lineage transfer outperforms stronger but differently trained sources, and cross-family transfer is substantially weaker. Second, common intrinsic proxies fail: neither benchmark-level semantic similarity nor average per-token likelihood under the student predicts which corpora help. Third, this regime produces a surprising byproduct. In controlled Pythia experiments, capability and verbatim memorization decouple: benchmark utility is preserved or improved while held-out exact-match extraction drops by over 95 percent, with no forget set, privacy objective, or targeted unlearning. Together, these results suggest that prompt-free self-training works by amplifying what the student already knows, not by importing structure from the data. They also reveal a regime in which capability and verbatim memorization can be separated without any explicit unlearning objective.

</details>


### [115] [Differentially Private Preference Data Synthesis for Large Language Model Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30808)
*Fengyu Gao, Jing Yang*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.LG, cs.CR

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 合成数据与数据集蒸馏

Importance: 普通相关 (44.66) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 DPPrefSyn，一种在差分隐私保护下生成合成偏好数据的算法，用于大语言模型的对齐训练，首次实现了隐私保护的偏好数据合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的后训练对齐阶段依赖真实人类偏好数据，但这些数据包含敏感的用户提示和人类判断，存在隐私泄露风险。现有方法缺乏在严格隐私保护下生成高质量偏好数据的方案。

Method: DPPrefSyn 基于 Bradley-Terry 偏好模型和成对偏好数据的内在几何结构。首先从私有数据中学习带有差分隐私保证的偏好模型，然后利用该模型结合公共提示合成高质量偏好数据。通过利用每簇奖励模型的共享线性结构来捕获异构偏好，并使用差分隐私主成分分析（DP-PCA）提升学习精度。

Result: 实验结果表明，DPPrefSyn 在强差分隐私保证下实现了具有竞争力的对齐性能，证明了合成偏好数据作为隐私保护对齐的可行替代方案。

Conclusion: 本文首次实现了差分隐私合成偏好数据用于 LLM 对齐，为隐私保护的后训练对齐提供了实用且可扩展的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出差分隐私合成偏好数据生成方法，属于合成训练数据生成范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题"合成数据与数据集蒸馏"（aigc_synthetic_data）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel DP synthetic preference data for LLM alignment, but outside user's core multimodal generation and efficiency focus. Signals: First DP synthetic preference data for LLM alignment, Strong DP guarantees, Bradley-Terry model, DP-PCA, Privacy-preserving alignment

Abstract: Preference alignment is a crucial post-training step for large language models (LLMs) to ensure their outputs align with human values. However, post-training on real human preference data raises privacy concerns, as these datasets often contain sensitive user prompts and human judgments. To address this, we propose DPPrefSyn, a novel algorithm for generating differentially private (DP) synthetic preference data to enable privacy-preserving preference alignment. DPPrefSyn is a principled framework grounded in the Bradley-Terry preference model and the intrinsic geometric structure of pairwise human preference data. It first learns an underlying preference model from private data with formal differential privacy guarantees, and then leverages the learned model together with public prompts to synthesize high-quality preference data. It exploits the shared linear structure of per-cluster reward models to effectively capture heterogeneous human preferences in private datasets, and leverages DP Principal Component Analysis (DP-PCA) to improve learning accuracy. Extensive experimental results demonstrate that DPPrefSyn achieves competitive alignment performance under strong DP guarantees. These findings highlight the potential of synthetic preference data as a practical alternative for privacy-preserving preference alignment across a broad range of applications. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to generate DP synthetic preference data for LLM alignment. Our code is available at .

</details>

### [116] [MAAT: Multi-phase Adapter-Aware Targeted Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30514)
*Suryash Yagnik, Shubham Gaur, Saksham Thakur, Vinija Jain, Aman Chadha, Amitava Das*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CL

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 概念擦除与遗忘学习

Importance: 普通相关 (41.51) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文指出现有机器遗忘评测中"为什么"类因果知识占比极低（<1.3%），导致方法在因果知识上失效却无法被检测。作者构建了平衡的5W评测基准5WBENCH，并提出MAAT——一种三阶段LoRA适配器遗忘框架，首次在因果知识上同时实现高遗忘与高保留，达到遗忘-保留帕累托前沿的新操作点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器遗忘评测数据集（CounterFact、ZSRE、TOFU、MUSE、WMDP-Cyber）中，"为什么"类因果/关系型问题占比极低（<1.3%），导致方法即使完全无法遗忘因果知识，也能在聚合指标上获得高分。这种结构性偏差使得因果遗忘失败无法被检测，亟需平衡的评测基准和针对因果知识的遗忘方法。

Method: 1) 构建5WBENCH：5,000样本的平衡基准，每类5W问题（Who/What/When/Where/Why）各1,000例。2) 提出MAAT三阶段框架：阶段一为梯度投影上升（gradient-projected ascent）进行遗忘；阶段二为SVD秩维度剪枝与任务向量取反；阶段三为混合KL-隐状态保留修复（hybrid KL-hidden-state retain repair），在LoRA适配器权重上操作。

Result: 实验表明，现有基线方法无法在Why类问题上同时实现高遗忘与高保留——激进遗忘破坏保留知识，保守方法无法遗忘因果事实。MAAT是首个在因果知识上同时达到高遗忘与高保留的方法，在遗忘-保留帕累托前沿上取得了新的操作点。Why类问题的困难源于多跳推理链（44% vs 其他≤2%）和长答案跨度（平均40.1 token）导致的梯度稀释。

Conclusion: 机器遗忘评测存在严重的结构性偏差，因果知识遗忘需要专门的评测与方法设计。MAAT通过多阶段LoRA适配器操作，首次实现了因果知识上的遗忘-保留平衡，为机器遗忘研究提供了新的方向。

Classification reason: 该论文研究机器遗忘（machine unlearning），属于 AIGC 方向下的"概念擦除与遗忘学习"（aigc_unlearning_erasure）这一规范子主题。论文提出了针对因果知识的遗忘方法 MAAT 和评测基准 5WBENCH，核心是模型遗忘与知识保留的权衡，与子主题定义完全吻合。

Importance reason: Focuses on machine unlearning evaluation and causal knowledge forgetting, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and direction weights. Signals: machine unlearning, causal knowledge, 5W benchmark, LoRA adapter, forget-retain Pareto frontier

Abstract: Machine unlearning evaluation is structurally skewed: Why-type questions, which probe causal and relational knowledge, comprise less than 0.06% of CounterFact, 0.6% of ZSRE, and less than 1.3% of TOFU, MUSE, and WMDP-Cyber. This near-zero representation means that methods that fail on causal knowledge can score highly in aggregate, and this failure is undetectable without balanced evaluation. We present 5WBENCH, a balanced 5,000-sample benchmark with 1,000 examples per 5W category (Who, What, When, Where, Why), making causal unlearning failures quantifiable for the first time. Using 5WBENCH, we show that no existing baseline simultaneously achieves high forgetting and high retention on Why-type questions: aggressive forgetting degrades retained knowledge, while conservative methods fail to forget causal facts. Why-type difficulty stems from multi-hop reasoning chains (44% of Why entries vs. less than or equal to 2% for others) and gradient dilution over 40.1-token answer spans. We present MAAT (Multi-phase Adapter-Aware Targeted Unlearning), a three-phase framework operating on LoRA adapter weights, combining gradient-projected ascent, SVD rank-dimension pruning, task vector negation, and hybrid KL-hidden-state retain repair. MAAT is the first method to simultaneously achieve high forgetting and high retention on Why-type causal knowledge, reaching a new operating point on the forget-retain Pareto frontier. We make our code publicly available.

</details>


### [117] [AMNESIA: A Large Scale Medical Unlearning Benchmark Suite with Disease-Informed Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30599)
*Saeedeh Davoudi, Reihaneh Iranmanesh, Ophir Frieder, Nazli Goharian*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CL

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 概念擦除与遗忘学习

Importance: 普通相关 (34.05) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: AMNESIA 是首个大规模、开源医疗领域机器遗忘基准测试，包含 70,560 个问答对，覆盖 11 种疾病类别，用于评估遗忘方法在患者级和疾病级遗忘上的表现，并揭示遗忘个体患者会连带损害同疾病其他患者的知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学知识不断更新，已训练的医疗 LLM 需要选择性遗忘特定训练数据，但现有遗忘基准依赖合成或小规模通用数据，缺乏临床遗忘的系统性评估。

Method: 构建 AMNESIA 基准：从 8,820 份患者病历中生成 70,560 个问答对，涵盖事实性（直接回忆）和推理性（临床推断）两类问题，覆盖 11 种疾病类别。使用四种主流遗忘方法（如梯度上升、DPO-based unlearning 等）在随机患者级和疾病级两种遗忘设置下进行评估，并引入检测医学术语泄露的新指标。

Result: 遗忘个体患者会导致同疾病其他患者的知识受损，表明现有方法难以有效区分个体患者与共享临床知识；新指标有效检测了医学术语泄露。

Conclusion: AMNESIA 为医疗 LLM 遗忘提供了标准化评测平台，揭示了当前遗忘方法在临床场景中的局限性，呼吁开发能更好分离个体患者与共享临床知识的遗忘方法。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于医疗LLM的机器遗忘（machine unlearning）基准测试，属于生成模型遗忘学习范畴，与AIGC方向下的[CANONICAL] subtopic "概念擦除与遗忘学习"（aigc_unlearning_erasure）高度匹配。

Importance reason: The paper focuses on medical unlearning, which is outside your configured priority subtopics and is explicitly penalized by negative keywords. Signals: Medical unlearning benchmark, Disease-informed analysis, Patient-level forgetting, Clinical LLM safety, Low relevance to multimodal generation or autoregressive video/image generation

Abstract: Medical knowledge is continuously evolving. This creates a need to update or selectively forget information encoded in already-trained medical LLMs. Machine unlearning aims to remove the influence of specific training data from a model without full retraining. Yet, existing unlearning benchmarks rely on synthetic or small-scale general data, leaving clinical unlearning understudied. We introduce AMNESIA, the first large-scale, open source benchmark for medical unlearning, with 70,560 question-answer pairs from 8,820 patient notes across 11 disease categories. AMNESIA includes both factual questions testing direct recall and reasoning questions testing clinical inference. We use it to evaluate four widely used unlearning methods at both random patient and disease-level, and introduce a new metric for detecting leakage of medical terminology. We show that unlearning individual patients erodes knowledge of others with the same condition, calling for methods that can better separate patients from shared clinical knowledge.

</details>


### [118] [Divergence Decoding: Inference-Time Unlearning via Auxiliary Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31293)
*Humzah Merchant, Bradford Levy*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 概念擦除与遗忘学习

Importance: 普通相关 (41.51) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Divergence Decoding (DD)，一种推理时遗忘方法，通过小型辅助模型在推理阶段调整 LLM 的 logits，使其远离敏感训练数据，无需重新训练即可实现有效的知识遗忘，并在文本和图像领域均验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 经常记忆敏感训练数据，带来隐私和版权风险。现有的遗忘方法要么导致灾难性的效用损失，要么对复杂查询无效。因此需要一种高效、低成本且不损害模型性能的遗忘机制。

Method: 提出 Divergence Decoding (DD)，使用小型辅助模型（通过标准预训练和微调获得）在推理时调整基础 LLM 的 logits，使输出分布偏离目标遗忘数据。该方法可进一步将调整后的分布蒸馏回基础模型，实现模型级别的遗忘。

Result: 在多种模型规模和训练数据集上，DD 在遗忘基准测试中显著优于 SOTA 基线方法。该方法还展示了从文本生成到图像领域的泛化能力，验证了其作为通用遗忘机制的潜力。

Conclusion: Divergence Decoding 是一种有效且低成本的推理时遗忘方法，适用于任何概率模型，在文本和图像领域均表现出色，为隐私保护和版权合规提供了实用的技术方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Divergence Decoding 方法，通过辅助模型在推理时引导 LLM 的 logits 远离特定数据，实现模型遗忘（unlearning），属于生成模型的概念擦除与遗忘学习。该方向在 aigc 和 multimodal_generation 下均有 [CANONICAL] 的 aigc_unlearning_erasure 子主题，因此直接复用。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM unlearning, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and contains negative keywords. Signals: Inference-time unlearning, auxiliary model steering, logit adjustment, text and image domains, no retraining needed

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) frequently memorize sensitive training data thereby creating significant privacy and copyright risks. Addressing these risks, i.e., removing such knowledge from an existing model checkpoint, has proven challenging as many unlearning methods lead to catastrophic utility loss or are ineffective for complex queries. We introduce Divergence Decoding (DD), a mechanism that uses small auxiliary models to steer the logits of the LLM away from specific data during inference. Training these models is straight forward, i.e., we use standard pre-training and fine-tuning setups. We find the method decisively outperforms state-of-the-art (SOTA) baselines on unlearning benchmarks across a variety of model and training dataset scales consistent with DD being an effective and inexpensive solution to unlearning. We then demonstrate that this steered distribution can be trivially distilled back into the base model. Since the method is generally applicable to any probabilistic model, we explore its efficacy outside of text generation and find evidence of generalization to the domain of images.

</details>


### [119] [De-attribute to Forget for LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30919)
*Xinyang Lu, Jiabao Pan, Rachael Hwee Ling Sim, See-Kiong Ng, Anthony Kum Hoe Tung, Bryan Kian Hsiang Low*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.LG

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 概念擦除与遗忘学习

Importance: 普通相关 (41.51) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出DareU，一种基于数据属性归因奖励的LLM遗忘学习框架，通过强化学习降低模型生成响应与遗忘数据之间的归因分数（即去归因），从而实现有效遗忘，同时避免过度遗忘和模型效用下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM遗忘学习方法多依赖优化预测损失（如最大化遗忘集上的损失），但常面临过度遗忘和模型效用下降等问题。本文旨在通过一种新的优化视角——将遗忘目标重新定义为归因归零——来解决这些问题。

Method: 提出DareU框架，将LLM遗忘的优化目标重新定义为归因归零。具体地，使用强化学习更新LLM，降低模型生成响应与遗忘数据所有者之间的归因分数（即去归因）。采用LLM分类器作为归因的高效近似方法，实现可扩展的归因计算。

Result: 实验表明，DareU在遗忘质量和模型效用之间取得了良好的平衡，优于现有基线方法，有效实现了遗忘目标同时保持了模型性能。

Conclusion: 基于数据属性归因奖励的遗忘学习框架是一种有前景的LLM遗忘范式，能够有效解决传统方法中的过度遗忘问题，为LLM安全治理提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究LLM遗忘学习（unlearning），属于AIGC方向下的[CANONICAL]概念擦除与遗忘学习（aigc_unlearning_erasure）子主题。同时，该主题也出现在multimodal_generation和multimodal_understanding_generation方向的非规范子主题列表中，因此匹配多个方向。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM unlearning via data attribution, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative-keyword-filtered. Signals: LLM unlearning, data attribution, reinforcement learning, over-forgetting, model utility

Abstract: The rapid development of large language models (LLMs) has raised concerns on the use of inappropriate data for training, which has led to a growing interest in LLM unlearning. Many existing LLM unlearning approaches rely on optimizing prediction loss(es), such as maximizing the loss on the forget set, but often face critical issues like over-forgetting and poor model utility. To address them, this paper novelly frames the optimization objective for LLM unlearning as one of zeroing out data attribution instead. In particular, we propose the first LLM unlearning framework based on data attribution rewards called DareU that performs reinforcement learning to update the LLM by reducing the attribution score of its generated responses (i.e., de-attributing) to the forget data owners. Empirical evaluation using an LLM classifier as an efficient approximation of attribution shows that DareU outperforms existing baselines by achieving effective unlearning while balancing forget quality and model utility well.

</details>


### [120] [Forgetting Has Neighbors: Localized Collateral Forgetting in Machine Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31317)
*Polina Dolgova, Sebastian U. Stich*

Main category: cs.LG

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 概念擦除与遗忘学习

Importance: 普通相关 (38.26) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文研究了机器遗忘中一个被忽视的问题：标准聚合评估指标会掩盖局部失败。作者发现梯度上升和随机标签遗忘方法会导致与遗忘集几何邻近的样本预测偏差显著增大（局部附带遗忘），并揭示了其机制——替代目标与重训练局部预测结构不一致。为此提出局部教师蒸馏（Local Teacher Distillation）策略，用遗忘集邻近样本训练的教师模型生成软标签替代随机目标，有效缓解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器遗忘评估通常使用聚合指标（如准确率、遗忘分数），这些指标可能掩盖局部区域的失败模式。作者希望揭示并理解遗忘模型在样本级别的非均匀行为，特别是与遗忘集邻近的样本是否受到不成比例的影响。

Method: 1) 在样本级别比较遗忘模型与重训练模型的预测差异，发现梯度上升和随机标签方法中差异随与遗忘集的几何邻近度增大。2) 分析机制：遗忘使用的替代目标与重训练诱导的局部预测结构不一致，这种不一致通过共享表征传播到邻近样本。3) 提出局部教师蒸馏（Local Teacher Distillation）：在遗忘集邻近样本上训练一个小型教师模型，用其软标签替代随机目标进行遗忘。

Result: 在CIFAR-100部分类别删除任务上，局部教师蒸馏使遗忘模型在遗忘集附近样本上的预测显著更接近重训练模型，同时保持有竞争力的聚合遗忘指标。

Conclusion: 机器遗忘存在局部附带遗忘现象，聚合指标不足以评估遗忘质量；局部教师蒸馏是一种简单有效的缓解策略，为更精细的遗忘评估和算法设计提供了新方向。

Classification reason: 该论文研究机器遗忘（machine unlearning），属于概念擦除与遗忘学习（aigc_unlearning_erasure）这一规范子主题的核心范畴。论文分析了遗忘过程中的局部附带遗忘现象并提出缓解策略，与现有子主题定义高度吻合。

Importance reason: Focuses on machine unlearning, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative-keyword-filtered directions. Signals: machine unlearning, localized collateral forgetting, gradient ascent, random labeling, local teacher distillation

Abstract: Machine unlearning aims to remove the influence of selected training examples without full retraining. Standard evaluations often summarize unlearning quality with aggregate metrics, such as accuracy- and forgetting-based scores, which can hide localized failures. We study this failure mode at the example level by comparing the predictions of an unlearned model to those of the model retrained after deletion. We show that this pointwise discrepancy can be highly non-uniform: for gradient-ascent and random-labeling methods, with and without retain-set fine-tuning, it grows with geometric proximity to the forget set. We call this phenomenon localized collateral forgetting. Our analysis identifies a mechanism behind the effect: surrogate targets used during unlearning can be inconsistent with the local prediction structure induced by retraining, and this inconsistency propagates through shared representations to nearby examples. Motivated by this mechanism, we propose Local Teacher Distillation, a simple mitigation strategy that replaces random targets with soft labels from a small teacher trained only on retained neighbors of the forget set. On CIFAR-100 partial-class deletion, this local teacher brings the unlearned model substantially closer to retraining, especially near the forget set, while maintaining competitive aggregate unlearning metrics.

</details>

### [121] [Linear Ensembles Wash Away Watermarks: On the Fragility of Distributional Perturbations in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30501)
*Zhihao Wu, Gracia Gong, Qinglin Zhu, Yudong Chen, Runcong Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 水印与版权保护

Importance: 普通相关 (42.99) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文揭示了LLM文本水印的一个根本性漏洞：当用户可访问多个模型时，通过平均多个模型的输出概率分布即可有效消除水印信号。作者提出WASH方法，解决了异构模型间的词汇表不对齐和分词差异问题，实验表明平均3-5个模型即可将检测z-score从5-300降至2以下，同时提升生成质量27.5%且速度提升6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM文本水印方法通过在输出分布中嵌入统计信号来实现检测与归因，但实际场景中用户可同时访问多个模型（如ChatGPT、Claude、Gemini等）。这些水印扰动在不同提供商之间通常是独立的，因此存在被联合攻击消除的可能。本文旨在揭示这一根本性漏洞并量化其影响。

Method: 理论证明：对多个独立水印扰动模型的输出概率分布进行平均，可以恢复无水印分布，误差仅为二阶项。实践方法WASH（Watermark Attenuation via Statistical Hybridisation）：(1) 通过词汇表映射解决异构模型间的词汇不对齐问题；(2) 统一分词策略处理tokenization差异；(3) 对多个模型的输出概率分布进行加权平均后采样生成文本。

Result: 在6种水印方案和3种LLM上的实验表明：平均3个模型即可将检测z-score从5-300降至2以下（低于检测阈值4），TPR@5%FPR降至50%以下；同时生成质量提升27.5%，长序列生成速度比最优基线快6倍。

Conclusion: LLM文本水印存在根本性脆弱性：当用户可访问多个模型时，通过简单的输出分布平均即可有效消除水印。鲁棒的AI文本检测要么接受这一漏洞，要么需要模型提供商之间前所未有的协调（如共享水印密钥或统一扰动策略）。

Classification reason: 该论文研究LLM生成文本的水印脆弱性问题，属于AIGC方向下的水印与版权保护（aigc_watermark_copyright）这一[CANONICAL]子主题，直接对应水印的鲁棒性与安全性分析。

Importance reason: This paper is about LLM watermark vulnerability, which is outside your configured priority subtopics and negative-keyword-filtered. Signals: LLM watermark vulnerability, ensemble attack, distributional perturbation, WASH method, model averaging

Abstract: Watermarking embeds statistical signatures in AI-generated text for detection and attribution. We reveal a fundamental vulnerability: when users access multiple models (today's reality), watermarks trivially fail. Watermarks perturb output distributions away from the original, and in competitive markets, these perturbations are typically independent across providers. We theoretically prove that averaging output probability distributions recovers the unwatermarked distribution with up to a second-order error term. Empirically, simply averaging 3-5 models cancels out these perturbations. We introduce WASH (Watermark Attenuation via Statistical Hybridisation), which solves practical challenges in ensemble generation: vocabulary misalignment and tokenisation differences across heterogeneous models. Experiments across six watermarking schemes and three LLMs show that averaging across 3 models suppresses detection z-scores from 5-300 to below 2 (below the detection threshold of 4) and reduces TPR at 5% FPR to below 50%, while improving quality by 27.5% and running 6 times faster than the best baseline on the long sequence generation. Our results suggest that robust AI-text detection via watermarking requires either accepting this fundamental vulnerability or unprecedented coordination among model providers.

</details>
