### [122] [COMPASS: Cognitive MCTS-Guided Process Alignment for Safe Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30838)
*Wenkai Shen, Pengyang Zhou, Jiahe Xu, Jiaming Qian, Haozhe He, Zhihao Huang, Chaochao Chen, Xiaolin Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 生成内容安全与治理

Importance: 普通相关 (43.34) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: COMPASS 提出了一种基于认知 MCTS 引导的过程对齐框架，用于增强 LLM 搜索智能体的安全性，通过认知树探索合成攻击轨迹和自省式步骤级对齐实现细粒度过程监督，在保持通用能力的同时显著提升安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 驱动的搜索智能体在多步推理和工具使用中，可能将有害意图分解为看似无害的子查询，导致检索引发的安全退化。现有对齐方法难以捕获稀疏的安全信号，也无法有效监督多步交互中的各类违规行为。

Method: COMPASS 包含两个核心模块：(1) 认知树探索（CTE），利用 MCTS 高效合成隐蔽攻击轨迹；(2) 自省式步骤级对齐（ISA），隔离风险中间动作以实现细粒度的过程监督。

Result: 实验结果表明，COMPASS 在安全性与通用能力之间取得了良好的权衡，同时所需训练数据显著减少。

Conclusion: COMPASS 通过过程级安全对齐有效解决了 LLM 搜索智能体的检索诱导安全退化问题，为智能体安全对齐提供了一种高效且数据节约的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文关注 LLM 搜索智能体的安全对齐问题，属于 AIGC 方向下的生成内容安全与治理（aigc_safety_governance）范畴，同时涉及多步推理与工具使用的智能体工作流，也与 aigc_agent_workflow 相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM agent safety alignment, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous VLM, video generation, or unified multimodal models. Signals: LLM agent safety, process alignment, MCTS, search agents, safety-utility trade-off

Abstract: LLM-powered search agents enable multi-step reasoning and tool use. However, these capabilities introduce retrieval-induced safety degradation, as harmful intents may decompose into seemingly innocuous sub-queries that lead to unsafe outcomes. Existing alignment methods struggle to capture sparse safety signals and fail to supervise diverse violations across multi-step interactions. We propose COMPASS, a Cognitive MCTS-Guided Process Alignment framework designed to achieve robust safety alignment throughout the agent workflow while preserving general utility. COMPASS integrates cognitive tree exploration (CTE) to efficiently synthesize stealthy attack trajectories, and introspective step-wise alignment (ISA) to isolate risky intermediate actions for fine-grained process supervision. Empirical results show that COMPASS achieves a favorable safety-utility trade-off while requiring substantially less training data.

</details>


### [123] [MosaicLeaks:Privacy Risks in Querying-in-the-Open for Deep Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30727)
*Alexander Gurung, Spandana Gella, Alexandre Drouin, Issam H. Laradji, Perouz Taslakian, Rafael Pardinas*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 生成内容安全与治理

Importance: 普通相关 (44.66) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出MosaicLeaks基准，包含1001个多跳深度研究任务，评估深度研究智能体在查询外部工具时泄露私有文档信息的风险。研究发现各类模型普遍存在隐私泄露，零样本提示只能部分缓解，仅优化任务性能的强化学习反而加剧泄露。作者提出PA-DR框架，结合任务奖励和隐私分类器进行密集信用分配，将Qwen3-4B-Instruct的准确率从48.7%提升至58.7%，并将答案和完整信息泄露从34.0%降至9.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度研究智能体越来越多地将私有本地文档与外部工具（如网络检索）结合使用，这带来了隐私风险：智能体的外部查询可能泄露本地上下文中的敏感信息。这种风险被马赛克效应放大——单个查询看似无害，但聚合起来可能暴露敏感信息。现有研究缺乏对此类隐私泄露的系统性评估和缓解方法。

Method: 作者构建了MosaicLeaks基准，包含1001个多跳深度研究任务，将私有企业文档与公共网络语料链接，迫使智能体做出依赖本地信息的外部查询。使用对抗LLM仅观察智能体的外部查询，在三个层面推断私有信息：研究意图、具体问题答案、可验证声明。提出PA-DR（Privacy-Aware Deep Research）框架，结合任务成功的情境奖励与学习到的隐私分类器，对每个查询和马赛克级泄露提供密集信用分配。

Result: 各类模型普遍存在三个层面的隐私泄露；零样本隐私提示只能部分缓解泄露；仅优化任务性能的强化学习反而加剧泄露。PA-DR框架训练Qwen3-4B-Instruct将准确率从48.7%提升至58.7%，并将答案和完整信息泄露从34.0%降至9.9%。

Conclusion: 深度研究智能体的隐私泄露是一个严重且尚未充分解决的问题，马赛克效应放大了这一风险。PA-DR框架展示了通过联合优化任务性能和隐私保护来缓解泄露的有效性，为构建更安全的AI智能体系统提供了可行方向。

Classification reason: 该论文研究深度研究智能体在查询外部工具时泄露私有文档信息的隐私风险，属于AIGC方向下的生成内容安全与治理（aigc_safety_governance）范畴，关注AI系统的隐私泄露与安全治理问题。

Importance reason: Focuses on privacy leakage in deep research agents, which is outside the user's core interests in generation and multimodal models. Signals: Privacy risk in agent queries, MosaicLeaks benchmark, RL-based mitigation, Not aligned with user's priority subtopics

Abstract: Deep research agents increasingly combine private local documents with external tools like web retrieval, creating a privacy risk: an agent's external queries may leak sensitive information from its local context. This risk is amplified by the mosaic effect, where individual queries may appear harmless but become revealing in aggregate. We introduce MosaicLeaks, a benchmark of 1,001 multi-hop deep research tasks that chain private enterprise documents and a public web corpus, forcing agents to make external queries that depend on local information. We evaluate leakage with an adversary LLM that observes only the agent's external queries and attempts to infer private information at three levels: the agent's research intent, answers to specific private questions and verifiable claims about the enterprise documents. We find that models across families and sizes frequently leak at all three levels, that zero-shot privacy prompting reduces but does not eliminate leakage and that reinforcement learning for task performance alone worsens leakage. To address this, we propose Privacy-Aware Deep Research (PA-DR), an RL framework that combines situational rewards for task success with a learned privacy classifier to provide dense credit assignment over both per-query and mosaic-level leakage. Training Qwen3-4B-Instruct with PA-DR improves accuracy from 48.7% to 58.7% and reduces answer and full-information leakage from 34.0% to 9.9%.

</details>


### [124] [Mental Damage: Caption Poisoning Attacks on Retrieval-Augmented Text-to-Music Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30365)
*Yizhu Wen, Shuhao Zhang, Nan Zhang, Long Cheng, Hanqing Guo*

Main category: eess.AS, cs.AI, cs.SD

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 生成内容安全与治理

Importance: 普通相关 (41.51) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对检索增强文生音乐（TTM）系统的字幕投毒攻击方法。攻击者通过向音乐知识库注入少量精心构造的音乐字幕，使系统检索到恶意字幕，从而偏置提示增强过程，使生成结果偏离用户意图。作者提出双层字幕投毒策略，在保留高层检索锚点的同时注入低层声学描述符，实现对下游音乐生成的定向操控。实验表明该方法能有效使生成结果接近攻击者目标，同时保持与原始查询的合理对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强文生音乐系统依赖外部音乐知识库的字幕质量来增强用户提示，这种设计引入了对数据库完整性的依赖。现有研究尚未充分探索针对此类系统的投毒攻击风险，因此作者旨在揭示这一安全漏洞。

Method: 提出双层字幕投毒策略：第一层保留高层检索锚点（如音乐风格、乐器等关键词）以确保恶意字幕能被检索到；第二层注入低层声学描述符（如节奏、音色、动态等）以偏置提示增强和下游音乐生成朝向攻击者选择的目标意图。使用MusicCaps作为知识库、CLAP作为检索器、MusicGen作为生成器进行实验验证。

Result: 在MusicCaps知识库、CLAP检索器和MusicGen流水线上，被投毒后的生成结果在声学特征上显著接近攻击者目标，同时与原始用户查询的对齐程度保持可比水平，验证了攻击的有效性。

Conclusion: 检索增强文生音乐系统存在实际的安全完整性风险，攻击者可通过少量精心构造的字幕投毒操控生成结果。该工作揭示了检索增强创意AI系统面临的新型安全威胁，呼吁关注此类系统的数据完整性保护。

Classification reason: 该论文研究针对检索增强文生音乐系统的字幕投毒攻击，属于AIGC安全与治理范畴。aigc_safety_governance（生成内容安全与治理）是[CANONICAL]子主题，涵盖有害内容检测、安全攻击等，与此论文高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on security attack for text-to-music, not aligned with user's priority subtopics on generation models or efficiency. Signals: Novel attack method, specific to music domain, low overlap with user's boost keywords, negative keywords absent, security angle not prioritized

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented text-to-music (TTM) systems augment underspecified user prompts using captions retrieved from a music caption dataset. This design introduces an integrity dependency on the music knowledge database. We show that an attacker can poison the database by injecting a small number of crafted music captions, causing the system to retrieve malicious captions that bias prompt augmentation and steer generation away from the user's intended function, without modifying the user prompt, retriever, or generator. To achieve the music caption poisoning attack, we propose a dual-layer caption poisoning strategy that preserves high-level retrieval anchors while injecting low-level acoustic descriptors to steer prompt augmentation and downstream music generation toward an attacker-chosen target intent. In a MusicCaps knowledge database, CLAP retriever, and MusicGen pipeline, poisoned generations move substantially closer to the attacker's target, while remaining comparably aligned with the original user query. These results expose a practical integrity risk for retrieval-augmented creative AI systems. Our demo can be found at:

</details>


### [125] [Latent Geometric Chords for Query-Efficient Decision-Based Adversarial Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31219)
*Ei Hmue Khine, Yao Li, Jiebao Sun, Shengzhu Shi, Zhichang Guo, Boying Wu*

Main category: cs.CR, cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 生成内容安全与治理

Importance: 普通相关 (41.51) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Latent Geometric Chords (LGC)，一种基于决策的黑盒对抗攻击方法。通过曲率感知的几何搜索在压缩语义流形中导航决策边界，并引入残差对抗生成（RAG）机制将语义扰动作为几何弦叠加到原始图像上，解决了像素级攻击产生高频伪影和潜空间方法搜索空间受限的问题。实验表明LGC在极低扰动（SSIM>0.99, LPIPS<0.01）下保持高攻击成功率，并具有跨数据集迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有决策级黑盒对抗攻击存在两类根本局限：像素级攻击常引入不自然的高频视觉伪影，而潜空间方法受限于低维流形的搜索空间和固有的重建缺陷。亟需一种既能保证视觉保真度又能扩展搜索空间的高效攻击方法。

Method: 提出 Latent Geometric Chords (LGC) 方法，核心包括：(1) 在压缩语义流形上执行曲率感知的几何搜索以导航决策边界；(2) 引入残差对抗生成（RAG）机制，将语义扰动作为几何弦（geometric chords）分离出来并直接叠加到原始源图像上，从而解决基线重建缺陷并将允许的搜索空间维度有效翻倍。

Result: LGC 在 5000 次查询下达到 SSIM > 0.99、LPIPS < 0.01 的视觉保真度，显著优于现有基线方法；在严格的感知约束下保持高攻击成功率，能够成功攻破对抗训练过的鲁棒模型；并展现出跨数据集的迁移能力。

Conclusion: LGC 通过几何弦与残差对抗生成机制，在决策级黑盒攻击中实现了视觉保真度与攻击效能的最佳平衡，为对抗攻击领域提供了新的有效范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究黑盒对抗攻击方法，属于AI安全领域。在AIGC方向下，[CANONICAL] subtopic "生成内容安全与治理"（aigc_safety_governance）涵盖内容审核、有害内容检测等安全主题，对抗攻击是安全治理的重要组成部分，因此映射至此。

Importance reason: Focuses on adversarial attack security, not aligned with user's priority on generation and multimodal models. Signals: Black-box adversarial attack, Decision-based attack, Geometric search, Visual fidelity, Not generation-focused

Abstract: While decision-based black-box adversarial attacks present a severe security threat, current methodologies suffer from fundamental limitations. Pixel-wise attacks frequently introduce unnatural, high-frequency visual artifacts, while latent-space frameworks are confined by the limited search space of low-dimensional manifolds and inherent reconstruction flaws. To resolve these limitations, we propose Latent Geometric Chords (LGC) for Query-Efficient Decision-Based Adversarial Attacks alongside a variant, LGC-H. At its core, LGC navigates decision boundaries by executing a curvature-aware geometric search within a compressed semantic manifold. To guarantee high visual fidelity and circumvent dimensionality bottlenecks, we introduce a Residual-based Adversarial Generation (RAG) mechanism. RAG isolates semantic perturbations as geometric chords and superimposes them directly onto the original source image. RAG substantially resolves baseline reconstruction flaws and effectively doubles the permissible search space dimensions. Experimental results demonstrate that LGC achieves robust cross-dataset transferability and substantially outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. Notably, our method, LGC, minimizes perturbation magnitudes while achieving state-of-the-art visual fidelity--with a Structural Similarity Index Measure (SSIM) exceeding 0.99 and a Learned Perceptual Image Patch Similarity (LPIPS) below 0.01 at 5000 queries--and sustaining high attack success rates under stringent perceptual constraints, successfully compromising adversarially trained robust models. The source code is available at: .

</details>


### [126] [Steering LLMs? Actually, Sparse Autoencoders can outperform simple baselines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31183)
*Mikkel Godsk Jørgensen, Lars Kai Hansen*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.LG, cs.CL

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 生成内容安全与治理

Importance: 普通相关 (45.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文对Wu等人(2025)在AxBench基准上的结论提出部分反驳，证明稀疏自编码器（SAE）在LLM模型引导任务上实际表现接近LoRA水平，且高稀疏性并非成功引导的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究（Wu et al., 2025）在AxBench基准上评估SAE的模型引导性能时，发现SAE远不如简单的基线方法，导致SAE在模型引导领域的价值受到质疑。本文旨在重新审视这一结论，为SAE正名。

Method: 提出一个监督式特征选择与标注流水线，用于从SAE中挑选因果性强的特征进行模型引导。在AxBench基准上系统比较SAE与LoRA及简单基线的引导性能，并分析稀疏性（l0）对引导效果的影响。

Result: SAE在AxBench上的引导性能接近LoRA水平；基于可解释性组件选择的特征具有令人惊讶的因果性；高稀疏性（低l0）对基于可解释性的成功引导并非关键，这与Wang et al. (2025)的发现相反。

Conclusion: SAE在模型引导任务上被低估了，通过合适的特征选择方法可以达到与LoRA相当的性能；稀疏性并非引导效果的决定性因素。

Classification reason: 该论文研究使用稀疏自编码器（SAE）对LLM进行模型引导（steering），属于生成内容安全与治理范畴下的模型行为控制与安全引导。映射到AIGC方向下的[CANONICAL] subtopic "生成内容安全与治理"（aigc_safety_governance），因为模型引导是确保生成内容安全、可控的重要手段。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM steering and SAE interpretability, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative-keyword-filtered. Signals: LLM steering, sparse autoencoders, model interpretability, rebuttal of prior benchmark, not aligned with user's multimodal generation focus

Abstract: Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) have been seen as a promising avenue for exploring the internals of Large Language Models (LLMs) and for steering model output generation. When AxBench - a model steering benchmark - was introduced in Wu et al. (2025), SAEs did not seem to live up to their original hype due to poor steering performance relative to a set of simple baselines. This work serves as a partial rebuttal for Sparse Autoencoders and suggests that the results of Wu et al. (2025) did not do them full justice. We find that Sparse Autoencoders can, in fact, perform close to on par with the reference LoRA performance on the AxBench benchmark, when features are selected and labelled with our supervised pipeline. We also find that our pipeline selects features that are surprisingly causal of their identified labels when using only its interpretability-based components. Lastly, we present evidence that high sparsity (low l0) may not be crucial for successful steering based on interpretability, which is in contrast to the earlier findings in Wang et al. (2025).

</details>


### [127] [AdvScene: Rethinking Adversarial Patch Evaluation Through Scene Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30578)
*Xiaoyong, Yuan, Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.CR

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 生成内容安全与治理

Importance: 普通相关 (39.84) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 AdvScene，一个基于场景重建的对抗性补丁鲁棒性评估框架。通过将补丁嵌入到重建的真实3D场景中，系统性地评估补丁在不同视角、距离和场景条件下的攻击有效性（即"场景鲁棒性"），并引入 APSE（对抗补丁到场景嵌入）方法解决跨视角外观一致性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗性补丁评估方法存在不足：真实图像基准固定不变，无法覆盖多种场景条件；模拟器虽可控但缺乏真实场景的锚定。实际部署中，补丁的有效性取决于其在真实环境中随视角、距离等变化的表现（场景鲁棒性），但缺乏系统性的评估框架。

Method: 提出 AdvScene 框架，将评估重新定义为操作测量：给定固定部署的补丁，刻画其"操作包络"（operational envelope）——攻击在哪些视角和距离下成功。核心挑战是补丁仅在单个锚定视角定义，而评估需要跨视角一致的表示。为此提出 APSE（Adversarial Patch-to-Scene Embedding），通过约束提升（constrained lifting）解决跨视角歧义，同时保留攻击关键外观、强制局部性、目标表面附着和跨视角一致性。

Result: 使用真实世界物理数据验证 AdvScene，并对现有对抗性补丁进行全面评估。结果表明，攻击有效性存在显著的场景依赖性变化，这是现有基于图像或模拟器的评估无法捕捉的。

Conclusion: AdvScene 提供了一种更贴近实际部署的对抗性补丁评估方法，揭示了场景鲁棒性作为关键评估维度的必要性，为未来对抗性攻击与防御研究提供了更可靠的评估基准。

Classification reason: 该论文研究对抗性补丁（adversarial patches）的场景鲁棒性评估，属于AI生成/对抗内容的安全评估范畴，与AIGC方向下的"生成内容安全与治理"（aigc_safety_governance）高度相关，该子主题涵盖有害内容检测与安全评测。

Importance reason: Focuses on adversarial patch evaluation in 3D scenes, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative-keyword-filtered. Signals: adversarial patch robustness, scene-grounded evaluation, cross-view consistency, not aligned with user's core interests

Abstract: Adversarial patches are physical patterns attached to real objects to mislead AI vision systems. Their real-world risk is not determined by a single successful prediction, but by whether they remain effective after deployment under changing viewpoints, distances, and scene conditions. We refer to this property as scene robustness, the effectiveness of a deployed patch across conditions in a real environment. Yet existing evaluations do not measure scene robustness well: real image benchmarks are realistic but fixed, while simulators are controllable but not grounded in a specific real scene. We present AdvScene, a scene-grounded framework for measuring the scene robustness of adversarial patches in reconstructed real environments. AdvScene reframes evaluation as operational measurement: given a fixed deployed patch, it characterizes the patch's operational envelope - where and when the attack succeeds - as a function of viewpoint, distance, and scene context. A key challenge is that the attack is typically defined only in a single anchor view, while evaluation requires a representation that remains faithful under viewpoint changes. We formalize this as a constrained lifting problem and introduce Adversarial Patch-to-Scene Embedding (APSE), which resolves cross-view ambiguity while preserving attack-critical appearance and enforcing locality, target-surface attachment, and cross-view consistency. We validate AdvScene using real-world physical data and conduct a comprehensive evaluation of existing adversarial patches. Our results show that AdvScene reveals substantial scene-dependent variation in attack effectiveness that is not captured by existing image-centric or simulator-based evaluations.

</details>


### [128] [EvoDefense: Co-Evolving Black-Box Defense with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31140)
*Yu Li, Yuenan Hou, Yingmei Wei, Yanming Guo, Chaochao Lu*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.CR

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 生成内容安全与治理

Importance: 普通相关 (39.84) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: EvoDefense 提出一种经验引导的协同进化黑盒防御范式，利用守卫LLM和攻击生成器在持续攻防进化循环中迭代优化防御策略，无需重训练即可泛化到未见攻击类型和目标模型，在多个基准上显著降低攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM黑盒防御方法依赖预定义的过滤启发式规则，难以泛化到未见攻击类型和目标模型架构，亟需一种能自适应进化的通用防御范式。

Method: EvoDefense 包含三个核心组件：1）守卫LLM，用于检测恶意查询；2）经验记忆模块，积累历史交互中的防御知识；3）持续攻防进化循环，攻击生成器与守卫模型通过经验引导优化迭代改进攻击策略和防御策略。

Result: 在HarmBench、AdvBench和AlpacaEval上，EvoDefense在7个流行模型和5种代表性LLM攻击下均取得一致的强防御性能。在HarmBench上，将AutoDAN-turbo对Gemini-3-flash和LLaMA-3-8B-Instruct的攻击成功率分别从29.4%和43.4%降至8.4%和6.2%。

Conclusion: EvoDefense通过经验引导的协同进化范式有效提升了LLM黑盒防御的泛化能力，在保持通用能力的同时显著降低攻击成功率，为LLM安全防御提供了新的自适应框架。

Classification reason: 该论文提出针对LLM的黑盒防御方法，通过对抗攻击与防御的协同进化来检测恶意查询，属于生成内容安全与治理范畴，与AIGC方向下的[CANONICAL] subtopic "生成内容安全与治理"（aigc_safety_governance）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM security defense, not aligned with user's priority subtopics in multimodal generation, video/image generation, or model efficiency. Signals: Black-box defense, LLM security, Co-evolution, No retraining, Generalization to unseen attacks

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) remain highly vulnerable to diverse attacks, particularly in black-box settings where the internals of target models are inaccessible. Existing black-box defenses typically rely on pre-defined filtering heuristics, which often fail to generalize to unseen attack types and target model architectures. We introduce EvoDefense, an experience-guided co-evolving black-box defense paradigm. EvoDefense employs a guard LLM to detect malicious queries and an experience memory module to accumulate defense knowledge from previous interactions. At the core of EvoDefense is a continuous attack-defense evolution loop, where an attack generator and the guard model iteratively refine their attack strategies and defense policies through experience-guided optimization. This design enables EvoDefense to generalize across unseen attacks and target models without retraining. Experiments on HarmBench, AdvBench, and AlpacaEval show that EvoDefense achieves consistently strong defense performance across seven popular models and five representative LLM attacks, while preserving competitive general capabilities. On HarmBench, EvoDefense reduces the attack success rate (ASR) of AutoDAN-turbo on Gemini-3-flash and LLaMA-3-8B-Instruct from 29.4% and 43.4% to 8.4% and 6.2%, respectively.

</details>


### [129] [Send a SCOUT First: Pre-hoc Reasoning for Adaptive Detector Allocation in Prompt-Injection Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30837)
*Shuhao Zhang, Jiarui Li, Qi Cao, Ruiyi Zhang, Pengtao Xie*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CR

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 生成内容安全与治理

Importance: 普通相关 (45.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出SCOUT框架，将提示注入防御重新定义为检测器分配问题：针对每个输入请求，根据历史行为预测各检测器的可靠性和延迟，动态选择运行哪些检测器以及是否升级到LLM裁判，从而在安全性和实用性之间取得更好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示注入检测系统采用固定单检测器流水线，但不同检测器在不同攻击类型上各有优劣，没有一种检测器始终可靠。固定流水线会将每个请求都暴露于某个检测器的盲区，缺乏灵活性。

Method: 提出SCOUT框架，核心思想是"先侦察后决策"：通过分析历史输入上各检测器的表现，预测当前样本上每个检测器的可靠性和延迟，从而动态决定运行哪些检测器以及是否升级到LLM裁判。框架暴露一个安全-效用阈值供操作员调节。同时构建了SCOUT-450基准，包含结构复杂、面向智能体的提示注入样本。

Result: 在SCOUT-450上，安全导向的操作点相比始终使用GPT-4o裁判，攻击成功率降低46%，总延迟降低40%，仅牺牲5.1个百分点的良性效用。在BIPIA、IPI和IHEval三个外部基准上也改善了安全-效用边界。

Conclusion: 动态检测器分配策略比固定单检测器流水线更有效，SCOUT框架能够在安全性和实用性之间实现灵活权衡，且可迁移到多个外部基准。

Classification reason: 该论文研究提示注入攻击的防御策略，属于AIGC安全治理范畴。提示注入是LLM/AIGC系统面临的关键安全威胁，论文提出的自适应检测器分配框架SCOUT属于内容安全与治理方向，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "生成内容安全与治理" (aigc_safety_governance)。

Importance reason: Focuses on prompt-injection defense, which is not a priority subtopic and is penalized by negative keywords. Signals: Prompt-injection defense, dynamic detector allocation, safety-utility tradeoff, not in priority subtopics, no boost keywords matched

Abstract: Prompt-injection detectors are heterogeneous: each is strong on a different slice of attacks, and none is always reliable. Yet existing systems still treat detection as a fixed single-detector pipeline, committing every request to one detector's blind spots. We reframe defense as detector allocation: given a heterogeneous pool, decide per request which detectors to run and whether to escalate to an LLM judge. Our framework SCOUT (Scalable and Controllable Outcome-prediction for Uncertainty-aware Triage) makes this decision dynamic by predicting each detector's per-sample reliability and latency from how it behaved on similar past inputs, and exposes a single safety-utility threshold to the operator (where utility bundles benign-pass rate and wall-clock). To evaluate this setting, we build SCOUT-450, a benchmark that captures the structurally complex, agent-facing injections that older prompt-injection sets under-represent. On SCOUT-450, a safety-oriented operating point reduces attack-success rate by 46% and total wall-clock by 40% relative to an always-on GPT-4o judge, at a 5.1-point benign-utility drop. SCOUT also transfers to three external benchmarks (BIPIA, IPI, and IHEval), improving the safety-utility frontier.

</details>

### [130] [Configurable Reward Model for Balanced Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30487)
*Zhengping Jiang, Mehran Khodabandeh, Akash Bharadwaj, Manik Bhandari, Mayur Srungarapu, Anqi Liu, Benjamin Van Durme, Li Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

Importance: 普通相关 (46.59) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出可配置安全奖励模型（CSRM），通过配置目标数据增强和联合优化，实现对细粒度安全配置的敏感响应，在 CoSApien 和 DynaBench 基准上达到 SOTA，并显著改善下游安全对齐中 helpfulness-safety 的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指令微调 LLM 和安全分类器难以泛化到新的安全配置，缺乏可显式配置以适应不断变化安全需求的奖励模型。

Method: 提出 CSRM，联合优化校准的安全合规性与奖励建模；采用配置目标数据增强，强制指令遵循同时保留相对严重性结构。

Result: 在 CoSApien 上达到 94.6% F1，DynaBench 上达到 75.8% F1，无需额外人工标注；下游安全对齐中显著改善 helpfulness-safety 权衡。

Conclusion: CSRM 对细粒度安全配置和对话细微差别敏感，显著提升对未见安全配置的泛化能力，为安全对齐提供了更灵活有效的奖励建模方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出可配置的安全奖励模型（CSRM），用于大语言模型的安全对齐，核心贡献在于奖励建模与安全对齐。该主题与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励"（generation_alignment）高度匹配，该 subtopic 在 aigc 和 multimodal_generation 方向下均有定义，且明确包含 reward modeling、alignment 等关键词。

Importance reason: Focuses on safety alignment reward modeling, which is peripheral to the user's core interests in multimodal generation and continuous VLM. Signals: Safety alignment, reward model, helpfulness-safety tradeoff, configurable safety, LLM alignment

Abstract: Aligning large language models (LLMs) to heterogeneous and rapidly evolving safety requirements remains a critical challenge. Existing instruction-tuned LLMs and standalone safety classifiers often fail to generalize to new safety configurations, motivating the need for Reward Models (RMs) that are explicitly configurable to changing specifications. We introduce the Configurable Safety Reward Model (CSRM), which is jointly optimized for calibrated safety compliance and reward modeling. Our approach is supported by configuration-targeted data augmentation that enforces instruction adherence while preserving relative severity structure. The resulting RM is sensitive to fine-grained safety configurations and conversational nuances, substantially improving generalization to previously unseen safety configurations. CSRM achieves state-of-the-art performance on recent configurable safety benchmarks, including CoSApien (94.6% F1) and DynaBench (75.8% F1), without requiring additional human annotation. When used for downstream safety alignment, CSRM yields LLMs with a significantly improved helpfulness-safety tradeoff compared to existing baselines.

</details>

### [131] [Sound effects in media:A comparative analysis of recorded and synthetic samples in live-action and animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31082)
*Nelly Garcia, Joshua Reiss*

Main category: cs.SD, cs.MM

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 视频音频生成应用

Importance: 普通相关 (36.69) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文比较了程序化生成的合成音效与真实录音在真人实拍和动画两种视觉场景中的可信度感知。实验表明，合成音效在戏剧和科幻场景（如激光、打击、空气、火箭等音效模型）中表现良好且被认为可信，但在卡通动画中表现日常动作时可信度较低。研究还识别了需要优化的音效模型和音频特征，并收集了音频专业人士的反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 影视和游戏中的音效制作通常需要重复录制、叠加真实物体或使用音效库，过程耗时且重复。程序化音频（数字拟音）可以快速生成样本，但合成样本相比真实样本的可信度仍存疑问。本文旨在系统比较合成音效与真实音效在不同视觉场景下的感知可信度。

Method: 使用在线程序化音频引擎生成合成音效样本，将其分别与真人实拍和动画两种视觉内容结合，通过主观听感实验评估参与者的可信度感知。实验涵盖多种音效模型（激光、打击、空气、火箭等），并收集音频专业人士的反馈以识别需要优化的模型和音频特征。

Result: 程序化音频在戏剧和科幻场景中效果良好且被认为可信，尤其适用于激光、打击、空气和火箭等音效模型。但在卡通动画中表现日常动作时，合成音效的可信度较低。研究识别了需要优化的特定音效模型和需要改进的音频特征。

Conclusion: 程序化音频在特定场景（如戏剧、科幻）和特定音效类型中可以作为真实录音的有效替代方案，但在卡通动画的日常动作场景中仍需改进。研究为音效设计师提供了关于何时以及如何使用合成音效的实用指导，并指出了未来优化的方向。

Classification reason: 该论文研究程序化音频（数字拟音）在影视和动画中的合成音效与真实录音的对比感知，属于AIGC方向下的音视频内容创作应用。现有[CANONICAL] subtopic "视频音频生成应用"（aigc_video_audio_creation）涵盖音频编辑、内容创作产品级生成应用，与该论文主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on sound effects believability, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous VLM, autoregressive video generation, or world models. Signals: sound effects, procedural audio, comparative analysis, live-action vs animation, not multimodal generation

Abstract: Creating sound for storytelling is crucial to establishing the environment in productions such as films, TV series and video games. This process often involves repeating, layering and recording real objects or using sound libraries, which can be time-consuming and repetitive. To address these challenges, procedural audio, also known as digital foley, offers a solution by allowing sound designers to quickly generate samples. Despite its efficiency, questions remain about the believability of synthetic samples compared to real ones. In our study, we compared synthetic samples generated by an online procedural engine and integrated them with both animated and live-action visuals. Our results indicate that procedural audio is highly effective and perceived as believable in drama and sci-fi scenes, particularly for sound models such as lasers, hits, air and rockets, whereas synthetic sounds weren't as believable in cartoon productions when representing everyday actions. Finally, we identified specific models that needed optimisation and highlighted audio features that needed improvement with feedback from audio professionals.

</details>


### [132] [AnchorSteer: Self-Discovered Concept Injection for Structure-Preserving Music Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31053)
*Chih-Heng Chang, Keng-Seng Ho, Chih-Yu Tsai, Kuan-Lin Chen, Yi-Hsuan Yang, Jian-Jiun Ding*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.SD

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 视频音频生成应用

Importance: 值得关注 (67.49) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: AnchorSteer 提出一种自发现概念注入框架，通过结构锚定与自监督语义引导解耦，实现保留节奏和旋律结构的可控音乐编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可控音乐编辑面临语义-结构纠缠问题：编辑方法常破坏音乐结构，而结构保持方法又抑制语义编辑能力。现有方法难以同时实现显著的语义变换和高保真结构保持。

Method: 提出 AnchorSteer 框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 自发现概念向量提取——通过自监督重建目标从模型内部表征中提取可解释、无标签的概念向量；(2) 结构锚定——在编辑过程中将概念向量注入扩散模型的隐空间，同时使用结构适配器保持节奏和旋律结构的一致性。提供无条件注入和有条件注入两种变体以平衡鲁棒性和语义强度。

Result: 在 ZoME-Bench 基准和主观测试上，AnchorSteer 优于纯引导方法和纯锚定方法，实现了显著的语义变换同时保持高保真结构。

Conclusion: AnchorSteer 通过解耦语义编辑与结构保持，有效解决了音乐编辑中的语义-结构纠缠问题，为可控音乐编辑提供了新的框架思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究可控音乐编辑（音频编辑），属于AIGC方向下的"视频音频生成应用"（aigc_video_audio_creation）规范子主题，该子主题涵盖音视频编辑与内容创作应用。同时与多模态生成方向下的"音频与多模态联合生成"（audio_av_generation）也相关，但主要侧重编辑应用而非基础生成方法。

Importance reason: Focuses on music editing, not aligned with user's priority subtopics in multimodal generation or video/image generation. Signals: music editing, diffusion, concept injection, structure preservation, self-supervised

Abstract: Controllable music editing is to modify high-level attributes while strictly preserving rhythmic and melodic structures. However, this task is challenged by a semantic-structural entanglement: steering methods often degrade structure to achieve editing performance, while structural adaptors suppress semantic responsiveness. We propose AnchorSteer, a framework that disentangles this tension by coupling structural anchoring with self-discovered semantic steering. The proposed approach probes internal representations to extract interpretable, label-free concept vectors via a self-supervised reconstruction objective, isolating attributes without curated data. During editing, these portable, plug-and-play concept vectors are injected into diffusion hidden manifolds while a structural adaptor enforces consistency. Variants for unconditioned and conditioned injections are provided to balance robustness and semantic strength. Experiments on ZoME-Bench and subjective tests show that the proposed framework outperforms both steering-only and anchoring-only baselines, enabling significant semantic transformations with high-fidelity structural preservation.

</details>

### [133] [UXR PoV for Neuroinclusive Emotion Regulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31131)
*Melike Akca, Mona Giff, Deniz Cetinkaya, Huseyin Dogan, Stephen Giff*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.HC

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 领域内容生成应用

Importance: 普通相关 (62.41) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种生成式AI增强的用户体验研究方法论，基于UXR PoV Playbook，结合DBT、SDT和COM-B心理学框架，为ADHD成人设计神经包容性的数字情绪调节干预系统。该方法通过四阶段UXR流程（AI辅助假设生成、基础规划、Building Blocks洞察生成、利益相关者PoV叙事构建），产出了10张理论驱动的UXR Play Cards，将心理学机制转化为可操作的设计指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数字情绪调节干预系统存在理论整合薄弱、对神经多样性包容不足、缺乏结构化用户体验研究方法论等问题，无法有效支持ADHD成人的情绪调节需求。

Method: 提出生成式AI增强的UXR方法论，包含四阶段流程：(1) AI辅助假设生成，(2) 基础规划，(3) 通过Building Blocks生成洞察，(4) 构建利益相关者特定的PoV叙事。该方法整合DBT、SDT和COM-B心理学框架，利用生成式AI作为协同分析工具支持综合、假设形成和设计表达。

Result: 产出了10张理论驱动的UXR Play Cards，将心理学机制和实证发现转化为可操作的设计指导，形成了一套可复制的、具有偏差意识的生成式AI集成UXR框架。

Conclusion: 该工作为将生成式AI整合到UXR实践中提供了可复制的框架，推动了以人为本和神经包容性的数字心理健康设计方法发展。

Classification reason: 该论文将生成式AI应用于数字心理健康领域（ADHD情绪调节），属于垂直领域内容生成应用，与aigc_domain_application（领域内容生成应用）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on ADHD emotion regulation UX, not core AI generation or multimodal research. Signals: ADHD, emotion regulation, generative AI as co-analytic tool, UXR methodology, neuroinclusive design

Abstract: Attention-deficit/hyperactivity disorder (ADHD) is a psychiatric disorder which presents itself in individuals through patterns of developmentally inappropriate levels of inattentiveness, hyperactivity, and impulsivity, with difficulties in decision making and emotional regulation (ER). Although digital and AI-based interventions have expanded access to ER support, many existing systems remain limited by weak theoretical integration, insufficient accommodation of neurodiversity, and a lack of structured user experience research (UXR) methodologies, that bridge psychological insight with design practice. This paper introduces a Generative AI-augmented UXR methodology, grounded in the UXR Point of View (PoV) Playbook, to support the design of emotionally intelligent and Neuroinclusive digital ER interventions for adults with ADHD. The approach integrates empirical evidence with established psychological frameworks Dialectical Behaviour Therapy (DBT), Self-Determination Theory (SDT), and the COM-B behavioural model and leverages Generative AI as a co-analytic tool to support synthesis, hypothesis formation, and design articulation. The methodology is operationalized through a four-stage UXR process encompassing AI-supported hypothesis generation, foundational planning, insight generation via Building Blocks, and the construction of stakeholder-specific PoV narratives. This process results in a set of ten theory informed UXR Play Cards that translate psychological mechanisms and empirical findings into actionable design guidance. The primary contribution of this work is a replicable, bias-aware framework for integrating Generative AI into UXR practice, advancing human-centred and Neuroinclusive approaches to digital mental health design.

</details>


### [134] [Translation Analytics for Freelancers II: Benchmarking Local LLMs for Confidential Translation Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31452)
*Yuri Balashov, Rex VanHorn, Mingxi Xu, Austin Downes*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.HC

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 领域内容生成应用

Importance: 普通相关 (38.19) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文面向自由译者和中小型语言服务提供商，构建了多语平行语料库（RFMC），并在离线环境下对多个本地可运行的 LLM（通过 Ollama）进行翻译基准测试，评估其在保密敏感领域的翻译质量。结果表明精心选择的本地 LLM 可匹敌甚至超越本地 NMT 系统和前沿云端 LLM，为隐私受限场景下的机器翻译提供了可行方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由译者和中小型语言服务提供商在处理保密敏感内容时无法使用云端翻译引擎和商业 LLM，需要离线、本地可运行的翻译方案。然而，本地 LLM 在翻译任务上的实际表现缺乏系统性的基准评测，现有研究多聚焦于云端大模型或通用 NMT 系统。

Method: 扩展 Reeve Foundation Trilingual Corpus (RFTC) 为多语语料库 (RFMC)，新增德语和简体中文参考译文。使用 Ollama 部署多个本地可运行的 LLM（不同参数量级），在四个语言方向上对 1000+ 句子进行单提示词翻译（无微调或领域适配），并与商业 NMT（DeepL、Baidu）、前沿 LLM（GPT-5.2）及专业级本地 NMT 系统（OPUS-CAT、NeuralDesktop、Promt）对比，使用 MATEO 进行自动评估。

Result: 本地 LLM 在不同语言方向和模型规模上表现差异显著。最佳本地 LLM 在翻译质量上可匹敌或超越本地 NMT 系统和前沿 LLM（GPT-5.2），但仍落后于顶级商业 NMT 系统（如 DeepL）。

Conclusion: 精心选择的本地 LLM 可为隐私受限的翻译专业人员提供可行的离线翻译方案，研究结果对模型缩放和多语言能力提升具有指导意义。

Classification reason: 该论文研究面向保密敏感领域的离线机器翻译，属于垂直领域（翻译）的生成应用，与 [CANONICAL] 领域内容生成应用（aigc_domain_application）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on translation application for freelancers, not core multimodal generation or autoregressive video/image generation. Signals: domain-specific translation, local LLM benchmarking, privacy-constrained workflow, no multimodal or generation focus, low alignment with priority subtopics

Abstract: Building on our previous work, this paper develops practical, low-barrier methods for freelance translators and smaller language service providers to evaluate translation technologies using rigorous yet accessible analytic methods. Here we address a high-stakes, specialized need: offline translation for confidentiality-sensitive domains in which privacy constraints preclude the use of cloud-based engines and commercial LLMs. We expand the Reeve Foundation Trilingual Corpus (RFTC) used in our previous work into a multilingual corpus (RFMC) by adding sentence-aligned German and Simplified Chinese reference translations. We then benchmark several locally runnable language models (via Ollama) across four language directions on 1000+ sentences selected from this corpus. We use consistent single-prompt calls without fine-tuning or domain adaptation, comparing local LLM outputs against commercial NMTs (DeepL, Baidu), a frontier LLM (GPT-5.2), and professional-grade local NMT systems (OPUS-CAT, NeuralDesktop, Promt). Automatic evaluation is conducted with MATEO. Results reveal substantial variation in local LLM performance across language directions and model sizes. The best local LLMs match or surpass local NMT systems and a frontier LLM, though they remain behind top commercial NMTs. These findings underscore the viability of carefully selected local LLM translation for privacy-constrained professionals and inform future research on model scaling and multilingual capability.

</details>


### [135] [On-Device Generative AI for GDPR-Compliant Visual Monitoring: Natural Language Alerts from Local Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30544)
*Gudrun Schappacher-Tilp, Nicoletta Kaehling, Jan Kornberger, Egon Teiniker*

Main category: cs.CR, cs.CV

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 领域内容生成应用

Importance: 普通相关 (35.11) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种隐私保护设计（privacy-by-design）的视觉监控流水线，在Raspberry Pi 5 + Hailo-8L AI加速器上运行YOLOv5n-seg进行实时目标检测，检测后立即丢弃原始图像数据，仅将JSON事件载荷发送给本地部署的Phi-3 Mini（3.8B参数，Q4_0量化）生成1-2句自然语言警报文本。整个过程中无图像数据离开边缘设备，符合GDPR第5(1)(c)条的数据最小化原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于云AI推理的视觉监控系统会将原始图像数据暴露给外部服务，这与GDPR的数据最小化原则存在根本冲突。现有方案缺乏在边缘设备上完成全部推理并生成可读警报的完整隐私保护流水线。

Method: 系统架构包括：(1) YOLOv5n-seg模型编译到Hailo-8L加速器，在Raspberry Pi 5上实时目标检测；(2) 检测后立即丢弃原始像素缓冲区；(3) 有状态触发引擎将最小JSON事件载荷转发给本地Phi-3 Mini（Q4_0量化）；(4) LLM合成1-2句自然语言警报文本，仅文本警报通过网络传输。

Result: 在目标硬件上测量了推理延迟和资源利用率，展示了代表性生成的警报文本。结果表明在单板计算机上结合专用神经网络加速器与本地LLM不仅可行，而且能产生实际可部署、人类可读的监控输出。

Conclusion: 该概念验证表明，通过将全部推理限制在边缘设备上，可以在实现GDPR合规的同时获得实用的视觉监控能力。这种隐私保护设计范式为未来边缘AI监控系统提供了可行路径。

Classification reason: 该论文属于AIGC方向，描述了一个边缘设备上的隐私保护视觉监控系统，使用本地LLM生成自然语言警报。这属于垂直领域（监控/安全）的生成应用，与aigc_domain_application（领域内容生成应用）高度匹配。

Importance reason: This paper focuses on GDPR-compliant edge deployment of small models, which is far from the user's priority on advanced multimodal generation and autoregressive video/image generation. Signals: Edge deployment, GDPR compliance, YOLOv5n-seg, Phi-3 Mini, Raspberry Pi 5

Abstract: Visual monitoring systems that rely on cloud-based AI inference expose raw image data to external services, creating fundamental tensions with the data-minimisation principle of the General Data Protection Regulation (GDPR). This paper presents a proof-of-concept privacy-by-design pipeline that resolves this tension by confining all inference entirely to the edge device. A YOLOv5n-seg model compiled for a Hailo-8L AI accelerator delivers real-time object detection on a Raspberry Pi 5, from which raw pixel buffers are immediately discarded after inference. A stateful trigger engine forwards minimal JSON event payloads to a locally hosted instance of Phi-3 Mini (3.8B parameters, Q4_0 quantisation), which synthesises one-to-two sentence natural-language alerts for a human operator. No image data crosses the network boundary at any point; only the generated text alert is transmitted. We describe the full system architecture and implementation, report measured inference latency and resource utilisation on the target hardware, and present representative generated alerts. The results demonstrate that combining a dedicated neural-network accelerator with an on-device large language model on a single-board computer is not only feasible but produces practically deployable, human-readable monitoring output while aligning with GDPR Art. 5(1)(c) by design.

</details>


### [136] [Generalistic or Specific Embeddings, Which is Better? An Empirical Study on Search for Clinical Coding in Non-English Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30529)
*David Rey-Blanco, Roberto Cruz*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.LG, cs.CL

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 领域内容生成应用

Importance: 普通相关 (31.76) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文研究非英语临床编码检索中句子嵌入模型的性能退化问题，提出利用大语言模型（Gemini）生成合成数据来构建两阶段检索器（bi-encoder + cross-encoder reranker），在西班牙语、加泰罗尼亚语、法语、葡萄牙语等多语言临床编码检索上显著提升召回率，并开源了构建领域医疗检索器的完整方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 句子嵌入模型在语义搜索中主要基于英语语料开发和评估，当应用于非英语语言的临床编码检索（如ICD-10-CM/CIE-10代码检索）时，召回率显著下降，而聚合基准测试往往掩盖了这种退化。现有方法缺乏针对非英语临床领域的有效检索方案。

Method: 提出两阶段检索器：第一阶段使用bi-encoder（基于西班牙语生物医学编码器PlanTL-GO-BES/bsc-bio-ehr-es微调）进行粗检索，第二阶段使用cross-encoder进行重排序。训练数据由Gemini大语言模型生成合成数据，覆盖英语、西班牙语、加泰罗尼亚语、意大利语、葡萄牙语和法语六种语言，共约19,500个合成配对。

Result: Bi-encoder在MRR上达到0.876（BioBERT-ST为0.866），R@3达0.650（BioBERT-ST为0.626），R@5达0.804（BioBERT-ST为0.790）。加入cross-encoder重排序后，R@5提升至0.822，在五种语言中的四种上超越BioBERT-ST（西班牙语+0.017，加泰罗尼亚语+0.033，法语+0.018，葡萄牙语+0.037），仅英语略有下降。葡萄牙语R@5达0.829 vs BioBERT-ST的0.714。

Conclusion: 大语言模型生成的合成数据可以有效弥补非英语临床编码检索的性能差距，无需英语生物医学预训练即可达到甚至超越BioBERT-ST的性能。两阶段检索架构在跨语言场景下表现出临床可接受的性能权衡，为构建领域特定医疗检索器提供了可复现的开源方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究利用大语言模型生成合成数据来构建临床编码检索系统（ICD-10-CM/CIE-10代码检索），属于医疗垂直领域的生成应用，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题"领域内容生成应用"高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on clinical coding retrieval in non-English languages, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and includes a negative keyword (clinical). Signals: Clinical coding retrieval, non-English embeddings, synthetic data from LLM, domain-specific medical retriever, low relevance to user's multimodal generation and autoregressive video/image generation priorities

Abstract: Sentence-embedding models for semantic search are overwhelmingly developed and evaluated on English corpora. When applied to clinical retrieval in other languages -- particularly retrieval of ICD-10-CM / CIE-10 codes -- recall degrades in ways often masked by aggregate benchmarks. We study whether large generative language models can serve as data factories to close this gap. We build a two-stage retriever (bi-encoder followed by cross-encoder reranker), fine-tuned from a Spanish biomedical encoder (PlanTL-GOB-ES/bsc-bio-ehr-es) on Gemini-generated synthetic data covering English, Spanish, Catalan, Italian, Portuguese and French, and evaluate against BioBERT-ST and the un-tuned Spanish encoder. The bi-encoder alone matches BioBERT-ST on MRR (0.876 vs. 0.866) and overtakes it on R@3 (0.650 vs. 0.626) and R@5 (0.804 vs. 0.790) without English biomedical pretraining. Adding a cross-encoder reranker lifts aggregate R@5 to 0.822 and dominates on four of five languages (+0.017 Spanish, +0.033 Catalan, +0.018 French, +0.037 Portuguese) at the cost of a small English regression. The trade-off is clinically acceptable: Portuguese reaches R@5 = 0.829 vs. BioBERT-ST's 0.714. Contributions: an open recipe for building domain-specific medical retrievers from LLM-generated data; quantification of the learning gain (MRR 0.755 to 0.876, +15.9% with ~19,500 synthetic pairs); and a characterisation of where gains concentrate by language and rank.

</details>


### [137] [ImmigrationQA: A Source-Grounded Dataset and Small-Model Adaptation for U.S. Immigration Law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30589)
*Nazarii Shportun*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CL

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 领域内容生成应用

Importance: 普通相关 (35.83) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文构建了 ImmigrationQA，一个包含 17,058 对美国移民法律问答对的源引证数据集，并使用 LoRA 对 Llama 3.2 3B 模型进行参数高效微调。微调模型在评估中相比基础模型提升了 27% 的平均得分，在程序性子领域表现突出，但在复杂法律推理方面仍显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 美国移民法律体系庞大复杂（数千页政策文件），许多申请人缺乏法律代理，亟需可访问的、源引证的法律问答系统来辅助理解移民程序。

Method: 从 11 个主要和次要来源（包括 USCIS 政策手册、8 CFR、BIA 先例判决和社区问答）收集语料，生成 10,056 个验证过的规范文档和 18,308 个文本块。使用 Claude Sonnet 4.6 通过五种模式特定提示生成结构化 QA 对，最终得到 17,058 对。采用 LoRA 对 Llama 3.2 3B Instruct 模型进行参数高效微调。

Result: 微调模型在 101 个示例的分层抽样评估中平均得分 1.08/3.0（16.8% 完全正确），相比 Llama 3 8B 基础模型的 0.85/3.0（4% 完全正确）提升了 27%；零样本 Claude Sonnet 基线得分为 1.52/3.0（25% 完全正确）。微调模型在旅行证件、身份调整、非移民签证等程序性子领域提升显著，但在复杂法律推理和时间敏感统计方面仍较弱。

Conclusion: 参数高效微调的小模型在法律领域问答中具有成本效益（总计算成本约 $29），但无法替代专业法律咨询，且不反映语料爬取后的法规变化。

Classification reason: 该论文构建了美国移民法律领域的问答数据集 ImmigrationQA，并对 Llama 3.2 3B 模型进行微调，属于法律垂直领域的生成应用。这与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "领域内容生成应用"（aigc_domain_application）高度匹配。

Importance reason: This paper is a domain-specific legal QA dataset and fine-tuning effort with no connection to the user's priority subtopics in multimodal generation, video/image generation, or efficient inference. Signals: domain-specific legal QA, small-model LoRA fine-tuning, no multimodal or generation focus, no priority subtopic keywords, negative keywords absent but topic irrelevant

Abstract: U.S. immigration law spans thousands of pages of official policy, federal regulations, and procedural guidance that change frequently and carry high stakes for petitioners who lack legal representation. We describe the construction of ImmigrationQA, a source-grounded question-answering dataset of 17,058 pairs across 13 immigration subdomains, and the fine-tuning of a Llama 3.2 3B Instruct model on that dataset using parameter-efficient LoRA. The corpus was assembled from 11 primary and secondary sources -- including the USCIS Policy Manual, 8 CFR, BIA precedent decisions, and community Q&A -- yielding 10,056 validated canonical documents and 18,308 text chunks. Structured QA pairs were generated from these chunks using Claude Sonnet 4.6 via five mode-specific prompts, with 22 pairs rejected for insufficient source-span overlap. The fine-tuned model was evaluated against a held-out split of 993 pairs using LLM-as-judge scoring on a 101-example stratified sample. The fine-tuned model scored a mean of 1.08/3.0 (16.8% fully correct; 101-example stratified eval) versus the Llama 3 8B base model at 0.85/3.0 (4% fully correct), a relative improvement of 27% in mean score; a zero-shot Claude Sonnet baseline scored 1.52/3.0 (25% fully correct). The fine-tuned model shows concentrated improvement in procedural subdomains (travel documents, adjustment of status, nonimmigrant visas) while remaining weak on complex legal reasoning and time-sensitive statistics. The full pipeline ran for approximately $29 in cloud compute. All artifacts -- dataset, model, code, and prompt templates -- are publicly released. The system is not a substitute for legal counsel and does not reflect regulatory changes after the corpus crawl date.

</details>


### [138] [Same Patient, Different Words, Different Diagnosis? Evaluating Semantic Stability in Clinical LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30646)
*Mahdi Alkaeed, Adnan Qayyum, Nabeel Abo Kashreef, Muhammad Bilal, Junaid Qadir*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CL

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 领域内容生成应用

Importance: 普通相关 (27.65) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出基于自然语言推理（NLI）的语义验证框架，用于筛选临床LLM中保持语义的提示改写，并引入三个指标（MVS、ΔC、WCI）量化模型对语义等价改写的敏感性。在DiagnosisQA和MedQA数据集上评估了16个开源通用和医学LLM，发现领域专业化并不一致地提升或降低鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在临床应用中，对语义等价的输入改写（如同义改写、句法变化）可能产生不一致的预测，这在安全关键的医疗场景中构成风险。现有基于嵌入的相似度度量无法捕捉否定、时间性、严重程度等关键语义差异，因此需要更可靠的语义验证方法来评估模型稳定性。

Method: 提出基于NLI的语义验证框架，先用NLI过滤保持语义的提示改写，再用LLM-as-a-judge进一步精炼，最后由临床专家审核。引入三个指标：MVS（语义保持改写敏感性）、ΔC（置信度变化）、WCI（最差情况不稳定性）。在DiagnosisQA和MedQA数据集上评估16个开源通用和医学LLM。

Result: 领域专用（DS）模型与通用（GP）模型之间的鲁棒性差异是混合且高度模型依赖的：领域专业化并不一致地提升或降低对语义保持改写的鲁棒性。部分DS模型排名最鲁棒，但强GP基线也保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 临床LLM对语义等价改写的鲁棒性高度模型依赖，领域专业化并非鲁棒性的可靠保证。需要更系统的语义稳定性评估框架来确保临床LLM的安全部署。

Classification reason: 该论文研究临床LLM在语义等价改写下的稳定性与鲁棒性，属于医疗垂直领域的LLM应用评估，与现有[AIGC]方向下的[领域内容生成应用]（aigc_domain_application）高度匹配。

Importance reason: This paper focuses on clinical LLM robustness, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and contains negative keywords. Signals: clinical LLM, semantic stability, NLI verification, domain-specific vs general-purpose, healthcare safety

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used in clinical applications. However, their behavior remains highly sensitive to subtle linguistic variations, such as rephrasing or syntactic variation. This sensitivity poses risks in safety-critical healthcare settings, where semantically equivalent inputs should produce consistent predictions. However, a key challenge is to ensure that prompt variations truly preserve clinical meaning, as embedding-based similarity metrics often fail to capture distinctions involving negation, temporality, or severity. To address this limitation, we propose a semantic verification framework based on Natural Language Inference (NLI) to filter meaning-preserving prompt variations, which are further refined using an LLM-as-a-judge and audited by a clinical expert. In addition, we introduce three metrics to quantify model sensitivity: MeaningPreserving Variation Sensitivity (MVS), confidence variation (\Delta C), and Worst-Case Instability (WCI). We evaluate 16 open-source general-purpose (GP) and medical LLMs within the same model families and parameter scales, using reformulated prompts derived from the DiagnosisQA and MedQA datasets. Our results demonstrate that robustness differences between domain-specific (DS) models are mixed and highly model-dependent, i.e., domain specialization does not consistently improve or reduce robustness to meaning-preserving prompt reformulations. Several DS models rank among the most robust (when compared with GP counterparts), and strong GP baselines remain competitive as well.

</details>


### [139] [Cross-Modal Clinical Knowledge Integration for Mammography Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31093)
*Jiayi Zhu, Fuxiang Huang, Yu Xie, Xi Wang, Zhixuan Chen, Yuan Guo, Qingcong Kong, Zhenhui Li, Qiong Luo, Hao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 领域内容生成应用

Importance: 普通相关 (35.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 MammoRG，一种模拟放射科医生临床报告流程的乳腺钼靶报告生成框架，通过两阶段训练（先学习临床先验知识，再以术语感知微调）生成符合 BI-RADS 标准的诊断报告，并配套开发了 MammoRGTool 报告解析工具用于临床效能评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乳腺钼靶筛查产生大量影像，给放射科医生带来沉重负担。现有自动报告生成方法仅做视觉到文本的直接映射，忽略了放射科医生在实际工作中遵循的结构化临床推理流程（如 BI-RADS 指南），导致生成的报告临床一致性不足。

Method: 提出 MammoRG 框架，采用两阶段训练：第一阶段通过分类监督学习从四视图钼靶影像中整合临床相关先验知识；第二阶段引入术语感知监督微调（terminology-aware SFT），将乳腺钼靶专用临床术语建模为原子语义单元，提升报告的临床一致性。同时开发 MammoRGTool 工具，从自由文本报告中提取结构化临床信息用于评估。

Result: 在内部数据集、两个外部数据集和 VinDr-Mammo 数据集上，MammoRG 在多项临床效能指标上持续优于现有方法，尤其在诊断相关的 BI-RADS F1 指标上分别超过第二名 2.73%、2.04%、1.90% 和 3.27%。

Conclusion: MammoRG 通过显式模拟临床报告工作流并融入先验临床知识，有效提升了乳腺钼靶报告生成的临床一致性和诊断准确性，验证了将领域专业知识融入生成模型的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦乳腺钼靶影像报告生成，属于医疗垂直领域的生成应用，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "领域内容生成应用" (aigc_domain_application) 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on a narrow medical domain (mammography report generation) with no overlap with user's priority subtopics or boost keywords. Signals: domain-specific medical application, no autoregressive/diffusion/video/unified multimodal generation, no continuous VLM or latent flow, negative keywords present (medical diagnosis)

Abstract: Breast cancer is a major global health concern, and mammography screening plays a central role in early detection. The large volume of screening examinations creates a substantial workload for radiologists, making accurate and consistent report generation a critical clinical challenge. Existing automated mammography report generation methods primarily focus on direct visual-to-text mapping, while overlooking the structured clinical reasoning process followed by radiologists in real-world practice. To address this limitation, we propose MammoRG, a mammography report generation framework that explicitly simulates the clinical reporting workflow by following the BI-RADS guideline and incorporating prior clinical knowledge to produce diagnostic reports. Specifically, MammoRG adopts a two-stage training framework. In the first stage, the model learns to integrate clinically relevant prior knowledge from a patient's four-view mammograms through classification-based supervision. In the second stage, a terminology-aware supervised fine-tuning strategy is introduced to model mammography-specific clinical terms as atomic semantic units, enabling the generation of high-quality reports with improved clinical consistency. To facilitate clinical efficacy evaluation of generated reports, we further develop MammoRGTool, a dedicated mammography report parsing tool that extracts structured clinical information from free-text reports. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MammoRG consistently outperforms existing methods across multiple clinical efficacy metrics, particularly in diagnosis-related BI-RADS F1, where it surpasses the second-best model by 2.73%, 2.04%, 1.90%, and 3.27% on the internal, external 1, external 2, and VinDr-Mammo datasets, respectively.

</details>


### [140] [Learning Whom to Trust: Market-Feedback Adaptive Retrieval for Frozen LLMs in Event-Driven Financial RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31201)
*Zijie Zhao, Roy E. Welsch*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 领域内容生成应用

Importance: 普通相关 (29.23) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种市场反馈自适应的检索增强生成方法，用于金融事件驱动的RAG系统。通过外部贝叶斯源记忆模块，根据已实现的残差收益反馈动态调整检索来源权重，在保持LLM冻结的情况下显著提升了金融信号预测和投资组合表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融RAG系统中，传统方法仅按文本相关性排序检索结果，但金融市场中不同事件类型、预测周期和市场环境下，有用的信息来源（如新闻 vs SEC文件）各不相同。现有方法无法自适应地调整检索策略。

Method: 提出Frozen Reader with Source Memory框架：保持LLM冻结，通过外部贝叶斯源记忆模块（Beta分布建模每个来源的可靠性）根据已实现的残差收益反馈动态更新检索来源权重，实现检索层的市场自适应调整。

Result: 在89只纳斯达克股票数据集上，Frozen Reader with Source Memory相比无记忆基线将宏F1从0.438提升至0.471，投资组合夏普比率从0.52提升至0.84。监督LoRA微调对静态RAG提升有限，且未超过冻结源记忆阅读器。

Conclusion: 对于金融RAG，学习从何处检索（学习信任谁）与学习如何阅读同等重要，提供了一种简单、模块化的市场反馈自适应路径。

Classification reason: 该论文研究金融领域的检索增强生成（RAG）系统，属于垂直领域（金融）的生成应用，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "领域内容生成应用"（aigc_domain_application）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on financial RAG with market feedback, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and contains negative keywords. Signals: Financial RAG, market-feedback adaptation, frozen LLM, event-driven prediction, portfolio Sharpe improvement

Abstract: Financial retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems typically rank evidence by textual relevance, but in financial markets the useful evidence source depends on event type, forecast horizon, and market context. We study news-triggered event-impact prediction as a point-in-time financial RAG problem. For each company-news anchor, the system retrieves related financial news and SEC filing passages, appends a pre-decision market-context card, and predicts multi-horizon residual-return signals. Our method keeps the large language model (LLM) reader frozen and adapts the retrieval layer through an external Bayesian source memory updated from matured residual-return feedback. On a fixed 89-stock Nasdaq-oriented universe derived from the FinRL-DeepSeek/FNSPID task, using original FNSPID news and point-in-time EDGAR filing passages, Frozen Reader with Source Memory improves held-out macro-F1 from 0.438 to 0.471 and downstream portfolio Sharpe from 0.52 to 0.84 relative to Frozen Reader with No Memory. A supervised LoRA reader improves static RAG modestly, but does not improve over the frozen source-memory reader. These results suggest that, for financial RAG, learning where to retrieve from can be as important as learning how to read, offering a simple, modular route to market-feedback adaptation.

</details>


### [141] [Wind Turbine Maintenance Log Labelling Framework: LLM-Driven Data Correction and Enrichment via Semantic Extraction of Reliability Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31281)
*Max Malyi, Jonathan Shek, Alasdair McDonald, Andre Biscaya*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: AIGC

Importance: 普通相关 (35.83) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于LLM的框架，用于自动标准化和结构化风力涡轮机维护日志，校正层级系统编码错误，提取故障模式和维修动作的实证分类体系，将非结构化文本转化为定量可靠性指标，在16,316条日志上实现了超过70%的结构化率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风力涡轮机维护日志包含大量非结构化的自然语言描述，无法直接用于定量可靠性分析。现有手动标注方法主观性强、成本高、难以规模化，亟需一种自动化、可扩展的方法来从历史维护记录中提取可靠性情报。

Method: 提出一个模型无关的LLM驱动流水线：(1) 基于系统编码的日志分批处理；(2) LLM自动校正层级系统编码（如识别并修正变桨系统误分类）；(3) 提取实证分类体系（维修动作、故障模式）；(4) 构建故障模式、可观察症状、主导机制和候选原因的实证词典，减少人工FMEA的主观性。

Result: 在280台涡轮机9年间的16,316条维护日志上，该流水线成功结构化超过70%的数据，纠正了系统编码错误（如隔离了之前未分类的变桨系统故障），恢复了缺失的系统编码，并利用经验分类体系标注了具体的维修动作和故障模式。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种高度可扩展、低成本的方案，将大量定性现场观测数据转化为定量可靠性指标，为可再生能源领域的根因分析、改进FMEA和预测性维护奠定了基础。

Classification reason: 该论文利用LLM对风力涡轮机维护日志进行结构化标注、数据校正与语义增强，属于能源/工业领域的垂直应用，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "领域内容生成应用"（aigc_domain_application）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on wind turbine maintenance logs, far outside user's core interests in multimodal generation and autoregressive video/image generation. Signals: domain-specific application, LLM for data correction, no connection to user's priority subtopics, negative keywords present (e.g., "maintenance")

Abstract: As wind turbine fleets age, data-driven reliability engineering is essential to optimise their operation and maintenance for service life extension and levelised cost of energy reduction. Failure event descriptions within historical maintenance logs are a source of valuable reliability intelligence. However, they typically appear as unstructured natural language entries, rendering them inaccessible for quantitative analysis. This paper presents a novel methodology leveraging a large language model (LLM) to systematically standardise and structure maintenance logs based on their free-text descriptors. Operating on a dataset of 16,316 maintenance logs from 280 turbines monitored over nine years, the developed model-agnostic framework autonomously corrected hierarchical system codes and extracted evidence-based taxonomies of maintenance actions and failure modes. The automated pipeline successfully structured over 70% of the dataset. It resolved pervasive misclassification issues, such as isolating previously unclassified pitch system faults and restoring missing system codes, and enriched the records by applying empirical taxonomies to label specific actions taken and failure modes addressed. By using system-based log batches to construct empirical dictionaries of failure modes, observable symptoms, dominant mechanisms, and candidate causes, this approach reduces the inherent subjectivity of manual failure modes and effects analysis (FMEA). Ultimately, the methodology provides a highly scalable, cost-effective blueprint for translating large sets of qualitative field observations into quantitative reliability metrics, laying the foundation for integrated root-cause analysis across the renewable energy sector, improved FMEA, and advanced predictive maintenance.

</details>


### [142] [Astra: a generalizable report generation foundation model for 3D computed tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31437)
*Zhuhao Wang, Fang Chen, Chaohui Yu, Zihan Li, Yuchao Zheng, Jing Wang, Xuan Yang, Jia Guo, Zhenlu Yang, Xingju Zheng, Yihua Sun, Haojie Han, Xiaoxiao Qin, Zhan Feng, Wenbo Xiao, Chao Zhu, Yuehua Li, Shipeng Zhang, Hao Luo, Yunsong Peng, Fan Wang, Hongen Liao*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 领域内容生成应用

Importance: 普通相关 (38.16) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Astra 是一个面向3D CT影像的通用报告生成基础模型，在9万余对胸腹部CT-报告数据上训练，通过报告风格统一和强化学习诊断一致性优化，在多个外部数据集上达到SOTA，并在真实临床流程中显著提升报告效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CT影像报告需要放射科医生逐层审阅数百张切片，耗时且高度依赖专家经验。现有自动报告生成方法缺乏支持多区域报告且跨外部真实队列鲁棒的通用基础模型，不同队列间报告风格和诊断术语的不一致性导致简单联合训练受噪声文本监督限制，模型泛化能力不足。

Method: Astra 构建了包含90,678对胸腹部CT-报告（CTRgDB）的训练集，涵盖8个器官系统的353,671个异常发现。首先通过报告风格统一（harmonization）处理跨队列风格不一致问题，再通过强化学习进一步优化诊断一致性，实现风格统一且诊断准确的报告生成。

Result: 在CTRgDB和6个外部队列上，Astra 达到SOTA性能，细粒度诊断指标平均提升44.1%（P<0.001）。在真实临床流程中，Astra辅助使胸部报告起草速度提升29.6%，腹部报告完整性提升11.3%（P<0.001）。此外，Astra作为CT AI开发的基础模型，通过高质量报告合成提升下游诊断性能和视觉语言预训练。

Conclusion: Astra 是一个通用、可泛化的CT报告生成基础模型，在多个解剖区域和机构间实现风格一致且诊断准确的报告生成，显著提升临床效率，并为下一代AI医疗基础设施提供了关键支撑。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于CT影像报告自动生成，属于医疗垂直领域的生成应用，与现有 canonical 子主题"领域内容生成应用"（aigc_domain_application）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical report generation, which is outside your configured priority subtopics and has negative keywords like medical diagnosis. Signals: 3D CT report generation, clinical workflow efficiency, reinforcement learning for diagnostic consistency, domain-specific medical application

Abstract: CT interpretation requires radiologists to review hundreds of volumetric slices per examination, making reporting time-consuming and highly expertise-dependent. Automated CT report generation offers a promising route to improving clinical efficiency, yet the field still lacks a generalizable CT report generation foundation model that supports multi-region reporting and remains robust across external real-world cohorts. Intrinsic inconsistencies in reporting style and diagnostic terminology across cohorts make naive joint training prone to noisy textual supervision, thereby limiting model generalizability. Here we present Astra, a generalizable CT report generation foundation model trained on 90,678 thoracoabdominal CT-report pairs (CTRgDB) with 353,671 abnormalities spanning eight organ systems. By harmonizing report style and further refining diagnostic consistency via reinforcement learning, Astra achieves style-consistent and diagnostically accurate report generation across diverse anatomical regions and institutions. Evaluating on CTRgDB and six external cohorts, Astra achieves state-of-the-art performance with a 44.1% average improvement in fine-grained diagnostic metrics (P<0.001). In real-world clinical workflows, Astra assistance accelerates chest report drafting by 29.6% and improves abdominal report completeness by 11.3% (P<0.001). Furthermore, Astra also demonstrates broad utility as a foundation for CT AI development, improving downstream diagnostic performance and scaling vision-language pretrain through high-quality report synthesis. Overall, Astra serves as a broadly accessible clinical assistant and a pivotal infrastructure for the next generation of AI-powered healthcare.

</details>


### [143] [Target-Side Paraphrase Augmentation for Sign Language Translation with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31393)
*Pedro Dal Bianco, Jean Paul Nunes Reinhold, Oscar Stanchi, Facundo Quiroga, Franco Ronchetti, Ulisses Brisolara Corrêa*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CL

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 领域内容生成应用

Importance: 普通相关 (37.4) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出利用 GPT-4o 对目标端参考句子进行受控释义增强，以缓解手语翻译中配对数据稀缺和词汇长尾分布问题。在 Signformer 架构上采用两阶段训练（先预训练于增强语料，再微调于原始参考），在 PHOENIX14T 上 BLEU-4 从 9.56 提升至 10.33，并首次将 LLM 生成的目标端释义和 LLM-as-a-Judge 评估引入手语翻译领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手语翻译面临配对手语-视频/文本语料库规模有限和目标端词汇长尾分布严重的双重挑战，传统数据增强方法难以有效缓解。

Method: 使用 GPT-4o 对参考句子生成受控释义变体（保持语义不变但改变表达方式），保持手语输入不变；采用 Signformer 风格的基于姿态的 Transformer 模型，进行两阶段训练：先在增强后的语料上预训练，再在原始参考句子上微调；使用 LLM-as-a-Judge 进行语义保真度评估。

Result: 在 PHOENIX14T（德语手语）上 BLEU-4 从 9.56 提升至 10.33；在 GSL（希腊手语，近饱和基线）和 LSA-T（阿根廷手语，极端稀疏）上效果有限，揭示了方法的边界条件。

Conclusion: 目标端释义增强能有效提升手语翻译性能，尤其在词汇多样性适中的场景下；语义评估显示忠实度提升被词汇重叠指标低估；该方法在近饱和或极端稀疏场景下效果有限。

Classification reason: 该论文研究手语翻译（SLT），属于垂直领域（手语）的生成/翻译应用，与现有 canonical 子主题 "领域内容生成应用"（aigc_domain_application）高度匹配。同时涉及多模态（视频-文本）生成，因此也匹配 multimodal_generation 方向。

Importance reason: Focuses on sign language translation augmentation, which is outside all priority subtopics and uses negative keywords. Signals: Sign language translation, LLM paraphrase augmentation, low BLEU-4 gains, domain-specific application, no overlap with priority subtopics

Abstract: Sign language translation (SLT) remains constrained by limited paired sign-video/text corpora and heavy-tailed target vocabularies. We study target-side augmentation in which GPT-4o generates controlled paraphrase variants of reference sentences while the sign input remains unchanged. A Signformer-style pose-based Transformer is trained under a two-stage schedule: pre-training on the augmented corpus followed by fine-tuning on the original references. We evaluate on three datasets spanning complementary challenges: PHOENIX14T (German Sign Language), with moderate lexical diversity; GSL (Greek Sign Language), with highly ontrolled, repetitive recordings; and LSA-T (Argentinian Sign Language), with severe long-tail sparsity. On PHOENIX14T, augmentation improves BLEU-4 from 9.56 to 10.33. The near-saturated GSL baseline and extremely sparse LSA-T setting reveal the limits of the approach. To our knowledge, this is the first study to apply LLM-generated target-side araphrases and LLM-as-a-Judge evaluation to SLT. The semantic evaluation reveals gains in fidelity that lexical overlap metrics understate.

</details>


### [144] [Reliable Multilingual Orthopedic Decision Support from Clinical Narratives: Language-Aware Adaptation and Verification-Guided Deferral](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31512)
*Danish Ali, Li Xiaojian, Sundas Iqbal, Farrukh Zaidi*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 领域内容生成应用

Importance: 普通相关 (27.65) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向多语言（英语、印地语、旁遮普语）骨科临床叙述的可靠性导向分类框架，通过语言感知的适配器增强 IndicBERT（IndicBERT-HPA），并结合置信度门控、证据一致性和语言风险筛查的选择性验证层，在自然临床分布下达到 0.8792 Macro-F1，选择性验证在 72.3% 覆盖率下达到 84.4% 准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言骨科临床决策支持在低资源医疗场景中面临专业术语、混合脚本、不完整证据、标签不平衡和语言依赖的文档模式等挑战，现有方法缺乏针对这些挑战的可靠性导向框架。

Method: 对比了任务对齐的多语言 Transformer 编码器、微调 DistilBERT、零样本指令微调 LLM 和领域自适应编码器 IndicBERT-HPA。IndicBERT-HPA 在 IndicBERT 基础上添加语言感知的骨科适配器头，实现多语言临床相关表示学习。进一步实现确定性选择性验证层，结合置信度门控、证据一致性检查和语言风险筛查。

Result: 在自然临床分布下，IndicBERT-HPA 达到平均 Macro-F1 0.8792、Macro-AUROC 0.894、AUPRC 0.902。零样本 LLM 在封闭集分类中远不如任务适配编码器。选择性验证层在 5000 条随机保留子集上达到 84.4% 选择性准确率和 0.76 选择性 Macro-F1（72.3% 覆盖率），优于全接受基线（71.5% 准确率，0.65 Macro-F1）。

Conclusion: 任务适配的编码器（特别是语言感知的 IndicBERT-HPA）在多语言骨科临床分类中显著优于零样本 LLM，结合选择性验证的 deferral 机制能有效提升可靠性，为低资源多语言临床决策支持提供了实用方案。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于多语言骨科临床决策支持，属于医疗垂直领域的自然语言处理与分类应用，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题"领域内容生成应用"（aigc_domain_application）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on multilingual clinical NLP for orthopedics, which is outside the user's core interests in multimodal generation, video/image generation, and efficient models. Signals: domain-specific clinical NLP, multilingual orthopedic classification, selective verification deferral, low-resource healthcare setting, no generative or multimodal focus

Abstract: Multilingual orthopedic decision support remains challenging in low-resource healthcare settings, where clinical narratives contain specialized terminology, mixed scripts, incomplete evidence, label imbalance and language-dependent documentation patterns. This article presents a reliability-oriented framework for classifying free-text orthopedic notes in English, Hindi and Punjabi. We compare task-aligned multilingual transformer encoders, a task-fine-tuned DistilBERT baseline, zero-shot instruction-tuned large language models (LLMs) and a domain-adaptive encoder, IndicBERT-HPA. IndicBERT-HPA augments IndicBERT with language-aware orthopedic adapter heads to support clinically relevant multilingual representation learning. Evaluation extends beyond aggregate accuracy to per-class performance, ROC-AUC, AUPRC, expected calibration error, cross-language stability and robustness under controlled balanced and natural-prevalence distributions. The evaluated zero-shot LLMs remain substantially less effective than task-adapted encoders for closed-set classification, with language-dependent instability. Under natural clinical prevalence, IndicBERT-HPA achieves the strongest overall performance, reaching an averaged Macro-F1 of 0.8792, Macro-AUROC of 0.894 and AUPRC of 0.902. We further implement a deterministic selective-verification layer combining confidence gating, evidence-consistency checking and language-risk screening. On a randomly selected held-out 5,000-record subset, it achieves 84.4% selective accuracy and 0.76 selective Macro-F1 at 72.3% coverage, compared with 71.5% accuracy and 0.65 Macro-F1 for accept-all prediction. These results support reliability-oriented multilingual clinical decision support with explicit deferral.

</details>


### [145] [Improving Small Language Models for Code Generation with Reinforcement Learning from Verification Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30478)
*Egor Skopin, Evgeny Kotelnikov*

Subtopic: 领域内容生成应用

Importance: 普通相关 (45.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了使用可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）提升小语言模型（Qwen3-0.6B 和 Llama3.2-1B）的 Python 代码生成能力。通过对比单元测试奖励、静态分析（Ruff linter）奖励以及组合奖励，结合 GRPO 和 GSPO 优化方法，发现组合奖励在 MBPP 基准上 pass@1 提升高达 13 个百分点，而纯静态分析奖励会导致模型偏向生成更短但功能正确性不稳定的代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小语言模型在代码生成任务上性能有限，强化学习（尤其是可验证奖励 RLVR）有望通过程序化检查信号（如单元测试结果）直接优化功能正确性，但奖励设计对效果的影响尚缺乏系统研究。

Method: 使用 Qwen3-0.6B 和 Llama3.2-1B 两个小模型，在 MBPP 基准上进行 LoRA 微调。设计了三种奖励公式：纯单元测试奖励、纯静态分析（Ruff linter）惩罚奖励、以及两者组合奖励。采用 GRPO（Group-based Policy Optimization）和 GSPO（Group-based Supervised Policy Optimization）两种策略优化方法进行对比实验。

Result: 组合奖励配置下，MBPP 测试集上 pass@1 最高提升 13 个百分点。纯静态分析奖励会导致模型生成更短代码以减少 lint 错误，但功能正确性提升不稳定。组合奖励能缓解这种退化，在正确性和代码风格之间取得更稳定的权衡。

Conclusion: RLVR 对代码生成的有效性高度依赖于奖励设计和优化粒度，仅靠 pass@1 不足以全面评估模型行为变化，需要结合生成长度、Ruff 严重度分布和执行错误类型等诊断指标来识别失败模式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究使用强化学习（RLVR）提升小语言模型的代码生成能力，属于代码生成这一垂直领域应用。现有 taxonomy 中 aigc_domain_application（领域内容生成应用）明确包含代码生成，且该 subtopic 在 aigc 和 multimodal_generation 两个方向下均有定义，因此直接复用。

Importance reason: Focuses on code generation for small models, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to multimodal generation or video/image generation. Signals: code generation, small language models, reinforcement learning, verification feedback, not multimodal

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) trains language models using programmatically checkable signals such as unit-test outcomes, enabling direct optimization for functional correctness in code generation. We conduct an empirical study of RLVR for Python code generation on the MBPP benchmark using two small models (Qwen3-0.6B and Llama3.2-1B) with LoRA fine-tuning. Across multiple reward formulations such as: unit-test-only rewards, static-analysis-only shaping via the Ruff linter, and a combined reward, we compare group-based policy optimization variants (GRPO and GSPO) and evaluate both functional correctness and behavioral diagnostics. In our experimental setting, RLVR improves pass@1 on MBPP test by up to 13 percentage points under proposed combined reward configuration. However, we find that reward shaping can induce systematic behavioral shifts: using only static-analysis penalties may bias the policy toward shorter completions that reduce lint errors without reliably improving functional correctness. In contrast, combined rewards mitigate this degeneration and yield more stable trade-offs between correctness and style constraints. Overall, our results highlight that RLVR effectiveness for code generation is highly sensitive to reward design and optimization granularity, and that diagnostics beyond pass@1, including generation length, Ruff severity profiles, and execution error types are useful for identifying failure modes.

</details>


### [146] [Knowledge Boundary Probing and Demand-Guided Intervention for LLM-Based Power System Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31478)
*Hui Wu, Xiaoyang Wang, Zhong Fan*

Main category: cs.CL, eess.SY, cs.SE

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 领域内容生成应用

Importance: 普通相关 (41.51) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 PowerCodeBench 基准和知识边界感知干预方法，用于提升 LLM 在电力系统代码生成中的可靠性。通过探测模型对 pandapower API 的知识边界，结合主动文档注入和路由式纠错，在 2000 个任务上使 7B 以上开源模型和商业 API 提升 32-56 个准确率点，且仅使用 41% 的 prompt token 成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力系统分析需要高可靠性的代码生成，但许多机构因保密、监管、可复现性和成本原因要求本地部署 LLM。开源模型在电力系统代码生成中首次失败的主要原因并非推理能力不足，而是结构化的 API 知识边界错误（如幻觉函数名、参数误用、结果表处理错误），亟需一种不依赖微调或云端推理的部署时可靠性提升方法。

Method: 1) 构建 PowerCodeBench：一个执行验证的基准生成器，将自然语言操作员查询与 pandapower 代码和数值真值配对；2) 提出 L0-L3 文档驱动探测流程，测量每个模型的 API 知识轮廓；3) 设计边界感知干预方法，结合查询侧 API 需求估计、目标性主动文档注入和路由式反应性纠错。

Result: 在 2000 个冻结任务上评估了 10 个开源 LLM（1.5B-480B 参数）和 4 个商业中端 API。干预使每个 7B 以上开源模型和所有商业 API 提升 32-56 个准确率点。70B-120B 开源模型达到商业中端 API 的准确率水平，Llama-3.1-405B 和 Qwen3-Coder-480B 表现最佳。目标性提示在保持全上下文准确率上限的同时，仅使用 41% 的 prompt token 成本。

Conclusion: 本文证明了通过知识边界探测和需求引导的文档注入，可以在不进行微调或云端推理的情况下，显著提升开源 LLM 在电力系统代码生成中的可靠性，为本地部署的 LLM 辅助电网分析工作流提供了一条实用路径。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦电力系统领域的代码生成，属于垂直领域（能源/电力）的生成应用，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "领域内容生成应用"（aigc_domain_application）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on domain-specific code generation for power systems, not aligned with user's priority subtopics in multimodal generation, video/image generation, or efficient inference. Signals: Power system code generation, API knowledge boundary probing, domain-specific benchmark, not multimodal or generative vision, negative keywords (medical, clinical) not triggered but irrelevant to user's core interests

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to automate power-system analysis, but many utilities and energy-research labs require on-premise serving for confidentiality, regulatory, reproducibility, and cost reasons. This makes the reliability of open-weight models a deployment issue. We show that first-pass failures in power-system code generation are dominated not by reasoning alone, but by structured API-knowledge boundary errors: hallucinated function names, misused parameters, and mishandled result tables in versioned simulation libraries. We introduce PowerCodeBench, an execution-validated benchmark generator that pairs natural-language operator queries with pandapower code and numerical ground truth; an L0-L3 documentation-driven probing procedure that measures per-model API knowledge profiles; and a boundary-aware intervention that combines query-side API demand estimation with targeted proactive documentation injection and routed reactive correction. On a 2,000-task frozen release, we evaluate ten open-weight LLMs (1.5B-480B parameters) and four commercial mid-tier APIs. The intervention improves every evaluated open-weight model of at least 7B parameters and every commercial API by 32 to 56 accuracy points. Open-weight models in the 70B-120B range match the commercial mid-tier accuracy range, while Llama-3.1-405B and Qwen3-Coder-480B lead the panel. The targeted prompts preserve the full-context accuracy ceiling while using 41% of the prompt-token cost. The result is an accuracy-side, deployment-time path toward reliable on-premise LLM assistance for grid-analysis workflows without fine-tuning or cloud inference.

</details>
