### [147] [GUI-C$^2$: Coarse-to-Fine GUI Grounding via Difficulty-Aware Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30884)
*Junlong Li, Chao Hao, Lap-Pui Chau, Yi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: AI 智能体与生成工作流

Importance: 普通相关 (59.89) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 GUI-C²，一种基于难度感知强化学习的粗到细 GUI 视觉定位方法。在数据层面，提出 GUI-D 数据挖掘与难度评分流水线，筛选高价值训练样本并分配难度权重；在策略层面，提出区域门控的粗到细精炼机制，通过模型内部不确定性信号逐步缩小视野，并辅以改进感知的阶段奖励来确保每次精炼都有效提升定位精度。实验表明该方法达到了 SOTA 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于智能体强化学习的 GUI 视觉定位方法存在两个层面的局限：数据层面，所有训练样本被同等对待，忽略了不同难度样本对基线模型的训练价值差异，导致训练效率低下甚至崩溃；策略层面，现有框架难以平衡裁剪大区域以获取充分上下文与裁剪小区域以减少冗余之间的权衡，且复杂决策对小参数模型不友好、推理时间过长。

Method: 数据层面提出 GUI-D 流水线：通过适当的测试筛选出有价值的训练样本，并为每个样本分配难度分数以指导后续训练权重。策略层面提出 GUI-C²：采用区域门控的粗到细精炼机制，通过模型内部的不确定性信号逐步缩小视觉范围，自适应地为大目标保留上下文、为小目标放大精度；同时引入改进感知的阶段奖励（improvement-aware stage rewards），确保每次精炼都真正提升定位效果。此外简化了决策过程以大幅减少额外推理时间。

Result: 在多个 GUI 视觉定位基准上进行了大量实验，结果表明所提方法达到了 SOTA 性能。

Conclusion: 本文通过数据层面的难度感知筛选与策略层面的粗到细精炼机制，有效解决了 GUI 视觉定位中训练效率低下和上下文-冗余权衡的问题，为 GUI 智能体提供了更高效、更精确的定位能力。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 GUI 智能体中的视觉定位（GUI grounding）问题，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的 AI 智能体与生成工作流（aigc_agent_workflow）范畴。论文提出的粗到细定位机制和强化学习训练策略属于智能体工具使用与自动化工作流优化，与现有 canonical 子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on GUI grounding for agents, not directly on your priority subtopics like continuous VLM, autoregressive video/image generation, or world models. Signals: GUI grounding, reinforcement learning, coarse-to-fine refinement, difficulty-aware training, agentic AI

Abstract: Existing agentic reinforcement learning methods for GUI grounding have limitations at two levels. At the data level, current approaches typically treat all training samples equally, although their training value to the baseline model varies with difficulty. Overlooking this can greatly reduce training efficiency or even cause collapse. At the strategy level, existing frameworks struggle to balance the trade-off between cropping larger regions for sufficient context and smaller ones for reduced redundancy, a tension inherent to tool-augmented grounding agents. In addition, overly complex decision-making is difficult for small-parameter models and significantly increases inference time. To address these issues, at the data level, we propose GUI-D, a data mining and difficulty scoring pipeline that identifies the training-worthy samples by proper testing and assigns difficulty scores to guide subsequent training weights. At the strategy level, we propose GUI-C$^2$, which employs an area-gated coarse-to-fine refinement mechanism that progressively narrows the visual field via model-internal uncertainty signals, adaptively reserving context for large targets while amplifying precision for small ones, reinforced by improvement-aware stage rewards that ensure each refinement genuinely advances grounding. Meanwhile, we simplify the decision-making process to greatly reduce additional inference time. Finally, extensive experiments show that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance. The code and data will be publicly available.

</details>


### [148] [Task-Focused Memorization for Multimodal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31075)
*Tao Zou, Yichen He, Tian Qiu, Yuan Lin, Hang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: AI 智能体与生成工作流

Importance: 高优先级 (83.26) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 TaskMem，一种基于强化学习的任务聚焦记忆策略学习框架，使多模态智能体能够动态选择性地记忆与未来任务相关的多模态观测内容，在流式视频问答基准上显著提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态智能体（如具身智能体）持续接收无限的多模态观测流，面临信息组合爆炸问题。现有记忆方法主要关注记忆模块设计或记忆的准确性与保真度，但缺乏对"应该记忆什么"这一核心问题的学习机制。智能体需要选择性保留与自身角色相关且对未来任务有价值的内容。

Method: TaskMem 将记忆生成建模为可学习的记忆策略，采用两阶段训练范式：第一阶段学习如何记忆，在基本保真度要求下优化记忆质量；第二阶段在部署后，通过强化学习在基础 MLLM 上微调适配器，利用环境中的实际任务定义奖励模型，引导记忆策略聚焦于任务相关的内容。基于 Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B 实现。

Result: 在重新构建的流式 VideoMME、EgoLife 和 EgoTempo 基准上，TaskMem 的 VQA 准确率分别提升 6.3%、7.0% 和 5.3%。

Conclusion: 将记忆生成建模为可学习的策略，通过强化学习根据实际任务需求动态调整记忆焦点，能有效提升多模态智能体在流式环境中的长期记忆能力和任务表现。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多模态智能体的长期记忆策略，属于多模态智能体（multimodal agent）工作流范畴。在 multimodal_understanding_generation 方向下，aigc_agent_workflow（AI 智能体与生成工作流）是唯一匹配的现有 subtopic，涵盖 LLM 智能体、工具使用、自动化等关键词。同时论文也涉及 AIGC 方向下的智能体工作流。

Importance reason: Strongly relevant to multimodal agents and memory, but not directly aligned with top-priority subtopics like continuous VLM or autoregressive generation. Signals: multimodal agent memory, reinforcement learning memorization policy, streaming video QA, Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B, task-focused memorization

Abstract: Long-term memory is essential for multimodal agents to build coherent experience, accumulate world knowledge, and achieve continual learning. However, constructing effective memory goes beyond memory module design and basic requirements such as accuracy and fidelity; the key challenge lies in determining what to memorize. Multimodal agents, such as embodied agents, continuously perceive, reason, and act in real or virtual environments, receiving an unbounded stream of multimodal observations. From this combinatorial explosion of information, an agent must selectively retain content that is relevant to its role in the environment and valuable for future tasks. To bridge this gap, we frame memory generation as a learnable memorization policy and introduce TaskMem (Task-focused Memorization Policy Learning), a reinforcement-learning-based framework that enables the policy to dynamically adjust its focus to the demands of real tasks encountered in the environment. TaskMem adopts a two-phase training paradigm: Phase One learns how to memorize by optimizing memory quality under fundamental fidelity requirements; Phase Two occurs after deployment, where the agent learns what to memorize by tuning an adapter on its base MLLM, using recent environment tasks to define a reward model that guides the memorization policy toward task-relevant content. To evaluate our approach, we reformulate VideoMME, EgoLife, and EgoTempo into streaming benchmarks that simulate a realistic setting in which an agent processes streaming observations and handles tasks arriving online. To isolate memory assessment, the questions must be answered using only the agent's memory, without access to raw video. Built on Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B, TaskMem improves VQA accuracy by 6.3%, 7.0%, and 5.3% on these benchmarks, respectively.

</details>


### [149] [Detect in Any Scene: An Agentic Framework for Object Detection with Experience-Aware Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31174)
*Wenlun Zhang, Jun Yin, Kentaro Yoshioka*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: AI 智能体与生成工作流

Importance: 高优先级 (81.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 DetAS/DetAS-X，一个基于 MLLM 智能体的目标检测框架，将目标检测建模为动态决策过程，通过自适应图像恢复和多专家检测器组合，并引入经验积累机制实现经验感知推理，在多个复杂场景检测基准上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有目标检测器在真实场景中因图像退化（如低光照、模糊）和物体分布异质性而泛化能力不足，传统方法依赖预定义条件且缺乏对动态环境的适应性。

Method: 提出 DetAS 框架，以 MLLM 为核心智能体，包含两个关键组件：(1) 自适应图像恢复模块，动态决定是否及如何增强图像；(2) 多专家检测模块，集成多个领域专用检测器并通过实例级推理融合预测。进一步提出 DetAS-X，引入自进化经验收集机制，从少量标注数据中积累节点级决策经验，实现推理时的经验感知推理。

Result: 在六个具有挑战性的基准上，DetAS-X 相比现有 MLLM 检测器平均 F1 提升 28.36%，在 DarkFace 上提升高达 37.01%。

Conclusion: 智能体式检测框架在复杂动态环境中展现出显著优势，为将目标检测从静态流水线转向自适应决策过程提供了有效范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于 MLLM 智能体的目标检测框架，通过智能体动态组合恢复模块和检测器来构建检测工作流，属于 LLM 智能体与自动化工作流范畴，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 的 aigc_agent_workflow（AI 智能体与生成工作流）。

Importance reason: Agentic detection framework with MLLM reasoning, but not aligned with top priority subtopics like continuous VLM or autoregressive generation. Signals: MLLM agent, object detection, adaptive restoration, multi-expertise detection, experience-aware reasoning

Abstract: Object detection in real-world scenarios remains challenging due to diverse image degradations and heterogeneous object distributions, which significantly hinder the generalization of existing detectors. Conventional approaches, including scene-specific representation learning and end-to-end pipeline design, are inherently limited by their reliance on predefined conditions and lack adaptability to dynamic environments. In this paper, we propose DetAS, an agentic detection framework that formulates object detection as a dynamic decision process. Instead of relying on static pipelines, DetAS leverages a Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM) as a central agent to adaptively compose detection workflows by selecting from a toolbox of restoration modules and specialized detectors. Specifically, DetAS consists of two key components: Self-Adaptive Image Restoration, which dynamically determines whether and how to enhance images for downstream detection, and Multi-Expertise Detection, which integrates multiple domain-specialized detectors and resolves their predictions through instance-level reasoning. To further improve decision quality under fine-grained conditions, we introduce Self-Evolving Experience Harvesting and extend the framework to DetAS-X, which accumulates node-level decision experience from a small set of annotated data and enables experience-aware reasoning during inference. This mechanism allows the system to progressively refine its decision policy and adapt to diverse real-world scenarios. Extensive experiments on six challenging benchmarks demonstrate that DetAS-X significantly outperforms existing MLLM-based detectors, achieving an average improvement of 28.36% in F1 score, with up to 37.01% gain on DarkFace. These results demonstrate the promise of agentic detection and establish a solid foundation for its application in complex and dynamic environments.

</details>


### [150] [DeMaVLA: A Vision-Language-Action Foundation Model for Generalizable Deformable Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31286)
*Taiyi Su, Jian Zhu, Tianjian Wang, Youzhang He, Zitai Huang, Jianjun Zhang, Chong Ma, Hanyang Wang, Tianjiao Zhang, Munan Yin, Weihao Ding, Yi Xu*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.RO

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: AI 智能体与生成工作流

Importance: 高优先级 (88.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 DeMaVLA，一个面向可变形物体操作的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）基础模型。它采用 VLM 骨干网络与动作专家模块，通过流匹配（flow matching）生成连续动作，并通过隔层剪枝策略提升效率。模型在约 5000 小时真实双臂演示数据上预训练，再通过人类参与的 DAgger 数据聚合流水线进行后训练。实验表明 DeMaVLA 在 RoboTwin 和家庭折叠基准上表现优异，展示了可扩展真实数据、高效动作生成和纠正学习对通用 VLA 策略的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 系统通常为不同物体类别分别训练策略，而简单混合多任务训练常导致任务干扰和性能下降。可变形物体（如衣物）折叠是代表性挑战，需要机器人从随机初始状态处理不同类别、几何形状、材料和场景的物体。作者希望超越类别特定的折叠策略，构建一个通用的 VLA 基础模型。

Method: DeMaVLA 采用 VLM 骨干网络（如 SigLIP + Qwen2）与动作专家模块，使用流匹配（flow matching）生成连续动作。动作专家通过隔层剪枝（pruning every other transformer layer）构建，同时保持与 VLM 骨干的逐层对齐，减少训练和推理成本。先在约 5000 小时真实双臂演示数据上预训练获取通用操作先验，再通过人类参与的 DAgger 数据聚合流水线，在混合折叠数据上进行后训练，聚合自收集演示和真实机器人失败中的纠正轨迹。

Result: DeMaVLA 在 RoboTwin 基准上取得有竞争力的性能，并在家庭折叠基准上展示了强大的真实世界结果。实验验证了可扩展真实数据、高效动作生成和纠正学习对通用 VLA 策略的有效性。

Conclusion: DeMaVLA 证明了通过大规模真实世界数据预训练、高效动作生成和 DAgger 纠正学习，可以构建超越类别特定策略的通用可变形物体操作 VLA 基础模型，为家庭机器人操作提供了有前景的方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 DeMaVLA，一个面向可变形物体操作的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）基础模型，涉及机器人操作智能体、数据聚合工作流（DAgger）和自动化操作流水线。这与 multimodal_understanding_generation 方向下的 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "AI 智能体与生成工作流"（aigc_agent_workflow）高度匹配，因为该子主题涵盖 LLM 智能体、工作流、工具使用和自动化流水线，VLA 模型正是多模态理解与机器人动作生成的智能体系统。

Importance reason: Strong on flow matching for continuous action generation and efficient VLM pruning, but focuses on deformable manipulation robotics, not core multimodal generation. Signals: flow matching for continuous action, efficient VLM pruning, real-world robot data scaling, DAgger corrective learning, not core video/image generation

Abstract: Real-world household robots require Vision-Language-Action (VLA) foundation models that can acquire reusable manipulation skills across diverse objects, task conditions, and household environments. Deformable-object folding is a representative challenge, requiring robots to handle clothing items from random initial states across varying categories, geometries, materials, and scenes. However, existing VLA systems commonly train separate policies for different object categories, while naively mixed multi-task training often suffers from task interference and degraded performance. To move beyond category-specific folding policies, we introduce DeMaVLA, a VLA foundation model for generalizable Deformable Manipulation. DeMaVLA adopts a VLM backbone with an action expert and formulates continuous action generation using flow matching. To improve efficiency, the action expert is constructed by pruning every other transformer layer while preserving layer-wise alignment with the VLM backbone, reducing training and inference cost. DeMaVLA is first pre-trained on approximately 5,000 hours of selected real-world dual-arm demonstrations to acquire general manipulation priors. It is then post-trained on mixed folding data that aggregates self-collected demonstrations and corrective trajectories from real-robot failures across multiple folding tasks through a human-in-the-loop Data Aggregation~(DAgger) pipeline. Experiments show that DeMaVLA achieves competitive performance on RoboTwin and strong real-world results on our household folding benchmark. These results highlight the value of scalable real-world data, efficient action generation, and corrective learning for general-purpose VLA policies in deformable-object manipulation.

</details>

## 多模态安全与攻防 [[Back]](#toc)

多模态越狱、对抗攻击、防御、安全评测。

### [151] [Hide-and-Seek in Trajectories: Discovering Failure Signals for VLA Runtime Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30834)
*Seongheon Park, Wendi Li, Changdae Oh, Samuel Yeh, Zsolt Kira, Michael Hagenow, Sharon Li*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.RO

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 普通相关 (57.96) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Hide-and-Seek 框架，将 VLA 模型的执行故障检测建模为粗监督学习问题，通过轨迹间和轨迹内对比学习，仅利用轨迹级标签即可定位故障指示性动作，无需逐帧标注。在 LIBERO、VLABench 和真实机器人平台上，使用 OpenVLA、π₀ 和 π₀.₅ 三种策略，实现了多任务故障检测的 SOTA 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型在真实部署中容易发生执行故障，现有故障检测方法要么依赖昂贵的动作重采样或外部模型，要么将轨迹级标签均匀传播到每个时间步，掩盖了局部故障信号。需要一种仅利用轨迹级粗粒度标签就能定位细粒度故障动作的方法。

Method: 提出 Hide-and-Seek 框架，将 VLA 故障检测建模为粗监督学习问题。设计两种对比学习目标：(1) 轨迹间对比——区分成功与失败轨迹的整体表征；(2) 轨迹内对比——在同一轨迹内区分故障指示性动作与正常动作。通过联合优化，模型仅从轨迹级标签中学习到时间结构化的故障信号，无需逐帧标注。

Result: 在 LIBERO、VLABench 和真实机器人平台上，使用 OpenVLA、π₀ 和 π₀.₅ 三种 VLA 策略进行评测。方法在多任务故障检测上达到 SOTA 性能，在保形预测框架下实现了准确的精度-时效性权衡，并且对已见和未见任务均具有良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: Hide-and-Seek 通过粗监督对比学习有效解决了 VLA 故障检测中轨迹级标签与细粒度故障定位之间的鸿沟，为机器人系统的鲁棒部署提供了一种实用且高效的运行时监控方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 VLA（视觉-语言-动作）模型在执行过程中的故障检测，属于多模态系统在真实部署中的安全性与可靠性问题。这与 [CANONICAL] 多模态安全与攻防（multimodal_safety_attack）主题高度相关，该主题涵盖多模态系统的安全评测、防御与可靠性提升。

Importance reason: Focuses on VLA runtime monitoring, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous VLM or autoregressive generation. Signals: VLA failure detection, coarse supervision, contrastive learning, robot execution monitoring, OpenVLA

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models enable robots to follow natural language instructions and generalize across diverse tasks, but they remain vulnerable to execution failures that compromise reliability in real-world deployment. Detecting such failures during execution is therefore critical for the robust deployment of embodied systems. Existing failure detection methods either rely on expensive action resampling or external models, while alternatives propagate trajectory-level labels uniformly across every timestep, obscuring localized failure signals. In this paper, we propose \textbf{Hide-and-Seek}, a framework that formulates VLA failure detection as a coarsely supervised learning problem. By combining inter-trajectory and intra-trajectory contrastive objectives, Hide-and-Seek localizes failure-indicative actions and induces temporally structured failure signals from trajectory-level supervision alone, without any step-level annotation. We evaluate Hide-and-Seek on LIBERO, VLABench, and a real-world robotic platform across three representative VLA policies: OpenVLA, $\pi_0$, and $\pi_{0.5}$.Our method achieves state-of-the-art multi-task failure detection performance with a practical accuracy--timeliness trade-off under conformal prediction, and generalizes well to both seen and unseen tasks.

</details>

### [152] [Vision-Language Models Suppress Female Representations Under Ambiguous Input](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31556)
*Arnau Marin-Llobet, Simon Henniger, Mahzarin R. Banaji*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.CY, cs.CL, cs.HC, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (54.71) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文发现VLM在模糊输入（如背影、全副武装的工人）下，即使内部编码了女性关联，输出仍系统性坍缩为男性。作者提出LALS指标，通过将视觉token激活投影到文本嵌入空间来逐层测量概念关联，揭示了从中间层到输出层的"不对称滤波"——男性信号被放大而女性信号被抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对齐方法在性别清晰可见时能有效消除VLM的性别偏见，但模糊输入（如工人背影、从背后看到的人物）在实际中很常见却鲜有研究。作者希望探究VLM在模糊输入下是否仍存在性别偏差，以及模型内部表征与最终输出是否一致。

Method: 提出LALS（Latent Association Leaning Score），一种零样本度量方法，将视觉token的激活值投影到模型的文本嵌入空间，逐token、逐层测量概念关联强度。在15个职业、800+张性别模糊图像上，对4个VLM（LLaVA、CLIP等）进行内部表征与输出的一致性分析，并进行逐层分析和颜色消融实验。

Result: 内部表征与输出系统性解耦：模型内部编码了女性关联，但输出却为男性。逐层分析发现不对称滤波——男性信号从编码到输出端到端放大，女性信号在中间层达到峰值后在被生成前被抑制。颜色消融实验表明，服装颜色等文化负载视觉线索会进一步调节内部关联。

Conclusion: VLM在模糊输入下存在系统性性别表征偏差，即使内部编码了正确的女性关联，输出仍被抑制为男性。这表明当前对齐方法在模糊场景下失效，且模型内部存在不对称的性别信号滤波机制，为构建更公平的多模态模型提供了新的诊断工具和方向。

Classification reason: 该论文研究VLM在模糊输入下对性别表征的偏差问题，发现模型内部编码女性关联但输出被抑制为男性，属于多模态模型的事实性、可靠性与忠实性问题，与[多模态幻觉与可靠性]（multimodal_hallucination）主题高度吻合。

Importance reason: Focuses on gender bias in VLMs, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous VLM, video generation, or model efficiency. Signals: VLM bias, ambiguous input, gender representation, LALS metric, internal vs output decoupling

Abstract: Alignment teaches vision-language models (VLMs) to avoid expressing demographic biases, and when gender is clearly visible they largely succeed. Far less is known about ambiguous inputs (a worker in full gear, a figure seen from behind) cases common in practice yet rarely studied. We find that minimal prompting pressure exposes occupation-gender defaults when prompting ambiguous input images, with models collapsing to male even for strongly female-stereotyped occupations. But do these outputs reflect what models actually encode internally? We introduce LALS (Latent Association Leaning Score), a zero-shot metric that projects visual-token activations into the model's text-embedding space to measure concept associations per token and layer. Across 15 occupations, over 800 gender-ambiguous images, and four VLMs, internal representations and outputs are systematically decoupled: models often encode a female association internally yet output male. Layer-wise analysis reveals an asymmetric filter -- male signal amplifies end-to-end while female signal peaks mid-network and is suppressed before generation -- and a color ablation shows that culturally loaded visual cues such as clothing color further modulate these internal associations.

</details>


### [153] [Immuno-VLM: Immunizing Large Vision-Language Models via Generative Semantic Antibodies for Open-World Trustworthiness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30745)
*Xiang Fang, Wanlong Fang, Wei Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (57.96) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Immuno-VLM 受生物免疫负选择机制启发，利用大语言模型的生成推理能力主动生成"语义抗体"（近分布异常文本描述），为大视觉语言模型在开放世界中的零样本识别提供可靠性保障，在 ImageNet-1K 和多个 OOD 基准上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大视觉语言模型在零样本识别中因语义抽象而存在"语义傲慢"问题：缺乏显式的负知识，导致模型将未知异常以高置信度强行归入已知类别，在开放世界部署中产生严重安全隐患。现有开放集识别方法依赖被动密度估计或像素级异常生成，效率低且泛化能力有限。

Method: 提出 Immuno-VLM 框架，将生物免疫负选择原理适配到高维潜空间。利用大语言模型的生成推理能力主动"幻觉"出语义抗体——即近分布异常（如相似物、上下文异常）的文本描述，这些抗体有效约束已知类别的决策边界，从而提升模型对未知异常的识别能力。

Result: 在 ImageNet-1K 和四个具有挑战性的开放集识别（OOD）基准上，Immuno-VLM 取得了新的最优结果。

Conclusion: Immuno-VLM 通过生物启发的语义抗体生成策略，有效解决了 VLM 在开放世界中的可信度悖论，为提升多模态模型的可靠性和安全性提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文关注大视觉语言模型在开放世界部署中的可靠性问题，即模型将未知异常强制分类为已知类别（语义幻觉），并提出免疫式框架生成语义抗体来缓解这一问题。这与[多模态幻觉与可靠性]（multimodal_hallucination）这一规范子主题高度匹配，该子主题涵盖幻觉缓解、事实性与可靠性提升。

Importance reason: Focuses on VLM trustworthiness via OOD detection, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous VLM, autoregressive generation, or world models. Signals: OOD detection, VLM reliability, bio-inspired negative selection, zero-shot recognition, not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models have achieved unprecedented success in zero-shot recognition by aligning visual features with broad semantic concepts. However, this semantic abstraction creates a critical vulnerability in open-world deployment: the ``Hubris of Semantics'', where models force-fit unknown anomalies into known categories with high confidence due to the lack of explicit negative knowledge. To address this \textit{Open-World Trustworthiness Paradox}, we propose \textbf{Immuno-VLM}, a bio-inspired framework that adapts the biological principle of \textbf{Immunological Negative Selection} to high-dimensional latent spaces. Departing from traditional Open-Set Recognition methods that rely on passive density estimation or inefficient pixel-space outlier generation, Immuno-VLM leverages the generative reasoning of Large Language Models to actively hallucinate ``Semantic Antibodies'', textual descriptions of near-distribution outliers (e.g., look-alikes, contextual anomalies) that effectively bound the decision space of known experiments on ImageNet-1K and four challenging OOD benchmarks reveal that Immuno-VLM establishes a new state-of-the-art.

</details>


### [154] [What Makes LVLMs Hallucinate Less? Unveiling the Architectural Factors Behind Hallucination Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30911)
*Yusheng He, Jizhe Zhou, Xia Du, Zheng Lin, Jun Luo, Jiancheng Lv*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (63.88) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文系统探究了LVLM架构设计（语言基础、视觉表征、语义对齐三个维度）对三种幻觉（共现、相似性、不确定性）的影响，提出了细粒度幻觉基准CoSimUE，揭示了参数规模对幻觉缓解效果有限、联合增强视觉保真度与对齐质量效果最佳等关键发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 幻觉是制约LVLM可靠性的关键问题，现有研究多关注改进模型内部组件，但缺乏从架构设计层面系统探究幻觉成因的工作。

Method: 将架构设计分解为语言基础（LF）、视觉表征（VR）和语义对齐（SA）三个维度，将幻觉分为共现、相似性和不确定性三类。提出CoSimUE基准，通过受控文本扰动和随机扰动创建细粒度幻觉场景，在7个设计方面进行实验分析。

Result: 1）参数规模扩展对减少三类幻觉效果有限；2）更大更好的语言基础可减少共现幻觉；3）更强的视觉编码器和更高分辨率可缓解相似性错误；4）有效的对齐策略可减少不确定性幻觉；5）联合增强视觉保真度与对齐质量效果最佳。

Conclusion: 本文首次系统探索了架构级设计与幻觉鲁棒性之间的联系，为开发可靠高效的LVLM提供了实用指导。

Classification reason: 该论文系统研究LVLM幻觉的架构因素，属于多模态幻觉缓解与可靠性提升方向，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态幻觉与可靠性"（multimodal_hallucination）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Provides systematic architectural insights into LVLM hallucination, but focuses on understanding/generation rather than user's top-priority continuous VLM or autoregressive generation. Signals: systematic architectural analysis, hallucination robustness, limited parameter scaling effect, cross-dimensional design insights, not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: Hallucination remains one of the key challenges undermining the reliability of Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs). But what makes an LVLM hallucinate less? Many existing efforts focus on improving internal components of the model. We argue that hallucination fundamentally stems from how the model architecture is designed. To investigate this, we factor the architecture design into three dimensions: Linguistic Foundation (LF), Visual Representation (VR), and Semantic Alignment (SA), and categorize hallucinations into Co-occurrence, Similarity, and previously overlooked Uncertainty types. Building on this formulation, we propose CoSimUE, a benchmark that creates fine-grained hallucination scenarios through controlled textual perturbations and random perturbations, enabling mapping between design choices and hallucination behaviors. Experiments across 7 design aspects show that: 1) the widely emphasized scaling of model parameters has only limited impact on reducing all three types of hallucinations; 2) larger and better-trained language foundations can reduce co-occurrence hallucinations; 3) stronger visual encoders and higher resolutions mitigate similarity errors; 4) effective alignment strategies alleviate uncertainty hallucinations. 5) Furthermore, cross-dimensional analysis reveals that jointly enhancing visual fidelity and alignment quality yields the most comprehensive improvements. This study provides the first systematic exploration linking architecture-level design to hallucination robustness, offering practical guidance for developing reliable and efficient LVLMs.

</details>


### [155] [Human-Alignment, Calibration, and Activation Patterns in Large Language Model Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30675)
*Kyle Moore, Jesse Roberts, Daryl Watson, William Ward, Grayson Heyboer*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (49.64) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型的不确定性是否与人类不确定性相似，提出了"不确定性对齐"（uncertainty alignment）概念，在多项选择题和开放式事实回忆数据集上分析了模型的行为和内部激活模式，考察了模型是否同时具备对齐与校准能力，并分析了指令微调对这两方面的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不确定性量化研究主要关注校准（模型不确定性判断与任务准确率的一致性），但很少探究模型的不确定性是否与人类的不确定性相似。理解这种相似性对于构建更可信、更符合人类直觉的AI系统至关重要。

Method: 在多项选择题和开放式事实回忆数据集上，通过行为实验和内部激活模式分析，考察LLM的不确定性信号是否与人类不确定性相似（不确定性对齐），同时评估模型的校准能力，并分析指令微调对对齐和校准的影响。

Result: 发现LLM在行为层面和内部激活层面均表现出与人类相似的不确定性信号；模型可以同时具备对齐与校准能力；指令微调对不确定性对齐和校准均有显著影响。

Conclusion: 不确定性对齐是评估LLM可信度的重要维度，与校准互补；未来研究应同时关注对齐与校准，以构建更可靠的语言模型。

Classification reason: 该论文研究大语言模型的不确定性量化（uncertainty quantification），核心关注模型校准（calibration）与人类对齐（human-alignment），旨在识别和缓解幻觉（hallucination）。这与多模态理解与生成方向下的[CANONICAL]子主题"多模态幻觉与可靠性"（multimodal_hallucination）高度相关，因为该子主题涵盖幻觉缓解、事实性与可靠性提升。虽然论文主要针对LLM而非多模态，但其核心问题（不确定性校准与幻觉）是该子主题的核心关切，且论文方法可推广至多模态场景。

Importance reason: Focuses on uncertainty alignment and calibration in LLMs, which is peripheral to the user's core interests in multimodal generation and continuous VLM. Signals: uncertainty alignment, calibration, human similarity, instruction fine-tuning, factual recall

Abstract: Uncertainty Quantification is a large and growing subfield of large language model behavioral analysis. Primarily to recognize and combat hallucination, the field has largely focused on measuring and improving calibration, the accuracy of uncertainty judgments to task efficacy. In this work, we investigate the relatively underexplored question of how similar large language model uncertainty is to human uncertainty. We investigate the presence and strength of human-similar uncertainty signals, deemed uncertainty alignment, in large language model overt behavior and internal activation patterns. We identify whether the models show evidence of simultaneous alignment and calibration on a variety of datasets covering both multiple choice and open ended factual recall. And we characterize the effect of instruct fine-tuning on each of these facets.

</details>


### [156] [Learning from Fine-Grained Visual Discrepancies: Mitigating Multimodal Hallucinations via In-Context Visual Contrastive Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31312)
*Haolin Deng, Xin Zou, Zhiwei Jin, Chen Chen, Haonan Lu, Xuming Hu*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (62.55) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出IC-VCO方法，通过将对比图像置于共享多图像上下文中实现数学上严谨的视觉偏好优化，并引入视觉对比蒸馏（VCDist）正则化器以及对比样本编辑策略生成硬负样本，有效缓解多模态幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准文本DPO因缺乏显式视觉监督难以缓解多模态幻觉；现有视觉偏好DPO方法存在配分函数不匹配的理论不一致问题，且依赖粗粒度负样本可能导致捷径学习。

Method: 提出In-Context Visual Contrastive Optimization (IC-VCO)，将对比图像置于共享多图像上下文中确保目标函数数学严谨；引入Visual Contrast Distillation (VCDist)作为可靠性门控正则化器，促进多图像对比训练与单图像推理之间的一致性；设计对比样本编辑策略，通过精确语义扰动生成硬负样本。

Result: 在五个基准上取得最佳综合性能，验证了IC-VCO及样本编辑策略的有效性。

Conclusion: IC-VCO通过理论严谨的视觉对比优化和硬负样本生成策略，有效缓解了多模态幻觉，为VLM的视觉偏好对齐提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于缓解多模态大模型的幻觉问题，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的[多模态幻觉与可靠性]规范子主题。

Importance reason: Addresses multimodal hallucination in VLMs with a novel contrastive optimization, but falls outside the user's top priority subtopics. Signals: multimodal hallucination, visual preference optimization, contrastive learning, VLM reliability, hard negative generation

Abstract: Multimodal hallucination remains a persistent challenge for Vision-Language Models (VLMs). Standard textual Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) often fails to mitigate it due to a lack of explicit visual supervision. While existing works introduce visual preference DPO by contrasting original images against negative ones, they suffer from a theoretically inconsistent objective caused by partition function mismatches and rely on coarse-grained negatives that could enable shortcut learning. In this work, we propose In-Context Visual Contrastive Optimization (IC-VCO). By placing contrastive images within a shared multi-image context, IC-VCO ensures a mathematically rigorous objective. We further introduce Visual Contrast Distillation (VCDist), an auxiliary reliability-gated regularizer that encourages consistency between multi-image contrastive training and single-image inference. Finally, we propose a contrastive sample editing strategy that generates hard negatives via precise semantic perturbations. Experiments on five benchmarks demonstrate IC-VCO's best overall performance and the effectiveness of our sample editing strategy. Code and data are available at .

</details>


### [157] [YARD: Y-Architecture Register Decoding for Efficient Hallucination Mitigation in Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31429)
*Ting Chen, Geng Li, Guohao Chen, Yu Hu, Guan Huang, Mai Chen, Langsheng Lei, Jun Du*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (63.88) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: YARD提出了一种无需训练的Y-Architecture Register Decoding框架，通过共享浅层计算并在中间层分支，用register tokens替换patch-level视觉token来构建视觉退化分支，实现高效的LVLM幻觉缓解，同时避免两次完整前向传播的高延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无训练对比解码（CD）方法存在两个问题：完全丢弃视觉token过于极端，会引发语言幻觉；而破坏输入图像的方式对视觉证据的控制粗糙，且需要两次完整前向传播导致高推理延迟。因此需要一种更高效且有效的幻觉缓解方法。

Method: YARD提出Y-Architecture Register Decoding框架。基于"可靠的文本到视觉定位主要出现在中间解码层"的观察，YARD共享浅层计算，在关键中间层进行分支。退化分支用register tokens（保留全局图像语义但缺乏细粒度局部证据）替换patch-level视觉token，提供忠实对比信号而不产生极端模态不匹配，同时Y架构避免了第二次完整前向传播。

Result: 在生成式和判别式幻觉基准测试上的大量实验表明，YARD在多个LVLM上持续实现了最先进的幻觉缓解效果，同时显著降低了推理延迟。

Conclusion: YARD通过巧妙的Y架构设计和register token机制，在无需训练的情况下有效缓解了LVLM的幻觉问题，同时大幅提升了推理效率，为对比解码方法提供了新的高效范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究大型视觉语言模型（LVLM）中的幻觉缓解问题，提出Y-Architecture Register Decoding（YARD）框架，属于多模态幻觉缓解方向。该方向对应[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态幻觉与可靠性"（multimodal_hallucination），因此直接复用该分类。

Importance reason: Addresses hallucination in LVLMs with efficient inference, but is not directly aligned with top-priority subtopics like continuous VLM or autoregressive generation. Signals: Training-free hallucination mitigation, Y-architecture for efficient contrastive decoding, Register tokens for visual degradation, Reduces inference latency, Applicable to multiple LVLMs

Abstract: Contrastive decoding (CD) seeks to mitigate hallucinations in Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) by contrasting the output distributions of a standard model and a visually degraded model. However, existing training-free CD methods suffer from sub-optimal degraded branches: completely dropping visual tokens is too extreme and induces language hallucinations, while corrupting input images offers coarse control over visual evidence and suffers from high inference latency due to requiring two full forward passes. To address these dilemmas, we propose YARD, a training-free Y-Architecture Register Decoding framework. Motivated by the observation that reliable text-to-vision grounding predominantly emerges in the middle decoder layers, YARD constructs the degraded branch internally by sharing shallow-layer computations and branching exactly at this critical stage. For the degraded branch, YARD replaces patch-level visual tokens with register tokens, which preserve global image semantics but lack fine-grained local evidence. This image-aware yet locally under-grounded design provides a faithful contrastive signal without extreme modality mismatch, while the Y-architecture strictly avoids a costly second forward pass. Extensive experiments on generative and discriminative hallucination benchmarks demonstrate that YARD consistently achieves state-of-the-art hallucination mitigation across multiple LVLMs, alongside a significant reduction in inference latency.

</details>


### [158] [BenHalluEval: A Multi-Task Hallucination Evaluation Framework for Large Language Models on Bengali](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31483)
*Shefayat E Shams Adib, Ahmed Alfey Sani, Ekramul Alam Esham, Ajwad Abrar, Ishmam Tashdeed, Md Taukir Azam Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (48.77) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: BenHalluEval 是首个针对孟加拉语大语言模型的细粒度幻觉评测框架，覆盖生成式问答、代码混合问答、摘要和推理四个任务，构建了12,000个幻觉候选样本，并提出双轨校准指标 BenHalluScore 来联合惩罚假阳性和漏检两种失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孟加拉语是世界第六大语言，但此前没有工作系统性地评估大语言模型在该语言上的幻觉问题。现有幻觉评测主要针对英语等资源丰富的语言，低资源语言的幻觉行为尚未被充分研究。

Method: 从三个现有孟加拉语数据集中抽取样本，使用 GPT-5.4 生成12,000个覆盖12种任务特定幻觉类型的候选样本。采用双轨评测协议：Track A 在真实实例上测量假阳性率，Track B 在幻觉候选上测量幻觉检测率。提出 BenHalluScore 双轨校准指标联合惩罚两种失败模式。评估了7个涵盖推理型、多语言型和孟加拉语专用型的大语言模型。

Result: BenHalluScore 在各模型和任务上范围为7.72%到55.42%，揭示了模型间幻觉校准的显著差异。思维链提示作为缓解策略，改变了响应分布但未能持续改善幻觉判别能力。

Conclusion: BenHalluEval 建立了首个孟加拉语专用幻觉评测基准，揭示了单轨评测和仅提示策略在低资源语言场景中的不足，为低资源语言幻觉研究提供了重要基础。

Classification reason: 该论文构建了孟加拉语大语言模型的幻觉评测框架，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的"多模态幻觉与可靠性"（multimodal_hallucination）规范子主题，该子主题涵盖幻觉评测、事实性与可靠性提升，与本论文高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on Bengali hallucination evaluation, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative-keyword-filtered directions. Signals: Low-resource language benchmark, hallucination evaluation, no overlap with user's priority subtopics, negative keywords not triggered but direction mismatch

Abstract: Despite Bengali being the sixth most spoken language in the world, no prior work has systematically evaluated hallucination in large language models (LLMs) for Bengali. We introduce BenHalluEval, a fine-grained hallucination evaluation framework for Bengali covering four tasks: Generative Question Answering (GQA), Bangla-English Code-Mixed QA, Summarization, and Reasoning. We construct 12,000 hallucinated candidates using GPT-5.4 across twelve task-specific hallucination types, drawn from three existing Bengali datasets, and evaluate seven LLMs spanning reasoning-oriented, multilingual, and Bengali-centric categories under a dual-track protocol that independently measures false-positive rate on ground-truth instances (Track A) and hallucination detection rate on hallucinated candidates (Track B). To jointly penalise both failure modes and prevent inflated scores from uniform response bias, we propose BenHalluScore, a dual-track calibration metric that ranges from 7.72% to 55.42% across models and tasks, revealing substantial variation in hallucination calibration. Chain-of-thought prompting, applied as a mitigation strategy, shifts response distributions without consistently improving hallucination discrimination. BenHalluEval establishes the first dedicated hallucination benchmark for Bengali and highlights the inadequacy of single-track and prompting-only evaluation approaches for low-resource language settings. The dataset and code are available at .

</details>


### [159] [Fighting Numerical Hallucinations via Data-centric Compilation for Online Financial QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31064)
*Hao Chen, Xing Tang, Qirui Liu, Weijie Shi, Shiwei Li, Fuyuan Lyu, Weihong Luo, Xiku Du, Xiuqiang He*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.IR

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (51.46) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出数据为中心的推理编译器DCRC，通过对抗数据构建、多阶段训练和编译执行推理流程，解决金融QA中RAG系统的数值推理幻觉问题，在离线基准和在线系统中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统在金融QA中存在噪声敏感、计算脆弱和审计危机三大挑战，而模型中心方法（单独优化检索器或生成器）难以整体解决这些问题。

Method: 提出DCRC框架，包含三个阶段：(1) 对抗数据构建——合成含可控噪声的训练样本；(2) 多阶段训练——培养数据中心结构化智能体(DSA)，具备显式证据审计和程序合成能力；(3) 编译执行推理——DSA将用户查询和检索文档转化为可验证、可执行的推理程序。

Result: 在离线金融QA基准上取得显著提升，并在真实在线金融QA系统中部署验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 数据中心的编译执行范式能够有效缓解数值推理幻觉，为高可靠性金融应用提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦金融QA中的数值推理幻觉问题，属于多模态/文本幻觉与可靠性范畴，与[多模态幻觉与可靠性]（multimodal_hallucination）主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on financial QA numerical hallucination, not aligned with user's priority on multimodal generation and world models. Signals: data-centric compilation, numerical reasoning hallucination, financial QA, RAG robustness, multi-stage training

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have significantly advanced online data services, particularly in the domain of financial question answering (FinQA). However, such systems remain susceptible to numerical reasoning hallucinations, which critically undermine reliability in high-stakes financial applications. Although retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) has been widely adopted to ground responses in external knowledge, it introduces three persistent challenges: noise sensitivity, calculation fragility, and an auditability crisis. Existing model-centric approaches, which primarily focus on optimizing either the retriever or generator in isolation, still struggle to address these issues in an integrated manner. In this work, we pioneer a data-centric paradigm and propose a novel framework, the Data-centric Reasoning Compiler (DCRC). The framework operates through three cohesive phases: (1) adversarial data construction, which synthesizes training examples with controlled noise to teach robustness; (2) multi-stage training that cultivates a Data-centric Structuring Agent (DSA) capable of explicit evidence auditing and program synthesis; and (3) a compile-and-execute inference process, where the DSA transforms user queries and retrieved documents into verifiable, executable reasoning programs. This data-driven framework ensures faithful numerical reasoning by design. We conduct extensive experiments on established offline benchmarks and further validate our framework through deployment in a real-world online financial QA system.

</details>

### [160] [Attend to Evidence: Evidence-Anchored Spatial Attention Supervision for Multimodal RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30912)
*Ruina Hu, Chen Wang, Lai Wei, Jionghao Bai, Bin Yu, Weiran Huang, Kai Wang, Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 普通相关 (56.64) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 EASE（Evidence-Anchored Spatial Attention），一种通过视觉证据过程监督增强多模态 RLVR 的方法。EASE 将标注的证据区域转换为平滑的视觉 token 目标，在 RL 训练中引导响应到图像的注意力，仅在高奖励轨迹上生效。在 Qwen2.5-VL-7B、Qwen3-VL-4B 和 Qwen3-VL-8B 上，EASE 在感知、幻觉、视觉数学和多模态推理基准上比 DAPO 平均提升 2.5-3.1 分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态 RLVR 仅使用最终答案的奖励信号进行优化，无法区分基于视觉证据的正确回答与依赖语言先验或运气猜测的回答。对于需要视觉定位的问题，这种仅关注结果的奖励无法引导模型关注正确的图像区域，导致模型可能学习到捷径而非真正的视觉推理能力。

Method: EASE 方法包含三个关键步骤：(1) 将标注的证据区域（bounding box）转换为平滑的视觉 token 注意力目标分布；(2) 在 RL 训练中，仅对高奖励轨迹施加响应到图像的注意力引导损失，使模型关注与答案相关的视觉区域；(3) 标注仅在训练阶段作为特权标签使用，推理时仅需原始图像和问题。该方法可集成到 DAPO 等 RLVR 框架中。

Result: 在 Qwen2.5-VL-7B、Qwen3-VL-4B 和 Qwen3-VL-8B 三个模型上，EASE 在感知、幻觉、视觉数学和多模态推理四个维度的基准测试中，相比 DAPO 基线平均提升 2.5 到 3.1 个点。诊断实验和消融研究表明，EASE 能更好地将视觉注意力与标注的证据区域对齐。

Conclusion: EASE 通过引入视觉证据过程监督，有效弥补了多模态 RLVR 仅依赖结果奖励的不足，使模型在需要视觉定位的任务中表现更优。该方法在多个模型和基准上均取得一致提升，且推理时无额外开销，是一种实用且有效的多模态 RL 增强方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 EASE 方法，通过视觉证据过程监督增强多模态 RLVR（带可验证奖励的强化学习），属于多模态强化学习与对齐方向，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态强化学习与对齐" (multimodal_rl_alignment) 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on RLVR for multimodal grounding, which is relevant but not a top priority subtopic. Signals: Multimodal RLVR, Visual evidence supervision, Attention alignment, Qwen2.5-VL, Qwen3-VL

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves vision-language models (VLMs) by optimizing outcome rewards derived from final answers. However, such outcome-only rewards do not tell the model which image regions justify an answer. For questions that require visual grounding, these rewards cannot distinguish responses supported by relevant visual evidence from those produced by language-prior shortcuts or lucky guesses. We introduce EASE (Evidence-Anchored Spatial Attention), which augments multimodal RLVR with visual-evidence process supervision. EASE converts annotated evidence regions into a smoothed visual-token target and uses it to guide response-to-image attention during RL training, but only on high-reward trajectories. The annotations are used solely as privileged training labels, while inference requires only the original image and question. Across Qwen2.5-VL-7B, Qwen3-VL-4B, and Qwen3-VL-8B, EASE raises average scores over DAPO by 2.5 to 3.1 points on perception, hallucination, visual math, and multimodal reasoning benchmarks. Diagnostics and ablations show that EASE better aligns visual attention with annotated evidence regions.

</details>

### [161] [ReGuLaR: Relation-Grounded Latent Reasoning for Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30587)
*Zihu Wang, Karthik Somayaji N.S, Peng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 值得关注 (68.31) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ReGuLaR 提出一种关系引导的潜空间推理框架，通过训练时引入 ReGFormer 模块聚焦于问题相关的视觉对象及其关系，在推理时无需该模块即可生成答案，并构建了 RGROUNDING-351K 数据集，在多个基准上取得 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LVLM 的 CoT 推理使用离散文本作为中间推理步骤，难以充分编码连续的视觉证据。虽然已有工作将推理引入连续潜空间，但这些方法对视觉证据中的组合结构和关系信息建模不足，导致潜推理与视觉证据之间的连接不够紧密。

Method: 提出 ReGuLaR 框架，包含两个阶段：(1) 训练阶段使用 ReGFormer 模块，该模块通过关系引导机制聚焦于问题相关的目标对象及其相互关系，将视觉关系信息注入潜空间推理过程；(2) 推理阶段无需 ReGFormer，模型直接进行潜空间推理并生成答案。同时构建了 RGROUNDING-351K 数据集，包含目标边界框和对象间关系标注。

Result: 在多个视觉推理基准上，ReGuLaR 一致优于现有方法，取得了 SOTA 性能，验证了关系引导的潜空间推理对 LVLM 推理能力的提升效果。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模视觉对象间关系并将其注入潜空间推理，可以有效弥补离散文本推理在编码连续视觉证据方面的不足，为提升 LVLM 的视觉推理能力提供了新方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出关系引导的潜空间推理框架 ReGuLaR，旨在增强大视觉语言模型（LVLM）的视觉推理能力，属于多模态推理与思维链（CoT）范畴。论文核心贡献是将推理过程从离散文本扩展到连续潜空间，并显式建模视觉对象间关系，这与 [CANONICAL] 多模态推理与思维链 子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Advances continuous latent reasoning for VLM, aligning with priority subtopic on multimodal latent reasoning. Signals: continuous latent reasoning, relation grounding, state-of-the-art on benchmarks, new dataset RGROUNDING-351K, no inference overhead

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning has significantly improved the reasoning ability of large vision-language models (LVLMs) by verbalizing intermediate reasoning steps in natural language. However, such discrete textual rationales are often insufficient for encoding continuous visual evidence. Recent work addresses this limitation by moving reasoning into continuous latent space. Despite promising progress, existing methods leave latent reasoning insufficiently connected to the compositional and relational structure of visual evidence. To address this gap, we introduce ReGuLaR, a relation grounded latent reasoning framework that explicitly grounds latent states in these critical yet overlooked visual evidence. ReGuLaR uses a training-time ReGFormer to focus latent reasoning on question-relevant objects and inter-object relations, while at inference time the model reasons and generates answers without invoking the ReGFormer. To support training ReGuLaR, we construct RGROUNDING-351K, a real-world vision-language dataset annotated with key object bounding boxes and inter-object relations. Extensive experiments across diverse benchmarks show that ReGuLaR consistently outperforms existing approaches and achieves state-of-the-art performance. We include our code in the submission and will release the code and training data publicly upon acceptance.

</details>


### [162] [Seeing Before Agreeing: Aligning Multi-Agent Consensus with Visual Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30698)
*Yuhan Wang, Shuochen Chang, Yalin Feng, Dongsheng Ma, Yuanzi Li, Zhengren Wang, Yinglong Yang, Yufei Chen, Yikang Wang, Shaoxu Sun, Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.MA, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (56.64) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 EAGLE，一个无需训练的多智能体视觉问答框架，通过显式暴露每个 VLM 智能体的视觉定位区域作为证据，让智能体相互验证证据一致性，并基于证据对齐程度指导最终决策，在六个 VQA 基准上取得最佳平均性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体 VQA 方法主要沿用文本问答的协议，聚焦于文本讨论而忽略了对视觉信息的对齐。作者发现仅靠答案层面的一致不足以实现可靠的多智能体 VQA，对齐的视觉证据（智能体所依赖的图像区域）才是可信共识的关键。

Method: 提出 EAGLE（Evidence-Aligned Grounded Multi-Agent Reasoning）框架，包含三个步骤：(1) 显式暴露每个 VLM 智能体的视觉定位区域作为证据；(2) 智能体之间对证据进行相互验证；(3) 基于证据一致性指导最终决策。整个框架无需额外训练。

Result: 在六个 VQA 基准上，EAGLE 取得了跨领域的最佳平均性能，同时保持轻量、可解释且易于部署。

Conclusion: 对齐视觉证据是多智能体 VQA 中实现可信共识的关键，EAGLE 通过证据对齐的框架有效提升了多智能体视觉问答的可靠性和性能。

Classification reason: 该论文提出多智能体协作视觉问答框架，通过视觉证据对齐实现多模态推理共识，属于多模态推理范畴，因此映射到 canonical 子主题"多模态推理与思维链"。

Importance reason: Novel multi-agent VLM alignment method but outside core priority subtopics and lacks direct connection to continuous VLM or autoregressive generation. Signals: Multi-agent VLM, visual evidence alignment, training-free, VQA, interpretable

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have achieved strong performance on visual question answering (VQA). To mitigate individual hallucinations and blind spots, aggregating diverse perspectives via multi-agent collaboration has emerged as a promising paradigm. While this approach has shown great success in textual QA, its potential in the multimodal domain remains under-explored. Existing multi-agent VQA methods predominantly adapt text-centric protocols, focusing on textual discussions while ignoring the alignment of visual information. In this work, we reveal a key insight: answer-level agreement is insufficient for reliable multi-agent VQA; \textit{aligned visual evidence} -- shared support from the image regions agents rely on -- is essential for trustworthy consensus. To leverage this insight, we propose EAGLE (\textbf{E}vidence-\textbf{A}ligned \textbf{G}rounded mu\textbf{L}ti-agent r\textbf{E}asoning), a training-free evidence-centered framework for coordinating multiple VLM agents. EAGLE explicitly exposes each agent's grounding regions as visual evidence, enables mutual verification over the evidence, and uses evidence consistency to guide final decision-making. Experiments on six VQA benchmarks show that EAGLE achieves best average performance across domains while remaining lightweight, interpretable, and practical for deployment.

</details>


### [163] [Diversity Matters: Revisiting Test-Time Compute in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30713)
*Yijie Tong, Yifan Hou, Shaobo Cui, Antoine Bosselut, Mrinmaya Sachan*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV, cs.MM

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (63.99) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了视觉语言模型（VLM）中的测试时计算（TTC）策略，发现特征评分和多数投票在单模型设置下效果有限，原因是输出缺乏多样性。为此提出基于预测熵的ETTC方法，在多模型集成中利用置信度差异优先选择更强模型，理论证明其优于多数投票，实验表明小模型可协同增强大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时计算（TTC）策略在纯文本大语言模型中已被证明能有效提升推理能力，但在视觉语言模型（VLM）中的应用和收益尚未被充分探索。本文旨在系统研究TTC在VLM中的有效性，并解决多模型集成时标准多数投票无法区分模型能力差异的问题。

Method: 本文首先在7个VLM和6个基准上系统分析了特征评分和多数投票两种TTC策略。理论分析表明，多数投票在单模型设置下收益有限的原因是输出缺乏多样性（预测高度相关）。针对多模型集成场景，提出基于熵的测试时计算（ETTC）方法：计算每个模型预测的熵作为置信度指标，选择熵最低（最自信）的预测作为最终输出。在单模型情况下ETTC退化为多数投票，而在多模型集成中则利用置信度差异优先选择更强模型。

Result: 实验表明：1）特征启发式评分方法在VLM上效果不佳；2）单模型多数投票仅带来微小提升；3）ETTC在多模型集成中持续优于多数投票和最佳单个模型；4）小模型可以通过ETTC协同增强大模型性能，实现标准策略无法达到的集成增益。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了多样性是测试时计算在VLM中有效性的关键因素，提出ETTC方法有效利用多模型集成中的置信度差异来提升推理性能。理论分析和实验验证了ETTC的优越性，并展示了小模型辅助大模型的协同潜力，为VLM的测试时推理优化提供了新方向。

Classification reason: 该论文系统研究视觉语言模型（VLM）的测试时计算（TTC）策略，提出基于熵的ETTC方法以提升多模型集成推理性能，属于多模态推理与推理时计算扩展范畴，与[多模态推理与思维链]（multimodal_reasoning_cot）子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly addresses test-time compute for VLMs, a key efficiency topic, but lacks direct connection to top-priority continuous VLM or autoregressive generation subtopics. Signals: VLM test-time compute, diversity analysis, entropy-based selection, multi-model ensemble, efficiency focus

Abstract: Test-time compute (TTC) strategies have emerged as a lightweight approach to boost reasoning in large language models (LLMs). However, their application and benefits for vision-language models (VLMs) remain underexplored. We present a systematic study of TTC across seven VLMs and six benchmarks, specifically analyzing feature-based scoring and majority voting methods. We find that feature heuristics fail and voting yields only modest gains in single-model settings. We theoretically show that this limitation stems from a lack of prediction diversity: when outputs are highly correlated, voting provides little benefit. In contrast, multi-model ensembles offer richer diversity, yet standard majority voting fails to account for varying model capabilities. To address this, we propose Entropy-based TTC (ETTC), which selects the most confident prediction based on predictive entropy. Our method reduces to majority voting in the single-model case, but in model ensembles, it leverages confidence disparities to prioritize stronger models. We prove that ETTC outperforms majority voting under mild assumptions and empirically demonstrate that it consistently surpasses both voting and the best individual model. Crucially, our results show that smaller models can synergistically enhance larger ones, unlocking ensembling gains not achievable with standard strategies.

</details>


### [164] [LARK: Learnability-Grounded Trajectory Selection for Efficient Reasoning Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30651)
*Tianrun Yu, Kaixiang Zhao, Chih-Chun Chen, Amanda Hughes, Taylor W. Killian, Fenglong Ma, Weitong Zhang, Porter Jenkins*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (56.64) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: LARK 提出了一种基于可学习性的推理轨迹选择方法，通过估计学生模型对每条轨迹的学习速率 ρ，并结合 χ² 正则化策略平衡可学习性与分布覆盖，从而高效筛选教师推理轨迹用于蒸馏训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理蒸馏中的轨迹选择方法多依赖启发式规则（如轨迹质量或模型置信度），忽略了轨迹对学生模型是否可学习这一关键因素，导致蒸馏效率低下。

Method: 提出 LARK 方法，核心是定义一个可学习性因子 ρ 表征学生模型训练损失下降速率；引入可学习性代理（learnability proxy）高效估计 ρ，并设计 χ² 正则化选择策略在可学习性与分布覆盖之间取得平衡，同时提供理论保证。

Result: 在多个基座模型和推理任务上，LARK 一致优于现有数据选择基线；诊断分析表明 LARK 分数能有效预测下游训练效用，且 LARK 选择的轨迹能加速监督微调损失下降。

Conclusion: LARK 验证了可学习性引导的轨迹选择在推理蒸馏中的有效性，为高效推理蒸馏提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究推理蒸馏中的轨迹选择方法，属于多模态推理（reasoning distillation）范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 多模态推理与思维链（multimodal_reasoning_cot）方向高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on reasoning distillation trajectory selection, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like continuous VLM or video generation. Signals: reasoning distillation, trajectory selection, learnability estimation, χ² regularization, supervised fine-tuning

Abstract: We study trajectory selection for reasoning distillation, where teacher-generated reasoning trajectories are selectively used as supervision for a student model. Existing methods rely on heuristics such as trajectory quality or model confidence, but they often overlook whether a trajectory is learnable by the student. In this paper, we present LARK, a learnability-grounded method for reasoning trajectory selection. LARK selects trajectories that the student can learn efficiently while preserving the generalization of the full training distribution. At the core of LARK is a learnability factor $\rho$, which characterizes the rate at which the student's training loss decreases. To estimate this rate efficiently and maintain generalization, we introduce a learnability proxy and a $\chi^2$-regularized selection policy that balances learnability and distributional coverage, both with strong theoretical guarantees on their estimation error. Empirically, LARK consistently outperforms data selection baselines across multiple base models and reasoning tasks. Diagnostic analyses show that the LARK score predicts downstream training utility and that LARK-selected trajectories induce faster supervised fine-tuning loss reduction. Our code is available at .

</details>


### [165] [iVGR: Internalizing Visually Grounded Reasoning for MLLMs with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31096)
*Chang-Bin Zhang, Yujie Zhong, Qiang Zhang, Kai Han*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 高优先级 (90.72) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 iVGR 框架，通过强化学习将 MLLM 的视觉定位能力内化到文本推理过程中，使得模型在推理时无需显式输出目标框即可实现精准的视觉定位推理，在细粒度评测上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉定位思维链（visually grounded CoT）在推理时强制要求模型输出显式目标框，反而会干扰答案预测，导致性能低于纯文本 CoT。作者希望将视觉定位能力内化到文本推理过程中，避免显式定位带来的干扰。

Method: 提出 iVGR 框架，采用双流训练策略：一个文本推理流（textual stream）与一个高质量视觉定位流（visually grounded stream）通过一致性奖励（consistency reward）进行对齐，利用强化学习将定位能力迁移到文本推理流中，使模型在推理时无需显式定位即可实现精准的视觉推理。

Result: 在多个细粒度视觉推理基准上，iVGR 显著优于现有基线方法，同时保持了支持工具辅助推理工作流的灵活性。

Conclusion: 视觉定位能力可以通过强化学习内化到文本推理过程中，无需在推理时强制显式定位，这为 MLLM 的细粒度视觉推理提供了一种更高效、更灵活的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 MLLM 的视觉定位推理与思维链（CoT），属于多模态推理与思维链这一 [CANONICAL] 子主题。

Importance reason: Directly advances multimodal understanding generation with a novel RL-based internalization of visual grounding, aligning with user's high-priority direction and boosting MLLM reasoning efficiency. Signals: multimodal large language model, reinforcement learning, visual grounding, chain-of-thought, fine-grained perception

Abstract: While visually grounded Chain-of-Thought (CoT) has emerged as a promising paradigm to enhance fine-grained perception in multimodal large language models (MLLMs), its efficacy during the inference phase remains underexplored. In this work, we empirically find that mandating explicit object boxes in visually grounded CoT during inference often degrades performance compared to standard textual CoT, which reasons without explicit visual grounding. We hypothesize that the visual localization capability can be internalized into the textual CoT and that the mandatory explicit grounding introduces unnecessary interference with the model's primary objective of answer prediction. To address this problem, we propose Internalizing Visually Grounded Reasoning (\textbf{iVGR}), a novel reinforcement learning framework that transfers localization capabilities into the textual reasoning process. We employ a dual-stream training strategy, where a textual stream is aligned with a high-quality visually grounded stream via a proposed consistency reward, enabling the model to localize accurately without explicit grounding during inference. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms existing baselines on fine-grained benchmarks, while maintaining the flexibility to support tool-assisted inference workflows.

</details>


### [166] [The Sword, Shield, and Achilles' Heel: Characterizing the Linguistic Inductive Bias of Large Language Models for Spatial Reasoning in Navigation Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31404)
*Xudong Zhang, Jian Yang, Shengkai Wang, Jiangpeng Tian, Shaowen Chen, Xian Wei, Ke Li, Xiong You*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (64.84) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一个双干预框架，系统研究LLM在导航规划任务中处理文本化空间表示时的语言归纳偏置。通过表示干预（改变语言格式与压缩程度）和上下文干预（组合与冲突探测不同上下文特征），发现拓扑信息是鲁棒规划的坚实支柱，语言格式是双刃剑，而语义信息是致命弱点——错误语义会系统性破坏规划过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的导航系统通常将空间表示（如拓扑图、语义栅格图）转化为文本描述作为LLM输入，但语言结构的选择和上下文特征（拓扑、几何等）的选取常被视为中性工程决策，而非影响LLM行为的关键因素。本文旨在填补这一空白，系统评估LLM在导航规划中的语言归纳偏置。

Method: 提出双干预框架：(1) 表示干预——改变语言格式（自然语言 vs 结构化格式）和语言压缩程度，分析何时语言表示支持或抑制导航规划；(2) 上下文干预——通过上下文特征组合与冲突探测，明确LLM处理不同上下文特征时的偏好与弱点。在多种空间推理任务和多个模型规模上进行实验。

Result: 实验揭示一致模式：拓扑信息是鲁棒规划的坚实支柱；语言格式是双刃剑，其效果取决于模型大小、任务需求和压缩程度；语义信息是致命弱点——错误语义线索会系统性破坏规划过程。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的导航中，有效的文本化空间表示应保持拓扑完整性、根据模型容量校准表示压缩程度、确保语义正确性，而非简单采用单一表示格式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究LLM在导航规划中的空间推理能力，分析语言归纳偏置对空间推理的影响，属于多模态推理范畴。映射到[多模态推理与思维链]（multimodal_reasoning_cot）这一规范子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM spatial reasoning bias for navigation, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous VLM, video generation, or unified multimodal models. Signals: LLM inductive bias, spatial reasoning, navigation planning, linguistic representation, not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-based navigation systems commonly construct explicit spatial representations (e.g., topological graphs, semantic raster maps) and translate them into textual descriptions as LLMs' inputs. However, the linguistic structures of such text-based spatial representations and the choices of contextual features (e.g., topology, geometry) they contain are often treated as neutral engineering decisions rather than key factors that shape LLMs' behavior. To fill the gap, we propose a dual-interventional framework that disentangles linguistic structures from different contextual cues to evaluate the linguistic inductive bias of LLMs for navigation planning. In the framework, representation intervention varies the linguistic format and the degree of linguistic compression, clarifying when linguistic representations support or inhibit navigation planning. Context intervention, combined with contextual feature combination and conflict probing, explicitly clarifies the preferences and weaknesses of LLMs when processing different contextual cues. Experiments across diverse spatial reasoning tasks and multiple model scales reveal a consistent pattern: topological information is a sturdy shield and the backbone of robust planning; linguistic format is a double-edged sword whose effect depends on model size, task demands, and the compression level; and semantic information is a fatal Achilles' heel -- incorrect semantic cues can systematically derail the planning process. Overall, our study shows that effective text-based spatial representations in LLM-based navigation should preserve topological integrity, calibrate representational compression to model capacity, and ensure semantic correctness, rather than simply adopting a single representation. Our code is publicly available at .

</details>


### [167] [GSAM: A Generalizable and Safe Robotic Framework for Articulated Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30740)
*Beichen Shao, Mengying Xie, Heng Su, Wanyi Zhang, Mingyan Li, Yan Ding, Fausto Giunchiglia, Chao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.RO

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (51.56) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: GSAM 是一个面向关节物体操作的通用且安全的机器人框架，通过视觉感知器生成运动学参数，并用微调后的 VLM 结合链式思维常识推理进行精炼，同时利用 LLM 将交互约束函数化以规划安全轨迹，在 50 个铰链任务上相比基线将操作成功率提升 36.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法忽略了关节物体的多样性和末端执行器与手柄之间交互的复杂性，导致泛化能力有限且容易发生破坏性碰撞。

Method: 提出 GSAM 框架，包含：(1) 基于视觉的感知器生成运动学参数；(2) 微调后的 VLM 精炼器，使用链式思维（COT）常识推理修正感知偏差；(3) 交互约束函数生成器，整合关节物体、交互姿态和避障知识；(4) LLM 将这些约束函数化并应用于轨迹和姿态规划；(5) 运动学感知的操作规划器验证轨迹可达性。

Result: 在 5 类物体的 50 个铰链任务和 50 个随机初始化的末端执行器-手柄配置上，GSAM 相比最佳基线将标准差降低 3.1%，操作成功率提升 36.0%。

Conclusion: GSAM 通过 VLM 常识推理和 LLM 约束规划有效提升了关节物体操作的泛化能力和安全性，验证了多模态推理在机器人操作中的实用价值。

Classification reason: 该论文使用VLM进行链式思维（COT）常识推理来优化关节物体操作中的感知结果，属于多模态推理与思维链范畴，因此映射到 canonical subtopic "多模态推理与思维链"。

Importance reason: Focuses on robotic manipulation, not core multimodal generation or understanding. Signals: robotics, articulated object manipulation, VLM fine-tuning, chain-of-thought reasoning, safety constraints

Abstract: Articulated object manipulation is a unique challenge for service robots. Existing methods employ end-to-end policy learning, visionmotion planning, and large-language/visual-language model (LLM/VLM), but often overlook the diversity of articulated objects and the complexity of interactions between end-effector and handle, leading to limited generalization and destructive collisions. To address this, we propose GSAM, a generalizable and safe robotic framework for articulated object manipulation. Specifically, a vision-based perceiver generates the kinematic parameters. Considering that pre-trained markers in perceiver yield raw estimations that may deviate from commonsense, we present a f ine-tuned VLM-based refiner, using chain-of-thought (COT) commonsense reasoning to refine perception. To prevent destructive collisions, we design an interaction constraint function generator, integrating articulated object, interaction pose, and obstacle avoidance knowledge into a base. LLM then functionalize these constraints and apply them to trajectory and posture planning. A kinematic-aware manipulation planner verifies reachability for trajectory and posture. Experiments on 50 hinge tasks across 5 object categories and 50 randomly initialized end-effectorhandle configurations show that GSAM reduces standard deviation by 3.1% and improves manipulation success rate by 36.0% compared to the best baseline, respectively demonstrating the superior object generalization and interaction safety of GSAM in practical scenarios.

</details>


### [168] [LongTraceRL: Learning Long-Context Reasoning from Search Agent Trajectories with Rubric Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31584)
*Nianyi Lin, Jiajie Zhang, Lei Hou, Juanzi Li*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.LG, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (54.71) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: LongTraceRL 提出利用搜索智能体轨迹构建高混淆度的长上下文训练数据，并设计基于实体的细粒度过程奖励（rubric reward）来监督推理步骤，从而提升 LLM 的长上下文推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长上下文推理方法受限于低混淆度的干扰项和稀疏的最终结果奖励信号，无法有效监督中间推理步骤，导致模型难以在大量干扰信息中定位和整合关键信息。

Method: 通过知识图谱随机游走生成多跳问题，利用搜索智能体轨迹构建分层干扰项（已读未引用/搜索结果未打开），形成高挑战性训练上下文；提出 rubric reward，利用推理链上的实体作为细粒度过程监督信号，仅对答案正确的响应施加该奖励，以区分正确响应中的推理质量并防止奖励黑客。

Result: 在三个推理 LLM（4B-30B）和五个长上下文基准上，LongTraceRL 持续优于强基线方法，并促进了全面、基于证据的推理行为。

Conclusion: 基于搜索智能体轨迹构建的高混淆度训练数据与细粒度过程奖励相结合，能有效提升 LLM 的长上下文推理能力，为推理模型的训练提供了新的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究长上下文推理，通过搜索智能体轨迹构建多跳问题与分层干扰项，并设计基于实体的细粒度过程奖励（rubric reward）来提升推理质量。这属于多模态推理与思维链（multimodal_reasoning_cot）范畴，该 canonical subtopic 涵盖推理、CoT、推理时计算扩展等主题，与本论文高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on long-context reasoning via search agent trajectories, which is tangential to the user's core interests in multimodal generation and continuous VLM. Signals: Long-context reasoning, RL with verifiable rewards, search agent trajectories, rubric reward, entity-level process supervision

Abstract: Long-context reasoning remains a central challenge for large language models, which often fail to locate and integrate key information in extensive distracting content. Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has shown promise for this task, yet existing methods are limited by low-confusability distractors and sparse, outcome-only reward signals that cannot supervise intermediate reasoning steps. To address these issues, we introduce \textsc{LongTraceRL}. For data construction, we generate multi-hop questions via knowledge graph random walks and leverage search agent trajectories to build \emph{tiered distractors}: documents the agent read but did not cite (high confusability) and documents that appeared in search results but were never opened (low confusability), producing training contexts that are far more challenging than those built by random sampling or one-shot search. For reward design, we propose a \emph{rubric reward} that uses the gold entities along each reasoning chain as fine-grained, entity-level process supervision. This rubric reward is applied only to responses with correct final answers (positive-only strategy), distinguishing the reasoning quality among correct responses and preventing reward hacking. Experiments on three reasoning LLMs (4B--30B) across five long-context benchmarks demonstrate that \textsc{LongTraceRL} consistently outperforms strong baselines and encourages comprehensive, evidence-grounded reasoning. Codes, datasets and models are available at \href{ }{ }.

</details>

### [169] [Variational Adapter for Cross-modal Similarity Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30968)
*WenZhang Wei, Zhipeng Gui, Dehua Peng, Tiandi Ye, Huayi Wu*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 值得关注 (68.09) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 VACSR（Variational Adapter for Cross-modal Similarity Representation），将图文匹配中因细粒度语义标注稀缺导致的二值分类问题重新建模为变分推理问题，通过构建跨模态相似性的潜空间并引入正则化技术缓解对二值标注的过拟合，在图像-文本检索、域泛化和基类到新类泛化任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数图文匹配和多类图像分类数据集缺乏细粒度的跨模态匹配标注，迫使连续的相似性空间被压缩为二值分类边界，导致假负样本问题并严重损害跨模态任务的泛化性能。现有方法仅建模模态内歧义性，忽视了标注本身的缺陷，导致不确定性分配次优。

Method: 提出 VACSR，将细粒度语义稀缺下的图文匹配重新表述为变分推理问题。具体地，构建跨模态相似性的潜空间，利用变分推断和正则化技术来缓解模型对二值标注的过拟合，从而更合理地分配不确定性。

Result: 在图像-文本检索、域泛化和基类到新类泛化三个任务上的实验表明，VACSR 方法有效且具有鲁棒的泛化能力。

Conclusion: VACSR 通过变分推理框架有效解决了图文匹配中因细粒度标注稀缺导致的二值化过拟合问题，提升了跨模态相似性表示的泛化性能。

Classification reason: 论文提出变分适配器（VACSR）用于改进图文匹配中的跨模态相似性表示，核心任务是图像-文本检索（image-text retrieval），属于跨模态检索范畴。该方向在 multimodal_understanding_generation 下有 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "多模态检索"（multimodal_retrieval），在 multimodal_generation 下也有同名子主题，因此直接复用。

Importance reason: Focuses on cross-modal retrieval with binary annotation issues, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks strong alignment with user's key boost keywords. Signals: Cross-modal retrieval, Variational inference, Binary annotation problem, Domain generalization, Not autoregressive or video generation

Abstract: The core of vision-language models lies in measuring cross-modal similarity within a unified representation space. However, most image-text matching or multi-class image classification datasets lack fine-grained cross-modal matching annotations, forcing the continuous similarity space into binary classification boundaries. This compression induces false negative samples and significantly impairs the generalization performance of cross-modal tasks. While prior research has attempted to mitigate this by modeling intra-modal ambiguity, it often overlooks inherent annotation flaws, leading to suboptimal uncertainty allocation. To address these challenges, we propose a Variational Adapter for Cross-modal Similarity Representation (VACSR). This approach reformulates image-text matching with fine-grained semantic scarcity as a variational inference problem. It constructs a latent space for cross-modal similarity and uses regularization techniques to mitigate overfitting to binary annotations. Experiments on image-text retrieval, domain generalization, and base-to-novel generalization demonstrate the proposed method's effectiveness and robust generalization ability.

</details>


### [170] [FOCUS: Forcing In-Context Object Localization through Visual Support Constraints and Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31145)
*Mohammed Asad Karim, Vinay Kumar Verma*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 普通相关 (56.64) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 FOCUS 框架，通过两阶段训练（显式优化上下文注意力 + GRPO 强化学习）实现无需类别监督的上下文目标定位，7B 模型超越 72B 模型，证明了上下文感知定位目标可以超越单纯规模扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文定位方法依赖显式类别监督，导致类别偏差（偏向语义先验而非视觉证据），且无法处理未命名或实例特定的对象，限制了在图像编辑、个性化搜索等实际场景中的应用。

Method: 提出两阶段训练框架：第一阶段显式优化支持框与查询图像之间的上下文注意力（in-context attention），无需类别监督；第二阶段使用 GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimization）强化学习直接最小化定位误差，强制模型依赖视觉对应关系而非语义先验。

Result: 7B 参数模型在上下文定位任务上超越了包括 72B 在内的更大模型，消融实验验证了各组件的贡献。

Conclusion: 上下文感知的定位目标函数可以超越单纯模型规模扩展，为无需类别监督的实例级视觉定位提供了有效方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究上下文目标定位（in-context localization），通过支持示例在查询图像中定位目标对象，属于跨模态检索与视觉搜索范畴。multimodal_understanding_generation 方向下的 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态检索"（multimodal_retrieval）涵盖跨模态检索、组合检索、多模态搜索，与本论文的视觉定位与检索任务高度相关。

Importance reason: Novel in-context localization method but lacks direct connection to priority subtopics like continuous VLM or video generation. Signals: In-context localization, GRPO reinforcement learning, category-agnostic, outperforms larger models, no priority subtopic match

Abstract: In-context localization (ICL) seeks to localize a target object specified by a small set of support examples in a query image, operating on the fly without training or parameter updates. Despite rapid advances in vision-language models (VLMs), achieving category-agnostic and visually grounded ICL remains an open problem, even though it is essential for applications such as image editing, personalized visual search, and retrieval. Existing methods are fragile and rely on explicit category supervision, which not only limits applicability in realistic settings with unnamed or instance-specific objects but also introduces category bias that steers predictions toward semantic priors rather than visual evidence. We introduce a two-stage training framework that explicitly optimizes in-context attention between support bounding boxes and query images without category supervision. We further refine localization via reinforcement learning using Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) to directly minimize localization error. This formulation enforces visual correspondence over semantic priors, yielding robust instance-level localization. Empirically, a 7B-parameter model trained with our objectives outperforms models up to 72B parameters, demonstrating that context-aware localization objectives can surpass scaling alone. Comprehensive ablations validate the contribution of each component.

</details>


### [171] [Beyond Classification: Dynamic Adapter Routing for Continual Multimodal Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31229)
*Alicja Dobrzeniecka, Filip Szatkowski, Sebastian Cygert, Szymon Lukasik, Bartlomiej Twardowski*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 普通相关 (58.31) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出持续多模态检索（CMR）的新评估框架，并发现标准类增量学习方法在此场景下效果有限。作者提出动态适配器路由（DAR）方法，通过原型路由选择适配器并组合，在跨域持续检索任务中取得优于基线的性能，并展现出良好的分布外泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型的检索功能需要持续更新以适应新领域，但现有工作大多从类增量学习（CIL）角度处理持续检索，未能充分捕捉检索任务的独特动态。缺乏专门针对持续多模态检索的评估框架和方法。

Method: 提出 Dynamic Adapter Routing (DAR) 方法：基于原型路由（prototype-based routing）选择适配器（adapters），并通过模型组合的方式实现持续多模态检索。同时构建了覆盖多个视觉领域的持续多模态检索评估框架。

Result: DAR 方法在持续多模态检索任务上优于现有基线方法，并在分布外（OOD）评估下展现出良好的泛化能力。标准 CIL 方法在更具挑战性的 CMR 场景中未能带来有意义的性能提升。

Conclusion: 持续多模态检索（CMR）具有独特的挑战，不能简单套用类增量学习方法。DAR 方法通过动态适配器路由有效应对了这些挑战，为该方向提供了新的研究思路和基线。

Classification reason: 论文聚焦于持续多模态检索（Continual Multimodal Retrieval），属于多模态检索方向。该方向在 multimodal_understanding_generation 下有 [CANONICAL] 的 multimodal_retrieval 子主题，同时在 multimodal_generation 下也有同名子主题，因此直接复用。

Importance reason: Focuses on continual multimodal retrieval, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to the user's key interests in generation and continuous VLM. Signals: Continual multimodal retrieval, Dynamic adapter routing, Out-of-distribution generalization, Not aligned with priority subtopics, No boost keywords matched

Abstract: While retrieval is a core function of vision-language models, continually updating these models for retrieval tasks remains critically underexplored. Existing work often approaches continual retrieval through the lens of class-incremental learning (CIL), evaluating both standard CIL methods and retrieval-oriented adaptations in settings that may not fully capture the retrieval-specific dynamics. To address this, we introduce a new, principled evaluation framework for continual multimodal retrieval (CMR) spanning diverse visual domains, and systematically evaluate common approaches within this setting. Our empirical analysis shows that standard CIL methods fail to yield meaningful gains in our more challenging scenario. Therefore, we propose Dynamic Adapter Routing (DAR), a novel approach based on adapters selected through prototype-based routing and combined via model achieves superior performance over the previous baselines and demonstrates strong generalization under out-of-distribution evaluation. Our results highlights the unique challenges of CMR and encourages further research in this direction.

</details>
