### [172] [Seeing Isn't Knowing: Do VLMs Know When Not to Answer Spatial Questions (and Why)?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30557)
*Yue Zhang, Zun Wang, Han Lin, Yonatan Bitton, Idan Szpektor, Mohit Bansal*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (57.96) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 SpatialUncertain 评测框架，系统评估 VLM 在空间推理中面对遮挡和视角歧义时是否知道何时应拒绝回答。实验发现主流 VLM 存在过度自信回答的失败模式，在遮挡和视角歧义下平均准确率分别仅约 30% 和低于 10%，且在选择可靠额外视角时接近随机水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间推理基准通常假设视觉观察充分可靠，仅关注模型能否给出正确答案，而忽略了模型是否能够识别问题不可回答的情况以及需要哪些额外观察信息。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 构建 SpatialUncertain 评测框架，引入两类观察挑战：(1) 遮挡——隐藏目标信息；(2) 视角歧义——产生误导性视觉线索。针对每种配置设计在干净观察下可回答但在挑战下需拒绝回答的空间问题，并评估模型能否识别哪些额外视角可解决歧义。

Result: 在多种前沿开源和闭源 VLM 上，模型在遮挡下平均准确率约 30%，在视角歧义下低于 10%；即使提供额外视角，部分模型在选择可靠视角时接近随机水平。

Conclusion: VLM 在空间推理中存在过度自信回答的系统性失败模式，研究应超越答案正确性，转向评估模型是否知道何时应拒绝回答以及如何寻求可靠证据。

Classification reason: 该论文构建了 SpatialUncertain 评测框架，系统评估 VLM 在空间推理中面对遮挡和视角歧义时是否知道何时应拒绝回答，属于多模态评测与基准方向，因此复用 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准"。

Importance reason: Focuses on VLM spatial reasoning failure modes but is a benchmark/evaluation paper, not a method advancing the user's priority subtopics. Signals: VLM evaluation, spatial reasoning, overconfidence, occlusion, perspective ambiguity

Abstract: Spatial reasoning is a fundamental capability for vision-language models (VLMs) deployed in real-world environments. However, visual observations are inherently limited representations of a 3D world: occlusion can render objects invisible, and perspective can make geometric properties misleading. Despite this, existing spatial reasoning benchmarks typically assume that observations are sufficient and reliable, focusing on whether models produce correct answers rather than whether they recognize when a question cannot be answered and what additional observations would be needed. In this work, we challenge this assumption by constructing a controlled evaluation framework, SpatialUncertain, and introducing two types of observation challenges: (1) occlusion, which hides target information, and (2) perspective ambiguity, which produces misleading visual cues. For each configuration, we design spatial questions that are answerable under clean observations but require abstention under the introduced challenges. We further evaluate whether models can identify which additional viewpoints would resolve perspective ambiguity. Our results across a diverse set of frontier open- and closed-source VLMs reveal two consistent failure modes. First, models are prone to overconfident answering, attempting to solve spatial reasoning tasks even when visual evidence is incomplete or misleading, with average accuracy around 30\% under occlusion and below 10\% under perspective ambiguity. Second, even when additional views are available, some models perform near random chance in identifying which would provide reliable evidence. Together, our findings call for moving beyond answer correctness toward evaluating whether models know when to abstain and how to seek reliable evidence.

</details>


### [173] [PInVerify: An Offline Embodied Benchmark for Active Instance Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30639)
*Yuhang Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.RO, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (76.51) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Active Instance Verification (AIV) 任务，要求具身智能体主动选择视角验证候选物体是否匹配细粒度自然语言描述。作者构建了 PInVerify 离线基准（3000 个评估片段，18 个类别），并基于开源 MLLM（≤8B）搭建了无需训练和 LoRA 微调的基线系统，实验表明最佳 MLLM 基线超过最佳 embedding 基线 4.9 个百分点，LoRA 微调后达到 85.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身导航任务仅关注到达目标物体附近，但到达后无法保证智能体找到了正确的实例——细微的属性差异（如"白色花卉"vs"白色条纹"）需要近距离多视角检查。现有基准缺乏对主动、细粒度语义验证能力的评估。

Method: 形式化 AIV 为有限时域决策过程；构建 PInVerify 离线基准（6 扇区导航拓扑、陷阱视角、不可达扇区）；搭建基于开源 MLLM 的无需训练流水线和 LoRA 微调端到端智能体，包含属性分解、可见性加权多视角追踪器和三种 next-best-view 策略。

Result: 最佳 MLLM 基线（Qwen3-VL-8B）超过最佳 embedding 基线（SigLIP2）4.9 个百分点；GT-box 消融显示 +3.1 个百分点的检测差距；主动视角选择策略未带来可靠提升；LoRA 微调（SFT+GSPO）达到 85.6%。

Conclusion: PInVerify 为具身 AI 中的主动细粒度语义验证提供了标准化评测平台，当前 MLLM 基线在该任务上仍有提升空间，主动视角选择策略需要进一步探索。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 PInVerify，一个面向具身智能体主动实例验证（AIV）任务的离线多模态评测基准，包含 3000 个评估片段和 18 个物体类别，属于多模态评测与基准范畴，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准"。

Importance reason: Focuses on embodied AI benchmark for instance verification, not directly on user's priority subtopics like continuous VLM or autoregressive generation. Signals: Embodied AI benchmark, Active Instance Verification, MLLM baseline, LoRA fine-tuning, offline evaluation

Abstract: Embodied agents have made strong progress in navigating to target objects, but reaching the goal vicinity does not guarantee that the agent has found the correct instance: subtle attribute differences (e.g., "white floral" vs. "white striped") often require close-range, multi-view inspection. We address this gap with Active Instance Verification (AIV), a task in which an agent actively selects viewpoints around a candidate object to decide whether it matches a fine-grained natural-language description. We formalize AIV as a finite-horizon decision process and introduce PInVerify, an offline embodied benchmark for AIV: 3,000 evaluation episodes across 18 object categories, delivered as multi-view captures with a 6-sector navigation topology that exposes trap views (navigable but uninformative) and unreachable sectors. As reference baselines we build a training-free pipeline and a LoRA-fine-tuned end-to-end agent around open-source multimodal large language models (MLLMs) at on-device scale ($\leq$8B parameters), with attribute decomposition, a visibility-weighted multi-view tracker, and three next-best-view (NBV) strategies. In our evaluation across Qwen3-VL (4B/8B), SenseNova-SI-1.2-InternVL3-8B, CLIP, and SigLIP2, the best MLLM-based baseline exceeds the best embedding baseline by 4.9 pp; GT-box ablations show a +3.1 pp detection gap; and we do not observe reliable gains from active viewpoint selection within the tested NBV strategies. A LoRA-fine-tuned agent (SFT+GSPO) reaches 85.6%. PInVerify aims to support further work on active, fine-grained semantic verification in embodied AI. Code: .

</details>


### [174] [BilliardPhys-Bench: Benchmarking Physical Reasoning and Visual Dynamics of Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30900)
*Ben Wang, Xiaogang Li, Ruochen Gao, Peiyao Xiao, Chengliang Xu, Zeyu Wang, Zichao Chen, Bing Zhao, Hu Wei*

Main category: cs.AI, physics.app-ph

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (76.16) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 BilliardPhys-Bench，一个基于合成台球环境的物理推理基准，用于评估多模态大模型从单张图像预测物体运动与交互的能力。该基准通过程序化引擎生成随机场景，测试碰撞预测、墙壁反弹和最终位置估计三项能力。评估了 GPT、Claude、Gemini、Qwen 等系列模型，发现随着模拟时间增长和场景复杂度增加，模型性能下降，并存在一种称为"静态偏差"的常见失败模式——当正确物理结果难以推断时，模型倾向于预测无交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态模型在静态图像识别方面表现良好，但在直觉物理推理方面仍然薄弱。从单张图像预测物体如何运动与交互对现有系统来说仍然困难，因此需要专门的基准来系统评估和揭示这一缺陷。

Method: 设计了一个基于合成台球环境的程序化引擎，生成包含摩擦和弹性碰撞的随机场景。基准测试三项能力：(1) 预测球与球碰撞，(2) 推理墙壁反弹，(3) 估计运动停止后球的最终位置。评估了 GPT、Claude、Gemini、Qwen 系列的最新多模态大模型。

Result: 随着模拟时间增加和场景几何复杂度提升，模型性能显著下降。发现一种称为"静态偏差"（stasis bias）的常见失败模式：当正确物理结果更难推断时，模型倾向于预测无交互发生。

Conclusion: 这些发现揭示了当前多模态大模型在视觉动态推理方面的局限性，表明需要更好的物理归纳偏置来改进多模态架构。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 BilliardPhys-Bench，一个用于评估多模态大模型物理推理能力的基准，属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一 [CANONICAL] 子主题。

Importance reason: This benchmark paper on physical reasoning is not aligned with the user's priority subtopics on generation and efficiency. Signals: Physical reasoning benchmark, Multimodal LLM evaluation, Stasis bias failure mode, Synthetic billiards environment, Not generation-focused

Abstract: Current multimodal models handle static image recognition well, but intuitive physical reasoning remains a weakness. Predicting how objects will move and interact from a single image is still difficult for these systems. We present BilliardPhys-Bench, a benchmark for physical reasoning in synthetic billiards environments. Its procedural engine generates randomized scenarios with friction and elastic collisions. The benchmark tests three abilities: (1) predicting ball-to-ball collisions, (2) reasoning about wall bounces, and (3) estimating final ball positions after motion stops. We evaluate recent MLLMs from the GPT, Claude, Gemini, and Qwen families. Performance drops as simulation time increases and scene geometry grows more complex. We also observe a consistent failure mode we call "stasis bias": when the correct physical outcome is harder to infer, models tend to predict no interaction. These findings show where current MLLMs break down on visual dynamics and point toward the need for better physical inductive biases in multimodal architectures.

</details>


### [175] [FBHM: Functional Benchmarking and Steering of VLMs for Hateful Meme Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31349)
*Paramananda Bhaskar, Naquee Rizwan, Daksh Jogchand, Saurabh Kumar Pandey, Animesh Mukherjee*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV, cs.CL, cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出FBHM基准，沿修辞功能与目标社区两个正交轴构建5000个仇恨模因，揭示VLM在标准数据集上高准确率但在FBHM上近乎随机，并提出LSV（可学习转向向量）方法，仅用500个样本即可提升约30个Macro-F1点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仇恨模因检测基准存在结构性观察偏差，混淆了修辞机制与目标社区特征，无法对模型脆弱性进行因果评估。需要一种能够解耦这些因素的系统性基准来真正评测VLM的鲁棒多模态推理能力。

Method: 1) 构建FBHM基准：沿25种修辞功能×10个目标社区两个正交轴，生成5000个模因，实现因果解耦评测。2) 提出LSV（可学习转向向量）：在超低数据 regime（500个样本，50个独特基础模因）下，通过因果干预目标学习转向向量，引导VLM关注真正的多模态推理而非数据集捷径。

Result: SOTA VLM在FBHM上性能从标准数据集的~90%骤降至接近随机；LSV方法仅用500个样本即可提升约30个Macro-F1点，优于上下文学习和PEFT，且不降低源域性能。

Conclusion: 现有VLM在仇恨模因检测中严重依赖数据集特定启发式而非鲁棒多模态推理；FBHM提供了因果解耦的评测框架，LSV提供了一种高效、低成本的模型纠偏方法，为构建更可靠的VLM提供了新方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了FBHM基准，系统性地构建了基于功能性的仇恨模因数据集，用于评测VLM在多模态理解中的鲁棒性和泛化能力，属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一[CANONICAL]子主题。同时涉及AIGC方向的安全治理（仇恨内容检测）。

Importance reason: Focuses on hateful meme detection benchmark, not aligned with user's priority subtopics like continuous VLM, video generation, or model efficiency. Signals: VLM benchmarking, hateful meme detection, causal evaluation, learnable steering vectors, low-data intervention

Abstract: Hateful meme detection remains a formidable challenge for vision-language models, as existing benchmarks are structurally observational - confounding rhetorical hate mechanisms with target community features and preventing causal evaluation of model vulnerabilities. To address this, we introduce FBHM, a systematically curated benchmark of Functionality Based Hateful Memes constructed along two orthogonal axes: 25 distinct rhetorical functionalities and 10 target communities (5,000 memes total). Benchmarking state-of-the-art VLMs reveals a severe generalization gap: models highly accurate on standard datasets catastrophically drop to near-random performance on FBHM, proving they exploit dataset-specific heuristics rather than robust multimodal reasoning. To efficiently close this gap, we propose LSV (learnable steering vectors), an ultra-low data regime strategy that applies a causal intervention objective on as few as 500 steering samples (50 unique base memes), boosting FBHM performance by ~30 Macro-F1 points while outperforming in-context learning and PEFT without degrading source-domain performance.

</details>


### [176] [TeachObs: A Human-Validated Benchmark for Multimodal Teaching Observation and Model Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30673)
*Yeil Jeong, Youngjin Yoo, Seobin Sohn, Hyejin Han, Jinseo Lee, Scott Howard, Unggi Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

TL;DR: TeachObs 是一个人工验证的多模态课堂教学观察基准，包含来自8个国家的30节公开课视频，划分为5158个15秒场景，由7名研究者标注39个二元观察编码（20个视觉+19个非视觉），并提供了课程级专家评分。基于该基准评估了5个前沿视觉 LLM，发现没有单一模型在所有任务上一致最优，添加视频帧会同时增加正确和错误归因，且模型对程序清晰的课程评分偏高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 课堂视频中包含丰富的可观察教学行为，但其教学和视觉信号很少以适合模型评估的形式组织，缺乏标准化的多模态教学观察基准来评估 AI 系统在课堂视频分析中的能力。

Method: 收集30节来自8个国家的公开课视频，分割为5158个15秒场景；7名研究者对每个场景标注39个二元观察编码（20个视觉+19个非视觉），使用基于 Krippendorff's alpha 的信度和流行度感知规则构建金标准标签；3名专家对30节课进行课程级评分。在此基础上设计三个评测轨道：纯文本场景编码、文本+帧场景编码、以及 LLM-as-judge 的课程级覆盖评估。

Result: 5个前沿视觉 LLM 在三个评测轨道上表现各异，没有单一模型在所有轨道上一致最优；添加视频帧会同时增加正确和错误的归因数量；模型对程序清晰的课程评分高于专家评分。

Conclusion: TeachObs 支持细粒度标注基准测试和整体课程评估，揭示了 AI 系统在课堂视频分析中可辅助的环节以及仍需专家判断的领域，为多模态教学观察提供了标准化评测平台。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个针对课堂教学视频的多模态教学观察基准 TeachObs，包含细粒度标注和课程级评估，属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一规范主题。同时该基准涉及 AI 生成内容评测，也匹配 AIGC 评测与基准（aigc_evaluation_benchmark）。

Importance reason: The paper focuses on a niche educational benchmark, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous VLM, autoregressive video generation, or world models. Signals: Multimodal benchmark, classroom video analysis, no priority subtopic match, negative keywords absent but not relevant, low direction weight for multimodal_understanding_generation

Abstract: Classroom videos contain observable teaching practices, but their pedagogical and visual signals are rarely organized in forms suitable for model evaluation. We present \textit{TeachObs}, a human-validated benchmark for multimodal teaching observation in classroom videos. \textit{TeachObs} includes 30 public lesson videos from eight countries divided into 5,158 fixed 15-second scenes. Seven researchers annotated each scene with 39 binary observation codes, covering 20 visual codes, such as gesture, board work, pointing, and visual materials, and 19 nonvisual codes, such as instruction, monitoring, questioning, feedback, and reflection. Gold segment labels are constructed using reliability- and prevalence-aware rules based on Krippendorff's alpha. In addition to segment-level labels, three expert raters produced lesson-level ratings and qualitative evaluations of instructional design, instructional delivery, learner response, learning materials, and lesson closure across the 30 lessons, with rater coverage detailed in the body. Using these two human reference layers, we evaluate five vision-capable frontier LLMs across three tracks - text-only segment coding, text + frame segment coding, and lesson-level coverage scored under an LLM-as-judge protocol - and find that no single model consistently outperforms others across all three tracks, that adding a mid-frame inflates both true and false attributions per scene, and that model evaluations over-rate procedurally clear lessons relative to expert raters. \textit{TeachObs} therefore supports both fine-grained annotation benchmarking and whole-lesson evaluation, showing where AI systems can assist classroom video analysis and where expert judgment remains necessary across varied subjects, classroom formats, and annotation difficulty levels.

</details>


### [177] [FAM-Bench: A Multimodal Benchmark for Condition-Aware Food-as-Medicine Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31410)
*Mingyang Mao, Bhargav Rishi Medisetti, Utkarsh Grover, Tanvir Ibrahim, Wenyan Li, Tingting Zhang, Xiaomin Lin*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有食物AI基准主要评估菜肴识别、食谱理解、营养估计或一般营养问答，缺乏对"食物是否适合特定健康状况"这一健康感知决策层的测试。作者希望填补这一空白，为语言和视觉语言模型提供标准化的健康感知推理测试平台。

Method: 构建了包含2500个营养专家验证实例的FAM-Bench基准，覆盖13种饮食相关健康状况。设计两个互补任务：(1) 菜肴级适宜性评估——模型根据图像和配料列表判断菜肴是否适合某种健康状况；(2) 比较菜肴分析——模型对四个候选菜肴按条件适宜性排序。两个任务都需要整合配料证据、视觉制备线索和临床营养约束。

Result: 论文主要贡献是基准的构建和验证，具体实验结果在摘要中未详细给出，但基准本身经过营养专家验证，为后续评估提供了标准化测试平台。

Conclusion: FAM-Bench填补了食物AI在健康感知推理方面的评估空白，为研究模型在"食物即药物"场景下的多模态推理能力提供了标准化基准。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了FAM-Bench，一个面向"食物即药物"场景的多模态基准，包含两个任务（菜肴适宜性评估和比较分析），属于多模态评测与基准方向。该基准涉及视觉和语言理解，因此归类到多模态理解与生成方向下的[多模态评测与基准]（multimodal_benchmark）子主题。

Importance reason: Niche food-health benchmark with low alignment to user's priority subtopics on continuous VLM, video/image generation, or model efficiency. Signals: food-as-medicine benchmark, health-aware reasoning, no autoregressive or diffusion generation, no continuous VLM or latent flow, no world model or efficient inference

Abstract: Food-as-Medicine requires models to reason beyond what a dish is or what nutrition it contains: they must decide whether a concrete food choice is appropriate for a specific health condition. Existing food AI benchmarks primarily evaluate dish recognition, recipe understanding, nutrient estimation, or general nutrition question answering, leaving this health-aware decision layer largely untested. We introduce FAM-Bench, a multi-modal Food-as-Medicine benchmark with 2500 nutrition-expert-verified instances across 13 diet-related health conditions. The benchmark contains two complementary tasks: dish-level suitability assessment, where models judge whether a dish is suitable for a condition from its image and ingredient list, and comparative dish analysis, where models rank four candidate dishes by condition-specific suitability. Both tasks require integrating ingredient evidence, visual preparation cues, and clinical nutrition constraints, providing a standardized testbed for grounded health-aware reasoning in language and vision-language models.

</details>


### [178] [Does Visual Information Play a Decisive Role in Vision-Language-Action Model Driving Behavior?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31041)
*Jingtao He, Hongliang Lu, Xiaoyun Qiu, Yixuan Wang, Xinhu Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个结构化多层级视觉扰动框架，从通道退化、信息层级破坏和结构层级修改三个维度系统分析VLA驾驶模型对视觉信息的依赖程度，并在开环轨迹预测和闭环安全评估下揭示了评估依赖的视觉-行为依赖模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA驾驶模型评估主要关注聚合性能指标，缺乏结构化的诊断方法来量化视觉信息对驾驶行为的影响程度，导致对VLA模型如何利用视觉信息进行决策的理解不足。

Method: 提出一个多层级视觉扰动框架，包含三个互补维度：通道级退化（颜色/噪声扰动）、信息级破坏（遮挡/裁剪）、结构级修改（布局/语义变化）。在开环轨迹预测和交互式闭环安全评估两种设置下，系统性地扰动视觉输入并观察VLA驾驶模型的行为变化。

Result: 实验发现VLA驾驶模型在不同评估设置下表现出不同的视觉依赖模式，且在不同抽象层级上的视觉信息利用不均匀，揭示了当前VLA模型视觉-行为依赖的复杂性和不稳定性。

Conclusion: 需要更结构化的分析和更原则性的VLA驾驶模型设计，以更好地理解视觉信息如何塑造驾驶行为，从而开发更安全、更鲁棒的系统。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一种结构化多层级视觉扰动框架，用于系统分析VLA驾驶模型对视觉信息的依赖程度，本质上是一种针对VLA模型行为的新型评测协议。这与[多模态评测与基准]（multimodal_benchmark）方向高度吻合，属于评测方法与协议范畴。

Importance reason: Systematic analysis of visual grounding in VLA driving models, but outside user's priority subtopics and lacks direct connection to generative or continuous multimodal methods. Signals: VLA driving model, visual-behavior dependency, multi-level visual perturbation, open-loop and closed-loop evaluation, autonomous driving

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated promising capability in autonomous driving, highlighting the potential of unified multimodal architectures for jointly modeling perception and planning. However, how current VLA-based driving behavior is grounded in visual information remains poorly understood. Existing evaluation protocols mainly focus on aggregate performance metrics, lacking structured and practical diagnostics to quantify visual-behavior dependency. In this work, we introduce a structured multi-level visual perturbation framework to analyze visual-behavior dependency in VLA-based driving models systematically. The framework organizes controlled visual perturbations along three complementary dimensions: channellevel degradation, information-level disruption, and structurelevel modification. We apply it to VLA-based driving systems and evaluate behavioral responses under both open-loop trajectory prediction and interactive closed-loop safety evaluation. Experimental results reveal evaluation-dependent dependency patterns and uneven visual grounding across abstraction levels. These findings call for more structured analyses and principled design of VLA driving models to better understand how visual information shapes behavior and develop safer, more robust systems.

</details>


### [179] [MineExplorer: Evaluating Open-World Exploration of MLLM Agents in Minecraft](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30931)
*Tianjie Ju, Yueqing Sun, Zheng Wu, Wei Zhang, Yaqi Huo, Xi Su, Qi Gu, Xunliang Cai, Gongshen Liu, Zhuosheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (76.51) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 MineExplorer，一个用于评估多模态大语言模型（MLLM）智能体在 Minecraft 开放世界中探索能力的基准。通过筛选原子任务、构建 ReAct 风格能力框架、设计隐式多跳任务，并采用多智能体合成工作流自动生成任务图、沙盒场景和里程碑评估器，构建了可靠的评测实例。实验表明开放世界探索对当前 MLLM 智能体仍具挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身和游戏基准通常将交互压缩为短视任务，或与领域特定游戏机制纠缠不清，难以评估 MLLM 智能体在动态开放世界中的持续探索能力。

Method: 首先筛选不依赖 Minecraft 特定知识的原子任务以反映通用开放世界推理；围绕 ReAct 风格能力框架组织基准，将原子任务组合为隐式多跳任务；使用多智能体合成工作流联合设计任务图、沙盒场景和基于规则的里程碑评估器，自动构建可靠评测实例。

Result: 人类评估表明多智能体合成工作流比单智能体基线产生更可靠的实例。实验显示开放世界探索仍具挑战性：强模型能处理许多单跳任务，但在需要协调隐藏先决条件的长轨迹任务中性能急剧下降。任务难度与智能体完成度相关，更大模型或思维模式并不一致地带来更好性能。

Conclusion: MineExplorer 为评估 MLLM 智能体的开放世界探索能力提供了有效基准，揭示了当前模型在长轨迹多跳任务中的局限性，为未来研究指明了方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 MineExplorer 基准，用于评估 MLLM 智能体在 Minecraft 开放世界中的探索能力，属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一规范主题。同时涉及 AIGC 方向下的 AI 智能体与生成工作流（aigc_agent_workflow），因为使用了多智能体合成工作流来构建评测实例。

Importance reason: Evaluates MLLM agents in Minecraft but focuses on general exploration rather than the user's priority subtopics like continuous VLM or autoregressive generation. Signals: MLLM agent benchmark, open-world exploration, Minecraft, multi-agent synthesis, implicit multi-hop tasks

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have shown strong capabilities in perception, reasoning, and action generation. However, their ability to sustain exploration in dynamic open worlds remains unclear. Existing embodied and game-based benchmarks often compress interaction into short-horizon tasks or entangle success with domain-specific game mechanics. In this paper, we introduce MineExplorer benchmark for evaluating open-world exploration capabilities of MLLM agents in Minecraft. We first filter atomic tasks whose solutions rely heavily on Minecraft-specific knowledge to better reflect general open-world reasoning. Then we organize the benchmark around a ReAct-style capability formulation and compose atomic tasks into implicit multi-hop tasks. To further construct reliable instances, MineExplorer uses a multi-agent synthesis workflow that jointly designs task graphs, sandbox scenes, and rule-based milestone evaluators. Human evaluation shows that the multi-agent synthesis workflow produces significantly more reliable instances than a single-agent baseline. Experiments with advanced MLLM agents show that open-world exploration remains challenging, as strong models can handle many single-hop tasks but degrade sharply when hidden prerequisites must be coordinated over longer trajectories. Further analysis finds that task difficulty tracks agent completion, and larger models or thinking modes do not consistently translate into better performance. Code and dataset are available at .

</details>


### [180] [Probing Collision Grounding in Vision-Language Models for Safe Human-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31196)
*Jun Wang, Xiaohao Xu, Xiaonan Huang*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI, cs.RO, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (57.96) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出TouchSafeBench，一个基于Habitat 3.0物理仿真的基准，用于评估视觉语言模型（VLM）在安全人机协作中的"碰撞接地"能力——即判断机器人是否与场景或人发生碰撞或即将碰撞。基准包含2940个室内共现场景，测试当前VLM在安全状态分类和碰撞预警两个任务上的表现，发现最佳平均Macro-F1低于50%，表明视觉流畅性不等于物理可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全人机协作需要监控系统不仅具备视觉描述能力，还需判断机器人是否与场景或人发生碰撞或即将碰撞。现有VLM缺乏对这种"碰撞接地"能力的系统评估，因此需要专门的基准来揭示当前模型的局限性。

Method: 在Habitat 3.0中构建TouchSafeBench基准，包含2940个室内共现场景（社交导航和社交重排任务），提供多视角RGB-D观测、俯视轨迹图、相机元数据和仿真接触标签。定义两个任务：当前安全状态分类和即将碰撞预警。评估了三个前沿/机器人VLM和九种视觉表示。

Result: 最佳模型平均Macro-F1低于50%；显式深度信息并未自动转化为机器人碰撞证据；机器人与场景的接触检测比人机接触更难。

Conclusion: 当前VLM在碰撞接地任务上远不可靠，视觉流畅性不等于物理可靠性。可靠的机器人安全监控需要显式绑定视角、机器人形态、度量几何和未来碰撞的表示。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了TouchSafeBench，一个用于评估VLM碰撞接地能力的物理仿真基准，属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一[CANONICAL]子主题。同时涉及机器人安全监控，也匹配AIGC方向下的生成内容安全与治理（aigc_safety_governance）子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on VLM safety evaluation for robotics, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous VLM, autoregressive generation, or world models. Signals: VLM safety benchmark, collision grounding, human-robot collaboration, low model performance, not aligned with user's generative or efficiency focus

Abstract: Safe human--robot collaboration requires more than visual description: a monitor must determine whether the robot body is safely separated, already colliding with the scene or a person, or about to collide. We call this capability collision grounding: binding visual observations to robot body geometry, camera viewpoint, scene layout, human proximity, and temporal motion in order to infer present and imminent contact. We introduce TouchSafeBench, a physics-grounded benchmark for evaluating collision grounding in vision-language models (VLMs). Built in Habitat~3.0, TouchSafeBench contains 2,940 simulated indoor co-presence episodes across social navigation and social rearrangement, with synchronized multi-view RGB-D observations, top-down trajectory maps, calibrated camera metadata, and simulator-derived contact labels. We study two deployment-facing tasks: classifying the current safety state and warning about imminent collision before contact. Across three frontier or robotics-oriented VLMs and nine visual representations, current models remain far from reliable: the best average Macro-F1 stays below 50\%, explicit depth is not automatically transformed into robot-body collision evidence, and robot--scene contact is consistently harder than human-contact risk. TouchSafeBench reveals a central limitation of embodied VLMs: visual fluency does not imply physical accountability. Reliable robot safety monitors will need representations that explicitly bind viewpoint, robot morphology, metric geometry, and future collision. We will release the benchmark upon acceptance.

</details>


### [181] [On the Robustness of Multilingual Text Embedding Rankings Across Learning Tasks, Languages, and Benchmark Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31142)
*Ana Gjorgjevikj, Barbara Koroušić Seljak, Tome Eftimov*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (52.89) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文对多语言文本嵌入模型在MTEB基准上的排名鲁棒性进行了元研究，提出了数据集组成鲁棒性和排名方案鲁棒性两个指标，分析了五种主要语言和九类任务，发现基于LLM的大模型通常是鲁棒的顶尖模型，但在检索等任务上并非一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模多语言文本嵌入模型在研究和工业中广泛应用，但MTEB等基准测试中模型优越性的结论往往依赖于隐式的数据集组成选择和性能聚合方法，其鲁棒性尚未被系统研究。

Method: 采用多准则决策分析（MCDA）方法，引入两种鲁棒性指标：数据集组成鲁棒性（排名对数据集组成变化的敏感性）和排名方案鲁棒性（对聚合方法变化的敏感性），对五种语言（英、法、德、印地、西）在九类任务上进行系统敏感性分析，并扩展至约230种语言。

Result: 基于LLM的大规模模型通常是鲁棒的顶尖模型，但在检索任务上表现不一致；任务无关分析显示只有少数模型在所有任务、排名方案和数据子样本上保持稳定强表现。

Conclusion: 多语言文本嵌入模型的基准排名对评测设计选择敏感，需要更系统的鲁棒性分析来确保结论的可靠性，所提出的鲁棒性指标为未来基准设计提供了方法论指导。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多语言文本嵌入模型在MTEB基准上的鲁棒性，分析不同任务、语言和聚合方法下排名稳定性的元研究，属于多模态/文本嵌入评测基准的鲁棒性分析，与[多模态评测与基准]（multimodal_benchmark）高度相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on multilingual embedding robustness, not aligned with user's priority on multimodal generation, video/image generation, or efficient inference. Signals: Multilingual text embeddings, MTEB benchmark robustness, Ranking sensitivity analysis, Not multimodal generation, No video/image generation

Abstract: Large-scale multilingual text embedding models play crucial role in both research and industry, yet their behavior in language-specific, multi-task settings remains insufficiently understood. Although benchmarking platforms such as MTEB report results across more than 250 languages, conclusions about model superiority often depend on implicit choices of dataset compositions and performance aggregation methods. To address this gap, we present a meta-study of multilingual model performance robustness in MTEB, applying a diverse set of multi-criteria decision-making ranking schemes and introducing two robustness indicators: dataset-composition robustness (sensitivity of rankings to changing dataset compositions) and ranking-scheme robustness (sensitivity to aggregation method change). They enable systematic sensitivity analysis of whether benchmarking conclusions remain stable under different evaluation designs. We conduct an in-depth analysis on five languages (English, French, German, Hindi, and Spanish) across nine tasks (e.g., classification, clustering, retrieval) and release results for approximately 230 additional languages. The task-specific analyses show that large-scale LLM-based models are often robust top performers, though not uniformly (e.g., in retrieval task), while task-agnostic results reveal that only a small subset of models remains consistently strong across tasks, ranking schemes, and data subsamples.

</details>


### [182] [ERGeoBench:A Comprehensive Benchmark for Embodied Reasoning and Geo-localization in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31251)
*Kaiwen Xue, Tao Wei, Guoxin Zhang, Zhonghong Ou, Kaoyan Lu, Yu Feng, Yifan Zhu, Haoran Luo*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

TL;DR: ERGeoBench是一个面向具身地理定位的多模态大模型诊断基准，包含2207个全球街景全景图，在单视图、全景视图和具身视图三种渐进设置下评估模型的感知、空间、常识推理和地理定位能力。实验表明现有MLLM能推断高层地理语义，但在细粒度感知、度量定位和跨视图空间一致性上仍存在困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型在具身智能方面展现出潜力，但具身地理定位（embodied geo-localization）由于缺乏细粒度评测而未被充分探索。现有基准无法系统诊断模型在主动获取观测（通过偏航、俯仰、缩放等操作）条件下的地理定位能力。

Method: 构建了包含2207个全球分布街景全景图的ERGeoBench基准，设计了三种渐进评测设置（单视图、全景视图、具身视图），并定义了四种互补能力维度（基础感知、空间意识、常识推理、地理定位推理），对主流闭源和开源MLLM进行系统评测。

Result: 现有MLLM能推断高层地理语义（如国家/城市），但在细粒度感知操作、度量定位和跨视图空间一致性上表现不佳。地理定位能力与其他能力维度高度相关，表明准确定位依赖于感知、空间推理和常识推理的整合。

Conclusion: ERGeoBench为诊断和推进类人具身地理定位提供了统一框架，揭示了当前MLLM在该任务上的关键瓶颈，为未来研究指明了方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了ERGeoBench，一个面向具身地理定位的多模态大模型评测基准，属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一[CANONICAL]子主题。

Importance reason: Benchmark for embodied geo-localization is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct alignment with user's focus on generation and efficiency. Signals: Multimodal understanding benchmark, Embodied geo-localization, Not in priority subtopics, No generation or efficiency focus, Negative keywords absent but not relevant

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have shown strong potential as embodied agents, yet embodied geo-localization remains underexplored due to the lack of fine-grained evaluation. We introduce ERGeoBench, a diagnostic benchmark for vision-driven embodied geo-localization. ERGeoBench evaluates models under three progressive settings -- single-view, panorama-view, and embodied-view -- where agents may actively acquire observations through sequential changes in yaw, pitch, and zoom. The benchmark contains 2,207 globally distributed street-view panoramas and measures four complementary capabilities: foundational perception, spatial awareness, common sense reasoning, and geo-localization reasoning. Evaluations of leading proprietary and open-source MLLMs show that current models can infer high-level geographic semantics, but still struggle with fine-grained perceptual operations, metric localization, and spatial consistency across views. We further observe that geo-localization is strongly correlated with the other capability dimensions, suggesting that accurate localization depends on integrated perception, spatial reasoning, and commonsense inference rather than isolated visual recognition. Overall, ERGeoBench provides a unified framework for diagnosing and advancing human-like embodied geo-localization. Project Page:

</details>


### [183] [A Visually Impaired Assistance Benchmark for VLM-as-a-Judge Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31351)
*Yi Zhao, Siqi Wang, Zhe Hu, Yushi Li, Jing Li*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (79.09) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 VIABLE，首个面向视障辅助（VIA）场景的 VLM-as-a-Judge 评测基准，包含 30 万+评判样本和 Effectiveness-Impartiality-Stability 评估框架。实验发现现有 VLM 评判者普遍不可靠（最强模型 GPT-5.4 单故障诊断准确率仅 52.6%，自偏好率高达 94.2%），并提出了 VIA-Judge-Agent 推理时增强框架来提升诊断准确率和下游响应质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 辅助视障（VIA）任务依赖人工评估，成本高昂。VLM-as-a-Judge 范式在通用领域有潜力，但在 VIA 场景下是否可信尚不清楚。本文旨在系统评估 VLM 作为评判者在 VIA 任务中的可靠性。

Method: 构建 VIABLE 基准，包含 300K+ 评判样本，覆盖三个场景，提出 Effectiveness-Impartiality-Stability 评估框架和 12 种故障模式分类。基于此系统评测 7 个不同规模的 VLM 评判者。提出 VIA-Judge-Agent，一种模型无关的推理时增强框架，通过视觉证据提取和分类引导工作流提升评判质量。

Result: 现有 VLM 评判者在所有评估维度上均不可靠：GPT-5.4 单故障诊断准确率仅 52.6%，自偏好率高达 94.2%；开源模型存在严重偏见且对抗性脆弱。VIA-Judge-Agent 在诊断准确率和下游 VIA 响应偏好上均取得正向提升。

Conclusion: VLM-as-a-Judge 在 VIA 场景下尚不可靠，需要专门的评测基准和增强方法。VIA-Judge-Agent 提供了一种有效的推理时增强方案，为构建可信的 VIA 自动评估系统指明了方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 VIABLE，一个针对视障辅助（VIA）场景的 VLM-as-a-Judge 评测基准，属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一规范主题。同时涉及 AIGC 评测（aigc_evaluation_benchmark）方向，但核心贡献是 VLM 作为评判者的多模态评测基准，因此主方向为多模态理解与生成。

Importance reason: Focuses on VLM-as-a-Judge for visually impaired assistance, which is a niche evaluation benchmark not aligned with your priority subtopics. Signals: VLM-as-a-Judge, visually impaired assistance, evaluation benchmark, reliability analysis, inference-time enhancement

Abstract: AI-based Visually Impaired Assistance (VIA) remains challenging, largely due to the high cost of human evaluation. The VLM-as-a-Judge paradigm may offer a promising alternative, although it has mostly been studied in general domains. We therefore ask whether such judges can be trusted for VIA tasks. To investigate this question, we introduce VIABLE (Visually Impaired Assistance Benchmark for VLM-as-a-Judge Evaluation), the first benchmark for VLM-as-a-Judge evaluation in VIA. VIABLE contains over 300K judgment samples across three scenarios and introduces an Effectiveness--Impartiality--Stability framework with a 12-mode failure taxonomy. Based on VIABLE, our systematic study of seven judges across different model scales shows that existing models are largely unreliable across all evaluation axes. The strongest judge, GPT-5.4, achieves only 52.6% single-failure diagnostic accuracy, yet exhibits the highest self-preference rate at 94.2%; while open-source judges are strongly biased and adversarially fragile. To address these issues, we propose VIA-Judge-Agent, a model-agnostic inference-time harness that augments judges with visual evidence extraction and a taxonomy-guided workflow. It enables positive improvements in diagnostic accuracy and downstream VIA responses more preferred by BLV users. Data and code are available at:

</details>


### [184] [SVI-Bench: A Dynamic Microworld for Strategic Video Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31529)
*Yulu Pan, Han Yi, Seongsu Ha, Md Mohaiminul Islam, Benjamin Zhang, Lorenzo Torresani, Gedas Bertasius*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (61.46) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SVI-Bench 是一个大规模视频智能评测基准，利用团队体育作为动态微世界，包含约 35K 小时广播视频、15M 标注动作等数据，构建了从动态场景理解、因果推理、战略模拟到智能体合成的四层九任务评测体系。实验发现模型在感知任务上表现尚可（~73%），但在因果和战略推理任务上急剧下降，最强模型在智能体任务上仅达 5% 准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频基准要么使用真实世界视频（缺乏因果和战略问题的可验证真实标签），要么使用合成环境（缺乏真实多智能体系统的复杂性）。作者希望弥合这一差距，构建一个兼具真实世界复杂性和可验证性的视频智能评测基准。

Method: 利用团队体育（篮球、足球、冰球）作为动态微世界，通过数据引擎将原始比赛数据转化为密集交叉引用的语料库，包含广播视频、动作标注、专家解说、比赛报告和统计数据。评测分为四个层次共 9 个任务：动态场景理解、因果推理、战略模拟和智能体合成。

Result: 强多模态和智能体基线模型在感知任务（细粒度动作 QA）上达到约 73% 准确率，但在因果推理和战略模拟任务上急剧下降。最难的智能体任务中，最强模型仅达到 5% 准确率，揭示了当前模型在战略视频智能方面的严重不足。

Conclusion: SVI-Bench 揭示了现有模型在视频智能方面存在"能力悬崖"：感知能力尚可，但因果推理、战略模拟和自主智能体能力严重不足。该基准为未来视频智能研究提供了有价值的评测平台。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 SVI-Bench，一个面向战略视频智能（从感知到因果推理、模拟再到策略规划）的大规模多模态评测基准，属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一规范主题。同时涉及视频理解与生成（视频世界模型/模拟）方向，因此也匹配 multimodal_generation 下的 video_world_model。

Importance reason: Strong benchmark for multimodal understanding and reasoning, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous VLM or autoregressive generation. Signals: Large-scale benchmark, team sports microworld, four-pillar hierarchy, capability cliff in reasoning, no direct alignment with priority subtopics

Abstract: True video intelligence demands more than recognizing what is visible: it requires reasoning about why events unfold, predicting what would change under different conditions, and deciding what to do next. We refer to this progression, from perception through causal reasoning and simulation to strategic planning, as Strategic Video Intelligence (SVI). No existing benchmark evaluates this capability stack: in-the-wild videos lack verifiable ground truth for causal and strategic questions, while synthetic environments sacrifice the complexity of real multi-agent systems. To bridge this gap, we introduce SVI-Bench, a large-scale benchmark that leverages team sports as a dynamic microworld, combining the complexity of real-world multi-agent interaction (10-22 agents making coordinated decisions under adversarial pressure) with the verifiability of explicit rules and definitive outcomes. SVI-Bench comprises approximately 35K hours of broadcast video, 15M annotated actions, 15K hours of expert commentary, 23K game reports, and 103K structured statistical records across basketball, soccer, and hockey, all constructed via a data engine that transforms raw game data into a dense, cross-referenced corpus. We organize evaluation into 9 tasks spanning a progressive four-pillar hierarchy: Dynamic Scene Understanding, Causal Reasoning, Strategic Simulation, and Agentic Synthesis. Evaluating strong multimodal and agentic baselines, we find a capability cliff: models perform competently on perceptual tasks, achieving approximately 73% on fine-grained action QA, but degrade sharply at each successive cognitive level. Agentic tasks prove hardest: the strongest model achieves only 5% accuracy when required to autonomously gather and integrate evidence across a corpus of 1.8M clips.

</details>


### [185] [EGOSTREAM: A Diagnostic Benchmark for Streaming Episodic Memory in Egocentric Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31557)
*Rosario Forte, Giuseppe Lando, Antonino Furnari*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (76.16) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: EGOSTREAM是一个用于第一视角视频流式情景记忆评测的诊断基准，包含2250个问题，覆盖细节、空间、时间、事件、社交、因果和前瞻记忆7个认知维度，并引入答案有效窗口（AVW）概念，将问题扩展为8528个召回条件评估。在统一Qwen3-VL框架下对多种记忆管理机制进行基准测试，发现所有方法均远低于实时处理速度，最佳方法准确率仅约45%，揭示了当前架构的严重不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前流式视频基准缺乏诊断模型记忆内容和记忆时长的工具，无法区分模型真正的遗忘与自然世界状态变化导致的答案失效，亟需一个细粒度的诊断基准来评估和指导流式情景记忆能力的提升。

Method: 构建EGOSTREAM基准，包含2250个精心设计的问题，覆盖7个认知记忆维度；引入答案有效窗口（AVW）概念，将问题扩展为8528个召回条件评估，支持从即时到超长期的受控测试；设计统一的流式MLLM框架，集成滑动窗口、注意力汇聚、KV缓存剪枝、合并和卸载等多种记忆管理机制，在Qwen3-VL骨干上进行系统比较。

Result: 所有记忆管理机制均远低于实时处理速度（>1秒/帧），最佳方法准确率仅约45%。不同机制展现出截然不同的记忆特征：token剪枝在保留细节和时间结构方面显著优于token合并，而量化卸载有助于超长期召回。总体准确率相近但记忆轮廓差异显著。

Conclusion: EGOSTREAM揭示了当前流式MLLM架构在情景记忆方面的严重不足，为未来研究提供了诊断测试平台。现有方法在速度和准确性上均存在巨大差距，需要新的架构设计来弥合这些差距。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了EGOSTREAM，一个用于第一视角视频流式情景记忆评测的诊断基准，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）子主题，因为其核心贡献是构建了一个新的多模态评测基准，涵盖7个认知维度的2250个问题，用于诊断模型在流式视频中的记忆能力。

Importance reason: Strong diagnostic benchmark for egocentric memory, but outside user's priority subtopics and lacks direct relevance to generation or efficiency. Signals: streaming episodic memory benchmark, egocentric vision, seven cognitive dimensions, answer validity window, Qwen3-VL baseline

Abstract: Continuous episodic memory is a core capability for autonomous agents operating in dynamic, real-world environments, yet current streaming video benchmarks provide limited tools for diagnosing what models remember and for how long. We introduce \egostream, a diagnostic benchmark for streaming episodic memory evaluation in egocentric vision. \egostream organizes 2,250 curated questions along seven cognitive dimensions: detail, spatial, temporal, event, social, causal, and prospective memory. We introduce the Answer Validity Window (AVW), which specifies the temporal span an answer remains valid as the observed scene evolves. This allows us to expand the questions into 8,528 recall-conditioned evaluations, enabling controlled testing from instant to ultra-long-term recall while separating genuine model forgetting from natural world-state changes. We rigorously establish baseline performance through a unified streaming MLLM framework that compares several state-of-the-art memory-management mechanisms, covering sliding windows, attention sinks, KV-cache pruning, merging, and offloading. Experiments within a unified Qwen3-VL backbone reveal that comparable aggregate accuracies mask starkly different memory profiles. For instance, token pruning preserves fine-grained details and temporal structure significantly better than token merging, while quantized offloading rescues ultra-long-term recall. Ultimately, all mechanisms operate well below real-time (>1s per frame), and top performing methods ceil at about 45\% accuracy, exposing critical gaps in current architectures. \egostream provides the diagnostic testbed needed to close these gaps.

</details>


### [186] [nuReasoning: A Reasoning-Centric Dataset and Benchmark for Long-Tail Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31572)
*Zhiyu Huang, Johnson Liu, Rui Song, Zewei Zhou, Ruining Yang, Yun Zhang, Tianhui Cai, Hanyin Zhang, Mingxuan Gao, Valeria Xu, Jiali Chen, Yishan Shen, Yiluan Guo, Tony, Jiaqi Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Importance: 普通相关 (57.96) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: nuReasoning 是一个面向自动驾驶长尾场景推理的大规模真实世界数据集与基准，包含 20,000 个 20 秒长的视频片段，带有空间推理、决策推理和反事实推理的人工验证标注。实验表明，在该数据集上微调 VLM 能显著提升驾驶场景问答能力，且将推理监督融入 VLA 训练可改善规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶数据集和基准主要关注感知、预测或规划，缺乏对长尾场景中推理能力（常识推理、空间关系、智能体交互、安全决策）的充分监督和评测。因此需要构建一个以推理为中心的自动驾驶数据集和基准。

Method: 基于 nuScenes/nuPlan 数据，构建包含 20,000 个 20 秒片段的多城市数据集，提供同步多相机图像、LiDAR、HD 地图、目标标注以及人工验证的推理标注（空间推理、决策推理、反事实推理）。支持推理评测和规划评测，并通过微调 VLM 和 VLA 训练验证推理监督对驾驶性能的影响。

Result: 在 nuReasoning 上微调 VLM 显著提升了驾驶场景问答能力；将推理监督融入 VLA 训练后，即使在推理时关闭文本推理输出，规划性能仍得到改善。

Conclusion: nuReasoning 为评估和改进长尾场景中鲁棒、可解释、推理驱动的自动驾驶系统提供了基础性数据集和基准。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个面向自动驾驶长尾场景推理的数据集和基准（nuReasoning），包含空间推理、决策推理和反事实推理标注，并支持推理评测和规划评测。这属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）范畴，是[CANONICAL] subtopic，因此直接复用。

Importance reason: Focuses on autonomous driving reasoning, not aligned with user's priority subtopics on continuous VLM, video generation, or unified multimodal models. Signals: autonomous driving reasoning, long-tail scenarios, spatial/decision/counterfactual reasoning, VLM fine-tuning improves planning, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Reasoning is essential for autonomous driving (AD) in long-tail scenarios, where vehicles must apply commonsense knowledge, understand spatial relations, infer agent interactions, and make safe decisions. However, existing AD datasets and benchmarks mainly target perception, prediction, or planning, and provide limited supervision for reasoning over realistic long-tail driving scenes. We introduce nuReasoning, a large-scale real-world dataset and benchmark for reasoning-centric AD. Following the lineage of nuScenes and nuPlan, nuReasoning advances real-world AD datasets and benchmarks toward reasoning in long-tail driving scenarios. The dataset contains 20,000 clips, each 20 seconds long, collected across multiple cities, with synchronized multi-camera images, LiDAR data, HD maps, object annotations, and human-verified reasoning annotations spanning Spatial Reasoning, Decision Reasoning, and Counterfactual Reasoning. Unlike prior datasets that focus primarily on visual question answering, nuReasoning supports both reasoning evaluation and planning evaluation, enabling a direct study of how reasoning supervision affects driving performance. Experiments show that fine-tuning VLMs on nuReasoning substantially improves driving-specific question answering, while incorporating reasoning supervision into VLA training improves planning performance even when textual reasoning outputs are disabled at inference time. These results establish nuReasoning as a foundation for evaluating and improving robust, interpretable, reasoning-driven AD systems in realistic long-tail settings.

</details>


### [187] [SOCO: Benchmarking Semantic Object Correspondence in Vision Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31597)
*Olaf Dünkel, Basavaraj Sunagad, Haoran Wang, David T. Hoffmann, Christian Theobalt, Adam Kortylewski*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (57.96) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SOCO 是一个新的语义对象对应（Semantic Object Correspondence）评测基准，包含 100 个类别、超过 100 万对关键点标注，并引入对应类型分类法。实验发现视觉基础模型编码了强语义结构但跨类别对应迁移差，LVLM 在文本引导定位上强于跨图像视觉匹配，且对应性能比 ImageNet 分类更能预测下游密集任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉基础模型的评估协议不一致，缺乏对结构化对象理解（特别是部件级语义对应）的系统评测，且缺少支持视觉语言模型评估的细粒度部件级标注。

Method: 构建 SOCO 基准：定义对应类型分类法（实例级、类别级、跨类别对应），在 100 个类别上标注超过 100 万对功能上有意义的关键点，并为关键点提供语言描述以支持 LVLM 评估。在多种视觉基础模型和 LVLM 上进行系统实验。

Result: (i) 视觉基础骨干编码了强语义结构但跨类别对应迁移差，仅部分捕获部件位置；(ii) LVLM 在文本提示定位上强于视觉参考跨图像匹配，存在语言定位与细粒度视觉对应之间的差距；(iii) 对应性能比 ImageNet 分类更能预测分割、跟踪、3D 姿态估计和 3D 检测等下游密集任务表现。

Conclusion: SOCO 可作为评估视觉和多模态基础模型中结构化、部件级表示质量的基准，揭示了当前模型在细粒度语义对应上的局限性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 SOCO 基准，用于评估视觉基础模型和视觉语言模型在语义对象对应（semantic object correspondence）上的细粒度部件级理解能力，属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一 [CANONICAL] 子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on vision benchmark for object correspondence, not aligned with user's priority on generation or continuous VLM. Signals: Benchmark for semantic correspondence, Vision foundation models, LVLM evaluation, Weak cross-category transfer, Dense task prediction

Abstract: Measuring structured object understanding in vision foundation models remains challenging due to inconsistent evaluation protocols and limited part-level supervision. Semantic correspondence (SC) evaluates this capability by testing whether object parts can be matched across instances and categories under large variations in appearance, viewpoint, and geometry. To enable a systematic SC evaluation, we introduce SOCO, a new benchmark for Semantic Object Correspondence that introduces a taxonomy of correspondence types and provides consistent, functionally meaningful keypoint annotations across 100 categories and over 1M correspondence pairs. In addition, SOCO includes keypoint language descriptions, enabling the evaluation of large vision-language models (LVLMs) and their fine-grained part-level understanding. Comprehensive experiments reveal that (i) vision foundation backbones encode strong semantic structure but transfer correspondences poorly across related categories and only partially capture object-part position, (ii) LVLMs are stronger at text-prompted part localization than at visual-reference cross-image matching, exposing a gap between language-grounded localization and fine-grained visual correspondence, and (iii) correspondence performance predicts performance on dense downstream tasks, including segmentation, tracking, 3D pose estimation, and 3D detection, more strongly than ImageNet classification. Together, these findings position SOCO as a benchmark for structured, part-level representation quality in vision and multimodal foundation models.

</details>

### [188] [TRINE: A Token-Aware, Runtime-Adaptive FPGA Inference Engine for Multimodal AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22867)
*Hyunwoo Oh, Hanning Chen, Sanggeon Yun, Yang Ni, Suyeon Jang, Behnam Khaleghi, Fei Wen, Mohsen Imani*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.LG, cs.AR

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 值得关注 (67.24) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: TRINE 是一个面向多模态 AI 的单比特流 FPGA 推理加速引擎与编译器，通过统一计算模式（DDMM/SDDMM/SpMM）、运行时模式切换、token 剪枝和依赖感知层卸载（DALO）等技术，在 20-21W 功耗下实现比 RTX 4090 最高 22.57 倍、比 Jetson Orin Nano 最高 6.86 倍的延迟降低，支持 ViT、CNN、GNN、Transformer 等多种架构的端到端多模态推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态模型（ViT、CNN、GNN、Transformer 等混合架构）在嵌入式平台上面临计算/内存模式差异大、硬实时目标难以满足的挑战，现有 FPGA 加速方案通常需要重新配置或仅支持单一架构，缺乏统一的端到端多模态推理加速方案。

Method: TRINE 将各层统一为 DDMM/SDDMM/SpMM 计算模式，映射到可运行时模式切换的 PE 阵列（支持权重/输出驻留脉动阵列、1xCS SIMD、可路由加法树 RADT）；设计宽度匹配的两阶段 top-k 单元实现流式 token 剪枝；提出依赖感知层卸载（DALO）在可重构处理单元间重叠独立内核；结合 int8 量化实现高效推理。

Result: 在 Alveo U50 和 ZCU104 上评估，TRINE 相比 RTX 4090 延迟降低最高 22.57 倍，相比 Jetson Orin Nano 降低最高 6.86 倍，功耗仅 20-21W；token 剪枝在 ViT 密集型流水线上带来最高 7.8 倍加速；DALO 贡献最高 79% 的吞吐量提升；int8 量化下精度损失 <2.5%。

Conclusion: TRINE 通过统一的硬件架构和编译优化，在单个比特流中实现了对视觉、语言和图神经网络等多种模态模型的高效端到端推理加速，在延迟和能效上达到 SOTA，为嵌入式多模态 AI 部署提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出面向多模态AI的FPGA推理加速引擎TRINE，核心贡献在于通过硬件加速、量化、token剪枝、依赖感知任务调度等手段提升多模态模型推理效率，属于多模态高效化与轻量化（efficient_multimodal）这一[CANONICAL]子主题。同时涉及多模态生成方向的生成模型加速与高效化（efficient_generation）子主题。

Importance reason: Strong FPGA acceleration for multimodal inference with token pruning and runtime adaptation, but lacks direct focus on user's priority subtopics like continuous VLM or autoregressive generation. Signals: FPGA inference engine, multimodal AI acceleration, token pruning, runtime mode switching, dependency-aware layer offloading

Abstract: Multimodal stacks that mix ViTs, CNNs, GNNs, and transformer NLP strain embedded platforms because their compute/memory patterns diverge and hard real-time targets leave little slack. TRINE is a single-bitstream FPGA accelerator and compiler that executes end-to-end multimodal inference without reconfiguration. Layers are unified as DDMM/SDDMM/SpMM and mapped to a mode-switchable engine that toggles at runtime among weight/output-stationary systolic, 1xCS SIMD, and a routable adder tree (RADT) on a shared PE array. A width-matched, two-stage top-k unit enables in-stream token pruning, while dependency-aware layer offloading (DALO) overlaps independent kernels across reconfigurable processing units to sustain utilization. Evaluated on Alveo U50 and ZCU104, TRINE reduces latency by up to 22.57x vs. RTX 4090 and 6.86x vs. Jetson Orin Nano at 20-21 W; token pruning alone yields up to 7.8x on ViT-heavy pipelines, and DALO contributes up to 79% throughput improvement. With int8 quantization, accuracy drops remain <2.5% across representative tasks, delivering state-of-the-art latency and energy efficiency for unified vision, language, and graph workloads-in one bitstream.

</details>


### [189] [VisionPulse: Dynamic Visual Sparsity for Efficient Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31457)
*Hengbo Xu, Shengjie Jin, Yanbiao Ma, Zhiwu Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 值得关注 (70.48) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: VisionPulse 提出了一种推理时逐步视觉 token 剪枝框架，通过轻量级视觉注意力质量估计每步的保留预算，仅保留最关键的 5% 视觉 token，同时将推理链缩短 11.2%，且几乎不损失精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常在预填充阶段剪枝视觉 token，假设推理过程中所需的视觉证据是静态的。但本文实证发现视觉证据具有强烈的步骤依赖性：每个解码步骤仅需少量关键视觉 token，且关键集合随推理过程动态变化。此外，冗余的视觉上下文会引导模型关注与查询无关的区域，导致推理链变长。因此需要一种推理时动态剪枝方法。

Method: VisionPulse 包含两个核心组件：(1) 轻量级视觉注意力质量估计器，利用视觉注意力质量与 LMM 有效视觉 token 使用率之间的强正相关性，为每个解码步骤估计保留预算；(2) 在该预算下仅保留最关键的视觉 token，实现逐步视觉稀疏化，从而过滤冗余视觉上下文并缩短推理链。

Result: 实验表明，VisionPulse 每步仅保留 5% 的视觉 token，推理链缩短 11.2%，同时准确率几乎保持不变，显著降低了推理开销。

Conclusion: VisionPulse 验证了推理过程中视觉证据的动态特性，并提出了一种高效的逐步剪枝策略，在保持性能的同时大幅降低计算成本，为 LMM 的高效部署提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 VisionPulse，一种在推理过程中对视觉 token 进行动态稀疏剪枝的方法，以降低多模态大模型（LMM）的推理开销。这属于多模态高效化与轻量化（efficient_multimodal）范畴，该 [CANONICAL] 子主题涵盖 token 压缩、稀疏推理等效率优化方法，与本文高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient multimodal reasoning via dynamic visual sparsity, aligning with user's priority on model efficiency and continuous VLM reasoning. Signals: dynamic visual token pruning, step-wise retention budget, inference-time efficiency, multimodal reasoning acceleration, visual attention mass estimation

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of large multimodal models (LMMs), inference-time overhead has become a key bottleneck for real-world deployment. Existing methods typically prune visual tokens at prefill, assuming the required visual evidence remains static during reasoning. However, we empirically show that visual evidence is strongly step-dependent: only a sparse subset of visual tokens is critical at each decoding step, and the critical set evolves across reasoning. Furthermore, we identify a coupled bottleneck where redundant visual context can steer the model toward query-irrelevant regions, lengthening the reasoning trace. Guided by these insights, we propose VisionPulse, a step-wise visual token pruning framework during reasoning. VisionPulse computes a lightweight visual attention mass to estimate the step-wise retention budget by exploiting its strong positive correlation with LMMs' effective visual token usage and retain only the most critical tokens under this budget. By enforcing visual sparsity during reasoning, VisionPulse filters redundant visual context while preserving relevant visual evidence, shortening reasoning traces naturally. Extensive experiments show that VisionPulse only retains 5% of visual tokens per step with reasoning traces shortened by 11.2%, while keeping accuracy almost unchanged.

</details>


### [190] [Linear Scaling Video VLMs for Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31598)
*Cristobal Eyzaguirre, Jiajun Wu, Juan Carlos Niebles*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 高优先级 (93.65) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: StateKV 是一种推理时方法，通过固定容量的重要性循环状态携带跨帧上下文，将预训练长视频 VLM 的视频预填充复杂度从二次降至线性，在多个长视频基准上接近全自注意力性能，且无需微调或架构改动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频 VLM 的视频编码器依赖时空自注意力，计算和延迟随帧数二次增长，难以扩展到长视频和流式场景。现有效率方法（如帧/令牌丢弃、粗粒度注意力近似）虽提升可扩展性但损失精度。

Method: StateKV 设计了一个固定容量的重要性循环状态（recurrent state）来跨帧传递上下文，同时保留第二个完整的逐帧缓存用于解码。该方法在推理时直接适配预训练模型，无需微调或架构改动。

Result: 在三个长视频基准上，使用七个模型（涵盖三个模型家族和多个规模），StateKV 保持接近全自注意力的性能，并一致优于滑动窗口/基于近因的流式近似方法。同时降低了视频预填充的 FLOPs，在固定计算预算下可使用更大模型获得更强精度。

Conclusion: StateKV 提供了一种实用的、无需训练的推理时方法，有效将长视频 VLM 扩展到线性时间预填充，为可扩展的长视频理解迈出了实际一步。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 StateKV 方法，通过固定容量的循环状态将长视频 VLM 的预填充复杂度从二次降至线性，属于多模态模型的高效推理优化，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题"多模态高效化与轻量化"（efficient_multimodal）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient inference for long video understanding, aligning with model efficiency and multimodal understanding priorities. Signals: linear scaling video prefill, inference-time method, no fine-tuning needed, strong benchmark results, matches user's efficient inference and multimodal understanding focus

Abstract: Video vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used in long-horizon and streaming settings, yet most video encoders still rely on spatiotemporal self-attention, causing compute and latency to grow quadratically with the number of frames. Existing efficiency methods improve scalability but often lose accuracy relative to full self-attention, for example through aggressive frame/token dropping or coarse attention approximations. We introduce StateKV, an inference-time method that adapts pretrained long-video VLMs to linear-time video prefill by carrying cross-frame context in a fixed-capacity, importance-based recurrent state, paired with a second full per-frame cache used for decoding. Across three long-video benchmarks and seven models spanning three families and multiple scales, StateKV remains close to full self-attention and consistently outperforms dominant sliding-window / recency-based streaming approximations, without fine-tuning or architectural changes. StateKV also reduces video-prefill cost measured FLOPs, enabling stronger accuracy at a fixed compute budget by running larger models. These results suggest a practical step toward scalable long-video understanding.

</details>

### [191] [VLM3: Vision Language Models Are Native 3D Learners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30561)
*Zhipeng Cai, Zhuang Liu, Yunyang Xiong, Zechun Liu, Vikas Chandra, Yangyang Shi*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 空间与 3D 多模态推理

Importance: 值得关注 (71.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: VLM3提出了一种极简方法，通过焦距统一、文本像素引用和数据混合缩放，使标准VLM无需架构修改即可原生掌握深度估计、像素对应、相机位姿估计等多种3D任务，在深度估计上达到0.9的准确率，匹配专家视觉模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前3D理解任务严重依赖专家视觉模型和复杂的任务特定设计，而VLM虽然在语义理解上表现出色，但在3D理解方面能力有限。作者希望证明VLM本身就是原生的3D学习者，只需简单设计即可掌握多种3D任务。

Method: VLM3的核心方法包含三个关键要素：(1) 焦距统一——将不同焦距的图像统一到标准焦距，消除相机内参差异；(2) 基于文本的像素引用——用文本坐标表示像素位置，使VLM能通过文本方式处理空间信息；(3) 数据混合与缩放——混合多种3D任务数据进行大规模训练。该方法无需改变模型架构、无需大模型、无需繁重数据增强或复杂损失函数。

Result: VLM3在深度估计任务上将准确率从0.84提升至0.9，同时在像素对应、相机位姿估计和物体级3D理解等任务上达到专家视觉模型的精度水平，且保持了标准VLM架构和基于文本的训练范式。

Conclusion: VLM3证明了标准VLM通过简单设计即可成为原生3D学习者，为简单可扩展的3D学习开辟了新范式，挑战了3D理解需要复杂专家模型设计的传统认知。

Classification reason: 该论文研究如何让标准VLM掌握3D深度估计、像素对应、相机位姿估计等3D任务，属于空间与3D多模态推理范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "空间与3D多模态推理"（spatial_3d_reasoning）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Shows standard VLM can master 3D tasks without architecture changes, aligning with unified multimodal and efficient inference priorities. Signals: unified multimodal model, efficient inference, 3D understanding, depth estimation, camera pose estimation

Abstract: Vision Language Models (VLMs) enable a unified model to solve various vision tasks through prompting. They have shown promising performance in semantic understanding. However, 3D understanding still largely relies on expert vision models with complex task-specific designs. The key argument this work wants to make is that VLMs are native 3D learners. Our in-depth large scale study shows that 1) focal length unification, 2) text-based pixel reference and 3) data mixture and scaling, are all you need for effective 3D learning. Model architecture changes, large models, heavy data augmentations, and complex losses including the regression formulation, many of which form the foundation of expert vision models, are actually not necessary conditions. As a result, we propose VLM3, a scalable method with the simplest design that enables standard VLMs to master diverse 3D tasks. VLM3 not only advances the VLM depth estimation accuracy by a large margin (0.84 -> 0.9), but also enables diverse 3D tasks such as pixel correspondence, camera pose estimation and object-level 3D understanding, matching expert vision model accuracy while maintaining standard architectures and text-based training. We believe VLM3 opens up a new paradigm for simple and scalable 3D learning.

</details>


### [192] [SpatialAct: Probing Spatial Reasoning-to-Action Capabilities of VLM Agents in 3D Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31148)
*Tianhui Liu, Jie Feng, Zhiheng Zheng, Shengyuan Wang, Yiming Guo, Yanxin Xi, Hangyu Fan, Yong Li, Pan Hui*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 空间与 3D 多模态推理

Importance: 普通相关 (59.89) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 SpatialAct，一个基于模拟器的基准，用于探测 VLM 智能体在 3D 场景中的动作条件空间推理能力。实验揭示了当前 VLM 存在明显的"推理到动作"鸿沟：它们在孤立空间推理任务上表现良好，但在多轮交互反馈中难以维持连贯的空间信念并产生可靠动作，显著落后于人类表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 在观察条件空间感知和推理任务上表现不错，但能否构建连贯的空间理解、据此采取行动并通过多轮反馈优化动作，这一问题尚未被系统研究。作者希望填补这一空白，系统探测 VLM 智能体的动作条件空间推理能力。

Method: 提出了 SpatialAct 基准，包含三个层次的任务设置：(1) 最具挑战性的多轮交互式精炼（Multi-turn Interactive Refinement）；(2) 分解后的单步错误检测与修复（Single-step Error Detection and Fix）；(3) 五个基础空间能力任务用于诊断模型失败的根源。基准基于模拟器环境构建，评估 VLM 在 3D 场景中的空间推理到动作的能力。

Result: 实验表明当前 VLM 在孤立空间推理任务上表现良好，但在多轮交互反馈中难以维持连贯的空间信念并产生可靠动作，存在明显的"推理到动作"鸿沟，整体表现显著落后于人类。

Conclusion: 当前 VLM 智能体在动作引起的环境变化下仍缺乏鲁棒的空间状态追踪能力，即使底层控制被抽象化。这表明需要进一步研究如何提升 VLM 在交互式 3D 场景中的空间推理与动作执行能力。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 VLM 智能体在 3D 场景中的空间推理到动作的能力，并提出了一个基于模拟器的基准 SpatialAct。这与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "空间与 3D 多模态推理"（spatial_3d_reasoning）高度匹配，该子主题涵盖空间推理、3D 视觉定位、具身推理等方向。

Importance reason: Probes VLM spatial reasoning-to-action gap but lacks direct connection to priority subtopics like continuous VLM or autoregressive generation. Signals: VLM spatial reasoning, 3D scene benchmark, reasoning-to-action gap, multi-turn feedback, simulator-grounded evaluation

Abstract: Humans can effortlessly perceive spatial layouts, form cognitive representations, reason about spatial relations, and translate such reasoning into actions in everyday 3D environments. Although recent vision-language models (VLMs) have shown promising performance on observation-conditioned spatial perception and reasoning tasks, it remains unclear whether they can build coherent spatial understanding, act upon it, and refine their actions through multi-turn feedback. To study this problem, we introduce \textbf{SpatialAct}, a simulator-grounded benchmark for probing \textit{action-conditioned spatial reasoning} in 3D scenes. Starting from the most challenging setting, Multi-turn Interactive Refinement, we further design its decomposed counterpart, Single-step Error Detection and Fix, together with five fundamental spatial ability tasks to diagnose the underlying causes of model failures. Experiments reveal a clear reasoning-to-action gap: current VLMs can perform well on isolated spatial reasoning tasks, but struggle to maintain coherent spatial beliefs and produce reliable actions during multi-turn feedback, substantially underperforming humans. These results suggest that current VLM agents still lack robust spatial state tracking under action-induced environment changes, even when low-level control is abstracted away.

</details>


### [193] [Multi-Turn Multi-Agent Dialogue for Collaborative Reconstruction Improves VLM Performance on Spatial Reasoning, But Only Barely](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31387)
*Chalamalasetti Kranti, Sherzod Hakimov, David Schlangen*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 空间与 3D 多模态推理

Importance: 普通相关 (53.49) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文通过一个多轮多智能体对话的协作结构重建任务，系统评估了多种VLM在空间推理上的表现。研究发现，VLM在视觉空间推理上仍存在显著困难，详细的文本描述比视觉输入更能提升重建成功率，分解式图像表示有一定帮助，但整体性能有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在多样化环境中依赖视觉输入理解物体和空间布局，并需要与人类通过语言协作。现有VLM在视觉解释、问答和指令跟随方面表现良好，但在需要空间推理的协作对话任务中的能力尚未被充分探索。

Method: 设计了一个协作结构重建任务框架：VLM通过多轮对话重建目标结构。评估了多种开源和闭源VLM，比较了不同交互设置、输入模态和图像表示方式对重建成功率的影响。

Result: VLM在视觉表示的空间推理上仍然困难；详细的文本描述比视觉输入带来更高的重建成功率；分解式图像表示（decomposed image representations）能提升性能，但提升幅度有限。

Conclusion: 当前VLM在视觉空间定位和基于定位的指令生成方面存在明显局限，尚不足以胜任需要精细空间推理的协作机器人任务。

Classification reason: 该论文研究VLM在协作式结构重建任务中的空间推理能力，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的空间与3D多模态推理（spatial_3d_reasoning）这一[CANONICAL]子主题，该子主题明确包含空间推理、具身推理等关键词，与论文内容高度匹配。

Importance reason: Spatial reasoning in VLMs is relevant but the paper's negative findings and narrow scope limit its impact for your priority topics. Signals: VLM spatial reasoning limitations, multi-agent dialogue framework, collaborative reconstruction, text beats vision for spatial tasks, decomposed images help slightly

Abstract: Robots operating in diverse environments rely on visual input to interpret objects and spatial layouts. In human-collaborative tasks, they are expected to communicate this understanding through language. Vision-language models (VLMs) support robotic tasks involving visual interpretation, question answering, and instruction following, but their capabilities in collaborative dialogue tasks requiring spatial reasoning remain underexplored. We study this gap through a collaborative structure-building task that combines visual interpretation, grounding, language-guided interaction, and action generation. We develop a framework in which VLMs use dialogue to reconstruct a target structure from visual and textual inputs. We evaluate open-weight and closed VLMs across interaction settings, input modalities, and image representations. Results show that spatial reasoning over visual representations remains difficult for the evaluated VLMs. Detailed text representations of the target yield higher reconstruction success across modality conditions, while decomposed image representations improve performance. These findings reveal limits in visual spatial grounding and grounded instruction generation for collaborative VLM agents.

</details>


### [194] [VLM-GLoc: Vision-Language Model Enhanced Monte Carlo Localization for Robust Semantic Global Localization in Cluttered Quasi-Static Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30506)
*Shivendra Agrawal, Bradley Hayes*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 空间与 3D 多模态推理

Importance: 普通相关 (54.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: VLM-GLoc 提出一种结合视觉语言模型（VLM）的层次化语义蒙特卡洛定位方法，用于在几何混淆、准静态环境（如杂货店、实验室）中实现鲁棒的全局定位。通过VLM提取判别性语义特征、隐式质量过滤和持久性推理增强，并引入逆语义提议机制，在真实场景中显著优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在杂货店、办公室等几何结构高度相似（如平行货架、重复家具）且存在长尾语义分布和瞬态视觉杂乱的环境中，传统基于几何特征或领域专用视觉管线的全局定位方法效果不佳。作者希望利用VLM的开放词汇语义理解能力来解决这一挑战。

Method: VLM-GLoc 采用层次化语义蒙特卡洛定位框架：1) 使用开放词汇VLM作为统一的语义观测前端，提取判别性文本特征；2) 隐式过滤模糊或动态物体的质量；3) 通过持久性推理进行针对性数据增强；4) 提出逆语义提议机制，通过文本到地图检索来初始化粒子。

Result: 在两个真实环境（3,500 sq. ft. 杂货店 + 手机平台；3,700 sq. ft. 实验室 + 四足机器人）上，VLM-GLoc 分别达到70%和74%的全局定位成功率，大幅超越传统纯几何方法和领域专用基线。

Conclusion: VLM-GLoc 证明了VLM在机器人全局定位中的有效性，通过语义特征提取、质量过滤和持久性推理三个层面的优势，在几何混淆的准静态环境中实现了鲁棒的语义全局定位，为移动机器人在复杂室内环境中的自主导航提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文利用VLM增强机器人全局定位，属于具身智能中的空间推理与定位任务，与[空间与 3D 多模态推理]（spatial_3d_reasoning）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on robotics localization, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous VLM, autoregressive video generation, or world models. Signals: VLM for semantic localization, Monte Carlo localization, quasi-static environments, inverse semantic proposal, real-world robot deployment

Abstract: Global localization in geometrically aliased, quasi-static environments such as grocery stores, offices, schools, and hospitals poses a significant challenge for mobile robots. Grocery stores with parallel aisles and a long tailed distribution of products, as well as offices and labs with repetitive furniture such as chairs, desks, monitors, and doors, exemplify common indoor environments that present geometric and even semantic ambiguity. Traditional approaches rely either on distinct geometric features or on domain-specific vision pipelines that struggle with long-tail semantic distributions and transient visual clutter. We present VLM-GLoc, a method for hierarchical semantic Monte Carlo Localization (MCL) that leverages open-vocabulary Vision-Language Models (VLMs) as a unified semantic observation front-end. We hypothesize a three-fold benefit from VLMs: (1) extracting highly discriminative rich text features, (2) implicit quality filtering of blurry or dynamic objects, and (3) permanence reasoning for targeted data augmentation. We introduce an inverse semantic proposal mechanism that seeds particles via text-to-map retrieval. Evaluated across two real-world environments with different characteristics and two different platforms: a 3,500 sq. ft. grocery store with a cellphone and a 3,700 sq. ft. lab space with a quadruped, VLM-GLoc achieves 70% and 74% global localization success respectively, substantially outperforming traditional geometry-only and domain-specific baselines.

</details>

### [195] [Representation Forcing for Bottleneck-Free Unified Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31604)
*Yuqing Wang, Zhijie Lin, Ceyuan Yang, Yang Zhao, Fei Xiao, Hao He, Qi Zhao, Zihan Ding, Fuyun Wang, Shuai Wang, Youliang Zhang, Haoqi Fan, Xihui Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 统一多模态模型

Importance: 高优先级 (96.67) - Top 1 精读候选

TL;DR: 本文提出 Representation Forcing (RF) 技术，通过让解码器在像素生成前自回归预测视觉表征作为中间 token，使统一多模态模型无需依赖外部预训练 VAE 即可实现高质量的图像理解与生成，消除了结构瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型（UMM）仍然依赖一个冻结的、单独预训练的 VAE 进行图像生成，这引入了结构瓶颈。直接移除 VAE 会导致质量下降，因为模型需要同时从原始像素中学习高层结构和低层细节。本文旨在消除这一瓶颈，实现端到端的无瓶颈统一多模态模型。

Method: 提出 Representation Forcing (RF) 技术：在统一多模态模型中，解码器在生成像素之前，先自回归地预测视觉表征（如 DINOv2 特征）作为中间 token；这些表征 token 保留在上下文中，在同一骨干网络内引导后续的像素扩散生成。通过将表征从感知输出转变为生成目标，RF 消除了对外部生成潜空间（如 VAE）的需求。

Result: 在图像生成方面，基于像素空间的 RF 模型达到了与基于 VAE 的最先进统一模型相当的性能。在图像理解方面，基于像素空间的 RF 模型通常优于其基于 VAE 的变体。实验表明 RF 同时有利于理解与生成任务。

Conclusion: Representation Forcing 是一种有效的技术，能够消除统一多模态模型对独立预训练 VAE 的依赖，实现无瓶颈的端到端统一模型，同时提升理解和生成能力，为构建更简洁、更统一的下一代多模态模型提供了可行方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Representation Forcing 方法，旨在构建无外部 VAE 瓶颈的统一多模态模型（UMM），同时支持理解与生成。这与 multimodal_understanding_generation 方向下的 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "统一多模态模型"（unified_multimodal_model）高度匹配，同时也涉及 multimodal_generation 方向下的统一多模态模型子主题。

Importance reason: Directly advances bottleneck-free unified multimodal models, a top priority subtopic, with a novel technique that eliminates external VAE dependency. Signals: Unified multimodal model, representation forcing, bottleneck-free, continuous visual token, autoregressive prediction

Abstract: Unified multimodal models (UMMs) aim to handle perception and generation in a single model. Yet existing UMMs still rely on a frozen, separately pretrained VAE for image generation, imposing a structural bottleneck. Naively removing it introduces a quality gap, as the model must learn both high-level structure and low-level details from raw pixels. In this paper, we propose Representation Forcing (RF), a technique that closes this gap by making representation prediction a native capability of the model. Concretely, RF forces the decoder to autoregressively predict visual representations as intermediate tokens before pixels; these tokens then stay in context to guide pixel diffusion within the same backbone. By turning representations from perception outputs into generation targets, RF eliminates the need for any external generative latent space. We find that RF benefits both understanding and generation. On image generation, our pixel-space model with RF matches state-of-the-art VAE-based unified models. On image understanding, pixel-space RF generally outperforms its VAE-based variant. Together, these results offer an effective step toward end-to-end, bottleneck-free UMMs.

</details>

## 视觉语言模型与 MLLM [[Back]](#toc)
